(0.0.0 ) oṃ namo'tharvavedāya namaḥ ||

(1,1.1 ) oṃ brahma ha vā idamagra āsītsvayaṃbhvekameva
(1,1.1 ) tadaikṣata
(1,1.1 ) mahadvai yakṣaṃ yadekamevāsmi
(1,1.1 ) hantāhaṃ madeva manmātraṃ dvitīyaṃ devaṃ nirmimā iti
(1,1.1 ) tadabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.1 ) tasya śrāntasya taptasya saṃtaptasya lalāṭe sneho yadārdramājāyata
(1,1.1 ) tenānandat
(1,1.1 ) tadabravīt
(1,1.1 ) mahadvai yakṣaṃ suvedamavidamahamiti
(1,1.1 ) tadyadabravīt
(1,1.1 ) mahadvai yakṣaṃ suvedamavidamahamiti
(1,1.1 ) tasmātsuvedo'bhavat
(1,1.1 ) taṃ vā etaṃ suvedaṃ santaṃ sveda ityācakṣate parokṣeṇa
(1,1.1 ) parokṣapriyā iva hi devā bhavanti pratyakṣadviṣaḥ || 1 ||

(1,1.2 ) sa bhūyo'śrāmyadbhūyo'tapyadbhūya ātmānaṃ samatapat
(1,1.2 ) tasya śrāntasya taptasya saṃtaptasya sarvebhyo romagartebhyaḥ pṛthaksvedadhārāḥ prāsyandanta
(1,1.2 ) tābhiranandat
(1,1.2 ) tadabravīt
(1,1.2 ) ābhirvā ahamidaṃ sarvaṃ dhārayiṣyāmi yadidaṃ kiṃ ca
(1,1.2 ) ābhirvā ahamidaṃ sarvaṃ janayiṣyāmi yadidaṃ kiṃ ca
(1,1.2 ) ābhirvā ahamidaṃ sarvaṃ āpsyāmi yadidaṃ kiṃ ceti
(1,1.2 ) tadyadabravīt
(1,1.2 ) ābhirvā ahamidaṃ sarvaṃ dhārayiṣyāmi yadidaṃ kiṃ ceti
(1,1.2 ) tasmāddhārā abhavan
(1,1.2 ) taddhārāṇāṃ dhārātvaṃ yaccāsu dhriyate
(1,1.2 ) tadyadabravīt
(1,1.2 ) ābhirvā ahamidaṃ sarvaṃ janayiṣyāmi yadidaṃ kiṃ ceti
(1,1.2 ) tasmājjāyā abhavan
(1,1.2 ) tajjāyānāṃ jāyātvaṃ yaccāsu puruṣo jāyate yacca putraḥ [ed.̱ yāc]
(1,1.2 ) punnāma narakamanekaśatatāram
(1,1.2 ) tasmāttrātīti putras
(1,1.2 ) tatputrasya putratvam
(1,1.2 ) tadyadabravīt
(1,1.2 ) ābhirvā ahamidaṃ sarvaṃ āpsyāmi yadidaṃ kiṃ ceti
(1,1.2 ) tasmādāpo'bhavan
(1,1.2 ) tadapāmaptvam
(1,1.2 ) āpnoti vai sa sarvān kāmān yān kāmayate || 2 ||

(1,1.3 ) tā apaḥ sṛṣṭvānvaikṣata
(1,1.3 ) tāsu svāṃ chāyāmapaśyat
(1,1.3 ) tāmasyekṣamāṇasya svayaṃ reto'skandat
(1,1.3 ) tadapsu pratyatiṣṭhat
(1,1.3 ) tāstatraivābhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.3 ) tāḥ śrāntāstaptāḥ saṃtaptāḥ sārdhameva retasā dvaidhamabhavan
(1,1.3 ) tāsāmanyatarā atilavaṇā apeyā asvādvyas
(1,1.3 ) tā aśāntā retaḥ samudraṃ vṛtvātiṣṭhan
(1,1.3 ) athetarāḥ peyāḥ svādvyaḥ śāntās
(1,1.3 ) tāstatraivābhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.3 ) tābhyaḥ śrāntābhyastaptābhyaḥ saṃtaptābhyo yadreta āsīttadabhṛjjyata [ed.̱ asīt]
(1,1.3 ) tasmādbhṛguḥ samabhavat
(1,1.3 ) tadbhṛgorbhṛgutvam
(1,1.3 ) bhṛguriva vai sa sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti ya evaṃ veda || 3 ||

(1,1.4 ) sa bhṛguṃ sṛṣṭvāntaradhīyata
(1,1.4 ) sa bhṛguḥ sṛṣṭaḥ prāṅaijata
(1,1.4 ) taṃ vāganvavadat
(1,1.4 ) vāyo vāya iti
(1,1.4 ) sa nyavartata sa dakṣiṇāṃ diśamaijata
(1,1.4 ) taṃ vāganvavadat
(1,1.4 ) mātariśvan mātariśvanniti
(1,1.4 ) sa nyavartata sa pratīcīṃ diśamaijata
(1,1.4 ) taṃ vāganvavadat
(1,1.4 ) pavamāna pavamāneti
(1,1.4 ) sa nyavartata sa udīcīṃ diśamaijata
(1,1.4 ) taṃ vāganvavadat
(1,1.4 ) vāta vāteti
(1,1.4 ) tamabravīt
(1,1.4 ) na nvavidamahamiti
(1,1.4 ) na hīti
(1,1.4 ) athārvāṅenametāsvevāpsvanviccheti
(1,1.4 ) tadyadabravīdathārvāṅenametāsvevāpsvanviccheti tadatharvābhavat
(1,1.4 ) tadatharvaṇo'tharvatvam
(1,1.4 ) tasya ha vā etasya bhagavato'tharvaṇa ṛṣeryathaiva brahmaṇo lomāni yathāṅgāni yathā prāṇa evamevāsya sarva ātmā samabhavat
(1,1.4 ) tamatharvāṇaṃ brahmābravītprajāpateḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā pālayasveti
(1,1.4 ) tadyadabravītprajāpateḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā pālayasveti tasmātprajāpatirabhavat
(1,1.4 ) tatprajāpateḥ prajāpatitvam
(1,1.4 ) atharvā vai prajāpatiḥ
(1,1.4ś) prajāpatiriva vai sa sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti ya evaṃ veda || 4 ||

(1,1.5 ) tamatharvāṇamṛṣimabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.5 ) tasmācchrāntāttaptātsaṃtaptāddaśatayānatharvaṇa ṛṣīnniramimataikarcān dvyṛcāṃstṛcāṃscaturṛcān pañcarcāntṣaḍarcāntsaptarcānaṣṭarcānnavarcān daśarcāniti [ed.̱ ṣaḍarcāṃt]
(1,1.5 ) tānatharvaṇa ṛṣīnabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.5 ) tebhyaḥ śrāntebhyastaptebhyaḥ saṃtaptebhyo daśatayānātharvaṇānārṣeyānniramimataikādaśān dvādaśāṃstrayodaśāṃścaturdaśān pañcadaśān ṣoḍaśāntsaptadaśānaṣṭādaśānnavadaśān viṃśāniti [ed.̱ ṣoḍaśāṃt, note also pañcarcāntabove]
(1,1.5 ) tānatharvaṇa ṛṣīnātharvaṇāṃścārṣeyānabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.5 ) tebhyaḥ śrāntebhyastaptebhyaḥ saṃtaptebhyo yān mantrānapaśyatsa ātharvaṇo vedo'bhavat
(1,1.5 ) tamātharvaṇaṃ vedamabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.5 ) tasmācchrāntāttaptātsaṃtaptādomiti mana evordhvamakṣaramudakrāmat
(1,1.5 ) sa ya icchetsarvairetairatharvabhiścātharvaṇaiśca kurvīyetyetayaiva tan mahāvyāhṛtyā kurvīta
(1,1.5 ) sarvairha vā asyaitairatharvabhiścātharvaṇaiśca kṛtaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametayā mahāvyāhṛtyā kurute || 5 ||

(1,1.6 ) sa bhūyo'śrāmyadbhūyo'tapyadbhūya ātmānaṃ samatapat
(1,1.6 ) sa ātamata eva trīṃllokānniramimīta pṛthivīmantarikṣaṃ divamiti
(1,1.6 ) sa khalu pādābhyāmeva pṛthivīṃ nirmamimīta
(1,1.6 ) udarādantarikṣaṃ mūrdhno divam
(1,1.6 ) sa tāṃstrīṃllokānabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.6 ) tebhyaḥ śrāntebhyastaptebhyaḥ saṃtaptebhyastrīn devānniramimīta
(1,1.6 ) agniṃ vāyumādityamiti
(1,1.6 ) sa khalu pṛthivyā evāgniṃ niramimītāntarikṣādvāyuṃ diva ādityam
(1,1.6 ) sa tāṃstrīn devānabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.6 ) tebhyaḥ śrāntebhyastaptebhyaḥ saṃtaptebhyastrīn vedānniramimīta ṛgvedaṃ yajurvedaṃ sāmavedamiti
(1,1.6 ) agnerṛgvedaṃ vāyoryajurvedaṃ ādityātsāmavedam
(1,1.6 ) sa tāṃstrīn vedānabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.6 ) tebhyaḥ śrāntebhyastaptebhyaḥ saṃtaptebhyastisro mahāvyāhṛtīrniramimīta bhūrbhuvaḥ svariti
(1,1.6 ) bhūrityṛgvedādbhuva iti yajurvedātsvariti sāmavedāt
(1,1.6 ) sa ya icchetsarvairetaistribhirvedaiḥ kurvīyetyetābhireva tan mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ kurvīta
(1,1.6 ) sarvairha vā asyaitaistribhirvedaiḥ kṛtaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametābhirmahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ kurute || 6 ||

(1,1.7 ) tā yā amū retaḥ samudraṃ vṛtvātiṣṭhaṃstāḥ prācyo dakṣiṇācyaḥ pratīcya udīcyaḥ samavadravanta
(1,1.7 ) tadyatsamavadravanta tasmātsamudra ucyate
(1,1.7 ) tā bhītā abruvan
(1,1.7 ) bhagavantameva vayaṃ rājānaṃ vṛṇīmaha iti
(1,1.7 ) yacca vṛtvātiṣṭhaṃstadvaraṇo'bhavat
(1,1.7 ) taṃ vā etaṃ varaṇaṃ santaṃ varuṇa ityācakṣate parokṣeṇa
(1,1.7 ) parokṣapriyā iva hi devā bhavanti pratyakṣadviṣaḥ
(1,1.7 ) sa samudrādamucyata
(1,1.7 ) sa mucyurabhavat
(1,1.7 ) taṃ vā etaṃ mucyuṃ santaṃ mṛtyurityācakṣate parokṣeṇa
(1,1.7 ) parokṣapriyā iva hi devā bhavanti pratyakṣadviṣas
(1,1.7 ) taṃ varuṇaṃ mṛtyumabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.7 ) tasya śrāntasya taptasya saṃtaptasya sarvebhyo'ṅgebhyo raso'kṣarat
(1,1.7 ) so'ṅgaraso'bhavat
(1,1.7 ) taṃ vā etaṃ aṅgarasaṃ santamaṅgirā ityācakṣate parokṣeṇa
(1,1.7 ) parokṣapriyā iva hi devā bhavanti pratyakṣadviṣaḥ || 7 ||

(1,1.8 ) tamaṅgirasamṛṣimabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.8 ) tasmācchrāntāttaptātsaṃtaptādviṃśino'ṅgirasa ṛṣīnniramimīta
(1,1.8 ) tān viṃśino'ṅgirasa ṛṣīnabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.8 ) tebhyaḥ śrāntebhyastaptebhyaḥ saṃtaptebhyo daśatayānāṅgirasānārṣeyānniramimīta ṣoḍaśino'ṣṭādaśino dvādaśina ekarcān dvyṛcāṃstṛcāṃścaturṛcān pañcarcān ṣaḍarcān saptarcāniti
(1,1.8 ) tānaṅgirasa ṛṣīnāṅgirasāṃścārṣeyānabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.8 ) tebhyaḥ śrāntebhyastaptebhyaḥ saṃtaptebhyo yān mantrānapaśyatsa āṅgiraso vedo'bhavat
(1,1.8 ) tāmāṅgirasaṃ vedamabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.8 ) tasmācchrāntāttaptātsaṃtaptājjanaditi dvaitamakṣaraṃ vyabhavat
(1,1.8 ) sa ya icchetsarvairetairaṅgirobhiścāṅgirasaiśca kurvīyetyetayaiva tan mahāvyāhṛtyā kurvīta
(1,1.8 ) sarvairha vā asyaitairaṅgirobhiścāṅgirasaiśca kṛtaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametayā mahāvyāhṛtyā kurute || 8 ||

(1,1.9 ) sa ūrdhvo'tiṣṭhat
(1,1.9 ) sa imāṃllokān vyaṣṭabhnāt
(1,1.9 ) tasmādaṅgiraso'dhīyāna ūrdhvastiṣṭhati
(1,1.9 ) tadvrataṃ sa manasā dhyāyedyadvā ahaṃ kiṃ ca manasā dhyāsyāmi tathaiva tadbhaviṣyati [ed.̱ yed]
(1,1.9 ) taddha sma tathaiva bhavati
(1,1.9 ) tadapyetadṛcoktam
(1,1.9 ) śreṣṭho ha vedastapaso'dhi jāto brahmajyānāṃ kṣitaye saṃbabhūva |
(1,1.9 ) ṛjyadbhūtaṃ yadasṛjyatedaṃ niveśanamanṛṇaṃ dūramasyeti [ed.̱ ṛcyṛgbhūtaṃ  see note ড়tyal]
(1,1.9 ) tā vā etā aṅgirasāṃ jāmayo yan menayaḥ
(1,1.9 ) karoti menibhirvīryaṃ ya evaṃ veda || 9 ||

(1,1.10 ) sa diśo'nvaikṣata prācīṃ dakṣiṇāṃ pratīcīmudīcīṃ dhruvāmūrdhvāmiti
(1,1.10 ) tāstatraivābhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.10 ) tābhyaḥ śrāntābhyastaptābhyaḥ saṃtaptābhyaḥ pañca vedānniramimīta
(1,1.10 ) sarpavedaṃ piśācavedamasuravedamitihāsavedaṃ purāṇavedamiti
(1,1.10 ) sa khalu prācyā eva diśaḥ sarpavedaṃ niramimīta
(1,1.10 ) dakṣiṇasyāḥ piśācavedam
(1,1.10 ) pratīcyā asuravedam
(1,1.10 ) udīcyā itihāsavedam
(1,1.10 ) dhruvāyāścordhvāyāśca purāṇavedam
(1,1.10 ) sa tān pañca vedānabhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.10 ) tebhyaḥ śrāntebhyastaptebhyaḥ saṃtaptebhyaḥ pañca mahāvyāhṛtīrniramimīta
(1,1.10 ) vṛdhatkaradruhan mahattaditi
(1,1.10 ) vṛdhaditi sarpavedāt
(1,1.10 ) karaditi piśācavedāt
(1,1.10 ) ruhadityasuravedāt
(1,1.10 ) mahaditītihāsavedāt
(1,1.10 ) taditi purāṇavedāt
(1,1.10 ) sa ya icchetsarvairetaiḥ pañcabhirvedaiḥ kurvīyetyetābhireva tan mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ kurvīta
(1,1.10 ) sarvairha vā asyaitaiḥ pañcabhirvedaiḥ kṛtaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametābhirmahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ kurute || 10 ||

(1,1.11 ) sa āvataśca parāvataścānvaikṣata
(1,1.11 ) tāstatraivābhyaśrāmyadabhyatapatsamatapat
(1,1.11 ) tābhyaḥ śrāntābhyastaptābhyaḥ saṃtaptābhyaḥ śamityūrdhvamakṣaramudakrāmat
(1,1.11 ) sa ya icchetsarvābhiretābhirāvadbhiśca parāvadbhiśca kurvīyetyetayaivā tan mahāvyāhṛtyā kurvīta
(1,1.11 ) sarvābhirha vā asyaitābhirāvadbhiśca parāvadbhiśca kṛtaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametayā mahāvyāhṛtyā kurute || 11 ||

(1,1.12 ) sa bhūyo'śrāmyadbhūyo'tapyadbhūya ātmānaṃ samatapat
(1,1.12 ) sa manasa eva candramasaṃ niramimīta
(1,1.12 ) nakhebhyo nakṣatrāṇi
(1,1.12 ) lomabhya oṣadhivanaspatīn
(1,1.12 ) kṣudrebhyaḥ prāṇebhyo'nyān bahūn devān
(1,1.12 ) sa bhūyo'śrāmyadbhūyo'tapyadbhūya ātmānaṃ samatapat
(1,1.12 ) sa etaṃ trivṛtaṃ saptatantumekaviṃśatisaṃsthaṃ yajñamapaśyat[ed. ekaviśatisaṃsthaṃ]
(1,1.12 ) tadapyetadṛcoktam
(1,1.12 ) <agniryajñaṃ trivṛtaṃ saptatantum [ড়ṣ5.28.1 ]> iti
(1,1.12 ) athāpyeṣa prākrīḍitaḥ ślokaḥ pratyabhivadati
(1,1.12 ) sapta sutyāḥ sapta ca pākayajñā iti || 12 ||

(1,1.13 ) tamāharat
(1,1.13 ) tenāyajata
(1,1.13 ) tasyāgnirhotāsīt
(1,1.13 ) vāyuradhvaryuḥ
(1,1.13 ) sūrya udgātā
(1,1.13 ) candramā brahmā
(1,1.13 ) parjanyaḥ sadasyaḥ
(1,1.13 ) oṣadhivanaspatayaścamasādhvaryavaḥ
(1,1.13 ) viśve devā hotrakāḥ
(1,1.13 ) atharvāṅgiraso goptāras
(1,1.13 ) taṃ ha smaitamevaṃvidvāṃsaḥ pūrve śrotriyā yajñaṃ tataṃ sāvasāya ha smāhetyabhivrajanti
(1,1.13 ) mā no'yaṃ gharma udyataḥ pramattānāmamṛtāḥ prajāḥ pradhākṣīditi
(1,1.13 ) tān vā etān parirakṣakāntsadaḥprasarpakānityācakṣate dakṣiṇāsamṛddhān
(1,1.13 ) tadu ha smāha prajāpatiḥ
(1,1.13 ) yadvai yajñe'kuśalā ṛtvijo bhavantyacaritino brahmacaryamaparāgyā vā
(1,1.13 ) tadvai yajñasya viriṣṭamityācakṣate
(1,1.13 ) yajñasya viriṣṭamanu yajamāno viriṣyate
(1,1.13 ) yajamānasya viriṣṭamanvṛtvijo viriṣyante
(1,1.13 ) ṛtvijāṃ viriṣṭamanu dakṣiṇā viriṣyante
(1,1.13 ) dakṣiṇānāṃ viriṣṭamanu yajamānaḥ putrapaśubhirviriṣyate
(1,1.13 ) putrapaśūnāṃ viriṣṭamanu yajamānaḥ svargeṇa lokena viriṣyate
(1,1.13 ) svargasya lokasya viriṣṭamanu tasyārdhasya yogakṣemo viris.yate yasminnardhe yajanta iti brāhmaṇam || 13 ||

(1,1.14 ) taṃ ha smaitamevaṃvidvāṃsaṃ brahmāṇaṃ yajñaviriṣṭī vā yajñaviriṣṭino vetyupādhāveran
(1,1.14 ) namaste astu bhagavan
(1,1.14 ) yajñasya no viriṣṭaṃ sandhehīti
(1,1.14 ) tadyatraiva viriṣṭaṃ syāttatrāgnīnupasamādhāya śāntyudakaṃ kṛtvā pṛthivyai śrotrāyeti trirevāgnīntsamprokṣati triḥ paryukṣati
(1,1.14 ) triḥ kārayamānamācāmayati ca saṃprokṣati ca
(1,1.14 ) yajñavāstu va samprokṣati
(1,1.14 ) athāpi vedānāṃ rasena yajñasya viriṣṭaṃ sandhīyate
(1,1.14 ) tadyathā lavaṇena suvarṇaṃ saṃdadhyātsuvarṇena rajataṃ rajatena lohaṃ lohena sīsaṃ sīsena trapvevamevāsya yajñasya viriṣṭaṃ saṃdhīyate
(1,1.14 ) yajñasya saṃdhitimanu yajamānaḥ saṃdhīyate [ed. saṃghīyate]
(1,1.14 ) yajamānasya saṃdhitimanvṛtvijaḥ saṃdhīyante
(1,1.14 ) ṛtvijāṃ saṃdhitimanu dakṣiṇāḥ saṃdhīyante
(1,1.14 ) dakṣiṇānāṃ saṃdhitimanu yajamānaḥ putrapaśubhiḥ saṃdhīyate
(1,1.14 ) putrapaśūnāṃ saṃdhitimanu yajamānaḥ svargeṇa lokena saṃdhīyate
(1,1.14 ) svargasya lokasya saṃdhitimanu tasyārdhasya yogakṣemaḥ saṃdhīyate yasminnardhe yajanta iti brāhmaṇam || 14 ||

(1,1.15 ) tadu ha smāhātharvā devo vijānan yajñaviriṣṭānandānītyupaśamayeran yajñe prāyaścittiḥ kriyate'pi ca yadu bahviva yajñe vilomaṃ kriyate na caivāsya kā canārtirbhavati na ca yajñaviṣkandhamupayātyapahanti punarmṛtyumapātyeti punārājātiṃ kāmacāro'sya sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvān brahmā bhavati yasya caivaṃvidvān brahmā dakṣiṇataḥ sado'dhyāste yasya caivaṃvidvān brahmā dakṣiṇata udaṅmukha āsīno yajña ājyāhutīrjuhotīti brāhmaṇam || 15 ||

(1,1.16 ) brahma ha vai brahmāṇaṃ puṣkare sasṛje
(1,1.16 ) sa khalu brahmā sṛṣṭaścintāmāpede
(1,1.16 ) kenāhamekenākṣareṇa sarvāṃśca kāmāntsarvāṃśca lokāntsarvāṃśca devāntsarvāṃśca vedāntsarvāṃśca yajñāntsarvāṃśca śabdāntsarvāśca vyuṣṭīḥ sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni sthāvarajaṅgamānyanubhaveyamiti
(1,1.16 ) sa brahmacaryamacarat
(1,1.16 ) sa omityetadakṣaramapaśyaddvivarṇaṃ caturmātraṃ sarvavyāpi sarvavibhvayātayāmabrahma brāhmīṃ vyāhṛtiṃ brahmadaivatām
(1,1.16 ) tayā sarvāṃśca kāmāntsarvāṃśca lokāntsarvāṃśca devāntsarvāṃśca vedāntsarvāṃśca yajñāntsarvāṃśca śabdāntsarvāśca vyuṣṭīḥ sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni sthāvarajaṅgamānyanvabhavat
(1,1.16 ) tasya prathamena varṇenāpaḥ snehaṃ cānvabhavat
(1,1.16 ) tasya dvitīyena varṇena tejo jyotīṃṣyanvabhavat || 16 ||

(1,1.17 ) tasya prathamayā svaramātrayā pṛthivīmagnimoṣadhivanaspatīnṛgvedaṃ bhūriti vyāhṛtiṃ gāyatraṃ chandastrivṛtaṃ stomaṃ prācīṃ diśaṃ vasantamṛtuṃ vācamadhyātmaṃ jihvāṃ rasamitīndriyāṇyanvabhavat || 17 ||

(1,1.18 ) tasya dvitīyayā svaramātrayāntarikṣaṃ vāyuṃ yajurvedaṃ bhuva iti vyāhṛtiṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ chandaḥ pañcadaśaṃ stomaṃ pratīcīṃ diśaṃ grīṣmamṛtuṃ prāṇamadhyātmaṃ nāsike gandhaghrāṇamitīndriyāṇyanvabhavat || 18 ||

(1,1.19 ) tasya tṛtīyayā svaramātrayā divamādityaṃ sāmavedaṃ svariti vyāhṛtiṃ jāgataṃ chandaḥ saptadaśaṃ stomamudīcīṃ diśaṃ varṣā ṛtuṃ jyotiradhyātmaṃ cakṣuṣī darśanamitīndriyānyanvabhavat || 19 ||

(1,1.20 ) tasya vakāramātrayāpaścandramasamatharvavedaṃ nakṣatrāṇyomiti svamātmānaṃ janadityaṅgirasāmānuṣṭubhaṃ chanda ekaviṃśaṃ stomaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ śaradamṛtuṃ mano'dhyātmaṃ jñānaṃ jñeyamitīndriyāṇyanvabhavat || 20 ||

(1,1.21 ) tasya makāraśrutyetihāsapurāṇaṃ vākovākyaṃ gāthā nārāśaṃsīrupaniṣado'nuśāsanānīti vṛdhatkaradruhan mahattacchamomiti vyāhṛtīḥ svaraśamyanānātantrīḥ svaranṛtyagītavāditrāṇyanvabhavaccaitrarathaṃ daivataṃ vaidyutaṃ jyotirbārhataṃ chandastriṇavatrayastriṃśau stomau dhruvāmūrdhvāṃ diśaṃ hemantaśiśirāvṛtū śrotramadhyātmaṃ śabdaśravaṇamitīndriyāṇyanvabhavat || 21 ||

(1,1.22 ) saiṣaikākṣarargbrahmaṇastapaso'gre prādurbabhūva brahmavedasyātharvaṇaṃ śukram
(1,1.22 ) ata eva mantrāḥ prādurbabhūvuḥ
(1,1.22 ) sa tu khalu mantrāṇāmatapasāsuśrūṣānadhyāyādhyayanena yadūnaṃ ca viriṣṭaṃ ca yātayāmaṃ ca karoti tadatharvaṇāṃ tejasā pratyāpyāyayet
(1,1.22 ) mantrāśca māmabhimukhībhaveyurgarbhā iva mātaramabhijighāṃsuḥ
(1,1.22 ) purastādoṃkāraṃ prayuṅkte
(1,1.22 ) etayaiva tadṛcā pratyāpyāyet
(1,1.22 ) eṣaiva yajñasya purastādyujyate
(1,1.22 ) eṣā paścāt
(1,1.22 ) sarvata etayā yajñastāyate
(1,1.22 ) tadapyetadṛcoktam
(1,1.22 ) yā purastādyujyata ṛco akṣare parame vyomanniti
(1,1.22 ) tadetadakṣaraṃ brāhmaṇo yaṃ kāmamicchettrirātropoṣitaḥ prāṅmukho vāgyato barhiṣyupaviśya sahasrakṛtva āvartayet
(1,1.22 ) sidhyantyasyārthāḥ sarvakarmāṇi ceti brāhmaṇam || 22 ||

(1,1.23 ) vasordhārāṇāmaindraṃ nagaram
(1,1.23 ) tadasurāḥ paryavārayanta
(1,1.23 ) te devā bhītā āsan
(1,1.23 ) ka imānasurānapahaniṣyatīti [ed. īmān]
(1,1.23 ) ta oṃkāraṃ brahmaṇaḥ putraṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ dadṛśrus
(1,1.23 ) te tamabruvan
(1,1.23 ) bhavatā mukhenemānasurāñjayemeti
(1,1.23 ) sa hovāca
(1,1.23 ) kiṃ me pratīvāho bhaviṣyatīti
(1,1.23 ) varaṃ vṛṇīṣveti
(1,1.23 ) vṛṇā iti
(1,1.23 ) sa varamavṛṇīta
(1,1.23 ) na māmanīrayitvā brāhmaṇā brahma vadeyuḥ
(1,1.23 ) yadi vadeyurabrahma tatsyāditi
(1,1.23 ) tatheti
(1,1.23 ) te devā devayajanasyottarārdhe'suraiḥ saṃyattā āsan
(1,1.23 ) tānoṃkāreṇāgnīdhrīyāddevā asurān parābhāvayanta
(1,1.23 ) tadyatparābhāvayanta tasmādoṃkāraḥ pūrvamucyate
(1,1.23 ) yo ha vā etamoṃkāraṃ na vedāvaśī syādityatha ya evaṃ veda brahmavaśī syāditi
(1,1.23 ) tasmādoṃkāra ṛcyṛgbhavati
(1,1.23 ) yajuṣi yajuḥ
(1,1.23 ) sāmni sāma
(1,1.23 ) sūtre sūtram
(1,1.23 ) brāhmaṇe brāhmaṇam
(1,1.23 ) śloke ślokaḥ
(1,1.23ś) praṇave praṇava iti brāhmaṇam || 23 ||

(1,1.24 ) oṃkāraṃ pṛcchāmaḥ
(1,1.24 ) ko dhātuḥ
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ prātipadikam
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ nāmākhyātam
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ liṅgam
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ vacanam
(1,1.24 ) kā vibhaktiḥ
(1,1.24 ) kaḥ pratyayaḥ
(1,1.24 ) kaḥ svara upasargo nipātaḥ
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ vai vyākaraṇam
(1,1.24 ) ko vikāraḥ
(1,1.24 ) ko vikārī
(1,1.24 ) katimātraḥ
(1,1.24 ) kativarṇaḥ
(1,1.24 ) katyakṣaraḥ
(1,1.24 ) katipadaḥ
(1,1.24 ) kaḥ saṃyogaḥ
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ sthānānupradānakaraṇaṃ śikṣukāḥ kimuccārayanti
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ chandaḥ
(1,1.24 ) ko varṇa iti pūrve praśrāḥ
(1,1.24 ) athottare
(1,1.24 ) mantraḥ kalpo brāhmaṇamṛgyajuḥ sāma
(1,1.24 ) kasmādbrahmavādina oṃkāramāditaḥ kurvanti
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ daivatam
(1,1.24 ) kiṃ jyotiṣam
(1,1.24ś) kiṃ niruktam
(1,1.24 a) kiṃ sthānam
(1,1.24 b) kā prakṛtiḥ
(1,1.24 c) kimadhyātmamiti
(1,1.24 d) ṣaṭtriṃśatpraśrnāḥ
(1,1.24 e) pūrvottarāṇāṃ trayo vargā dvādaśakāḥ
(1,1.24 f) etairoṃkāraṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || 24 ||

(1,1.25 ) indraḥ prajāpatimapṛcchat
(1,1.25 ) bhagavannabhiṣṭūya pṛcchāmīti
(1,1.25 ) pṛccha vatsetyabravīt
(1,1.25 ) kimayamoṃkāraḥ
(1,1.25 ) kasyaḥ putraḥ
(1,1.25 ) kiṃ caitacchandaḥ
(1,1.25 ) kiṃ caitadvarṇaḥ
(1,1.25 ) kiṃ caitadbrahmā brahma sampadyate
(1,1.25 ) tasmādvai tadbhadramoṃkāraṃ pūrvamālebhe
(1,1.25 ) svaritodātta ekākṣara oṃkāra ṛgvede
(1,1.25 ) traisvaryodāta ekākṣara oṃkāro yajurvede
(1,1.25 ) dīrghaplutodātta ekākṣara oṃkāraḥ sāmavede
(1,1.25 ) hrasvodātta ekākṣara oṃkāro'tharvavede
(1,1.25 ) udāttodāta dvipada a u ityardhacatasro mātrā makāre vyañjanamityāhuḥ
(1,1.25 ) yā sā prathamā mātrā brahmadevatyā raktā varṇena
(1,1.25 ) yastāṃ dhyāyate nityaṃ sa gacchedbrāhmaṃ padam
(1,1.25 ) yā sā dvitīyā mātrā viṣṇudevatyā kṛṣṇā varṇena
(1,1.25 ) yastāṃ dhyāyate nityaṃ sa gacchedvaiṣṇavaṃ padam
(1,1.25 ) yā sā tṛtīyā mātraiśānadevatyā kapilā varṇena
(1,1.25 ) yastāṃ dhyāyate nityaṃ sa gacchedaiśānaṃ padam
(1,1.25 ) yā sārdhacaturthī mātrā sarvadevatyā vyaktībhūtā khaṃ vicarati śuddhasphaṭikasannibhā varṇena
(1,1.25 ) yastāṃ dhyāyate nityaṃ sa gacchetpadamanāmakam
(1,1.25 ) oṃkārasya cotpattiḥ
(1,1.25 ) vipro yo na jānāti tatpunarupanayanam
(1,1.25 ) tasmādbrāhmāṇavacanamādartavyaṃ yathā lātavyo gotro brahmaṇaḥ putraḥ
(1,1.25ś) gāyatraṃ cchandaḥ
(1,1.25 a) śuklo varṇaḥ
(1,1.25 b) puṃso vatsaḥ
(1,1.25 c) rudro devatā
(1,1.25 d) oṃkāro vedānām || 25 ||

(1,1.26 ) ko dhāturiti
(1,1.26 ) āpṛdhātuḥ
(1,1.26 ) avatimapyeke
(1,1.26 ) rūpasāmānyādarthasāmānyaṃ nedīyas
(1,1.26 ) tasmādāperoṃkāraḥ sarvamāpnotītyarthaḥ kṛdantamarthavat
(1,1.26 ) prātipadikamadarśanam
(1,1.26 ) pratyayasya nāma saṃpadyate nipāteṣu cainaṃ vaiyākaraṇā udāttaṃ samāmananti tadavyayībhūtam
(1,1.26 ) anvarthavācī śabdo na vyeti kadā caneti
(1,1.26 ) sadṛśaṃ triṣu liṅgeṣu sarvāsu va vibhaktiṣu vacaneṣu ca sarveṣu yanna vyeti tadavyayam
(1,1.26 ) ko vikārī cyavate
(1,1.26 ) prasāraṇam
(1,1.26 ) āpnoterākarapakārau vikāryau
(1,1.26 ) ādita oṃkāro vikriyate
(1,1.26 ) dvitīyo makāraḥ
(1,1.26 ) eva dvivarṇa ekākṣara omityoṃkāro nirvṛttaḥ || 26 ||

(1,1.27 ) katimātra iti
(1,1.27 ) ādestisro mātrāḥ
(1,1.27 ) abhyādāne hi plavate
(1,1.27 ) makāraścaturthīm
(1,1.27 ) kiṃ sthānamiti
(1,1.27 ) ubhāvoṣṭhau sthānam
(1,1.27 ) nādānupradānakaraṇau ca dvisthānam [ed. nādāna, cf. ড়tyalp. 28]
(1,1.27 ) saṃdhyakṣaramavarṇaleśaḥ kaṇṭhyo yathoktaśeṣaḥ
(1,1.27 ) pūrvo vivṛtakaraṇasthitaśca
(1,1.27 ) dvitīyaspṛṣṭakaraṇasthitaśca
(1,1.27 ) na saṃyogo vidyate
(1,1.27 ) ākhyātopasargānudāttasvaritaliṅgavibhaktivacanāni ca saṃsthānādhyāyina ācāryāḥ pūrve babhūvuḥ
(1,1.27 ) śravaṇādeva pratipadyante na kāraṇaṃ pṛcchanti
(1,1.27 ) athāparapakṣīyāṇāṃ kaviḥ pañcālacaṇḍaḥ paripṛcchako babhūvāṃbu pṛthagudgīthadoṣān bhavanto bruvantviti
(1,1.27 ) tadvācyupalakṣayedvarṇākṣarapadāṅkaśaḥ
(1,1.27 ) vibhaktyāmṛṣiniṣevitāmiti vācaṃ stuvanti
(1,1.27 ) tasmātkāraṇaṃ brūmaḥ
(1,1.27 ) varṇānāmayamidaṃ bhaviṣyatīti
(1,1.27 ) ṣaḍaṅgavidastattathādhīmahe
(1,1.27 ) kiṃ chanda iti
(1,1.27 ) gāyatraṃ hi chandaḥ
(1,1.27 ) gāyatrī vai devānāmekākṣarā śvetavarṇā ca vyākhyātā
(1,1.27 ) dvau dvādaśakau vargau
(1,1.27 ) etadvai vyākaraṇaṃ dhātvarthavacanaṃ śaikṣyaṃ chandovacanaṃ ca
(1,1.27 ) athottarau dvau dvādaśakau vargau vedarahasikī vyākhyātā
(1,1.27ś) mantraḥ kalpo brāhamaṇamṛgyajuḥ sāmātharvāṇi
(1,1.27 a) eṣā vyāhṛtiścaturṇāṃ vedānāmānupūrveṇoṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svariti vyāhṛtayaḥ || 27 ||

(1,1.28 ) asamīkṣyapravalhitāni śrūyante
(1,1.28 ) dvāparādāvṛṣīṇāmekadeśo doṣapatiriha cintāmāpede tribhiḥ somaḥ pātavyaḥ samāptamiva bhavati
(1,1.28 ) tasmādṛgyajuḥsāmānyapakrāntatejāṃsyāsan
(1,1.28 ) tantra maharṣayaḥ paridevayāṃ cakrire
(1,1.28 ) mahacchokabhayaṃ prāptāḥ smaḥ
(1,1.28 ) na caitatsarvaiḥ samabhihitam
(1,1.28 ) te vayaṃ bhagavantamevopadhāvāma
(1,1.28 ) sarveṣāmeva śarma bhavānīti
(1,1.28 ) te tathetyuktvā tūṣṇīmatiṣṭhan
(1,1.28 ) nānupasannebhya iti
(1,1.28 ) upopasīdāmīti nīcairbabhūvuḥ
(1,1.28 ) sa ebhya upanīya provāca
(1,1.28 ) māmikāmeva vyāhṛtimādita āditaḥ kṛṇudhvamiti
(1,1.28 ) evaṃ māmakā ādhīyante narte bhṛgvaṅgirovidbhyaḥ somaḥ pātavyaḥ
(1,1.28 ) ṛtvijaḥ parābhavanti
(1,1.28 ) yajamāno rajasāpadhvasyati
(1,1.28 ) śrutiścāpadhvastā tiṣṭhatīti
(1,1.28 ) evamevottarottarādyogāttokaṃ tokaṃ praśādhvamiti
(1,1.28 ) evaṃ pratāpo na parābhaviṣyatīti
(1,1.28 ) tathā ha tathā ha bhagavanniti pratipedira āpyāyayan
(1,1.28 ) te tathā vītaśokabhayā babhūvus
(1,1.28 ) tasmādbrahmavādina oṃkāramāditaḥ kurvanti || 28 ||

(1,1.29 ) kiṃ devatamiti
(1,1.29 ) ṛcāmagnirdevatam
(1,1.29 ) tadeva jyotiḥ
(1,1.29 ) gāyatraṃ chandaḥ
(1,1.29 ) pṛthivī sthānam
(1,1.29 ) <agnimīḻe purohitaṃ yajñasya devamṛtvijaṃ hotāraṃ ratnadhātamam [ṛV 1.1.1]> ityevamādiṃ kṛtvā ṛgvedamadhīyate
(1,1.29 ) yajuṣāṃ vāyurdevatam
(1,1.29 ) tadeva jyotis
(1,1.29 ) traiṣṭubhaṃ chandaḥ
(1,1.29 ) antarikṣaṃ sthānam
(1,1.29 ) <iṣe tvorje tvā vāyava stha devo vaḥ savitā prārpayatu śreṣṭhatamāya karmaṇe [Vṣ1.1]>ityevamādiṃ kṛtvā yajurvedamadhīyate
(1,1.29 ) sāmnāmādityo devatam
(1,1.29 ) tadeva jyotiḥ
(1,1.29 ) jāgataṃ chandaḥ
(1,1.29 ) dyauḥ sthānam
(1,1.29 ) <agna ā yāhi vītaye gṛṇāno havyadātaye ni hotā satsi barhiṣi [ṣV 1.1]>ityevamādiṃ kṛtvā samāvedamadhīyate
(1,1.29 ) atharvaṇāṃ candramā devatam
(1,1.29 ) tadeva jyotiḥ
(1,1.29 ) sarvāṇi chandāṃsi
(1,1.29 ) āpaḥ sthānam
(1,1.29 ) <śaṃ no devīrabhiṣṭaya [ড়ṣ1.1.1]> ityevamādiṃ kṛtvātharvavedamadhīyate
(1,1.29 ) adbhyaḥ sthāvarajaṅgamo bhūtagrāmaḥ saṃbhavati
(1,1.29 ) tasmātsarvamāpomayaṃ bhūtaṃ sarvaṃ bhṛgvaṅgiromayam
(1,1.29 ) antaraite trayo vedā bhṛgūnaṅgirasaḥ śritā ityabiti prakṛtirapāmoṃkāreṇa ca
(1,1.29 ) etasmādvyāsaḥ purovāca
(1,1.29ś) bhṛgvaṅgirovidā saṃskṛto'nyān vedānadhīyīta
(1,1.29 a) nānyatra saṃskṛto bhṛgṅgiraso'dhīyīta
(1,1.29 b) sāmavede'tha khilaśrutirbrahmacaryeṇa caitasmādatharvāṅgiraso ha yo veda sa veda sarvamiti brāhmaṇam || 29 ||

(1,1.30 ) adhyātmamātmabhaiṣajyamātmakaivalyamoṃkāraḥ
(1,1.30 ) ātmānaṃ nirudhya saṅgamamātrīṃ bhūtārthacintāṃ cintayet
(1,1.30 ) atikramya vedebhyaḥ sarvaparamadhyātmaphalaṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ
(1,1.30 ) savitarkaṃ jñānamayamityetaiḥ praśnaiḥ prativacanaiśca yathārthaṃ padamanuvicintya prakaraṇajño hi prabalo viṣayī syātsarvasmin vākovākya iti brāhmaṇam || 30 ||

(1,1.31 ) etaddha smaitadvidvāṃsamekādaśākṣaṃ maudgalyaṃ glāvo maitreyo'bhyājagāma
(1,1.31 ) sa tasmin brahmacaryaṃ vasato vijñāyovāca kiṃ svin maryā ayaṃ tan maudgalyo'dhyeti yadasmin brahmacaryaṃ vasatīti
(1,1.31 ) taddhi maudgalyasyāntevāsī śuśrāva
(1,1.31 ) sa ācāryāyāvrajyācacaṣṭe
(1,1.31 ) duradhīyānaṃ vā ayaṃ bhavantamavocadyo'yamadyātithirbhavati
(1,1.31 ) kiṃ saumya vidvāniti
(1,1.31 ) trīn vedān brūte bho iti
(1,1.31 ) tasya saumya vipaṣṭo vijigīṣo'ntevāsī taṃ me hvayeti
(1,1.31 ) tamājuhāva
(1,1.31 ) tamabhyuvācāsāviti
(1,1.31 ) bho iti
(1,1.31 ) kiṃ saumya ta ācāryo'dhyetīti
(1,1.31 ) trīn vedān brūte
(1,1.31 ) bho iti
(1,1.31 ) yannu khalu saumyāsmābhiḥ sarve vedā mukhato gṛhītāḥ kathaṃ ta evamācāryo bhāṣate
(1,1.31 ) kathaṃ nu śiṣṭāḥ śiṣṭebhya evaṃ bhāṣeran
(1,1.31 ) yaṃ hyenamahaṃ praśnaṃ pṛcchāmi na taṃ vivakṣyati
(1,1.31 ) na hyenamadhyetīti
(1,1.31 ) sa ha maudgalyaḥ svamantevāsinamuvāca parehi saumya glāvaṃ maitreyamupasīda
(1,1.31 ) adhīhi bhoḥ sāvitrīṃ gāyatrīṃ caturviṃśatiyoniṃ dvādaśamithunām
(1,1.31 ) yasyā bhṛgvaṅgirasaścakṣuḥ
(1,1.31 ) yasyāṃ sarvamidaṃ śritaṃ tāṃ bhavān prabravītviti
(1,1.31 ) sa cetsaumya duradhīyāno bhaviṣyatyācāryovāca brahmacārī brahmācāriṇo sāvitrīṃ prāheti vakṣyati tattvaṃ brūyādduradhīyānaṃ taṃ vai bhavān maudgalyamavocat
(1,1.31 ) sa tvā yaṃ praśnamaprākṣīnna taṃ vyavocaḥ
(1,1.31 ) purā saṃvatsarādārtimāriṣyasīti || 31 ||

(1,1.32 ) sa tatrājagāma yatretaro babhūva
(1,1.32 ) taṃ ha papraccha
(1,1.32 ) sa ha na pratipede
(1,1.32 ) taṃ hovāca duradhīyānaṃ taṃ vai bhavān maudgalyamavocat
(1,1.32 ) sa tvā yaṃ praśnamaprākṣīnna taṃ vyavocaḥ
(1,1.32 ) purā saṃvatsarādārtimāriṣyasīti
(1,1.32 ) sa ha maitreyaḥ svānantevāsita uvāca yathārthaṃ bhavanto yathāgṛhaṃ yathāmano viprasṛjyantām
(1,1.32 ) duradhīyānaṃ vā ahaṃ maudgalyamavocam
(1,1.32 ) sa mā yaṃ praśnamaprākṣīnna taṃ vyavocam
(1,1.32 ) tamupaiṣyāmi
(1,1.32 ) śāntiṃ kariṣyāmīti
(1,1.32 ) sa ha maitreyaḥ prātaḥ samitpāṇirmaudgalyamupasasādāsau vā ahaṃ bho maitreyaḥ
(1,1.32 ) kimarthamiti
(1,1.32 ) duradhīyānaṃ vā ahaṃ bhavantamavocam
(1,1.32 ) tvaṃ mā yaṃ praśnamaprākṣīrna taṃ vyavocam
(1,1.32 ) tvāmupaiṣyāmi
(1,1.32 ) śāntiṃ kariṣyāmīti
(1,1.32 ) sa hovācātra vā upetaṃ ca sarvaṃ ca kṛtaṃ pāpakena tvā yānena carantamāhuḥ
(1,1.32 ) atho'yaṃ mama kalyāṇas[ড়tyal'stransl. impliesthe emendationāhū ratho]
(1,1.32 ) taṃ te dadāmi
(1,1.32 ) tena yāhīti
(1,1.32 ) sa hovācaitadevātrātviṣaṃ cānṛśaṃsyaṃ ca yathā bhavānāha
(1,1.32 ) upāyāmi tveva bhavantamiti
(1,1.32 ) taṃ hopeyāya
(1,1.32 ) taṃ hopetya papraccha kiṃ svidāhurbhoḥ saviturvareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya kavayaḥ kimāhurdhiyo vicakṣva yadi tāḥ pravettha
(1,1.32ś) pracodayātsavitā yābhiretīti [ed. pracodayānt, corr. ড়tyal]
(1,1.32 a) tasmā etatprovāca vedāṃśchandāṃsi saviturvareṇyam
(1,1.32 b) bhargo devasya kavayo'nnamāhuḥ karmāṇi dhiyas
(1,1.32 c) tadu te prabravīmi pracodayātsavitā yābhiretīti [ed. pracodayānt]
(1,1.32 d) tamupasaṃgṛhya papracchādhīhi bhoḥ kaḥ savitā kā sāvitrī || 32 ||
(1,1.33 ) mana eva savitā vāksāvitrī
(1,1.33 ) yatra hyeva manastadvāgyatra vai vāktan mana iti
(1,1.33 ) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 ) agnireva savitā pṛthivī sāvitrī
(1,1.33 ) yatra hyevāgnistatpṛthivī yatra vai pṛthivī tadagniriti
(1,1.33 ) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 ) vāyureva savitāntarikṣaṃ sāvitrī
(1,1.33 ) yatra hyeva vāyustadantarikṣaṃ yatra vā antarikṣaṃ tadvāyuriti
(1,1.33 ) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 ) āditya eva savitā dyauḥ sāvitrī
(1,1.33 ) yatra hyevādityastaddyauryatra vai dyaustadāditya iti
(1,1.33 ) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 ) candramā eva savitā nakṣatrāṇi sāvitrī
(1,1.33 ) yatra hyeva candramāstannakṣatrāṇi yatra vai nakṣatrāṇi taccandramā iti
(1,1.33 ) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 ) ahareva savitā rātriḥ sāvitrī
(1,1.33 ) yatra hyevāhastadrātriryatra vai rātristadahariti
(1,1.33 ) ete dve yonī eka mithunam
(1,1.33 ) uṣṇameva savitā śītaṃ sāvitrī
(1,1.33 ) yatra hyevoṣṇaṃ tacchītaṃ yatra vai śītaṃ taduṣṇamiti
(1,1.33 ) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 ) abhrameva savitā varṣaṃ sāvitrī
(1,1.33 ) yatra hyevābhraṃ tadvarṣaṃ yatra vai varṣaṃ tadabhramiti
(1,1.33 ) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 ) vidyudeva savitā stanayitnuḥ sāvitrī
(1,1.33ś) yatra hyeva vidyuttatstanayitnuryatra vai stanayitnustadvidyuditi
(1,1.33 a) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 b) prāṇa eva savitānnaṃ sāvitrī
(1,1.33 c) yatra hyeva prāṇastadannaṃ yatra vā annaṃ tatprāṇa iti
(1,1.33 d) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 e) vedā eva savitā chandāṃsi sāvitrī [ed. omitssentence endmarker]
(1,1.33 f) yatra hyeva vedāstacchandāṃsi yatra vai chandāṃsi tadvedā iti
(1,1.33 g) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 h) yajña eka savitā dakṣiṇāḥ sāvitrī
(1,1.33 i) yatra hyeva yajñastaddakṣiṇā yatra vai dakṣiṇāstadyajña iti
(1,1.33 j) ete dve yonī ekaṃ mithunam
(1,1.33 k) etaddha smaitadvidvāṃsamopākārimāsasturbrahmacārī te saṃsthita ityathaita āsasturācita iva cito babhūva
(1,1.33 l) athotthāya prāvrājīdityetadvā ahaṃ veda naitāsu yoniṣvita etebhyo vā mithunebhyaḥ saṃbhūto brahmacārī mama purāyuṣaḥ preyāditi || 33 ||

(1,1.34 ) brahma hedaṃ śriyaṃ pratiṣṭhāmāyatanamaikṣata
(1,1.34 ) tattapasva
(1,1.34 ) yadi tadvrate dhriyeta tatsatye pratyatiṣṭhat
(1,1.34 ) sa savitā sāvitryā brāhmaṇaṃ sṛṣṭvā tatsavitrīṃ paryadadhāt
(1,1.34 ) tatsaviturvareṇyamiti sāvitryāḥ prathamaḥ pādaḥ [ed. savitryāḥ]
(1,1.34 ) pṛthivyarcaṃ samadadhāt
(1,1.34 ) ṛcāgnim
(1,1.34 ) agninā śriyam
(1,1.34 ) śriyā striyam
(1,1.34 ) striyā mithunam
(1,1.34 ) mithunena prajām
(1,1.34 ) prajayā karma
(1,1.34 ) karmaṇā tapas[ed. kamaṇā]
(1,1.34 ) tapasā satyam
(1,1.34 ) satyena brahma
(1,1.34 ) brahmaṇā brāhmaṇam
(1,1.34 ) brāhmaṇena vratam
(1,1.34 ) vratena vai brāhmaṇaḥ saṃśito bhavati
(1,1.34 ) aśūnyo bhavatyavichinno bhavatyavichinno'sya tanturavichinnaṃ jīvanaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametaṃ sāvitryāḥ prathamaṃ pādaṃ vyācaṣṭe || 34 ||

(1,1.35 ) bhargo devasya dhīmahīti sāvitryā dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ
(1,1.35 ) antarikṣeṇa yajuḥ samadadhāt
(1,1.35 ) yajuṣā vāyum
(1,1.35 ) vāyunābhram
(1,1.35 ) abhreṇa varṣam
(1,1.35 ) varṣeṇauṣadhivanaspatīn
(1,1.35 ) oṣadhivanaspatibhiḥ paśūn
(1,1.35 ) paśubhiḥ karma
(1,1.35 ) karmaṇā tapas
(1,1.35 ) tapasā satyam
(1,1.35 ) satyena brahma
(1,1.35 ) brahmaṇā brāhmaṇam
(1,1.35 ) brāhmaṇena vratam
(1,1.35 ) vratena vai brāhmaṇaḥ saṃśito bhavati
(1,1.35 ) aśūnyo bhavatyavichinno bhavatyavichinno'sya tanturavichinnaṃ jīvanaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametaṃ sāvitryā dvitīyaṃ pādaṃ vyācaṣṭe || 35 ||

(1,1.36 ) dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāditi sāvitryāstṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ
(1,1.36 ) divā sāma samadadhāt
(1,1.36 ) sāmnādityam
(1,1.36 ) ādityena raśmīn [ed. raśmi+īn]
(1,1.36 ) raśmibhirvarṣam
(1,1.36 ) varṣeṇauṣadhivanaspatīn
(1,1.36 ) oṣadhivanaspatibhiḥ paśūn
(1,1.36 ) paśubhiḥ karma
(1,1.36 ) karmaṇā tapas
(1,1.36 ) tapasā satyam
(1,1.36 ) satyena brahma
(1,1.36 ) brahmaṇā brāhmaṇam
(1,1.36 ) brāhmaṇena vratam
(1,1.36 ) vratena vai brāhmaṇaḥ saṃśito bhavati
(1,1.36 ) aśūnyo bhavatyavichinno bhavatyavichinno'sya tanturavichinnaṃ jīvanaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametaṃ sāvitryāstṛtīyaṃ pādaṃ vyācaṣṭe || 36 ||

(1,1.37 ) tena ha vā evaṃviduṣā brāhmaṇena brahmābhipannaṃ grasitaṃ parāmṛṣṭam
(1,1.37 ) brahmaṇākāśamabhipannaṃ grasitaṃ parāmṛṣṭam
(1,1.37 ) ākāśena vāyurabhipanno grasitaḥ parāmṛṣṭaḥ
(1,1.37 ) vāyunā jyotirabhipannaṃ grasitaṃ parāmṛṣṭam
(1,1.37 ) jyotiṣāpo'bhipannā grasitāḥ parāmṛṣṭāḥ
(1,1.37 ) adbhirbhūmirabhipannā grasitā parāmṛṣṭā
(1,1.37 ) bhūmyānnamabhipannaṃ grasitaṃ parāmṛṣṭam
(1,1.37 ) annena prāṇo'bhipanno grasitaḥ parāmṛṣṭaḥ
(1,1.37 ) prāṇena mano'bhipannaṃ grasitaṃ parāmṛṣṭam
(1,1.37 ) manasā vāgabhipannā grasitā parāmṛṣṭā
(1,1.37 ) vācā vedā abhipannā grasitāḥ parāmṛṣṭāḥ
(1,1.37 ) vedairyajño'bhipanno grasitaḥ parāmṛṣṭas[ed. parimṛṣṭas]
(1,1.37 ) tāni ha vā etāni dvādaśamahābhūtānyevaṃvidi pratiṣṭhitāni
(1,1.37 ) teṣāṃ yajña eva parārdhyaḥ || 37 ||

(1,1.38 ) taṃ ha smaitamevāṃvidvāṃso manyante vidmainamiti yāthātathyamavidvāṃsaḥ
(1,1.38 ) ayaṃ yajño vedeṣu pratiṣṭhitaḥ
(1,1.38 ) vedā vāci pratiṣṭhitāḥ
(1,1.38 ) vāṅmanasi pratiṣṭhitā
(1,1.38 ) manaḥ prāṇe pratiṣṭhitam
(1,1.38 ) prāṇo'nne pratiṣṭhitaḥ
(1,1.38 ) annaṃ bhūmau pratiṣṭhitam
(1,1.38 ) bhūmirapsu pratiṣṭhitā
(1,1.38 ) āpo jyotiṣi pratiṣṭhitāḥ
(1,1.38 ) jyotirvāyau pratiṣṭhitam
(1,1.38 ) vāyurākāśe pratiṣṭhitaḥ
(1,1.38 ) ākāśaṃ brahmaṇi pratiṣṭhitam
(1,1.38 ) brahma brāhmaṇe brahmavidi pratiṣṭhitam
(1,1.38 ) yo ha vā evaṃvitsa brahmavit
(1,1.38 ) puṇyāṃ ca kīrtiṃ labhate surabhīṃśca gandhān
(1,1.38 ) so'pahatapāpmā
(1,1.38 ) anantāṃ śriyamaśnute ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānevametāṃ vedānāṃ mātaraṃ sāvitrīṃ saṃpadamupaniṣadamupāsta iti brāhmaṇam || 38 ||

(1,1.39 ) <āpo garbhaṃ janayantīr[ড়ṣ4.1.8]> iti
(1,1.39 ) apāṃ garbhaḥ puruṣaḥ
(1,1.39 ) sa yajñaḥ
(1,1.39 ) adbhiryajñaḥ praṇīyamānaḥ prāṅtāyate
(1,1.39 ) tasmādācamanīyaṃ pūrvamāhārayati
(1,1.39 ) sa yadācāmati trirācāmati
(1,1.39 ) dviḥ pariśumbhati
(1,1.39 ) āyuravaruhya pāpmānaṃ nirṇudati
(1,1.39 ) upasādya yajuṣoddhṛtya mantrān prayujyāvasāya prācīḥ śākhāḥ saṃdhāya niraṅguṣṭhe pāṇāvamṛtamasyamṛtopastaraṇamasyamṛtāya tvopastṛṇāmīti pāṇāvudakamānīya <jīvā stha [ড়ṣ19.55.1215, śṣ19.69.14]>iti sūktena trirācāmati
(1,1.39 ) sa yatpūrvamācāmati sapta prāṇāṃstānetenāsminnāpyāyayati
(1,1.39 ) yā hyemā bāhyāḥ śarīrān mātrāstadyathaitadagniṃ vāyumādityaṃ candramasamapaḥ paśūnanyāṃśca prajāstānetenāsminnāpyāyayati
(1,1.39 ) āpo'mṛtam
(1,1.39 ) sa yaddvitīyamācāmati saptāpānāṃstānetenāsminnāpyāyayati
(1,1.39 ) yā hyemā bāhyāḥ śarīrān mātrāstadyathaitatpaurṇamāsīmaṣṭakāmamāvāsyāṃ śraddhāṃ dīkṣāṃ yajñaṃ dakṣiṇāstānetenāsminnāpyāyayati
(1,1.39 ) āpo'mṛtam
(1,1.39 ) sa yattṛtīyamācāmati sapta vyānāṃstānetenāsminnāpyāyayati
(1,1.39 ) yā hyemā bāhyāḥ śarīrān mātrāstadyathaitatpṛthivīmantarikṣaṃ divam
(1,1.39 ) nakṣatrāṇyṛtūnārtavān saṃvatsarāṃstānetenāsminnāpyāyayati
(1,1.39 ) āpo'mṛtam
(1,1.39 ) puruṣo brahma
(1,1.39 ) athāpriyanigamo bhavati tasmādvai vidvān puruṣamidaṃ puṇḍarīkamiti [ed. athāprīṃgnigamo, butsee ড়tyalp. 44]
(1,1.39 ) prāṇa eṣa sa puri śete saṃpuri śeta iti
(1,1.39 ) puriśayaṃ santaṃ prāṇaṃ puruṣa ityācakṣate parokṣeṇa
(1,1.39 ) parokṣapriyā iva hi devā bhavanti pratyakṣadviṣaḥ
(1,1.39 ) sa yatpūrvamācāmati purastāddhomāṃstenāsminnavarunddhe
(1,1.39ś) sa yaddvitīyamācāmatyājyabhāgau tenāsminnavarunddhe
(1,1.39 a) sa yattṛtīyamācāmati saṃsthitahomāṃstenāsminnavarunddhe
(1,1.39 b) sa yaddviḥ pariśumbhati tatsamitsaṃbarhiḥ [ed. saṃmit, cf. ড়tyalp. 45]
(1,1.39 c) sa yatsarvāṇi khāni sarvaṃ dehamāpyāyayati yaccānyadātāraṃ mantrakāryaṃ yajñe skandati sarvaṃ tenāsminnavarunddhe
(1,1.39 d) sa yadoṃpūrvān mantrān prayuṅkta ā sarvamedhādete kratava eta evāsya sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu deveṣu sarveṣu vedeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣu sattveṣu kāmacāraḥ kāmavimocanaṃ bhavatyardhe ca na pramīyate ya evaṃ veda
(1,1.39 e) tadapyetadṛcoktamāpo bhṛgvaṅgiro rūpamāpo bhṛgvaṅgiromayaṃ sarvamāpomayaṃ bhūtaṃ sarvaṃ bhṛgvaṅgiromayamantaraite trayo vedā bhṛgūnaṅgiraso'nugāḥ ||
(1,1.39 f) apāṃ puṣpaṃ mūrtirākāśaṃ pavitramuttamamiti
(1,1.39 g) ācamyābhyukṣyātmānamanumantrayata <indra jīva [ড়ṣ20.43.1, śṣ19.70.1]>iti brāhmaṇam || 39 ||

(1,1.39 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇapūrvabhāge prathamaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||

(1,2.1 ) oṃ <brahmacārīṣṇaṃścarati rodasī ubhe [śṣ11.3.1 , ড়ṣ16.153.1 ]> ityācāryamāha
(1,2.1 ) <tasmin devāḥ saṃmanaso bhavanti [śṣ11.3.1, ড়ṣ16.153.1 (?)]>iti vāyumāha
(1,2.1 ) <sa sadya eti pūrvasmāduttaraṃ samudram [śṣ11.3.6, ড়ṣ16.153.6]> ityādityamāha
(1,2.1 ) dīkṣito dīrghaśmaśruḥ
(1,2.1 ) eṣa dīkṣita eṣa dīrghaśmaśrureṣa evācāryasthāne tiṣṭhannācārya iti stūyate
(1,2.1 ) vaidyutasthāne tiṣṭhan vāyuriti stūyate dyausthāne tiṣṭhannāditya iti stūyate
(1,2.1 ) tadapyetadṛcoktaṃ <brahmacārīṣṇan [śṣ11.3.1, ড়ṣ16.153.1]>iti brāhmaṇam || 1 ||

(1,2.2 ) jāyamāno ha vai brāhmaṇaḥ saptendriyāṇyabhijāyate brahmavarcasaṃ ca yaśaśca svapnaṃ ca krodhaṃ ca ślāghāṃ ca rūpaṃ ca puṇyameva gandhaṃ saptamam
(1,2.2 ) tāni ha vā asyaitāni brahmacaryamupetyopakrāmanti
(1,2.2 ) mṛgānasya brahmavarcasaṃ gacchati
(1,2.2 ) ācāryaṃ yaśaḥ
(1,2.2 ) ajagaraṃ svapnaḥ
(1,2.2 ) varāhaṃ krodhaḥ
(1,2.2 ) apaḥ ślāghā
(1,2.2 ) kumārīṃ rūpam
(1,2.2 ) oṣadhivanaspatīn puṇyo gandhaḥ
(1,2.2 ) sa yan mṛgājināni vaste tena tadbrahmavarcasamavarunddhe yadasya mṛgeṣu bhavati
(1,2.2 ) sa ha snāto brahmavarcasī bhavati
(1,2.2 ) sa yadaharaharācāryāya karma karoti tena tadyaśo'varunddhe yadasyācārye bhavati
(1,2.2 ) sa ha snāto yaśasvī bhavati
(1,2.2 ) sa yatsuṣupsurnidrāṃ ninayati tena taṃ svapnamavarunddhe yo'syājagare bhavati
(1,2.2 ) taṃ ha snātaṃ svapantamāhuḥ svapitu mainaṃ bobudhatheti
(1,2.2 ) sa yatkruddho vācā na kaṃ cana hinasti puruṣātpuruṣātpāpīyāniva manyamānastena taṃ krodhamavarunddhe yo'sya varāhe bhavati
(1,2.2 ) tasya ha snātasya krodhāḥ ślāghīyasaṃ viśante
(1,2.2 ) athādbhiḥ ślāghamāno na snāyāt
(1,2.2 ) tena tāṃ ślāghāmavarunddhe yāsyāpsu bhavati
(1,2.2 ) sa ha snātaḥ ślāghīyo'nyebhyaḥ ślāghyate
(1,2.2 ) athaitadbrahmacāriṇo rūpaṃ yatkumāryās
(1,2.2 ) tāṃ nagnāṃ nodīkṣeta
(1,2.2 ) iti veti vā mukhaṃ viparidhāpayet
(1,2.2 ) tena tadrūpamavarunddhe yadasya kumāryāṃ bhavati
(1,2.2 ) taṃ ha snātaṃ kumārīmiva nirīkṣante
(1,2.2ś) athaitadbrahmacāriṇaḥ puṇyo gandho ya oṣadhivanaspatīnāṃ tāsāṃ puṇyaṃ gandhaṃ pracchidya nopajighret
(1,2.2 a) tena taṃ puṇyaṃ gandhamavarunddhe yo'syauṣadhivanasvapatiṣu bhavati
(1,2.2 b) sa ha snātaḥ puṇyagandhirbhavati || 2 ||

(1,2.3 ) sa vā eṣa upayaṃścaturdhopaityagniṃ pādenācāryaṃ pādena grāmaṃ pādena mṛtyuṃ pādena
(1,2.3 ) sa yadaharahaḥ samidha āhṛtya sāyaṃprātaragniṃ paricarettena taṃ pādamavarunddhe yo'syāgnau bhavati
(1,2.3 ) sa yadaharaharācāryāya karma karoti tena taṃ pādamavarunddhe yo'syācārye bhavati
(1,2.3 ) sa yadaharahargrāmaṃ praviśya bhikṣāmeva parīpsati na maithunaṃ tena taṃ pādamavarunddhe yo'sya grāme bhavati
(1,2.3 ) sa yatkruddho vācā na kaṃ cana hinasti puruṣātpuruṣātpāpīyāniva manyamānastena taṃ pādamavarunddhe yo'sya mṛtyau bhavati || 3 ||

(1,2.4 ) pañca ha vā ete brahmacāriṇyagnayo dhīyante
(1,2.4 ) dvau pṛthagghastayormukhe hṛdaya upastha eva pañcamaḥ
(1,2.4 ) sa yaddakṣiṇena pāṇinā striyaṃ na spṛśati tenāharaharyājināṃ lokamavarunddhe
(1,2.4 ) yatsavyena tena pravrājinām
(1,2.4 ) yan mukhena tenāgnipraskandinām
(1,2.4 ) yaddhṛdayena tena śūrāṇām
(1,2.4 ) yadupasthena tena gṛhamedhinām
(1,2.4 ) taiścetstriyaṃ parāharatyanagniriva śiṣyate
(1,2.4 ) sa yadaharaharācāryāya kule'nutiṣṭhate so'nuṣṭhāya brūyāddharma gupto mā gopāyeti
(1,2.4 ) dharmo hainaṃ gupto gopāyati
(1,2.4 ) tasya ha prajā śvaḥ śvaḥ śreyasī śreyasīha bhavati
(1,2.4 ) dhāyyaiva pratidhīyate
(1,2.4 ) svarge loke pitṝnnidadhāti
(1,2.4 ) tāntavaṃ na vasīta
(1,2.4 ) yastāntavaṃ vaste kṣatraṃ vardhate na brahma
(1,2.4 ) tasmāttāntavaṃ na vasīta brahma vardhatāṃ mā kṣatramiti
(1,2.4 ) noparyāsīta
(1,2.4 ) yaduparyāste prāṇameva tadātmano'dharaṃ kurute yadvāto vahati
(1,2.4 ) adha evāsītādhaḥ śayītādhastiṣṭhedadho vrajet
(1,2.4 ) evaṃ ha sma vai tatpūrve brāhmaṇā brahmacaryaṃ caranti
(1,2.4 ) taṃ ha sma tatputraṃ bhrātaraṃ vopatāpinamāhurupanayetainamiti
(1,2.4 ) ā samiddhārātsvareṣyanto'nnamadyāt
(1,2.4 ) athāha jaghanamāhuḥ snāpayetainamitiyā samiddhārāt
(1,2.4 ) na hyetāni vratāni bhavanti
(1,2.4 ) taṃ cecchayānamācāryo'bhivadetsa pratisaṃhāya pratiśṛṇuyāt
(1,2.4ś) taṃ cecchayānamutthāya taṃ cedutthitamabhiprakramya taṃ cedabhiprakrāntamabhipalāyamānam
(1,2.4 a) evaṃ ha sma vai tatpūrve brāhmaṇā brahmacaryaṃ caranti
(1,2.4 b) teṣāṃ ha sma vaiṣā puṇyā kīrtirgacchatyā ha vā ayaṃ so'dya gamiṣyatīti || 4 ||

(1,2.5 ) janamejayo ha vai pārīkṣito mṛgayāṃ cariṣyan haṃsābhyāmasiṣyannupāvatastha iti
(1,2.5 ) tāvūcaturjanamejayaṃ pārīkṣitamabhyājagāma
(1,2.5 ) sa hovāca
(1,2.5 ) namo vāṃ bhagavantau kau nu bhagavantāviti
(1,2.5 ) tāvūcaturdakṣiṇāgniścāhavanīyaśceti
(1,2.5 ) sa hovāca namo vāṃ bhagavantau tadākīyatāmiti
(1,2.5 ) ihopārāmamiti
(1,2.5 ) api kila devā na ramante na hi devā na ramantae
(1,2.5 ) api caikopārāmāddevā ārāmamupasaṃkrāmantīti
(1,2.5 ) sa hovāca namo vāṃ bhagavantau kiṃ puṇyamiti
(1,2.5 ) brahmacaryamiti
(1,2.5 ) kiṃ laukyamiti
(1,2.5 ) brahmacaryameveti
(1,2.5 ) tatko veda iti
(1,2.5 ) dantāvalo dhaumraḥ
(1,2.5 ) atha khalu dantāvalo dhaumro yāvati tāvati kāle pārīkṣitaṃ janamejayamabhyājagāma
(1,2.5 ) tasmā utthāya svayameva viṣṭaraṃ nidadhau
(1,2.5 ) tamupasaṃgṛhya papracchādhīhi bho kiṃ puṇyamiti
(1,2.5 ) brahmacaryamiti
(1,2.5 ) kiṃ laukyamiti
(1,2.5 ) brahmacaryameveti
(1,2.5 ) tasmā etatprovācāṣṭācatvāriṃśadvarṣaṃ sarvavedabrahmacaryam
(1,2.5 ) taccaturdhā vedeṣu vyuhya dvādaśavarṣaṃ brahmacaryaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇyavarārdham
(1,2.5 ) api snāyaṃścaredyathāśaktyaparam
(1,2.5 ) tasmā uhasyṛṣabhau sahasraṃ dadau
(1,2.5ś) apyapakīrtitamācāryo brahmacārītyeka āhurākāśamadhidaivatam
(1,2.5 a) athādhyātmaṃ brāhmaṇo vratavāṃścaraṇavān brahmacārī || 5 ||

(1,2.6 ) brahma ha vai prajā mṛtyave saṃprāyacchat
(1,2.6 ) brahmacāriṇameva na saṃpradadau
(1,2.6 ) sa hovācāśyāmasminniti
(1,2.6 ) kimiti
(1,2.6 ) yāṃ rātrīṃ samidhamanāhṛtya vasettāmāyuṣo'varundhīyeti
(1,2.6 ) tasmādbrahmacāryaharahaḥ samidha āhṛtya sāyaṃprātaragniṃ paricaret
(1,2.6 ) noparyupasādayedatha pratiṣṭhāpayet
(1,2.6 ) yaduparyupasādayejjīmūtavarṣī tadahaḥ parjanyo bhavati
(1,2.6 ) te devā abruvan brāhmaṇo vā ayaṃ brahmacaryaṃ cariṣyati
(1,2.6 ) brūtāsmai bhikṣā iti gṛhapatirbrūta bahucārī gṛhapatnyā iti
(1,2.6 ) kimasyā vṛñjītādadatyā iti
(1,2.6 ) iṣṭāpūrtasukṛtadraviṇamavarundhyāditi
(1,2.6 ) tasmādbrahmacāriṇe'haraharbhikṣāṃ dadyādgṛhiṇī mā māyamiṣṭāpūrtasukṛtadraviṇamavarundhyāditi
(1,2.6 ) saptamīṃ nātinayetsaptamīmatinayanna brahmacārī bhavati
(1,2.6 ) samidbhaikṣe saptarātramacaritavān brahmacārī punarupaneyo bhavati || 6 ||

(1,2.7 ) nopariśāyī syānna gāyano na nartano na saraṇo na niṣṭhīvet
(1,2.7 ) yadupariśāyī bhavatyabhīkṣṇaṃ nivāsā jāyante
(1,2.7 ) yadgāyano bhavatyabhīkṣṇaśa ākrandān dhāvante
(1,2.7 ) yannartano bhavatyabhīkṣṇaśaḥ pretānnirharante
(1,2.7 ) yatsaraṇo bhavatyabhīkṣṇaśaḥ prajāḥ saṃviśante
(1,2.7 ) yanniṣṭhīvati madhya eva tadātmano niṣṭhīvati
(1,2.7 ) sa cenniṣṭhīved<divo nu mām [śṣ6.124.1, ড়ṣ19.40.4, Vaitṣ12.7]> <yadatrāpi madhorahaṃ [ড়ṣ20.38.6, Vaitṣ12.8]> <yadatrāpi rasasya me [ড়ṣ20.27.8, Vaitṣ12.9]>ityātmānamanumantrayate
(1,2.7 ) <yadatrāpi madhorahaṃ niraṣṭhaviṣamasmṛtamagniśca tatsavitā ca punarme jaṭhare dhattāṃ [ড়ṣ20.38.6, Vaitṣ12.8]> || <yadatrāpi rasasya me parāpapātāsmṛtaṃ tadihopahvayāmahe tan ma āpyāyatāṃ punaḥ [ড়ṣ20.27.8]>iti
(1,2.7 ) na śmaśānamātiṣṭhet
(1,2.7 ) sa cedabhitiṣṭhedudakaṃ haste kṛtvā <yadīdamṛtukāmyety[ড়ṣ20.54.9]> abhimantrya japantsamprokṣya parikrāmet
(1,2.7 ) samayāyoparivrajed
(1,2.7 ) <yadīdamṛtukāmyāghaṃ ripramupeyima | andhaḥ śloṇa iva hīyatāṃ mā no'nvāgādaghaṃ yataḥ [ড়ṣ20.54.9]>iti
(1,2.7 ) atha haitaddevānāṃ pariṣūtaṃ yadbrahmacārī
(1,2.7 ) tadapyetadṛcoktaṃ devānāmetatpariṣūtamanabhyārūḍhaṃ carati rocamānaṃ tasmin sarve paśavastatra yajñāstasminnannaṃ saha devatābhiriti brāhmaṇam || 7 ||

(1,2.8 ) prāṇāpānau janayanniti śaṅkhasya mukhe maharṣervasiṣṭhasya putra etāṃ vācaṃ sasṛje śītoṣṇāvihotsau prādurbhaveyātāmiti
(1,2.8 ) tathā tacchaśvadanuvartate
(1,2.8 ) atha khalu vipāṇmadhye vasiṣṭhaśilā nāma prathama āśramaḥ
(1,2.8 ) dvitīyaḥ kṛṣṇaśilās
(1,2.8 ) tasmin vasiṣṭhaḥ samatapat
(1,2.8 ) viśvāmitrajamadagnī jāmadagne tapataḥ
(1,2.8 ) gautamabharadvājau siṃhau prabhave tapataḥ
(1,2.8 ) guṃgurguṃguvāse tapati
(1,2.8 ) ṛṣirṛṣidroṇe'bhyatapat
(1,2.8 ) agastyo'gastyatīrthe tapati
(1,2.8 ) divyatrirha tapati
(1,2.8 ) svayambhūḥ kaśyapaḥ kaśyapatuṅge'bhyatapat
(1,2.8 ) ulavṛkarkṣutarakṣuḥ
(1,2.8 ) śvā varāhacilvaṭibabrukāḥ sarpadaṃṣṭranaḥ saṃhanukṛṇvānāḥ kaśyapatuṅgadarśanātsaraṇavāṭātsiddhirbhavati
(1,2.8 ) brāhmyaṃ varṣasahasramṛṣivane brahmacāryekapādenātiṣṭhati
(1,2.8 ) dvitīyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ mūrdhanyevāmṛtasya dhārāmādhārayat
(1,2.8 ) brāhmāṇyaṣṭācatvāriṃśadvarṣasahasrāṇi salilasya pṛṣṭhe śivo'bhyatapat
(1,2.8 ) tasmāttaptāttapaso bhūya evābhyatapat
(1,2.8 ) tadapyetā ṛco'bhivadanti prāṇāpānau janayanniti brāhmaṇam || 8 ||

(1,2.9 ) ekapāddvipada iti
(1,2.9 ) vāyurekapāt
(1,2.9 ) tasyākāśaṃ pādaḥ
(1,2.9 ) candramā dvipāt
(1,2.9 ) tasya pūrvapakṣāparapakṣau pādau
(1,2.9 ) ādityastripāt
(1,2.9 ) tasyeme lokāḥ pādāḥ
(1,2.9 ) agniḥ ṣaṭpādas
(1,2.9 ) tasya pṛthivyantarikṣaṃ dyaurāpa oṣadhivanaspataya imāni bhūtāni pādās
(1,2.9 ) teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ vedā gatirātmā pratiṣṭhitāścatasro brahmaṇaḥ śākhāḥ
(1,2.9 ) atho āhuḥ ṣaḍiti mūrtirākāśaśceti [ed. ahuḥ]
(1,2.9 ) ṛcā mūrtiḥ
(1,2.9 ) yājuṣī gatiḥ
(1,2.9 ) sāmamayaṃ tejaḥ
(1,2.9 ) bhṛgvaṅgirasā māyā
(1,2.9 ) etadbrahmaiva yajñaścatuṣpāddviḥ saṃsthita iti
(1,2.9 ) tasya bhṛgvaṅgirasaḥ saṃsthe
(1,2.9 ) atho āhurekasaṃsthita iti
(1,2.9 ) yaddhotarcāṃ maṇḍalaiḥ karoti pṛthivīṃ tenāpyāyayati
(1,2.9 ) etasyāṃ hyagniścarati
(1,2.9 ) tadapyetadṛcoktam <agnivāsāḥ pṛthivyasitajñūḥ [śṣ12.1.21, ড়ṣ17.3.2]>iti
(1,2.9 ) yadadhvaryuryajuṣā karotyantarikṣaṃ tenāpyāyayati
(1,2.9 ) tasmin vāyurna niviśate katamacca nāhariti
(1,2.9 ) tadapyetadṛcoktam <antarikṣe pathibhirhrīyamāṇo na ni viśate katamacca nāhaḥ | apāṃ yoniḥ prathamajā ṛtasya kva svijjātaḥ kuta ā babhūva [ড়ṣ1.107.4]>iti
(1,2.9 ) yadudgātā sāmnā karoti divaṃ tenāpyāyayati
(1,2.9ś) tatra hyādityaḥ śukraścarati
(1,2.9 a) tadapyetadṛcoktam <uccā patantamaruṇaṃ suparṇam [śṣ13.2.36, ড়ṣ18.24.3]> iti
(1,2.9 b) yadbrahmarcāṃ kāṇḍaiḥ karotyapastenāpyāyayati
(1,2.9 c) candramā hyapsu carati
(1,2.9 d) tadapyetadṛcoktaṃ candramā apsvantariti tāsāmoṣadhivanaspatayaḥ kāṇḍāni
(1,2.9 e) tato mūlakāṇḍaparṇapuṣpaphalapraroharasagandhairyajño vartate
(1,2.9 f) adbhiḥ karṇāṇi pravartante
(1,2.9 g) adbhiḥ somo'bhiṣūyate
(1,2.9 h) tadyadbrahmāṇāṃ karṇāṇi karmaṇyāmantrayatyapastenānujānāti
(1,2.9 i) eṣo hyasya bhāgas
(1,2.9 j) tadyathā bhokṣyamāṇaḥ
(1,2.9 k) apa eva prathamamācāmayedapa upariṣṭādevaṃ yajño'dbhireva pravartate
(1,2.9 l) apsu saṃsthāpyate tasmādbrahmā purastāddhomasaṃsthitahomairyajño vartate
(1,2.9 m) antarā hi purastāddhomasaṃsthitahomairyajñaṃ parigṛhṇāti
(1,2.9 n) antarā hi bhṛgvaṅgiraso vedānāduhya bhṛgvaṅgirasaḥ somapānaṃ manyante
(1,2.9 o) somātmako hyayaṃ veda
(1,2.9 p) tadapyetadṛcoktaṃ somaṃ manyate papivāniti
(1,2.9 ṝ) tadyathemāṃ pṛthivīmudīrṇāṃ jyotiṣā dhūmāyamānāṃ varṣaṃ śamayati
(1,2.9 r) evaṃ brahmā bhṛgvaṅgirobhirvyāhṛtibhiryajñasya viriṣṭaṃ śamayati
(1,2.9 s) agnirādityāya śamayati
(1,2.9 t) ete'ṅgirasaḥ
(1,2.9 u) eta idaṃ sarvaṃ samāpnuvanti
(1,2.9 v) vāyurāpaścandramā ityete bhṛgavaḥ
(1,2.9 w) eta idaṃ sarvaṃ samāpyāyayanti
(1,2.9 x) ekameva saṃsthaṃ bhavatīti brāhmaṇam || 9 ||

(1,2.10 ) vicārī ha vai kābandhiḥ kabandhasyātharvaṇasya putro medhāvī mīmāṃsako'nūcāna āsa
(1,2.10 ) sa ha svenātimānena mānuṣaṃ vittaṃ neyāya
(1,2.10 ) taṃ mātovāca
(1,2.10 ) ta evaitadannamavocaṃsta ima eṣu kurupañcāleṣvaṅgamagadheṣu kāśikauśaleṣu śālvamatsyeṣu savaśośīnareṣūdīcyeṣvannamadantīti
(1,2.10 ) atha vayaṃ tavaivātimānenānādyāḥ smaḥ
(1,2.10 ) vatsa vāhanamanviccheti
(1,2.10 ) sa māndhāturyauvanāśvasya sārvabhaumasya rājñaḥ somaṃ prasūtamājagāma
(1,2.10 ) sa sado'nupraviśyartvijaśca yajamānaṃ cāmantrayāmāsa
(1,2.10 ) tadyāḥ prācyo nadyo vahanti yāśca dakṣiṇācyo yāśca pratīcyo yāścodīcyastāḥ sarvāḥ pṛthaṅnāmadheyā ityācakṣate
(1,2.10 ) tāsāṃ samudramabhipadyamānānāṃ chidyate nāmadheyaṃ samudra ityācakṣate
(1,2.10 ) evamime sarve vedā nirmitāḥ sakalpāḥ sarahasyāḥ sabrāhmaṇāḥ sopaniṣatkāḥ setihāsāḥ sānvākhyānāḥ sapurāṇāḥ sasvarāḥ sasaṃskārāḥ saniruktāḥ sānuśāsanāḥ sānumārjanāḥ savākovākyās
(1,2.10 ) teṣāṃ yajñamabhipadyamānānāṃ chidyate nāmadheyaṃ yajña ityevācakṣate || 10 ||

(1,2.11 ) bhūmerha vā etadvicchinnaṃ devayajanaṃ yadaprākpravaṇaṃ yadanudakpravaṇaṃ yatkṛtrimaṃ yatsamaviṣamam
(1,2.11 ) idaṃ ha tveva devayajanaṃ yatsamaṃ samūlamavidagdhaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ prāgudakpravaṇaṃ samaṃ samāstīrṇamiva bhavati yatra brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇatāṃ vidyādbrahmā brahmatvaṃ karotīti
(1,2.11 ) voce chandastanna vindāmo yenottaramemahīti
(1,2.11 ) tān ha papraccha kiṃ vidvān hotā hautraṃ karoti kiṃ vidvānadhvaryurādhvaryavaṃ karoti kiṃ vidvānudgātaudgātraṃ karoti kiṃ vidvān brahmā brahmatvaṃ karotīti
(1,2.11 ) voce chandastanna vindāmo yenottaramemahīti
(1,2.11 ) te brūmo vāgeva hotā hautraṃ karoti
(1,2.11 ) vāco hi stomāśca vaṣaṭkārāścābhisampadyante
(1,2.11 ) te brūmo vāgeva hotā vāgbrahma vāgdeva iti
(1,2.11 ) prāṇāpānābhyāmevādhvaryurādhvaryavaṃ karoti
(1,2.11 ) prāṇapraṇītāni ha bhūtāni prāṇapraṇītāḥ praṇītās
(1,2.11 ) te brūmaḥ prāṇāpānāvevādhvaryuḥ prāṇāpānau brahma prāṇāpānau deva iti
(1,2.11 ) cakṣuṣaivodgātaudgātraṃ karoti
(1,2.11 ) cakṣuṣā hīmāni bhūtāni paśyanti
(1,2.11 ) atho cakṣurevodgātā cakṣurbrahma cakṣurdeva iti
(1,2.11 ) manasaiva brahmā brahmatvaṃ karoti
(1,2.11 ) manasā hi tiryakca diśa ūrdhvaṃ yacca kiṃ ca manasaiva karoti tadbrahma
(1,2.11 ) te brūmo mana eva brahmā mano brahma mano deva iti || 11 ||

(1,2.12 ) tadyathā ha vā idaṃ yajamānaśca yājayitāraśca divaṃ brūyuḥ pṛthivīti pṛthivīṃ vā dyauriti brūyustadanyo nānujānātyetāmevaṃ nānujānāti yadetadbrūyāt
(1,2.12 ) atha nu kathamiti
(1,2.12 ) hotetyeva hotāraṃ brūyādvāgiti vācaṃ brahmeti brahma deva iti devamadhvaryurityevādhvaryuṃ brūyātprāṇāpānāviti prāṇāpānau brahmeti brahma deva iti devamudgātetyevodgātāraṃ brūyāccakṣuriti cakṣurbrahmeti brahma deva iti devaṃ brahmetyeva brahmāṇaṃ brūyān mana iti mano brahmeti brahma deva iti devam || 12 ||

(1,2.13 ) nānāpravacanāni ha vā etāni bhūtāni bhavanti
(1,2.13 ) ye caivāsomapaṃ yājayanti ye ca surāpaṃ ye ca brāhmaṇaṃ vicchinnaṃ somayājinaṃ taṃ prātaḥ samitpāṇaya upodeyurupāyāmo bhavantamiti
(1,2.13 ) kimarthamiti
(1,2.13 ) yāneva no bhavāṃstān hyaḥ praśnānapṛcchattāneva no bhavān vyācakṣīteti
(1,2.13 ) tatheti
(1,2.13 ) tebhya etān praśnān vyācacaṣṭe
(1,2.13 ) tadyena ha vā idaṃ vidyamānaṃ cāvidyamānaṃ cābhinidadhāti tadbrahma
(1,2.13 ) tadyo veda sa brāhmaṇo'dhīyāno'dhītyācakṣata iti brāhmaṇam || 13 ||
