(1,2.14 ) athetadbhaumaṃ devayajanam
(1,2.14 ) yatrāpastiṣṭhanti yatra syandanti pra tadvahantyudvahanti taddevayajanam
(1,2.14 ) yatsamaṃ samūlamavidagdhaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ prāgudakpravaṇaṃ samaṃ samāstīrṇamiva bhavati yasya śvabhra ūrmo vṛkṣaḥ parvato nadī panthā vā purastātsyāt
(1,2.14 ) na devayajanamātraṃ purastātparyavaśiṣyet
(1,2.14 ) nottarato'gneḥ paryupasīderanniti brāhmaṇam || 14 ||

(1,2.15 ) aditirvai prajākāmaudanamapacat
(1,2.15 ) tata ucchiṣṭamāśnāt
(1,2.15 ) sā garbhamadhatta
(1,2.15 ) tata ādityā ajāyanta
(1,2.15 ) ya eṣa odanaḥ pacyata ārambhaṇamevaitatkriyata ākramaṇameva
(1,2.15 ) prādeśamātrīḥ samidho bhavanti
(1,2.15 ) etāvān hyātmā prajāpatinā saṃmitaḥ
(1,2.15 ) agnervai yā yajñiyā tanūraśvatthe tayā samagacchata
(1,2.15 ) eṣāsya ghṛtyā tanūryadghṛtam
(1,2.15 ) yadghṛtena samidho'nakti tābhyāmevainaṃ tattanūbhyāṃ samardhayati
(1,2.15 ) yannirmārgasyādadhātyavagūrtyā vai vīryaṃ kriyate
(1,2.15 ) yannirmārgasyādadhātyavagūrtyā eva
(1,2.15 ) saṃvatsaro vai prajananam
(1,2.15 ) agniḥ prajananam
(1,2.15 ) etatprajananam
(1,2.15 ) yatsaṃvatsara ṛcāgnau samidhamādadhāti prajananādevainaṃ tatprajanayitā prajanayati
(1,2.15 ) abhaktarturvai puruṣaḥ
(1,2.15 ) na hi tadveda yamṛtumabhijāyate
(1,2.15 ) yannakṣatraṃ tadāpnoti
(1,2.15 ) ya eṣa odanaḥ pacyate yonirevaiṣā kriyate
(1,2.15 ) yatsamidha ādhīyante retastaddhīyate
(1,2.15 ) saṃvatsare vai reto hitaṃ prajāyate
(1,2.15 ) yaḥ saṃvatsare paryete'gnimādhatte prajātamevainamādhatte
(1,2.15 ) dvādaśasu rātrīṣu purā saṃvatsarasyādheyās
(1,2.15 ) tā hi saṃvatsarasya pratimā
(1,2.15ś) atho tisṛṣvatho dvayoratho pūrvedyurādheyāsta evāgnimādadhānena
(1,2.15 a) ādityā vā ita uttamā amuṃ lokamāyan
(1,2.15 b) te pathirakṣayasta iyakṣamāṇaṃ pratinudantaḥ
(1,2.15 c) uccheṣaṇabhājā vā ādityāḥ
(1,2.15 d) [yaducchiṣṭam]
(1,2.15 e) yaducchiṣṭena samidho'nakti tebhya eva prāvocattebhya eva procya svargaṃ lokaṃ yāti || 15 ||

(1,2.16 ) prajāpatiratharvā devaḥ sa tapastaptvaitaṃ cātuḥprāśyaṃ brahmaudanaṃ niramimīta caturlokaṃ caturdevaṃ caturvedaṃ caturhautramiti
(1,2.16 ) catvāro vā ime lokāḥ pṛthivyantarikṣaṃ dyaurāpa iti
(1,2.16 ) catvāro vā ime devā agnirvāyurādityaścandramāḥ
(1,2.16 ) catvāro vā ime vedā ṛgvedo yajurvedaḥ sāmavedo brahmaveda iti
(1,2.16 ) catasro vā imā hotrā hautramādhvaryavamaudgātraṃ brahmatvamiti
(1,2.16 ) tadapyetadṛcoktaṃ <catvāri śṛṅgā trayo asya pādā dve śīrṣe sapta hastāso asya | tridhā baddho vṛṣabho roravīti maho devo martyāṃ āviveśa [ড়ṣ8.13.3]>iti
(1,2.16 ) catvāri śṛṅgeti vedā vā eta uktās
(1,2.16 ) trayo asya pādā iti savanānyeva
(1,2.16 ) dve śīrṣe iti brahmaudanapravargyāveva
(1,2.16 ) sapta hastāso asyeti chandāṃsyeva
(1,2.16 ) tridhā baddha iti mantraḥ kalpo brāhmaṇam
(1,2.16 ) vṛṣabho roravītyeṣa ha vai vṛṣabha eṣa tadroravīti yadyajñeṣu śastrāṇi śaṃsatyṛgbhiryajurbhiḥ sāmabhirbrahmabhiriti
(1,2.16 ) maho devo martyāṃ āviveśetyeṣa ha vai mahān devo yadyajñaḥ
(1,2.16 ) eṣa martyāṃ āviveśa
(1,2.16 ) yo vidyātsapta pravata iti prāṇānāha
(1,2.16 ) sapta vidyātparāvata ityapānānāha
(1,2.16 ) śiro yajñasya yo vidyādityetadvai yajñasya śiro yan mantravān brahmaudanaḥ
(1,2.16 ) yo ha vā etamamantravantaṃ brahmaudanamupeyādapaśirasā ha vā asya yajñamupeto bhavati
(1,2.16 ) tasmān mantravantameva brahmaudanamupeyānnāmantravantamiti brāhmaṇam || 16 || [ed. brāhmanam]

(1,2.17 ) kimupajña ātreyo bhavatīti
(1,2.17 ) ādityaṃ hi tamo jagrāha
(1,2.17 ) tadatrirapanunoda
(1,2.17 ) tadatriranvapaśyat
(1,2.17 ) tadapyetadṛcoktaṃ <srutādyamatrirdivamunnināya [śṣ13.2.4 , ড়ṣ18.20.8 ]>
(1,2.17 ) <divi tvātriradhārayatsūryā māsāya kartave [śṣ13.2.12 b, ড়ṣ18.21.6 b]>iti
(1,2.17 ) taṃ hovāca varaṃ vṛṇīṣveti
(1,2.17 ) sa hovāca dakṣiṇīyā me prajā syāditi
(1,2.17 ) tasmādātreyāya prathamaṃ dakṣiṇā yajñe dīyanta iti brāhmaṇam || 17 ||

(1,2.18 ) prajāpatirvedānuvācāgnīnādadhīyeti
(1,2.18 ) tān vāgabhyuvācāśvo vai sambhārāṇāmiti
(1,2.18 ) taṃ ghorātkrūrātsalilātsarasa udāninyus
(1,2.18 ) tān vāgabhyuvācāśvaḥ śamyeteti
(1,2.18 ) tatheti
(1,2.18 ) tamṛgveda etyovācāhamaśvaṃ śameyamiti
(1,2.18 ) tasmā abhisṛptāya mahadbhayaṃ sasṛje
(1,2.18 ) sa etāṃ prācīṃ diśaṃ bheje
(1,2.18 ) sa hovācāśānto nvayamaśva iti
(1,2.18 ) taṃ yajurveda etyovācāhamaśvaṃ śameyamiti
(1,2.18 ) tasmā abhisṛptāya mahadbhayaṃ sasṛje
(1,2.18 ) sa etāṃ pratīcīṃ diśaṃ bheje
(1,2.18 ) sa hovācāśānto nvayamaśva iti
(1,2.18 ) taṃ sāmaveda etyovācāhamaśvaṃ śameyamiti
(1,2.18 ) kena nu tvaṃ śamayiṣyasīti
(1,2.18 ) rathantaraṃ nāma me sāmāghoraṃ cākrūraṃ ca
(1,2.18 ) tenāśva abhiṣṭūyeteti
(1,2.18 ) tasmā apyabhisṛptāya tadeva mahadbhayaṃ sasṛje
(1,2.18 ) sa etāmudīcīṃ diśaṃ bheje
(1,2.18 ) sa hovācāśānto nvayamaśva iti
(1,2.18 ) tān vāgabhyuvāca śaṃyumātharvaṇaṃ gacchateti
(1,2.18 ) te śaṃyumātharvaṇamāsīnaṃ prāpyocurnamaste astu bhagavannaśvaḥ śamyeteti
(1,2.18 ) tatheti
(1,2.18 ) sa khalu kabandhasyātharvaṇasya putramāmantrayāmāsa vicārinniti
(1,2.18 ) bhago iti hāsmai pratiśrutaṃ pratiśruśrāva
(1,2.18ś) aśva śamyeteti
(1,2.18 a) tatheti
(1,2.18 b) sa khalu śāntyudakaṃ cakārātharvaṇībhiścāṅgirasībhiśca cātanairmātṛnāmabhirvāstoṣpatyairiti śamayati
(1,2.18 c) tasya ha snātasyāśvasyābhyukṣitasya sarvebhyo romasamarebhyo'ṅgārā āśīryanta
(1,2.18 d) so'śvastuṣṭo namaskāraṃ cakāra
(1,2.18 e) namaḥ śaṃyumātharvaṇāya yo mā yajñiyamacīkḷpaditi
(1,2.18 f) bhaviṣyanti ha vā ato'nye brāhmaṇā laghusambhāratamās
(1,2.18 g) ta ādityasya pada ādhāsyantyanaḍuho vatsasyājasya śravaṇasya brahmacāriṇo vā
(1,2.18 h) etadvā ādityasya padaṃ yadbhūmis
(1,2.18 i) tasyaiva pada āhitaṃ bhaviṣyatīti
(1,2.18 j) so'gnau praṇīyamāṇe'śve'nvārabdhaṃ brahmā yajamānaṃ vācayati <yadakrandaḥ prathamaṃ jāyamānaḥ [ṛV 1.163.1]>iti pañca
(1,2.18 k) taṃ brāhmaṇā upavahanti taṃ brahmopākuruta
(1,2.18 l) eṣa ha vai vidvāntsarvavidbrahmā yadbhṛṅgiroviditi brāhmaṇam || 18 ||

(1,2.19 ) devāśca ha vā asurāścāspardhanta
(1,2.19 ) te devā indramabruvannimaṃ nastāvadyajñaṃ gopāya yāvadasuraiḥ saṃyatāmahā iti
(1,2.19 ) sa vai nastena rūpeṇa gopāya yena no rūpeṇa bhūyiṣṭhaṃ chādayasi yena śakṣyasi goptumiti
(1,2.19 ) sa ṛgvedo bhūtvā purastātparītyopātiṣṭhat
(1,2.19 ) taṃ devā abruvannanyattadrūpaṃ kuruṣva naitena no rūpeṇa bhūyiṣṭhaṃ chādayasi naitena śakṣyasi goptumiti
(1,2.19 ) sa yajurvedo bhūtvā paścātparītyopātiṣṭhat
(1,2.19 ) taṃ devā abruvannanyattadrūpaṃ kuruṣva naitena no rūpeṇa bhūyiṣṭhaṃ chādayasi naitena śakṣyasi goptumiti
(1,2.19 ) sa samāvedo bhūtvottarataḥ parītyopātiṣṭhat
(1,2.19 ) taṃ devā abruvannanyadeva tadrūpaṃ kuruṣva naitena no rūpeṇa bhūyiṣṭhaṃ chādayasi naitena śakṣyasi goptumiti
(1,2.19 ) sa indra uṣṇīṣī brahmavedo bhūtvā dakṣiṇataḥ parītyopātiṣṭhat
(1,2.19 ) taṃ devā abruvannetattadrūpaṃ kuruṣvaitena no rūpeṇa bhūyiṣṭhaṃ chādayasyetena śakṣyasi goptumiti
(1,2.19 ) tadyadindra uṣṇīṣī brahmavedo bhūtvā dakṣiṇataḥ parītyopātiṣṭhattadbrahmābhavat
(1,2.19 ) tadbrahmaṇo brahmatvam
(1,2.19 ) tadvā etadatharvaṇo rūpaṃ yaduṣṇīṣī brahmā
(1,2.19 ) taṃ dakṣiṇato viśve devā upāsīdan
(1,2.19 ) taṃ yaddakṣiṇato viśve devā upāsīdaṃstatsadasyo'bhavat
(1,2.19 ) tatsadasyasya sadasyatvam
(1,2.19 ) balerha vā etadbalamupajāyate yatsadasye
(1,2.19 ) āmayato vai vrajasya bahulataraṃ vrajaṃ vidanti
(1,2.19 ) ghorā vā eṣā digdakṣiṇā śāntā itarās
(1,2.19 ) tadyāni stutāni brahmānumantrayate manasaiva tāni sadasyo janadityetāṃ vyāhṛtiṃ japati
(1,2.19 ) ātmānaṃ janayati najityātmānamapitve dadhāti
(1,2.19 ) taṃ devā abruvan varaṃ vṛṇīṣveti
(1,2.19 ) vṛṇā iti
(1,2.19 ) sa varamavṛṇīta
(1,2.19ś) asyāmeva māṃ hotrāyāmindrabhūtaṃ punanta stuvantaḥ śaṃsantastiṣṭheyuriti [ed. tistheyur, corr. ড়tyal]
(1,2.19 a) taṃ tasyāmeva hotrāyāmindrabhūtaṃ punanta stuvantaḥ śaṃsanto'tiṣṭhan
(1,2.19 b) taṃ yattasyāmeva hotrāyāmindrabhūtaṃ punanta stuvantaḥ śaṃsanto'tiṣṭhaṃstadbrāhmaṇācchaṃsyabhavat
(1,2.19 c) tadbrāhmaṇācchaṃsino brāhmaṇācchaṃsitvam
(1,2.19 d) saiṣaindrī hotrā yadbrāhmaṇācchaṃsīyā
(1,2.19 e) dvitīyaṃ varaṃ vṛṇīṣveti
(1,2.19 f) vṛṇā iti
(1,2.19 g) sa varamavṛṇīta
(1,2.19 h) asyāmeva māṃ hotrāyāṃ vāyubhūtaṃ punanta stuvantaḥ śaṃsantastistheyuriti [ed. tistheyur]
(1,2.19 i) taṃ tasyāmeva hotrāyāṃ vāyubhūtaṃ punanta stuvantaḥ śaṃsanto'tiṣṭhan
(1,2.19 j) taṃ yattasyāmeva hotrāyāṃ vāyubhūtaṃ punanta stuvantaḥ śaṃsanto'tiṣṭhaṃstatpotābhavat
(1,2.19 k) tatpotuḥ potṛtvam
(1,2.19 l) saiṣā vāyavyā hotrā yatpotrīyā
(1,2.19 m) tṛtīyaṃ varaṃ vṛṇīṣveti
(1,2.19 n) vṛṇā iti
(1,2.19 o) sa varamavṛṇīta
(1,2.19 p) asyāmeva māṃ hotrāyāmagnibhūtamindhānāḥ punanta stuvanta śaṃsantastiṣṭheyuriti  [ed. tistheyur]
(1,2.19 ṝ) taṃ tasyāmeva hotrāyāmagnibhūtamindhānāḥ punanta stuvantaḥ śaṃsanto'tiṣṭhan  [ed.'tisthaṃs, corr. ড়tyal]
(1,2.19 r) taṃ yattasyāmeva hotrāyāmagnibhūtamindhānāḥ punanta stuvantaḥ śaṃsanto'tiṣṭhaṃstadāgnīdhro'bhavat[ed.'tisthaṃs]
(1,2.19 s) tadāgnīdhrasyāgnīdhratvam
(1,2.19 t) saiṣāgneyī hotrā yadāgnīdhrīyeti brāhmaṇam || 19 ||

(1,2.20 ) brāhmaṇo ha vā imamagniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ babhāra
(1,2.20 ) so'yamagnirvaiśvānaro brāhmaṇena bhriyamāṇa imāṃllokāñjanayate
(1,2.20 ) athāyamīkṣate'gnirjātavedā brāhmaṇadvitīyo ha vā ayamidamagnirvaiśvānaro jvalati
(1,2.20 ) hantāhaṃ yan mayi teja idriyaṃ vīryaṃ taddarśayāmyuta vai mā bibhṛyāditi
(1,2.20 ) sa ātmānamāpyāyyaitaṃ payo'dhok
(1,2.20 ) tamimaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ darśayitvātmanyajuhot
(1,2.20 ) sa dvitīyamātmānamāpyāyyaitaṃ ghṛtamadhok
(1,2.20 ) tamimaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ darśayitvātmanyajuhot
(1,2.20 ) sa tṛtīyamātmānamāpyāyyaitadidaṃ viśvaṃ vikṛtamannādyamadhok
(1,2.20 ) tamimaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ darśayitvātmanyajuhot
(1,2.20 ) sa caturthamātmānamāpyāyyaitena brāhmaṇasya jāyāṃ virājamapaśyat
(1,2.20 ) tāmasmai prāyacchat
(1,2.20 ) sātmā apitvamabhavat
(1,2.20 ) tata imamagniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ parāsyurbrāhmaṇo'gniṃ jātavedasamadhatta
(1,2.20 ) so'yamabravīdagne jātavedo'bhinidhehi mehīti
(1,2.20 ) tasya dvaitaṃ nāmādhattāghoraṃ cākrūraṃ ca
(1,2.20 ) so'śvo bhavat
(1,2.20 ) tasmādaśvo vahena rathaṃ na bhavati pṛsthena sādinam
(1,2.20 ) sa devānāgacchat
(1,2.20 ) sa devebhyo'nvātiṣṭhat
(1,2.20 ) tasmāddevā abibhayus
(1,2.20 ) taṃ brahmaṇe prāyacchat
(1,2.20 ) tametayarcāśamayat || 20 ||

(1,2.21 ) <agniṃ tvāhurvaiśvānaraṃ sadanān pradahanvagāḥ | sa no devatrādhi brūhi mā riṣāmā vayaṃ tava [ড়ṣ1.95.3, sakala also atVaitṣ6.7]>iti
(1,2.21 ) tametābhiḥ pañcabhirṛgbhirupākurute <yadakrandaḥ prathamaṃ jāyamānaḥ [ṛV 1.163.1]>iti [ed. pratamaṃ, correctedp. 302]
(1,2.21 ) so'śāmyat
(1,2.21 ) tasmādaśvaḥ paśūnāṃ jighatsutamo bhavati
(1,2.21 ) vaiśvānaro hyeṣa
(1,2.21 ) tasmādagnipadamaśvaṃ brahmaṇe dadāti
(1,2.21 ) brahmaṇe hi prattam
(1,2.21 ) tasya rasamapīḍayat
(1,2.21 ) sa raso'bhavat
(1,2.21 ) raso ha vā eṣa
(1,2.21 ) taṃ vā etaṃ rasaṃ santaṃ ratha ityācakṣate parokṣeṇa
(1,2.21 ) parokṣapriyā iva hi devā bhavanti pratyakṣadviṣaḥ
(1,2.21 ) sa devānāgacchat
(1,2.21 ) sa devebhyo'nvātiṣṭhat
(1,2.21 ) tasmāddevā abibhayus
(1,2.21 ) taṃ brahmaṇe prāyacchat
(1,2.21 ) tametayarcājyāhutyābhyajuhot
(1,2.21 ) <indrasyaujo marutāmanīkam [śṣ6.125.3, cf. ড়ṣ15.11.7]> iti rathamabhihutya tametayarcātiṣṭhad<vanaspate vīḍvaṅgo hi bhūyāḥ [śṣ6.125.1, ড়ṣ15.11.8]> iti
(1,2.21 ) tasmādāgnyādheyikaṃ rathaṃ brahmaṇe dadāti
(1,2.21 ) brahmaṇe hi prattam
(1,2.21 ) tasya takṣāṇastanūṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ niramimata
(1,2.21 ) tāṃ pañcasvapaśyadṛci yajuṣi sāmni śānte'tha ghore
(1,2.21 ) tāsāṃ dve brahmaṇe prāyacchadvācaṃ ca jyotiśca
(1,2.21 ) vāgvai dhenurjyotirhiraṇyam
(1,2.21 ) tasmādāgnyādheyikāṃ cātuḥprāśyāṃ dhenuṃ brahmaṇe dadāti
(1,2.21ś) brahmaṇe hi prattā
(1,2.21 a) paśuṣu śāmyamāneṣu cakṣurhāpayanti
(1,2.21 b) cakṣureva tadātmani dhatte
(1,2.21 c) yadvai cakṣustaddhiraṇyam
(1,2.21 d) tasmādāgnyādheyikaṃ hiraṇyaṃ brahmaṇe dadāti
(1,2.21 e) brahmaṇe hi prattaṃ
(1,2.21 f) tasyātmannadhatta
(1,2.21 g) tena prājvalayat
(1,2.21 h) yannādhatta tadāglābhavat
(1,2.21 i) tadāglā bhūtvā sā samudraṃ prāviśat[cf. ড়ṣ17.28.1 forthisandthe followingsentences]
(1,2.21 j) sā samudramadahat
(1,2.21 k) tasmātsamudro durgiravapi
(1,2.21 l) vaiśvānareṇa hi dagdhaḥ
(1,2.21 m) sā pṛthivīmudait
(1,2.21 n) sā pṛthivīṃ vyadahat
(1,2.21 o) sā devānāgacchat
(1,2.21 p) sā devānaheḍat
(1,2.21 ṝ) te devā brahmāṇamupādhāvan
(1,2.21 r) sa naivāgāyannānṛtyat
(1,2.21 s) saiṣāglā
(1,2.21 t) eṣā kāruvidā nama
(1,2.21 u) taṃ vā etamāglāhataṃ santamāglāgṛdha ityācakṣate parokṣeṇa
(1,2.21 v) parokṣapriyā iva hi devā bhavanti pratyakṣadviṣāḥ [ed. dviṣo]
(1,2.21 w) ya eṣa brāhmaṇo gāyano nartano vā bhavati tamāglāgṛdha ityācakṣate
(1,2.21 x) tasmādbrāhmaṇo naiva gāyenna nṛtyen māglāgṛdhaḥ syāt
(1,2.21 y) tasmādbrāhmyaṃ pūrvaṃ haviraparaṃ prājāpatyaṃ
(1,2.21śś) prājāpatyādbrāhmyamevottaramiti brāhmaṇam || 21 ||

(1,2.22 ) atharvāṇaśca ha vā aṅgirasaśca bhṛgucakṣuṣī tadbrahmābhivyapaśyan
(1,2.22 ) tadajānanvayaṃ vā idaṃ sarvaṃ yadbhṛgvaṅgirasa iti
(1,2.22 ) te devā brāhmyaṃ haviryatsāṃtapane'gnāvajuhavuḥ
(1,2.22 ) etadvai brāhmyaṃ haviryatsāṃtapane'gnau hūyata
(1,2.22 ) eṣa ha vai sāṃtapano'gniryadbrāhmaṇas
(1,2.22 ) tasyorjayorjāṃ devā abhajanta sumanasa eva svadhāṃ pitaraḥ śraddhayā svargaṃ lokaṃ brāhmaṇās
(1,2.22 ) tena sunvantyṛṣayo'ntata striyaḥ kevala ātmanyavārundhata bāhyā ubhayena sunvanti
(1,2.22 ) yadvai yajñe brāhmyaṃ havirna nirupyetānṛjavaḥ prājāpatyahaviṣo manuṣyā jāyeran
(1,2.22 ) asau yāṃllokāñchṛṇviti pitā hyeṣa āhavanīyasya gārhapatyasya dakṣiṇāgneryo'gnihotraṃ juhotīti [ed. yāllokāñ, āhavaṇīyasya]
(1,2.22 ) devāḥ priye dhāmani madanti
(1,2.22 ) teṣāmeṣo'gniḥ sāṃtapanaḥ śreṣṭho bhavati
(1,2.22 ) etasya vāci tṛptāyāmagnistṛpyati
(1,2.22 ) prāṇe tṛpte vāyustṛpyati
(1,2.22 ) cakṣuṣi tṛpta ādityastṛpyati
(1,2.22 ) manasi tṛpte candramāstṛpyati
(1,2.22 ) śrotre tṛpte diśaścāntardeśāśca tṛpyanti
(1,2.22 ) sneheṣu tṛpteṣvāpastṛpyanti
(1,2.22 ) lomeṣu tṛpteṣvoṣadhivanaspatayastṛpyanti
(1,2.22 ) śarīre tṛpte pṛthivī tṛptati
(1,2.22 ) evameṣo'gniḥ sāṃtapanaḥ śreṣṭhastṛptaḥ sarvāṃstṛptāṃstarpayatīti brāhmaṇam || 22 ||

(1,2.23 ) sāṃtapanā idaṃ haviriti
(1,2.23 ) eṣa ha vai sāṃtapano'gniryadbrāhmaṇo yasya garbhādhānapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇaniṣkramaṇānnaprāśanagodānacūḍākaraṇopanayanāplavanāgnihotravratacaryādīni kṛtāni bhavanti sa sāṃtapanaḥ
(1,2.23 ) atha yo'yamanagnikaḥ sa kumbhe loṣṭas
(1,2.23 ) tadyathā kumbhe loṣṭaḥ prakṣipto naiva śaucārthāya kalpate naiva śasyaṃ nirvartayatyevamevāyaṃ brāhmaṇo'nagnikastasya brāhmaṇasyānagnikasya naiva daivaṃ dadyānna pitryaṃ na cāsya svādhyāyāśiṣo na yajñāśiṣaḥ svargaṃgamā bhavanti
(1,2.23 ) tadapyetadṛcoktam <agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe hotāraṃ viśvavedasam | asya yajñasya sukratum [śṣ20.101.1, ṛV 1.12.1]> iti brāhmaṇam || 23 ||

(1,2.24 ) atha ha prajāpatiḥ somena yakṣyamāṇo vedānuvāca kaṃ vo hotāraṃ vṛṇīya kamadhvaryuṃ kamudgātāraṃ kaṃ brāhmaṇamiti
(1,2.24 ) ta ūcurṛgvidameva hotāraṃ vṛṇīṣ.va yajurvidamadhvaryuṃ sāmavidamudgātāramatharvāṅgirovidaṃ brāhmaṇam
(1,2.24 ) tathā hāsya yajñaścaturṣu lokeṣu caturṣu deveṣu caturṣu vedeṣu catasṛṣu hotrāsu catuṣpādyajñaḥ pratitiṣṭhati
(1,2.24 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda
(1,2.24 ) tasmādṛgvidameva hotāraṃ vṛṇīṣva sa hi hautraṃ veda
(1,2.24 ) agnirvai hotā
(1,2.24 ) pṛthivī vā ṛcāmāyatanam
(1,2.24 ) agnirdevatā gāyatraṃ chando bhūriti śukram
(1,2.24 ) tasmāttameva hotāraṃ vṛṇīṣvetyetasya lokasya jitaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasya vijitaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasya saṃjitaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasyāvaruddhaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasya vivṛddhaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasya samṛddhaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasyodāttaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasya vyāptaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasya paryāptaye
(1,2.24 ) etasya lokasya samāptaye
(1,2.24 ) atha cennaivaṃvidaṃ hotāraṃ vṛṇute purastādevaiṣāṃ yajño ricyate
(1,2.24 ) yajurvidamevādhvaryuṃ vṛṇīṣva sa hyādhvaryavaṃ veda
(1,2.24 ) vāyurvā adhvaryuḥ
(1,2.24 ) antarikṣaṃ vai yajuṣāmāyatanam
(1,2.24 ) vāyurdevatā traiṣṭubhaṃ chando bhuva iti śukram
(1,2.24 ) tasmāttamevādhvaryuṃ vṛṇīṣvetyetasya lokasyetyeva
(1,2.24 ) atha cennaivaṃvidamadhvaryuṃ vṛṇute paścādevaiṣāṃ yajño ricyate
(1,2.24ś) sāmavidamevodgātāraṃ vṛṇīṣva
(1,2.24 a) sa hyaudgātraṃ veda
(1,2.24 b) ādityo vā udgātā
(1,2.24 c) dyaurvai sāmnāmāyatanam
(1,2.24 d) ādityo devatā jāgataṃ chandaḥ svariti śukram
(1,2.24 e) tasmāttamevodgātāraṃ vṛṇīṣvetyetasya lokasyetyeva
(1,2.24 f) atha cennaivaṃvidamudgātāraṃ vṛṇuta uttarata evaiṣāṃ yajño ricyate
(1,2.24 g) atharvāṅgirovidameva brahmāṇaṃ vṛṇīṣva
(1,2.24 h) sa hi brahmatvaṃ veda
(1,2.24 i) candramā vai brahmā [ed. brahāpo]
(1,2.24 j) āpo vai bhṛgavaṅgirasāmāyatanam
(1,2.24 k) candramā devatā vaidyutaścoṣṇikkākubhe chandasī omityatharvaṇāṃ śukraṃ janadityaṅgirasām
(1,2.24 l) tasmāttameva brahmāṇaṃ vṛṇīṣvetyetasya lokasya jitaye
(1,2.24 m) etasya lokasya vijitaye
(1,2.24 n) etasya lokasya saṃjitaye
(1,2.24 o) etasya lokasyāvaruddhaye
(1,2.24 p) etasya lokasya vivṛddhaye
(1,2.24 ṝ) etasya lokasya samṛddhaye
(1,2.24 r) etasya lokasyodāttaye
(1,2.24 s) etasya lokasya vyāptaye
(1,2.24 t) etasya lokasya paryāptaye
(1,2.24 u) etasya lokasya samāptaye
(1,2.24 v) atha cennaivaṃvidaṃ brahmāṇaṃ vṛṇute dakṣiṇata evaiṣāṃ yajño ricyate dakṣiṇata evaiṣāṃ yajño ricyate || 24 ||

(1,2.24 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇapūrvabhāge dvitīyaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||

(1,3.1 ) oṃ dakṣiṇāpravaṇā bhūmirdakṣiṇata āpo vahanti
(1,3.1 ) tasmādyajñāstadbhūmerunnatataramiva bhavati yatra bhṛgvaṅgiraso viṣṭhās
(1,3.1 ) tadyathāpa imāṃllokānabhivahantyevameva bhṛgvaṅgirasaḥ sarvān devānabhivahanti
(1,3.1 ) evamevaiṣā vyāhṛtiḥ sarvān vedānabhivahatyomiti harcāmomiti yajuṣāmomiti sāmnāmomiti sarvasyāhābhivādas
(1,3.1 ) taṃ ha smaitaduttaraṃ yajñe vidvāṃsaḥ kurvanti
(1,3.1 ) devā brahmāṇa āgacchatāgacchateti
(1,3.1 ) ete vai devā brahmāṇo yadbhṛgvaṅgirasas
(1,3.1 ) tānevaitadgṛṇānāstān vṛṇānā hvayanto manyante
(1,3.1 ) nānyo'bhṛgvaṅgirovido vṛto yajñamāgacchen
(1,3.1 ) yajñasya tejasā teja āpnotyūrjayorjāṃ yaśasā yaśaḥ
(1,3.1 ) nānyo'bhṛgvaṅgirovido vṛto yajñamāgacchennedyajñaṃ parimuṣṇīyāditi
(1,3.1 ) tadyathā pūrvaṃ vatso'dhītya gāṃ dhayedevaṃ brahmā bhṛgvaṅgirovidvṛto yajñamāgacchennedyajñaṃ parimuṣṇīyāditi
(1,3.1 ) tadyathā gaurvāśvo vāśvataro vaikapāddvipāttripāditi syātkimabhivahetkimabhyaśnuyāditi
(1,3.1 ) tasmādṛgvidameva hotāraṃ vṛṇīṣva yajurvidamadhvaryuṃ sāmavidamudgātāramatharvāṅgirovidaṃ brahmāṇam
(1,3.1 ) tathā hāsya yajñaścaturṣu lokeṣu caturṣu deveṣu vedeṣu catasṛṣu hotrāsu catuṣpādyajñaḥ pratitiṣṭhati
(1,3.1 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda yaścaivamṛtvijāmārtvijyaṃ veda yaśca yajñe yajanīyaṃ vedeti brāhmaṇam || 1 ||

(1,3.2 ) prajāpatiryajñamatanuta
(1,3.2 ) sa ṛcaiva hautramakarodyajuṣādhvaryavaṃ sāmnaudgātramatharvāṅgirobhirbrahmatvam
(1,3.2 ) taṃ vā etaṃ mahāvādyaṃ kurute yadṛcaiva hautramakarodyajuṣādhvaryavaṃ sāmnaudgātramatharvāṅgirobhirbrahmatvam
(1,3.2 ) sa vā eṣa tribhirvedairyajñasyānyataraḥ pakṣaḥ saṃskriyate
(1,3.2 ) manasaiva brahmā yajñasyānyatraṃ pakṣaṃ saṃskaroti
(1,3.2 ) ayamu vai yaḥ pavate sa yajñas
(1,3.2 ) tasya manaśca vākca vartanī
(1,3.2 ) manasā caiva hi vācā ca yajño vartate
(1,3.2 ) ada eva mana iyameva vāk
(1,3.2 ) sa yadvadannāsti vidyādardhaṃ me'sya yajñasyāntaragāditi
(1,3.2 ) tadyathaikapātpuruṣo yannekacakro vā ratho vartamāno bhreṣaṃ nyetyevamevāsya yajño bhreṣaṃ nyeti
(1,3.2 ) yajñasya bhreṣamanu yajamāno bhreṣaṃ nyeti
(1,3.2 ) yajamānasya bhreṣamanvṛtvijo bhreṣaṃ niyanti
(1,3.2 ) ṛtvijāṃ bhreṣamanu dakṣiṇā bhreṣaṃ niyanti
(1,3.2 ) dakṣiṇānāṃ bhreṣamanu yajamānaḥ putrapaśubhirbhreṣaṃ nyeti
(1,3.2 ) putrapaśūnāṃ bhreṣamanu yajamānaḥ svargeṇa lokena bhreṣaṃ nyeti
(1,3.2 ) svargasya lokasya bhreṣamanu tasyārdhasya yogakṣemo bhreṣaṃ nyeti yasminnardhe yajanta iti brāhmaṇam || 2 ||

(1,3.3 ) tadu ha smāha śvetaketurāruṇeyo brahmāṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhāṣamāṇamardhaṃ me'sya yajñasyāntaragāditi
(1,3.3 ) tasmādbrahmā stute bahiḥpavamāne vācoyamyamupāṃśvantaryāmābhyām
(1,3.3 ) atha ye pavamānā odṛcasteṣu
(1,3.3 ) atha yāni stotrāṇi saśastrāṇyā vaṣaṭkārātteṣu
(1,3.3 ) sa yadṛkto bhreṣaṃ nyṛcchedoṃ bhūrjanaditi gārhapatye juhuyāt
(1,3.3 ) yadi yajuṣṭa oṃ bhuvo janaditi dakṣiṇāgnau juduyāt
(1,3.3 ) yadi sāmata oṃ svarjanadityāhavanīye juhuyāt
(1,3.3 ) yadyanājñātādbrahmato voṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svarjanadomityāhavanīya eva juhuyāt
(1,3.3 ) tadvākovākyasyarcāṃ yajuṣāṃ sāmnāmatharvāṅgirasām
(1,3.3 ) athāpi vedānāṃ rasana yajñasya viriṣṭaṃ saṃdhīyate
(1,3.3 ) tadyathā lavaṇenetyuktam
(1,3.3 ) tadyathobhayapātpuruṣo yannubhayacakro vā ratho vartamāno'bhreṣaṃ nyeti evamevāsya yajño'bhreṣaṃ nyeti
(1,3.3 ) yajñasyābhreṣamanu yajamāno'bhreṣaṃ nyeti
(1,3.3 ) yajamānasyābhreṣamanvṛtvijo'bhreṣaṃ niyanti
(1,3.3 ) ṛtvijāmabhreṣamanu dakṣiṇā abhreṣaṃ niyanti
(1,3.3 ) dakṣiṇānāmabhreṣamanu yajamānaḥ putrapaśubhirabhreṣaṃ nyeti
(1,3.3 ) putrapaśūnāmabhreṣamanu yajamānaḥ svargeṇa lokenābhreṣaṃ nyeti
(1,3.3 ) svargasya lokasyābhreṣamanu tasyārdhasya yogakṣemo'bhreṣaṃ nyeti yasminnardhe yajanta iti brāhmaṇam || 3 ||

(1,3.4 ) tadyadaudumbaryāṃ ma āsiṣṭa hiṅṅakārṣīn me prāstāvīn ma udagāsīn me subrahmaṇyāmāhvāsīdityudgātre dakṣiṇā nīyante
(1,3.4 ) grahān me'grahītprācārīn me'śuśruvan me samanasaskārṣīdayākṣīn me'vaṣaṭkārṣīn ma ityadhvaryave
(1,3.4 ) hotṛṣadana āsiṣṭāyākṣīn me'śāṃsīn me'vaṣaṭkārṣīn ma iti hotre
(1,3.4 ) devayajanaṃ me cīkḷpadbrahmāsādaṃ me'sīsṛpadbrahmajapān me'japītpurastāddhomasaṃsthitahomān me'hauṣīdayākṣīn me'śāṃsīn me'vaṣaṭkārṣīn ma iti brahmaṇe
(1,3.4 ) bhūyiṣṭhena mā brahmaṇākārṣīditi
(1,3.4 ) etadvai bhūyiṣṭhaṃ brahma yadbhṛgvaṅgirasaḥ
(1,3.4 ) ye'ṅgirasaḥ sa raso ye'tharvāṇo ye'tharvāṇastadbheṣajam
(1,3.4 ) yadbheṣajaṃ tadamṛtaṃ yadamṛtaṃ tadbrahma
(1,3.4 ) sa vā eṣa pūrveṣāmṛtvijāmardhabhāgasyārdhamitareṣāmardhaṃ brahmaṇa iti brāhmaṇam || 4 ||

(1,3.5 ) devāśca ha vā asurāśca saṃgrāmaṃ samayatanta
(1,3.5 ) tatraitāstisro hotrā jihmaṃ pratipedire
(1,3.5 ) tāsāmindra ukthāni sāmāni lulopa
(1,3.5 ) tāni hotre prāyacchat
(1,3.5 ) ājyaṃ ha vai hoturbabhūva
(1,3.5 ) pra'ugaṃ poturvaiśvadevaṃ ha vai hoturbabhūva
(1,3.5 ) niṣkevalyaṃ neṣṭuḥ
(1,3.5 ) marutvatīyaṃ ha vai hoturbabhūva
(1,3.5 ) agnimārutamāgnīdhrasya
(1,3.5 ) tasmādetadabhyastataramiva śasyate yadāgnimārutam
(1,3.5 ) tasmādete saṃśaṃsukā iva bhavanti yaddhotā potā neṣṭā
(1,3.5 ) āgnīdhro mumuhe vasīta
(1,3.5 ) tadbrahmeyasāmivāsa
(1,3.5 ) tāsāmardhaṃ pratilulopa prathamārhaṇaṃ ca prathamapadaṃ caitaddakṣiṇāṃ caitatpariśiṣedediti brāhmaṇam || 5 ||

(1,3.6 ) uddālako ha vā āruṇirudīcyān vṛto dhāvayāṃ cakāra
(1,3.6 ) tasya ha niṣka upāhito babhūvopavādādbibhyato yo mā brāhmaṇo'nūcāna upavadiṣyati tasmā etaṃ pradāsyamīti
(1,3.6 ) taddhodīcyān brāhmaṇān bhayaṃ vivedoddālako ha vā ayamāyāti kaurupañcolo brahmā brahmaputraḥ
(1,3.6 ) sa ūrdhvaṃ vṛto na paryādadhīta
(1,3.6 ) kenemaṃ vīreṇa pratisaṃyatāmahā iti
(1,3.6 ) taṃ yata eva prapannaṃ dadhre tata evamanupratipedire te ha svaidāyanaṃ śaunakamūcuḥ svaidāyana tvaṃ vai no brahmiṣṭho'sīti
(1,3.6 ) tvayemaṃ vīreṇa pratisaṃyatāmahā iti
(1,3.6 ) taṃ yata eva prapannaṃ dadhre tata evamanupratipedire
(1,3.6 ) taṃ ha svaidāyanā ityāmantrayāmāsa
(1,3.6 ) sa bho gautamasya putreti hāsmā asūyā pratiśrutaṃ pratiśuśrāva
(1,3.6 ) sa vai gautamasya putra ūrdhvaṃ vṛto dhāvet || 6 ||

(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādimāḥ prajāḥ śirastaḥ prathamaṃ lomaśā jāyante kasmādāsāmaparamiva śmaśrūṇyupakakṣāṇyanyāni lomāni jāyante
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādimāḥ prājāḥ śirastaḥ prathamaṃ palitā bhavanti kasmādantataḥ sarvā eva palitā bhavanti
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādimāḥ prajā adantikā jāyante kasmādāsāmaparamiva jāyante
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādāsāṃ saptavarṣāṣṭavarṣāṇāṃ prabhidyante kasmādāsāṃ punareva jāyante kasmādantataḥ sarva eva prabhidyante
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādadhare dantāḥ pūrve jāyante para uttare
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādadhare dantāḥ aṇīyāṃso hrasīyāṃsaḥ prathīyāṃso varṣīyāṃsa uttare
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādimau daṃṣṭrau dīrghatarau kasmātsame iva jaṃbhe
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādime śrotre antarataḥ same iva dīrṇe
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmātpumāṃsaḥ śmaśruvanto'śmaśruvaḥ striyaḥ
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādāsāṃ saṃtatamiva śarīraṃ bhavati kasmādāsāmasthīni dṛḍhatarāṇīva bhavanti
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādāsāṃ prathame vayasi retaḥ siktaṃ na saṃbhavati kasmādāsāṃ madhyame vayasi retaḥ siktaṃ saṃbhavati kasmādāsāmuttame vayasi retaḥ siktaṃ na saṃbhavati
(1,3.7 ) yastaddarśapūrṇamāsayo rūpaṃ vidyātkasmādidaṃ śiśnamuccaśa eti nīcī padyate [ed. iṃ]
(1,3.7 ) kasmātsakṛdapānam || 7 ||

(1,3.8 ) atha yaḥ purastādaṣṭāvājyabhāgān vidyān madhyataḥ pañca havirbhāgāḥ ṣaṭprājāpatyā upariṣṭādaṣṭāvājyabhāgān vidyāt
(1,3.8 ) atha yo gāyatrīṃ hariṇīṃ jyotiṣpakṣāṃ sarvairyajñairyajamānaṃ svargaṃ lokamabhivahantīṃ vidyāt
(1,3.8 ) atha yaḥ apaṅktiṃ pañcapadāṃ saptadaśākṣarāṃ sarvairyajñairyajamānaṃ svargaṃ lokamabhivahantīṃ vidyāt
(1,3.8 ) tasmai ha niṣkaṃ prayacchannuvācānūcāno ha vai svaidāyanāsi suvarṇaṃ vai suvarṇavide dadāmīti
(1,3.8 ) tadupayamya niścakrāma
(1,3.8 ) tatrāpavavrāja yatretaro babhūva
(1,3.8 ) taṃ ha papraccha kimeṣa gautamasya putra iti
(1,3.8 ) eṣa brahmā brahmāputra iti hovāca yadenaṃ kaścidupavadetota mīmāṃseta ha vā mūrdhā vā asya vipatetprāṇā vainaṃ jahyuriti
(1,3.8 ) te mitha eva cikrandeyurviprāpavavraja yatretaro babhūva
(1,3.8 ) te prātaḥ samitpāṇaya upodeyurupāyāmo bhavantamiti
(1,3.8 ) kimarthamiti
(1,3.8 ) yāneva no bhavāṃstān hyaḥ praśnānapṛcchattāneva no bhavān vyācakṣīteti
(1,3.8 ) tatheti
(1,3.8 ) tebhya etān praśnān vyācacaṣṭe || 8 ||

(1,3.9 ) yatpurastādvedeḥ prathamaṃ barhi stṛṇāti tasmādimāḥ prajāḥ śirastaḥ prathamaṃ lomaśā jāyante
(1,3.9 ) yadaparamiva prastaramanuprastṛṇāti tasmādāsāmaparamiva śmaśrūṇyupakakṣāṇyanyāni lomāni jāyante
(1,3.9 ) yatprāgbarhiṣaḥ prastaramanupraharati tasmādimāḥ prajāḥ śirastaḥ prathamaṃ palitā bhavanti
(1,3.9 ) yadantataḥ sarvamevānupraharati tasmādantataḥ sarva eva palitā bhavanti
(1,3.9 ) yatprayājā apuro'nuvākyāvanto bhavanti tasmādimāḥ prajā adantikā jāyante
(1,3.9 ) yaddhavīṃṣi puro'nuvākyāvanti bhavanti tasmādāsāmaparamiva jāyante
(1,3.9 ) yadanuyājā apuro'nuvākyāvanto bhavanti tasmādāsāṃ saptavarṣāṣṭavarṣāṇāṃ prabhidyante
(1,3.9 ) yatpatnīsaṃyājāḥ puro'nuvākyāvanto bhavanti tasmādāsāṃ punareva jāyante
(1,3.9 ) yatsamiṣṭayajurapuro'nuvākyāvadbhavati tasmādantataḥ sarva eva prabhidyante
(1,3.9 ) yadgāyatryānūcya triṣṭubhā yajati tasmādadhare dantāḥ pūrve jāyante para uttare
(1,3.9 ) yadṛcānūcya yajuṣā yajati tasmādadhare dantā aṇīyāṃso hrasīyāṃsaḥ prathīyāṃso varṣīyāṃsa uttare [ed. varsīyāṃsa]
(1,3.9 ) yadāghārau dīrghatarau prāñcāvāghārayati tasmādimau daṃṣṭrau dīrghatarau
(1,3.9 ) yatsaṃyājye sacchandasī tasmātsame iva jambhe
(1,3.9 ) yaccaturthe prayāje samānayati tasmādime śrotre antarataḥ same iva dīrṇe
(1,3.9 ) yajjapaṃ japitvābhihiṃkṛṇoti tasmātpumāṃsaḥ śmaśruvanto'śmaśruva striyaḥ
(1,3.9 ) yatsāmidhenīḥ saṃtanvannanvāha tasmādāsāṃ saṃtatamiva śarīraṃ bhavati
(1,3.9 ) yatsāmidhenyaḥ kāṣṭhahaviṣo bhavanti tasmādāsāmasthīni dṛḍhatarāṇīva bhavanti
(1,3.9 ) yatprayājā ājyahaviṣo bhavanti tasmādāsāṃ prathame vayasi retaḥ siktaṃ na saṃbhavati
(1,3.9 ) yan madhye haviṣāṃ dadhnā ca puroḍāśena ca pracaranti tasmādāsāṃ madhyame vayasi retaḥ siktaṃ saṃbhavati
(1,3.9 ) yadanuyājā ājyahaviṣo bhavanti tasmādāsāmuttame vayasi retaḥ siktaṃ na saṃbhavati
(1,3.9 ) yaduttame'nuyāje sakṛdapāniti tasmādidaṃ śiśnamuccaśa eti nīcī padyate
(1,3.9 ) yannāpānetsakṛcchūnaṃ syāt
(1,3.9 ) yan muhurapānetsakṛtpannaṃ syāt
(1,3.9 ) tasmātsakṛdapāniti netsakṛcchūnaṃ syātsakṛtpannaṃ veti || 9 ||

(1,3.10 ) atha ye purastādaṣṭāvājyabhāgāḥ pañca prayājā dvāvāghārau dvāvājyabhāgāvāgneya ājyabhāgānāṃ prathamaḥ saumyo dvitīyo havirbhāgānām
(1,3.10 ) havirhyeva saumyam
(1,3.10 ) āgneyaḥ puroḍāśaḥ
(1,3.10 ) agnīṣomīyaḥ puroḍāśo'gniḥ sviṣṭakṛdityete madhyataḥ pañca havirbhāgāḥ
(1,3.10 ) atha ye ṣaṭprājāpatyā iḍā ca prāśitraṃ ca yaccāgnīdhrāyāvadyati brahmabhāgo yajamānabhāgo'nvāhārya eva ṣaṣṭhaḥ
(1,3.10 ) atha ya upariṣṭādaṣṭāvājyabhāgāstrayo'nuyājāścatvāraḥ patnīsaṃyājāḥ samiṣṭayajuraṣṭamam
(1,3.10 ) atha yā gāyatrī hariṇī jyotiṣpakṣā sarvairyajñairyajamānaṃ svargaṃ lokamabhivahati vedireva sā
(1,3.10 ) tasya ye purastādaṣṭāvājyābhāgāḥ sa dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ
(1,3.10 ) atha ya upariṣṭādaṣṭāvājyabhāgāḥ sa uttaraḥ pakṣaḥ [ed. ipariṣṭād]
(1,3.10 ) havīṃṣyātmā [ed. haviṃṣy]
(1,3.10 ) gārhapatyo jaghanam
(1,3.10 ) āhavanīyaḥ śiraḥ
(1,3.10 ) sauvarṇarājatau pakṣau
(1,3.10 ) tadyadādityaṃ purastātparyantaṃ na paśyanti tasmādajyotiṣka utkaro bhavati
(1,3.10 ) atha yā paṅktiḥ pañcapadā saptadaśākṣarā sarvairyajñairyajamānaṃ svargaṃ lokamabhivahati yājyaiva sā
(1,3.10 ) tasyā oṃ śrāvayeti caturakṣaram
(1,3.10 ) astu śrauṣaditi caturakṣaram
(1,3.10 ) yajeti dvyakṣaram
(1,3.10 ) ye yajāmaha iti pañcākṣaram
(1,3.10 ) dvyakṣaro vai vaṣaṭkāraḥ saiṣā paṅktiḥ
(1,3.10 ) pañcapadā saptadaśākṣarā sarvairyajñairyajamānaṃ svargaṃ lokamabhivahati
(1,3.10 ) tadyatrāsyaiśvaryaṃ syādyatra vainamabhivaheyurevaṃvidameva tatra brahmāṇaṃ vṛṇīyānnānevaṃvidamiti brāhmaṇam || 10 ||

(1,3.11 ) atha ha prācīnayogya ājagāmāgnihotraṃ bhavantaṃ pṛcchāmi gautameti
(1,3.11 ) pṛccha prācīnayogyeti
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ te gavīḍāyām
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamupahūtāyām
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamupasṛṣṭāyām
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ vatsamunnīyamānam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ vatsamunnītam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ duhyamānam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ dugdham
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ prakramyamāṇam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ hriyamāṇam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamadhiśrīyamāṇam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamadhiśritam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamabhyavajvālyamānam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamabhyavajvālitam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ samudvāntam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ viṣyaṇṇam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamadbhiḥ pratyānītam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamudvāsyamānam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamudvāsitam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamunnīyamānam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamunnītam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ prakramyamāṇam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyaṃ hriyamāṇam
(1,3.11 ) kiṃdevatyamupasādyamānam
(1,3.11ś) kiṃdevatyamupasāditam
(1,3.11 a) kiṃdevatyā samit
(1,3.11 b) kiṃdevatyāṃ prathamāmāhutimahauṣīḥ
(1,3.11 c) kiṃdevatyaṃ gārhapatyamavekṣiṣṭhāḥ
(1,3.11 d) kiṃdevatyottarāhutiḥ
(1,3.11 e) kiṃdevatyaṃ hutvā srucaṃ trirudañcamudanaiṣīḥ
(1,3.11 f) kiṃdevatyaṃ barhiṣi srucaṃ nidhāyonmṛjyottarataḥ pāṇī niramārkṣīḥ
(1,3.11 g) kiṃdevatyaṃ dvitīyamunmṛjya pitryupavītaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇataḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhāmakārṣīḥ
(1,3.11 h) kiṃdevatyaṃ prathamaṃ prāśīḥ
(1,3.11 i) kiṃdevatyaṃ dvitīyam
(1,3.11 j) kiṃdevatyamantataḥ sarvameva prāśīḥ
(1,3.11 k) kiṃdevatyamaprakṣālitayodakaṃ srucā nyanaiṣīḥ
(1,3.11 l) kiṃdevatyaṃ prakṣālitayā
(1,3.11 m) kiṃdevatyamapareṇāhavanīyamudakaṃ srucā nyanaiṣīḥ [ed. udaka]
(1,3.11 n) kiṃdevatyaṃ sruvaṃ srucaṃ ca pratyatāpsīḥ
(1,3.11 o) kiṃdevatyaṃ rātrau srugdaṇḍamavāmārkṣīḥ
(1,3.11 p) kiṃdevatyaṃ prātarudamārkṣīriti
(1,3.11 ṝ) etaccedvettha gautama hutaṃ te yadyu na vetthāhutaṃ ta iti brāhmaṇam || 11 ||

(1,3.12 ) sa hovāca raudraṃ me gavīḍayām
(1,3.12 ) mānavyamupahūtāyām
(1,3.12 ) vāyavyamupasṛṣṭāyām
(1,3.12 ) vairājaṃ vatsamunnīyamānam
(1,3.12 ) jāgatamunnītam
(1,3.12 ) āśvinaṃ duhyamānam
(1,3.12 ) saumyaṃ dugdham
(1,3.12 ) bārhaspatyaṃ prakramyamāṇam
(1,3.12 ) dyāvāpṛthivyaṃ hriyamāṇam [ed. hriyamānam, corr. ড়tyal]
(1,3.12 ) āgneyamadhiśrīyamāṇam
(1,3.12 ) vaiśvānarīyamadhiśritam
(1,3.12 ) vaiṣṇavamabhyavajvālyamānam
(1,3.12 ) mārutamabhyavajvālitam
(1,3.12 ) pauṣṇaṃ samudvāntam
(1,3.12 ) vāruṇaṃ viṣyannam
(1,3.12 ) sārasvatamadbhiḥ pratyānītam
(1,3.12 ) tvāṣṭramudvāsyamānam
(1,3.12 ) dhātramudvāsitam
(1,3.12 ) vaiśvadevamunnīyamānam
(1,3.12 ) sāvitramunnītam
(1,3.12 ) bārhaspatyaṃ prakramyamāṇam
(1,3.12 ) dyāvāpṛthivyaṃ hriyamāṇam
(1,3.12 ) aindramupasādyamānam
(1,3.12 ) balāyopasannam
(1,3.12 ) āgneyī samit
(1,3.12ś) yāṃ prathamāmāhutimahauṣaṃ māmeva tatsvarge loke'dhām
(1,3.12 a) yadgārhapatyamavekṣiṣamasya lokasya saṃtatyai
(1,3.12 b) prājāpatyottarāhutis
(1,3.12 c) tasmātpūrṇatarā manasaiva sā
(1,3.12 d) yaddhutvā srucaṃ trirudañcamudanaiṣaṃ rudrāṃstenāpraiṣam
(1,3.12 e) yadbarhiṣi srucaṃ nidhāyonmṛjyottarataḥ pāṇī niramārkṣamoṣadhivanaspatīṃstenāpraiṣam
(1,3.12 f) yaddvitīyamunmṛjya pitryupavītaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇataḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhāmakārṣaṃ pitṝṃstenāpraiṣam
(1,3.12 g) yatprathamaṃ prāśiṣaṃ prāṇāṃstenāpraiṣam
(1,3.12 h) yaddvitīyaṃ garbhāṃstena
(1,3.12 i) tasmādanaśnanto garbhā jīvanti
(1,3.12 j) yadantataḥ sarvameva prāśiṣaṃ viśvān devāṃstenāpraiṣam
(1,3.12 k) yadaprakṣālitayodakaṃ srucā nyanaiṣaṃ sarpetarajanāṃstenāpraiṣam
(1,3.12 l) yatprakṣālitayā sarpapuṇyajanāṃstena
(1,3.12 m) yadapareṇāhavanīyamudakaṃ srucā nyanaiṣaṃ gandharvāpsarasastenāpraiṣam
(1,3.12 n) yatsruvaṃ srucaṃ ca pratyatāpsaṃ saptarṣīṃstenāpraiṣam
(1,3.12 o) yadrātrau srugdaṇḍamavāmārkṣaṃ ye rātrau saṃviśanti dakṣiṇāṃstānudanaiṣam
(1,3.12 p) yatprātarudamārkṣaṃ ye prātaḥ pravrajanti dakṣiṇāṃstānudanaiṣamiti brāhmaṇam || 12 ||

(1,3.13 ) evamevaitadbho yathā bhavānāha
(1,3.13 ) pṛcchāmi tveva bhavantamiti
(1,3.13 ) pṛccha prācīnayogyeti
(1,3.13 ) yasya sāyamagnaya upasamāhitāḥ syuḥ sarve jvalayeyuḥ prakṣālitāni yajñapātrāṇyupasannāni syuratha ceddakṣiṇāgirudvāyātkiṃ vā tato bhayamāgacchediti
(1,3.13 ) kṣipramasya patnī praiti yo'vidvāñjuhoti
(1,3.13 ) vidyayā tvevāhamabhijuhomīti
(1,3.13 ) kā te vidyā kā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 ) gārhapatyādadhi dakṣ.iṇāgniṃ praṇīya prāco'ṅgārānuddhṛtya prāṇāpānābhyaṃ svāheti juhuyāt
(1,3.13 ) atha prātaryathāsthānamagnīnupasāmadhāya yathāpuraṃ juhuyāt
(1,3.13 ) sā me vidyā sā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 ) atha cedāhavanīya udvāyātkiṃ vā tato bhayamāgacchediti
(1,3.13 ) kṣipramasya putraḥ praiti yo'vidvāñjuhoti
(1,3.13 ) vidyayā tvevāhamabhijuhomīti
(1,3.13 ) kā te vidyā kā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 ) gārhapatyādadhyāhavanīyaṃ praṇīya pratīco'ṅgārānuddhṛtya samānavyānābhyāṃ svāheti juhuyāt
(1,3.13 ) atha prātaryathāsthānamagnīnupasamādhāya yathāpuraṃ juhuyāt
(1,3.13 ) sā me vidyā sā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 ) atha cedgārhapatya udvāyātkiṃ vā tato bhayamāgacchediti
(1,3.13 ) kṣipraṃ gṛhapatiḥ praiti yo'vidvāñjuhoti
(1,3.13 ) vidyayā tvevāhamabhijuhomīti
(1,3.13 ) kā te vidyā kā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 ) sabhasmakamāhavanīyaṃ dakṣiṇena dakṣiṇāgniṃ parihṛtya gārhapatyasyāyatane pratiṣṭhāpya tata āhavanīyaṃ praṇīya udīco'ṅgārānuddhṛtyodānarūpābhyāṃ svāheti juhuyāt
(1,3.13 ) atha prātaryathāsthānamagnīnupasamādhāya yathāpuraṃ juhuyāt
(1,3.13 ) sā me vidyā sā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 ) atha cetsarve'gnaya udvāyeyuḥ kiṃ vā tato bhayamāgacchediti
(1,3.13ś) kṣipraṃ gṛhapatiḥ sarvajyāniṃ jīyate yo'vidvāñjuhoti
(1,3.13 a) vidyayā tvevāhamabhijuhomīti
(1,3.13 b) kā te vidyā kā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 c) ānaḍuhena śakṛtpiṇḍenāgnyāyatanāni parilipya homyamupasādyāgniṃ nirmathya prāṇāpānābhyāṃ svāhā samānavyānābhyāṃ svāhodānarūpābhyāṃ svāheti juhuyāt
(1,3.13 d) atha prātaryathāsthānamagnīnupasamādhāya yathāpuraṃ juhuyāt
(1,3.13 e) sā me vidyā sā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 f) atha cennāgniṃ janayituṃ śaknuyurna kutaścana vāto vāyātkiṃ vā tato bhayamāgacchediti
(1,3.13 g) moghamasveṣṭaṃ ca hutaṃ ca bhavati yo'vidvāñjuhoti
(1,3.13 h) vidyayā tvevāhamabhijuhomīti
(1,3.13 i) kā te vidyā kā prāyaścittiriti
(1,3.13 j) ānaḍuhenaiva śakṛtpiṇḍenāgnyāyatanāni parilipya homyamupasādya <vāta ā vātu bheṣajam [ড়ṣ19.46.79]> iti sūktenātmanyeva juhuyāt
(1,3.13 k) atha prātaragniṃ nirmathya yathāsthānamagnīnupasamādhāya yathāpuraṃ juhuyāt
(1,3.13 l) sā me vidyā sā prāyaścittiriti brāhmaṇam || 13 ||

(1,3.14 ) evamevaitadbho bhagavan yathā bhavānāha
(1,3.14 ) upayāmi tveva bhavantamiti
(1,3.14 ) evaṃ cennāvakṣyo mūrdhā te vyapatiṣyaditi
(1,3.14 ) hanta tu te tadvakṣyāmi yathā te na vipatiṣyatīti
(1,3.14 ) yo ha vā evaṃvidvānaśnāti ca pibati ca vāktena tṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) vāci tṛptāyāmagnistṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) agnau tṛpte pṛthivī tṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) pṛthivyāṃ tṛptāyāṃ yāni pṛthivyāṃ bhūtānyanvāyattāni tāni tṛpyanti
(1,3.14 ) yo ha vā evaṃvidvānaśnāti ca pibati ca prāṇastena tṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) prāṇe tṛpte vāyustṛpyati [ed. praṇe]
(1,3.14 ) vāyau tṛpte'ntarikṣaṃ tṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) antarikṣe tṛpte yānyantarikṣe bhūtānyanvāyattāni tāni tṛpyanti
(1,3.14 ) yo ha vā evaṃvidvānaśnāti ca pibati ca cakṣustena tṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) cakṣuṣi tṛpta ādityastṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) āditye tṛpte dyaustṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) divi tṛptāyāṃ yāni divi bhūtānyanvāyattāni tāni tṛpyanti
(1,3.14 ) yo ha vā evaṃvidvānaśnāti ca pibati ca manastena tṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) manasi tṛpte candramāstṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) candramasi tṛpta āpastṛpyanti
(1,3.14 ) apsu tṛptāsu yānyapsu bhūtānyanvāyattāni tāni tṛpyanti
(1,3.14 ) yo ha vā evaṃvidvānaśnāti ca pibati ca śrotraṃ tena tṛpyati
(1,3.14 ) śrotre tṛpte diśaścāntardeśāśca tṛpyanti
(1,3.14 ) dikṣu cāntardeśeṣu ca tṛpteṣu yāni dikṣu cāntardeśeṣu ca bhūtānyanvāyattāni tāni tṛpyanti
(1,3.14 ) yo ha vā evaṃvidvānaśnāti ca pibati ca tasyāyameva dakṣiṇaḥ pāṇirjuhūḥ
(1,3.14 ) savya upabhṛt
(1,3.14ś) kaṇṭho dhruvā
(1,3.14 a) annaṃ haviḥ
(1,3.14 b) prāṇā jyotīṃṣi
(1,3.14 c) sadeṣṭaṃ sadā hutaṃ sadāśitaṃ pāyitamagnihotraṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānagnihotraṃ juhotīti brāhmaṇam || 14 ||

(1,3.15 ) priyamedhā ha vai bharadvājā yajñavido manyamānās
(1,3.15 ) te ha sma na kañcana vedavidamupayanti
(1,3.15 ) te sarvamavidus
(1,3.15 ) te sahaivāvidus
(1,3.15 ) te'gnihotra eva na samavadanta
(1,3.15 ) teṣāmekaḥ sakṛdagnihotramajuhoddvirekastrirekas
(1,3.15 ) teṣāṃ yaḥ sakṛdagnihotramajuhottamitarāvapṛcchatāṃ kasmai tvaṃ juhoṣīti
(1,3.15 ) ekadhā vā idaṃ sarvaṃ prajāpatiḥ
(1,3.15 ) prajāpataya evāhaṃ sāyaṃ juhomīti prajāpataye prātariti
(1,3.15 ) teṣāṃ yo dvirajuhottamitarāvapṛcchatāṃ kābhyāṃ tvaṃ juhoṣīti
(1,3.15 ) agnaye prajāpataya iti sāyaṃ sūryāya prajāpataya iti prātas
(1,3.15 ) teṣāṃ yastrirajuhottamitarāvapṛcchatāṃ kebhyastvaṃ juhoṣītyagnaye prajāpataye'numataya iti sāyaṃ sūryāya prajāpataye'gnaye sviṣṭakṛta iti prātas
(1,3.15 ) teṣāṃ yo dvirajuhotsa ārdhnot
(1,3.15 ) sa bhūyiṣṭho'bhavat
(1,3.15 ) prajayā cetarau śriyā cetarāvatyākrāmat
(1,3.15 ) tasya ha prajāmitarayoḥ praje sajātatvamupaitām
(1,3.15 ) tasmāddvirhotavyaṃ yajuṣā caiva manasā ca
(1,3.15 ) yāmeva sa ṛddhimārdhnottāmṛdhnoti ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānagnihotraṃ juhotīti brāhmaṇam || 15 ||

(1,3.16 ) svāhā vai kutaḥ saṃbhūtā
(1,3.16 ) kena prakṛtā
(1,3.16 ) kiṃ vāsyā gotram
(1,3.16 ) katyakṣarā
(1,3.16 ) kati padā
(1,3.16 ) kati varṇā
(1,3.16 ) kiṃ pūrvāvasānā
(1,3.16 ) kva citsthitā
(1,3.16 ) kimadhiṣṭhānā
(1,3.16 ) brūhi svāhāyā yaddaivataṃ rūpaṃ ca
(1,3.16 ) svāhā vai satyasaṃbhūtā
(1,3.16 ) brahmaṇā prakṛtā
(1,3.16 ) lāmagāyanasagotrā
(1,3.16 ) dve akṣare
(1,3.16 ) ekaṃ padam
(1,3.16 ) trayaśca varṇāḥ śuklaḥ padmaḥ suvarṇa iti
(1,3.16 ) sarvacchandasāṃ vedeṣu samāsabhūtaikocchvāsā varṇānte catvāro vedāḥ śarīre [ed. samāsabhut, corr. ড়tyal]
(1,3.16 ) ṣaḍaṅgānyoṣadhivanaspatayo lomāni
(1,3.16 ) cakṣuṣī sūryācandramasau
(1,3.16 ) sā svāhā sā svadhā yajñeṣu vaṣaṭkārabhūtā prayujyate
(1,3.16 ) tasyā agnirdaivatam
(1,3.16 ) brāhmaṇo rūpamiti brāhmaṇam || 16 ||

(1,3.17 ) athāpi kāravo ha nāma ṛṣayo'lpasvā āsan
(1,3.17 ) ta imamekagumagniṣṭomaṃ dadṛśus
(1,3.17 ) tamāharan
(1,3.17 ) tenāyajanta
(1,3.17 ) te svaryayuḥ
(1,3.17 ) sa ya icchetsvariyāmiti sa etenaikagunāgniṣṭomena yajeteti brāhmaṇam || 17 ||

(1,3.18 ) athātaḥ savanīyasya paśorvibhāgaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
(1,3.18 ) uddhṛtyāvadānāni hanū sajihve prastotuḥ
(1,3.18 ) kaṇṭhaḥ sakākudraḥ pratihartuḥ
(1,3.18 ) śyenaṃ vakṣa udgātuḥ
(1,3.18 ) dakṣiṇaṃ pārśvaṃ sāṃsamadhvaryoḥ
(1,3.18 ) savyamupagātṝṇām
(1,3.18 ) savyo'ṃsaḥ pratiprasthātuḥ
(1,3.18 ) dakṣiṇā śroṇirathyāstrī brahmaṇaḥ
(1,3.18 ) avarasakthaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ
(1,3.18 ) ūruḥ potuḥ
(1,3.18 ) savyā śroṇirhotuḥ
(1,3.18 ) avarasakthaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya
(1,3.18 ) aruracchāvākasya
(1,3.18 ) dakṣiṇā dorneṣṭuḥ
(1,3.18 ) savyā sadasyasya
(1,3.18 ) sadaṃ cānūkaṃ ca gṛhapateḥ
(1,3.18 ) jāghanī patnyās
(1,3.18 ) tāṃ sā brāhmaṇena pratigrāhayati
(1,3.18 ) vaniṣṭhurhṛdayaṃ vṛkkau cāṅgulyāni dakṣiṇo bāhurāgnīdhrasya
(1,3.18 ) savya ātreyasya
(1,3.18 ) dakṣiṇau pādau gṛhapatervratapradasya
(1,3.18 ) savyau pādau gṛhapatnyāḥ vratapradāyāḥ
(1,3.18 ) sahaivainayoroṣṭhas
(1,3.18 ) taṃ gṛhapatirevānuśinaṣṭi
(1,3.18 ) maṇikāśca skandhyāstisraśca kīkasā grāvastutas
(1,3.18ś) tisraścaiva kīkasā ardhaṃ cāpānasyonnetuḥ
(1,3.18 a) ata ūrdhvaṃ camasādhvaryūṇāṃ klomā śamayituḥ
(1,3.18 b) śiraḥ subrahmaṇyasya
(1,3.18 c) yaḥ śvaḥsutyāmāhvayate tasya carma
(1,3.18 d) tathā khalu ṣaṭtriṃśatsampadyante
(1,3.18 e) ṣaṭtriṃśadavadānā gauḥ
(1,3.18 f) ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣarā bṛhatī
(1,3.18 g) bārhato vai svargo lokaḥ
(1,3.18 h) bṛhatyā vai devāḥ svarge loke yajante
(1,3.18 i) bṛhatyā svarge loke pratitiṣṭhanti
(1,3.18 j) pratitiṣṭhanti prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ vibhajante
(1,3.18 k) atha yadato'nyathāśīliko vā pāpakṛto vā hutādo vānyajanā vā vimathnīrannevamevaiṣāṃ paśurvimathato bhavatyasvargyaḥ
(1,3.18 l) devabhājo ha vā imaṃ śrutaṛṣiḥ paśorvibhāgaṃ vidāṃ cakāra
(1,3.18 m) tamu girijāya bābhravyāyānyo manuṣyebhyaḥ provāca
(1,3.18 n) tato'yamarvāṅmanuṣyeṣvāsīditi brāhmaṇam || 18 || [ed. manuṣyesv]

(1,3.19 ) athāto dīkṣā
(1,3.19 ) kasya sviddhetordīkṣita ityācakṣate
(1,3.19 ) śreṣṭhāṃ dhiyaṃ kṣiyatīti
(1,3.19 ) taṃ vā etaṃ dhīkṣitaṃ santaṃ dīkṣita ityācakṣate parokṣeṇa
(1,3.19 ) parokṣapriyā iva hi devā bhavanti pratyakṣadviṣaḥ
(1,3.19 ) kasya sviddhetordīkṣito'pratyutthāyiko bhavatyanabhivādukaḥ pratyuttheyo'bhivādyaḥ
(1,3.19 ) ye pratyuttheyā abhivādyāsta enamāviṣṭā bhavantyatharvāṅgirasas
(1,3.19 ) tasya kimātharvaṇamiti
(1,3.19 ) yadātmanyeva juhvati na parasmin
(1,3.19 ) evaṃ hātharvaṇānāmodanasavānāmātmanyeva juhvati na parasmin [ed. adanasavānām, corr. ড়tyal]
(1,3.19 ) athāsya kimāṅgirasamiti
(1,3.19 ) yadātmanaśca pareṣāṃ ca nāmāni na gṛhṇātyevaṃ ha tasminnāsādātmanaścaiva pareṣāṃ ca nāmāni na gṛhyante
(1,3.19 ) vicakṣaṇavatīṃ vācaṃ bhāṣante canasitavatīm
(1,3.19 ) vicakṣayanti brāhmaṇaṃ canasayanti prājāpatyam
(1,3.19 ) saiṣā vratadhugatharvāṅgirasas
(1,3.19 ) tāṃ hyanvāyattāḥ
(1,3.19 ) kasya sviddhetordīkṣito'nāśyanno bhavati nāsya nāma gṛhṇanti
(1,3.19 ) annastho nāmastho bhavatītyāhustasya ye'nnamadanti te'sya pāpmānamadanti
(1,3.19 ) athāsya ye nāma gṛhṇanti te'sya nāmnaḥ pāpmānamapāghnate
(1,3.19 ) athāpi vedānāṃ garbhabhūto bhavatītyāhus
(1,3.19 ) tasyājātasyāvijñātasyākrītasomasyābhojanīyaṃ bhavatītyāhuḥ
(1,3.19 ) sa dīkṣāṇāṃ prātarjāyate somaṃ krīṇanti tasya jātasya vijñātasya krītasomasya bhojanīyaṃ bhavatītyāhuḥ
(1,3.19 ) kasya sviddhetoḥ saṃsavā parijihīrṣitā bhavanti
(1,3.19 ) yataro vīryavattaro bhavati sa parasya yajñaṃ parimuṣṇāti
(1,3.19 ) kasya sviddhetordaive na dhyāyetsaṃsthite nādhīyīteti
(1,3.19ś) saṃsavasyaiva hetoriti
(1,3.19 a) vidyotamāne stanayatyatho varṣati vāyavyamabhiṣuṇvanti vai devāḥ somaṃ ca bhakṣayanti
(1,3.19 b) tadabhiṣuṇvanti brāhmaṇāḥ śuśruvāṃso'nūcānās
(1,3.19 c) teṣāṃ sarvarasabhakṣāḥ pitṛpitāmahā bhavanti
(1,3.19 d) sa daive na dhyāyetsaṃsthite nādhīyīteti brāhmaṇam || 19 ||

(1,3.20 ) samāvṛttā ācāryā niṣedus
(1,3.20 ) tān ha yajño dīkṣiṣyamāṇān brāhmaṇarūpaṃ kṛtvopodeyāya
(1,3.20 ) itthaṃ cedvo'pasamavatsurhanta vo'haṃ madhye dīkṣā iti [ed.'pasamavatsur, ড়tyalproposesapasamavatasthur, ed. dikṣā]
(1,3.20 ) ta ūcurnaiva tvā vidma na jānīmaḥ
(1,3.20 ) ko hīdavijñāyamānena saha dīkṣiṣyatīti
(1,3.20 ) yannvidaṃ dīkṣiṣyadhve bhūyo na dīkṣiṣyadhve
(1,3.20 ) atha vā u evaṃ dīkṣayiṣyatha saṃ vai tarhi mohiṣyatha
(1,3.20 ) mohiṣyati vo yajñaḥ sarve te dīkṣayiṣyatheti
(1,3.20 ) atha vā u ekaṃ dīkṣayiṣyatha te vā ahīnartvijo gṛhapatayo bhaviṣyatha
(1,3.20 ) te tūṣṇīṃ dhyāyanta āsāṃ cakrire
(1,3.20 ) sa hovāca kiṃ nu tūṣṇīmādhve
(1,3.20 ) bhūyo vaḥ pṛcchāmaḥ
(1,3.20 ) pṛcchateti yannvidaṃ dīkṣiṣyadhva upame etasmin saṃvatsare mithunaṃ cariṣyatha nopaiṣyatheti [ড়tyalfindsupamayetasminin two BOṛī mss. andproposesto acceptthise reading]
(1,3.20 ) dhigiti hocuḥ
(1,3.20 ) kathaṃ na dīkṣitā upaiṣyāmo nopaiṣyāmahā iti
(1,3.20 ) te vai brāhmaṇānāmabhimantāro bhaviṣyatha
(1,3.20 ) reto ha vo ya etasmin saṃvatsare brāhmaṇāstadabhaviṣyaṃste bodhimatā bhaviṣyatheti
(1,3.20 ) atha vā upeṣyāmo nopeṣyāmahā iti
(1,3.20 ) te vai dīkṣitā avakīrṇino bhaviṣyatha
(1,3.20 ) na ha vai devayānaḥ panthā prādurbhaviṣyatīti
(1,3.20 ) tiro vai devayānaḥ panthā bhaviṣyatīti
(1,3.20 ) te vayaṃ bhagavantamevopadhāvāma yathā svasti saṃvatsarasyodṛcaṃ samaśnavāmahā iti brāhmaṇam || 20 ||

(1,3.21 ) sa hovāca dvādaśa ha vai vasūni dīkṣitādutkrāmanti
(1,3.21 ) na ha vai dīkṣito'gnihotraṃ juhuyāt
(1,3.21 ) na paurṇamāsena yajñena yajeta
(1,3.21 ) nāmāvāsyena
(1,3.21 ) asmin vasīta
(1,3.21 ) na pitṛyajñena yajeta
(1,3.21 ) na tatra gacchedyatra manasā jigamiṣet
(1,3.21 ) neṣṭyā yajeta
(1,3.21 ) na vācā yathākathā cidabhibhāṣeta
(1,3.21 ) na mithunaṃ caret
(1,3.21 ) nānnāsya yathākāmamupayuñjīta
(1,3.21 ) na paśubandhena yajñena yajeta
(1,3.21 ) na tatra gacchedyatra cakṣuṣā parāpaśyet
(1,3.21 ) kṛṣṇājinaṃ vasīta
(1,3.21 ) kurīraṃ dhārayet
(1,3.21 ) muṣṭī kuryāt
(1,3.21 ) aṅguṣṭhaprabhṛtayastisra ucchrayet
(1,3.21 ) mṛgaśṛṅgaṃ gṛhṇīyāt
(1,3.21 ) tena kaṣeta
(1,3.21 ) atha yasya dīkṣitasya vāgvāyatā syān muṣṭī vā visṛṣṭau sa etāni japet || 21 ||

(1,3.22 ) agnihotraṃ ca mā paurṇamāsaśca yajñaḥ purastātpratyañcamubhau kāmaprau bhūtvā kṣityā sahāviśatām
(1,3.22 ) vasatiśca māmāvāsyaśca yajñaḥ paścātprāñcamubhāviti samānam
(1,3.22 ) manaśca mā pitṛyajñaśca yajño dakṣiṇata udañcamubhāviti samānam
(1,3.22 ) vākca meṣṭiścottarato dakṣiṇāñcamubhāviti samānam
(1,3.22 ) retaśca mānnaṃ ceta ūrdhvamubhāviti samānam
(1,3.22 ) cakṣuśca mā paśubandhaśca yajño'muto'rvāñcamubhau kāmaprau bhūtvākṣityā sahāviśatāmiti khalu ha vai dīkṣito ya ātmani vasūni dhatte na caivāsya kā canārtirbhavati na ca yajñaviṣkandhamupayātyapahanti punarmṛtyum
(1,3.22 ) apātyeti punarājātim
(1,3.22 ) kāmacāro'sya sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvān dīkṣāmupaitīti brāhmaṇam || 22 ||

(1,3.23 ) atha yasya dīkṣitasyartumatī jāyā syātpratisnāvā pratisnāvā sarūpavatsāyā goḥ payasi sthālīpākaṃ śrapayitvābhighāryodvāsyoddhṛtyābhihiṃkṛtya garbhavedanapuṃsavanaiḥ saṃpātavantaṃ kṛtvā taṃ paraiva prāśnīyāt
(1,3.23 ) reto vā annam
(1,3.23 ) vṛṣā hiṃkāraḥ
(1,3.23 ) evaṃ hīśvarā yā dīkṣitāya dīkṣitā jāyā putraṃ labheteti
(1,3.23 ) etenaiva prakrameṇa yajeteti brāhmaṇam || 23 ||

(1,3.23 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇapūrvabhāge tṛtīyaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||


(1,4.1 ) omayaṃ vai yajño yo'yaṃ pavate
(1,4.1 ) tameta īpsanti ye saṃvatsarāya dīkṣante
(1,4.1 ) teṣāṃ gṛhapatiḥ prathamo dīkṣate
(1,4.1 ) ayaṃ vai loko gṛhapatiḥ
(1,4.1 ) asmin vā idaṃ sarvaṃ loke pratiṣṭhitam
(1,4.1 ) gṛhapatā u eva sarve sattriṇaḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ
(1,4.1 ) pratiṣṭhāyā evainaṃ tatpratiṣṭhityai dīkṣante || 1 ||

(1,4.2 ) atha brahmāṇaṃ dīkṣayati candramā vai brahmādhidaivaṃ mano'dhyātmam
(1,4.2 ) manasaiva tadoṣadhīḥ saṃdadhāti
(1,4.2 ) tadyā oṣadhīrveda sa eva brahmauṣadhīs
(1,4.2 ) tadanena lokena saṃdadhāti
(1,4.2 ) tasmādetāvantareṇānyo na dīkṣeta
(1,4.2 ) sa yadetāvantareṇānyo dīkṣetemaṃ taṃ lokamoṣadhibhirvyāpādayet
(1,4.2 ) ucchoṣukā ha syus
(1,4.2 ) tasmādetāvantareṇānyo na dīkṣeta || 2 ||

(1,4.3 ) athodgātāraṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.3 ) ādityo vā udgātādhidaivaṃ cakṣuradhyātmam
(1,4.3 ) parjanya ādityaḥ
(1,4.3 ) parjanyādadhi vṛṣṭirjāyate
(1,4.3 ) vṛṣṭireva tadoṣadhīḥ saṃdadhāti
(1,4.3 ) tasmādetāvantareṇānyo na dīkṣeta
(1,4.3 ) sa yadetāvantareṇānyo dīkṣetemaṃ taṃ lokaṃ varṣeṇa vyāpādayet
(1,4.3 ) avarṣukā ha syus
(1,4.3 ) tasmādetāvantareṇānyo na dīkṣeta || 3 ||

(1,4.4 ) atha hotāraṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.4 ) agnirvai hotādhidaivaṃ vāgadhyātmam
(1,4.4 ) annaṃ vṛṣṭiḥ
(1,4.4 ) vācaṃ caiva tadagniṃ cānnena saṃdādhāti
(1,4.4 ) tasmādetāvantareṇānyo na dīkṣeta
(1,4.4 ) sa yadetāvantareṇānyo dīkṣetemaṃ taṃ lokamannena vyāpādayet
(1,4.4 ) aśanāyukā ha syus
(1,4.4 ) tasmādetāvantareṇānyo na dīkṣeta || 4 ||

(1,4.5 ) athādhvaryuṃ pratiprasthātā dīkṣayati
(1,4.5 ) vāyurvā adhvaryuradhidaivaṃ prāṇo'dhyātmam
(1,4.5 ) annaṃ vṛṣṭiḥ
(1,4.5 ) vāyuṃ caiva tatprāṇaṃ cānnena saṃdadhāti
(1,4.5 ) tasmādetāvantareṇānyo na dīkṣeta [ed. etav]
(1,4.5 ) sa yadetāvantareṇānyo dīkṣetemaṃ taṃ lokaṃ prāṇena vyāpādayet
(1,4.5 ) pramāyukā ha syus
(1,4.5 ) tasmādetāvantareṇānyo na dīkṣeta || 5 ||

(1,4.6 ) atha brahmaṇe brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) athodgātre prastotāraṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) atha hotre maitrāvaruṇaṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) athādhvaryave pratiprasthātāraṃ neṣṭā dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) sa hainamanu
(1,4.6 ) eteṣāṃ vai navānāṃ kḷptimanvitare kalpante
(1,4.6 ) nava vai prāṇāḥ
(1,4.6 ) prāṇairyajñastāyate
(1,4.6 ) atha brahmaṇe potāraṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) athodgātre pratihartāraṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) atha hotre'cchāvākaṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) athādhvaryave neṣṭāramunnetā dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) sa hainamanu
(1,4.6 ) atha brahmaṇa āgnīdhraṃ dīkṣyati
(1,4.6 ) athodgātre subrahmaṇyaṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) atha hotre grāvastutaṃ dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) atha tamanyaḥ snātako vā brahmacārī vā dīkṣayati
(1,4.6 ) na pūtaḥ pāvayedityāhuḥ
(1,4.6 ) saiṣānupūrvaṃ dīkṣā
(1,4.6 ) tadya evaṃ dīkṣante dīkṣiṣyamāṇā eva te sattriṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ na vindante
(1,4.6 ) sattriṇāṃ prāyaścittamanu tasyārdhasya yogakṣemaḥ kalpate yasminnardhe dīkṣanta iti brāhmaṇam || 6 ||

(1,4.7 ) śraddhayā vai devā dīkṣaṇīyāṃ niramimatāditeḥ prāyaṇīyām
(1,4.7 ) somātkrayam
(1,4.7 ) viṣṇorātithyam
(1,4.7 ) ādityātpravargyam
(1,4.7 ) svadhāyā upasadaḥ
(1,4.7 ) agnīṣomābhyāmaupavasathyamahaḥ
(1,4.7 ) prātaryāvadbhyo devebhyaḥ prātaranuvākam
(1,4.7 ) vasubhyaḥ prātaḥsavanam
(1,4.7 ) rudrebhyo mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam
(1,4.7 ) ādityebhyastṛtīyasavanam
(1,4.7 ) varuṇādavabhṛtham
(1,4.7 ) aditerudayanīyām
(1,4.7 ) mitrāvaruṇābhyāmanūbandhyām
(1,4.7 ) tvaṣṭustvāṣṭram
(1,4.7 ) devībhyo devikābhyo devatāhavīṃṣi
(1,4.7 ) kāmāddaśātirātram
(1,4.7 ) svargāllokādudavasānīyām
(1,4.7 ) tadvā etadagniṣṭomasya janma
(1,4.7 ) sa ya evametadagniṣṭomasya janma vedāgniṣṭomena sātmā saloko bhūtvā devānapyetīti brāhmaṇam || 7 ||

(1,4.8 ) atha yaddīkṣaṇīyayā yajante śraddhāmeva taddevīṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 ) śraddhā devī devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 ) śraddhāyā devyāḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 ) atha yatprāyaṇīyayā yajante'ditimeva taddevīṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 ) aditirdevī devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 ) adityā devyāḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 ) atha yatkrayamupayanti somameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 ) somo devo devatā bhavanti [ed. davatā]
(1,4.8 ) somasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 ) atha yadātithyayā yajante viṣṇumeva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 ) viṣṇurdevo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 ) viṣṇordevasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 ) atha yatpravargyamupayantyādityameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante [ed. davatāṃ]
(1,4.8 ) ādityo devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 ) ādityasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 ) atha yadupasadamupayanti svadhāmeva taddevīṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 ) svadhā devī devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 ) svadhāyā devyāḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 ) atha yadaupavasathyamaharupayantyagnīṣomāveva taddevau devate yajante
(1,4.8 ) agnīṣomau devau devate bhavanti
(1,4.8 ) agnīṣomayordevatayoḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ye etadupayanti [ed.'gnīṣomayor]
(1,4.8 ) atha yatprātaranuvākamupayanti prātaryāvṇa eva taddevān devatā yajante
(1,4.8 ) prātaryāvāṇo devā devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 ) prātaryāvṇāṃ devānāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 ) atha yatprātaḥsavanamupayanti vasūneva taddevān devatā yajante
(1,4.8ś) vasavo devā devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 a) vasūnāṃ devānāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 b) atha yan mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanamupayanti rudrāneva taddevān devatā yajante
(1,4.8 c) rudrā devā devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 d) rudrāṇāṃ devānāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 e) atha yattṛtīyasavanamupayantyādityāneva taddevān devatā yajante
(1,4.8 f) ādityā devā devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 g) ādityānāṃ devānāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 h) atha yadavabhṛthamupayanti varuṇameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 i) varuṇo devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 j) varuṇasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 k) atha yadudayanīyayā yajante'ditimeva taddevīṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 l) aditirdevī devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 m) adityā devyāḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti [ed. ādityā]
(1,4.8 n) atha yadanūbandhyayā yajante mitrāvaruṇāveva taddevau devate yajante
(1,4.8 o) mitrāvaruṇau devau devate bhavanti
(1,4.8 p) mitrāvaruṇayordevayoḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 ṝ) atha yattvāṣṭreṇa paśunā yajante tvaṣṭārameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 r) tvaṣṭā devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 s) tvaṣṭurdevasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 t) atha yaddevikāhavirbhiścaranti yā etā upasatsu bhavantyagniḥ somo viṣṇuriti devyo devikā devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 u) devīnāṃ devikānāṃ devatānāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 v) atha yaddaśātirātramupayanti kāmameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8 w) kāmo devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 x) kāmasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 y) atha yadudavasānīyayā yajante svargameva tallokaṃ devaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.8śś) svargo loko devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.8 aa) svargasya lokasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.8 bb) tadvā etadagniṣṭomasya janma
(1,4.8 cc) sa ya evametadagniṣṭomasya janma vedāptvaiva tadagniṣṭomaṃ svarge loke pratitiṣṭhati
(1,4.8 dd) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda
(1,4.8 ee) agniṣṭomena sātmā saloko bhūtvā devānapyetīti brāhmaṇam || 8 ||

(1,4.9 ) ahorātrābhyāṃ vai devāḥ prāyaṇīyamatirātraṃ niramimata
(1,4.9 ) ardhamāsebhyaścaturviṃśamahaḥ
(1,4.9 ) brahmaṇo'bhiplavam
(1,4.9 ) kṣatrātpṛṣṭhyam
(1,4.9 ) agnerabhijitam
(1,4.9 ) adbhyaḥ svarasāmnaḥ
(1,4.9 ) sūryādviṣuvantam
(1,4.9 ) uktā āvṛttāḥ svarasāmānaḥ
(1,4.9 ) indrādviśvajitam
(1,4.9 ) uktau pṛṣṭhyabhiplavau
(1,4.9 ) mitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ gavāyuṣī
(1,4.9 ) viśvebhyo devebhyo daśarātram
(1,4.9 ) digbhyo dāśarātrikaṃ pṛṣṭhyaṃ ṣaḍaham
(1,4.9 ) ebhyo lokebhyaśchandomaṃ tryaham
(1,4.9 ) saṃvatsarāddaśamamahaḥ
(1,4.9 ) prajāpatermahāvratam
(1,4.9 ) svargāllokādudayanīyamatirātram
(1,4.9 ) tadvā etatsaṃvatsarasya janma
(1,4.9 ) sa ya evametatsaṃvatsarasya janma veda saṃvatsareṇa sātmā saloko bhūtvā devānapyetīti brāhmaṇam || 9 ||

(1,4.10 ) atha yatprāyaṇīyamatirātramupayantyahorātrāveva taddevau devate yajante
(1,4.10 ) ahorātrau devau devate bhavanti
(1,4.10 ) ahorātrayordevayoḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti [ed.'horātrayor]
(1,4.10 ) atha yaccaturviṃśamaharupayantyardhamāsāneva taddevān devatā yajante
(1,4.10 ) ardhamāsā devā devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 ) ardhamāsānāṃ devānāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 ) atha yadabhiplavamupayanti brahmāṇameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.10 ) brahmā devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 ) brahmaṇo devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 ) atha yatpṛṣṭhyamupayanti kṣatrameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.10 ) kṣatraṃ devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 ) kṣatrasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 ) atha yadabhijitamupayantyagnimeva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.10 ) agnirdevo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 ) agnerdevasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 ) atha yatsvarasāmna upayantyeva taddevīrdevatā yajanta āpo devyo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 ) apāṃ devīnāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 ) atha yadviṣuvantamupayanti sūryameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.10 ) sūryo devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 ) sūryasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 ) uktā āvṛttāḥ svarasāmānaḥ
(1,4.10 ) atha yadviśvajita mupayantīndrameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.10 ) indro devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 ) indrasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 ) uktau pṛṣṭhyābhiplavau
(1,4.10ś) atha yadgavāyuṣī upayanti mitrāvaruṇāveva taddevau devate yajante
(1,4.10 a) mitrāvaruṇau devau devate bhavanti
(1,4.10 b) mitrāvaruṇayordevayoḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 c) atha yaddaśarātramupayanti viśvāneva taddevān devatā yajante
(1,4.10 d) viśve devā devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 e) viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 f) atha yaddāśarātrikaṃ pṛṣṭhyaṃ ṣaḍahamupayanti diśa eva taddevīrdevatā yajante
(1,4.10 g) diśo devyo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 h) diśāṃ devīnāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 i) atha yacchandomaṃ tryahamupayantīmāneva tallokān devān devatā yajante
(1,4.10 j) ime lokā devā devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 k) eṣāṃ lokānāṃ devānāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 l) atha yaddaśamamaharupayanti saṃvatsarameva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.10 m) saṃvatsaro devo devatā bhavanti [ed. davatā]
(1,4.10 n) saṃvatsarasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 o) atha yan mahāvratamupayanti prajāpatimeva taddevaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.10 p) prajāpatirdevo devatā bhavanti [ed. davatā]
(1,4.10 ṝ) prajāpaterdevasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 r) atha yadudayanīyamatirātramupayanti svargameva tallokaṃ devaṃ devatāṃ yajante
(1,4.10 s) svargo loko devo devatā bhavanti
(1,4.10 t) svargasya lokasya devasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ yanti ya etadupayanti
(1,4.10 u) tadvā etatsaṃvatsarasya janma
(1,4.10 v) sa ya evametatsaṃvatsarasya janma vedāptvaiva tatsaṃvatsaraṃ svarge loke pratitiṣṭhati
(1,4.10 w) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda
(1,4.10 x) saṃvatsareṇa sātmā saloko bhūtvā devānapyetīti brāhmaṇam || 10 ||

(1,4.11 ) sa vā eṣa saṃvastaro'dhidaivaṃ cādhyātmaṃ ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ
(1,4.11 ) sa ya evametatsaṃvatsaramadhidaivaṃ cādhyātmaṃ ca pratiṣṭhitaṃ veda pratitiṣṭhati
(1,4.11 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 11 ||

(1,4.12 ) sa vā eṣa saṃvatsaro bṛhatīmabhisaṃpannaḥ
(1,4.12 ) dvāvakṣarāvahnāṃ ṣaḍahau dvau pṛṣṭhyābhiplavau
(1,4.12 ) gavāyuṣī daśarātras
(1,4.12 ) tathā khalu ṣaṭtriṃśadsaṃpadyante
(1,4.12 ) ṣaṭtriṃśadavadānā gauḥ
(1,4.12 ) ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣarā bṛhatī
(1,4.12 ) bārhato vai svargo lokaḥ
(1,4.12 ) bṛhatyā vai devāḥ svarge loke yajante
(1,4.12 ) bṛhatyā svarge loke pratitiṣṭhati
(1,4.12 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 12 ||

(1,4.13 ) sa vā eṣa saṃvatsarastrimahāvrataḥ
(1,4.13 ) caturviṃśe mahāvrataṃ viṣuvati mahāvrataṃ mahāvrata eva mahāvratam
(1,4.13 ) taṃ ha smaitamevaṃvidvāṃsaḥ pūrve trimahāvratamupayanti
(1,4.13 ) te tejasvina āsaṃtsatyavādinaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ
(1,4.13 ) ya enamadya tathopeyuryathāmapātramudaka āsikte nirmṛtyedevaṃ yajamānā nirmṛtyerannuparyupayanti
(1,4.13 ) tathā hāsya satyena tapasā vratena cābhijitamavaruddhaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda || 13 ||

(1,4.14 ) atha yaccaturviṃśamaharupetyānupetya viṣuvantaṃ mahāvratamupeyātkathamanāgūrtyai bhavatīti
(1,4.14 ) yamevāmuṃ purastādviṣuvato'tirātramupayanti teneti brūyāt
(1,4.14 ) abhiplavātpṛṣṭhyo nirmitaḥ
(1,4.14 ) pṛṣṭhyādabhijit
(1,4.14 ) abhijitaḥ svarasāmānaḥ
(1,4.14 ) svarasāmabhyo viṣuvān
(1,4.14 ) viṣuvataḥ svarasāmānaḥ
(1,4.14 ) svarasāmabhyo viśvajit
(1,4.14 ) viśvajitaḥ pṛṣṭhyābhiplavau
(1,4.14 ) pṛṣṭhyābhiplavābhyāṃ gavāyuṣī
(1,4.14 ) gavāyurbhyāṃ daśarātraḥ
(1,4.14 ) daśarātrān mahāvratam
(1,4.14 ) mahāvratādudayanīyo'tirātraḥ
(1,4.14 ) udayanīyo'tirātraḥ svargāya lokāyānnādyāya pratiṣṭhityai || 14 ||

(1,4.15 ) atha yaccaturviṃśamaharupetyānupetya viṣuvantaṃ mahāvratamupeyātkathamanāgūrtyai bhavatīti
(1,4.15 ) yamevāmuṃ purastādviṣuvato'tirātramupayanti teneti brūyāt
(1,4.15 ) abhiplavātpṛṣṭhyo nirmitaḥ
(1,4.15 ) pṛṣṭhyādabhijit
(1,4.15 ) abhijitaḥ svarasāmānaḥ
(1,4.15 ) svarasāmabhyo viṣuvān [ed. viṣavān]
(1,4.15 ) viṣuvataḥ svarasamānaḥ
(1,4.15 ) svarasāmabhyo viśvajit
(1,4.15 ) viśvajitaḥ pṛṣṭhyābhiplavau
(1,4.15 ) pṛṣṭhyābhiplavābhyāṃ gavāyuṣī
(1,4.15 ) gavāyurbhyāṃ daśarātraḥ
(1,4.15 ) atha ha devebhyo mahāvrataṃ na tasthe kathamūrdhvai stomairviṣuvantamupāgātāvṛttairmāmiti
(1,4.15 ) te devā ihasāmivāsur
(1,4.15 ) upa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīmo ca ūrdhvastomo yenaitadaharavāpnuyāmeti
(1,4.15 ) tata etaṃ dvādaśarātramūrdhvastomaṃ dadṛśus
(1,4.15 ) tamāharan
(1,4.15 ) tenāyajanta
(1,4.15 ) tata ebhyo'tiṣṭhan
(1,4.15 ) tiṣṭhati hāsmai mahāvratam
(1,4.15 ) pratitiṣṭhati
(1,4.15 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 15 ||

(1,4.16 ) atha yaccaturviṃśamaharupetyānupetya viṣuvantaṃ mahāvratamupeyātkathamanāgūrtyai bhavatīti
(1,4.16 ) yamevāmuṃ purastādviṣuvato'tirātramupayanti teneti brūyāt
(1,4.16 ) tadāhuḥ kati saṃvatsarasya parāñcyahāni bhavanti katyarvāñci
(1,4.16 ) tadyāni sakṛtsakṛdupayanti tāni parāñci
(1,4.16 ) atha yāni punaḥpunarupayanti tānyarvāñcītyevainānyupāsīran
(1,4.16 ) ṣaḍahayorhyāvṛttimanvāvartante || 16 ||

(1,4.17 ) atha yaccaturviṃśamaharupetyānupetya viṣuvantaṃ mahāvratamupeyātkathamanāgūrtyai bhavatīti
(1,4.17 ) yamevāmuṃ purastādviṣuvato'tirātramupayanti teneti brūyāt [ed.'tiratram]
(1,4.17 ) abhiplavaṃ purastādviṣuvataḥ pūrvamupayanti
(1,4.17 ) pṛṣṭyamupariṣṭāt
(1,4.17 ) pitā vā abhiplavaḥ putraḥ pṛṣṭhyas
(1,4.17 ) tasmātpūrve vayasi putrāḥ pitaramupajīvanti
(1,4.17 ) pṛṣṭhyaṃ paścādviṣuvataḥ pūrvamupayantyabhiplavamupariṣṭāt
(1,4.17 ) pitā vā abhiplavaḥ putraḥ pṛṣṭhyas
(1,4.17 ) tasmāduttame vayasi putrān pitopajīvati ya evaṃ veda
(1,4.17 ) tadapyetadṛcoktaṃ <śataminnu śarado anti devā yatrā naścakrā jarasaṃ tanūnām | putrāso yatra pitaro bhavanti mā no madhyā rīriṣatāyurgantoḥ [ṛV 1.89.9]>iti
(1,4.17 ) upa ha vā enaṃ pūrve vayasi putrāḥ pitaramupajīvantyupottame vayasi putrān pitopajīvati ya evaṃ veda || 17 ||

(1,4.18 ) atha haiṣa mahāsuparṇas
(1,4.18 ) tasya yān purastādviṣuvataḥ ṣaṇmāsānupayanti sa dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ
(1,4.18 ) atha yānāvṛttānupariṣṭātṣaḍupayanti sa uttaraḥ pakṣaḥ
(1,4.18 ) ātmā vai saṃvatsarasya viṣuvānaṅgāni pakṣau
(1,4.18 ) yatra vā ātmā tatpakṣau
(1,4.18 ) yatra vai pakṣau tadātmā
(1,4.18 ) na vā ātmā pakṣāvatiricyate
(1,4.18 ) no pakṣāvātmānamatiricyete iti
(1,4.18 ) evamu haiva tadapareṣāṃ sviditamahnāṃ pareṣāmityapareṣāṃ caiva pareṣāṃ ceti brūyāt || 18 || [ed. būyāt]

(1,4.19 ) tadāhuryaddvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaro'tha haitadaharavāpnuyāmeti
(1,4.19 ) yadvaiṣuvatamapareṣāṃ sviditamahāṃ pareṣāmiti
(1,4.19 ) apareṣāṃ caiva pareṣāṃ ceti brūyāt
(1,4.19 ) ātmā vai saṃvatsarasya viṣuvānaṅgāni māsāḥ
(1,4.19 ) yatra vā ātmā tadaṅgāni
(1,4.19 ) yatrāṅgāni tadātmā
(1,4.19 ) na vā ātmāṅgānyatiricyate no'ṅgānyātmānamatiricyanta iti
(1,4.19 ) evamu haiva tadapareṣāṃ sviditamahnāṃ pareṣāmityapareṣāṃ caiva pareṣāṃ ceti brūyāt
(1,4.19 ) sa vā eṣa saṃvatsaraḥ || 19 ||

(1,4.20 ) tadāhuḥ kathamubhayatojyotiṣo'bhiplavā anyataratojyotiḥ pṛṣṭhya iti
(1,4.20 ) ubhayatojyotiṣo vā ime lokā agnineta ādityenāmuta iti
(1,4.20 ) eṣa ha vā eteṣāṃ jyotirya enaṃ pramṛdīva tapati
(1,4.20 ) devacakre ha vā ete pṛṣṭhyapratiṣṭhite pāpmānaṃ tṛṃhatī pariplavete
(1,4.20 ) tadya evaṃviduṣāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ pāpakaṃ kīrtayadete evāsya taddevacakre śiraśchindataḥ
(1,4.20 ) daśarātramuddhiḥ
(1,4.20 ) pṛṣṭhyābhiplavau cakre
(1,4.20 ) daśarātramuddhiṃ pṛṣṭhyābhiplavau cakre tantraṃ kurvīteti ha smāha vāsyus
(1,4.20 ) tayo stotrāṇi ca śastrāṇi ca saṃcāreyat
(1,4.20 ) yaḥ saṃcārayettasmādime puruṣe prāṇā nānā santa ekodayāccharīramadhivasati yanna saṃcārayetpramāyuko ha yajamānaḥ syāt
(1,4.20 ) eṣa ha vai pramāyuko yo'ndho vā badhiro vā
(1,4.20 ) navāgniṣṭomā māsi saṃpadyante
(1,4.20 ) nava vai prāṇāḥ
(1,4.20 ) prāṇairyajñastāyate
(1,4.20 ) ekaviṃśatirukthyāḥ
(1,4.20 ) eka ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī
(1,4.20 ) annaṃ vā ukthyaḥ
(1,4.20 ) vīryaṃ ṣoḍaśyeva
(1,4.20 ) tathā rūḍhvā svargaṃ lokamadhyārohanti || 20 ||

(1,4.21 ) athāto'hnāmadhyārohaḥ
(1,4.21 ) prāyaṇīyenātirātreṇodayanīyamatirātramadhyārohanti caturviṃśena mahāvratam
(1,4.21 ) abhiplavena paramabhiplavam
(1,4.21 ) pṛṣṭhyena paraṃ pṛṣṭhyam
(1,4.21 ) abhijitābhijitaṃ svarasāmabhiḥ parāntsvarasāmānaḥ
(1,4.21 ) athaitadaharavāpnuyāmeti yadvaiṣuvatamapareṣāṃ sviditamahnāṃ pareṣāmityapareṣāṃ caiva pareṣāṃ ceti brūyāt
(1,4.21 ) sa vā eṣa saṃvatsaraḥ || 21 ||

(1,4.22 ) athāto'hnāṃ nivāhaḥ
(1,4.22 ) prāyaṇīyo'tirātraścaturviṃśāyāhne nivahati
(1,4.22 ) caturviṃśamaharabhiplavāya
(1,4.22 ) abhiplavaḥ pṛṣṭhyāya
(1,4.22 ) pṛṣṭhyo'bhijite
(1,4.22 ) abhijitsvarasāmabhyaḥ
(1,4.22 ) svarasāmāno viṣuvate
(1,4.22 ) viṣuvāntsvarasāmabhyaḥ
(1,4.22 ) svarasāmano viśvajite
(1,4.22 ) viśvajitpṛṣṭhyābhiplavābhyām
(1,4.22 ) pṛṣṭhyābhiplavau gavāyurbhyām
(1,4.22 ) gavāyuṣī daśarātrāya
(1,4.22 ) daśarātro mahāvratāya
(1,4.22 ) mahāvratamudanīyāyātirātrāya

(1,5.2 ) vedeti
