(1,5.5 ff) tadapyetadṛcoktaṃ <śramādanyatra parivartamānaścaran vāsīno yadi vā svapannapi | ahorātrābhyāṃ puruṣaḥ kṣaṇena kati kṛtvaḥ prāṇiti cāpānīti ca śataṃ śatāni parivatsarāṇāmaṣṭau ca śatāni saṃvatsarasya muhūrtān yān vadantyahorātrābhyāṃ puruṣaḥ samena kati kṛtvaḥ prāṇiti cāpānīti ca [śBṃ 12.3.2.78]>iti brāhmaṇam || 5 ||

(1,5.6 ) saṃvatsarasya samatā veditavyeti ha smāha vāsyuḥ
(1,5.6 ) ekameva purastādviṣuvato'tirātramupayantyekamupariṣṭāt
(1,5.6 ) tripañcāśatameva purastādviṣuvato'gniṣṭomānupayanti tripañcāśatamupariṣṭāt
(1,5.6 ) viṃśatiśatameva purastādviṣuvata ukthyānupayanti viṃśatiśatamupariṣṭāt
(1,5.6 ) ṣaḍeva purastādviṣuvataḥ ṣoḍaśina upayanti ṣaḍupariṣṭāt
(1,5.6 ) triṃśadeva purastādviṣuvataḥ ṣaḍahānupayanti triṃśadupariṣṭāt
(1,5.6 ) saiṣā saṃvatsarasya samatā
(1,5.6 ) sa ya evametāṃ saṃvatsarasya samatāṃ veda saṃvatsareṇa sātmā saloko bhūtvā devānapyetīti brahmaṇam || 6 ||

(1,5.7 ) athāto yajñakramāḥ
(1,5.7 ) agnyādheyam
(1,5.7 ) agnyādheyātpūrṇāhutiḥ
(1,5.7 ) pūrṇahuteragnihotram
(1,5.7 ) agnihotrāddarśapūrṇamāsau
(1,5.7 ) darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmāgrayaṇam
(1,5.7 ) āgrayaṇāccāturmāsyāni
(1,5.7 ) cāturmāsyebhyaḥ paśubandhaḥ
(1,5.7 ) paśubandhādagniṣṭomaḥ
(1,5.7 ) agniṣṭomādrājasūyaḥ
(1,5.7 ) rājasūyādvājapeyaḥ
(1,5.7 ) vājapeyādaśvamedhaḥ
(1,5.7 ) aśvamedhātpuruṣamedhaḥ
(1,5.7 ) puruṣamedhātsarvamedhaḥ
(1,5.7 ) sarvamedhāddakṣiṇāvantaḥ
(1,5.7 ) dakṣiṇāvadbhyo'dakṣiṇāḥ
(1,5.7 ) adakṣiṇāḥ sahasradakṣiṇe pratyatiṣṭhan
(1,5.7 ) te vā ete yajñakramāḥ
(1,5.7 ) sa ya evametān yajñakramān veda yajñena sātmā saloko bhūtvā devānapyetīti brāhmaṇam || 7 ||

(1,5.8 ) prajāpatirakāmayatānantyamaśnuvīyeti
(1,5.8 ) so'gnīnādhāya pūrṇāhutyāyajata
(1,5.8 ) so'ntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) so'gnihotreṇeṣṭvāntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiṣṭvāntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa āgrayaṇeneṣṭvāntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa cāturmāsyairiṣṭvāntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa paśubandheneṣṭvāntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) so'gniṣṭomeneṣṭvāntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa rājasūyeneṣṭvā rājeti nāmādhatta
(1,5.8 ) so'ntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa vājapeyeneṣṭvā samrāḍiti nāmādhatta
(1,5.8 ) so'ntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) so'śvamedheneṣṭvā svarāḍiti nāmādhatta
(1,5.8 ) so'ntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa puruṣamedheneṣṭvā virāḍiti nāmādhatta
(1,5.8 ) so'ntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa sarvamedheneṣṭvā sarvarāḍiti nāmādhatta
(1,5.8 ) so'ntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) so'hīnairdakṣiṇāvadbhiriṣṭvāntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) so'hīnairadakṣiṇāvadbhiriṣṭvāntamevāpaśyat
(1,5.8 ) sa sattreṇobhayato'tirātreṇāntato'yajata
(1,5.8 ) vācaṃ ha vai hotre prāyacchat
(1,5.8 ) prāṇamadhvaryave cakṣurudgātre mano brahmaṇe'ṅgāni hotrakebhya ātmānaṃ sadasyebhyaḥ
(1,5.8 ) evamānantyamātmānaṃ dattvānantyamāśnuta
(1,5.8ś) tadyā dakṣiṇā ānayattābhirātmānaṃ niṣkrīṇīya
(1,5.8 a) tasmādetena jyotiṣṭomenāgniṣṭomenātmaniṣkrayaṇena sahasradakṣiṇena pṛṣṭhaśamanīyena tvareta
(1,5.8 b) yo hyaniṣṭvā pṛṣṭhaśamanīyena praityātmānaṃ so'niṣkrīya praitīti brāhmaṇam || 8 ||

(1,5.9 ) yadvai saṃvatsarāya saṃvatsarasado dīkṣante kathameṣāmagnihotramanantaritaṃ bhavati
(1,5.9 ) vrateteti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāṃ darśo'nantarito bhavati
(1,5.9 ) dadhnā ca puroḍāśena ceti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāṃ paurṇamāsamanantaritaṃ bhavati
(1,5.9 ) ājyena ca puroḍāśena ceti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāmāgrayaṇamanantaritaṃ bhavati
(1,5.9 ) saumyena caruṇeti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāṃ cāturmāsyānyanantaritāni bhavanti
(1,5.9 ) payasyayeti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāṃ paśubandho'nantarito bhavati
(1,5.9 ) paśunā ca puroḍāśena ceti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāṃ saumyo'dhvaro'nantarito bhavati
(1,5.9 ) grahairiti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāṃ gṛhamedho'nantarito bhavati
(1,5.9 ) dhānākarambhairiti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāṃ pitṛyajño'nantarito bhavati
(1,5.9 ) aupāsanairiti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) kathameṣāṃ mithunamanantaritaṃ bhavati
(1,5.9 ) hiṃkāreneti brūyāt
(1,5.9 ) saiṣā saṃvatsare yajñakratūnāmapītiḥ
(1,5.9 ) sa ya evametāṃ saṃvatsare yajñakratūnāmapītiṃ veda yajñena sātmā saloko bhūtvā devānapyetīti brāhmaṇam || 9 ||

(1,5.10 ) devā ha vai sahasrasaṃvatsarāya didīkṣire
(1,5.10 ) teṣāṃ pañca śatāni saṃvatsarāṇāṃ paryupetānyāsannathedaṃ sarva śaśrāma ye stomā yāni pṛṣṭhāni yāni śastrāṇi
(1,5.10 ) te devā ihasāmivāsurupa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīmo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarasya pratimā
(1,5.10 ) ko hi tasmai manuṣyo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa yajeteti
(1,5.10 ) tadayātayāma madhye yajñasyāpaśyan
(1,5.10 ) tenāyātayāmnā yā vede vyaṣṭirāsīttāṃ pañcasvapaśyannṛci yajuṣi sāmni śānte'tha ghore
(1,5.10 ) tā vā etāḥ pañca vyāhṛtayo bhavantyo śrāvayāstu śrauṣaḍyaja ye yajāmahe vauṣaḍiti
(1,5.10 ) te devā ihasāmivāsurupa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīmo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarasya pratimā [ed. davā]
(1,5.10 ) ko hi tasmai manuṣyo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa yajeteti
(1,5.10 ) tata etaṃ tāpaścitaṃ sahasrasaṃvatsarasyāñjasyamapaśyan
(1,5.10 ) te hyeva stomā bhavanti tāni pṛṣṭhāni tāni śastrāṇi
(1,5.10 ) sa khalu dvādaśa māsān dīkṣābhireti dvādaśamāsānupasadbhirdvāśamāsāṃtsutyābhiḥ
(1,5.10 ) atha yaddvādaśa māsān dīkṣābhireti dvādaśamāsānupasadbhistenaitāvagnyarkāvāpnoti
(1,5.10 ) atha yaddvādaśa māsāṃtsutyābhistenedaṃ mahadukthamavāpnoti
(1,5.10 ) te devā ihasāmivāsurupa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīmo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarasya pratimā
(1,5.10 ) ko hi tasmai manuṣyo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa yajeteti
(1,5.10 ) tata etaṃ saṃvatsaraṃṃ tāpaścitasyāñjasyamapaśyan
(1,5.10 ) te hyeva stomā bhavanti tāni pṛṣṭhāni tāni śastrāṇi
(1,5.10 ) te devā ihasāmivāsurupa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīmo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarasya pratimā
(1,5.10 ) ko hi tasmai manuṣyo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa yajeteti
(1,5.10 ) tata etaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ saṃvatsarasyāñjasyamapaśyan
(1,5.10 ) te hyeva stomā bhavanti tāni pṛṣṭhāni tāni śastrāṇi
(1,5.10 ) sa khalu dvādaśāhaṃ dīkṣābhireti dvādaśāhamupasadbhirdvādaśāhaṃ sutyābhiḥ
(1,5.10 ) atha yaddvādaśāhaṃ dīkṣābhireti dvādaśāhamupasadbhistenaitāvagnyarkāvāpnoti
(1,5.10 ) atha yaddvādaśāhaṃ sutyābhistenedaṃ mahadukthamavāpnoti
(1,5.10ś) te devā ihasāmivāsurupa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīmo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarasya pratimā [ed. ihasamivāsurupa]
(1,5.10 a) ko hi tasmai manuṣyo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa yajeteti
(1,5.10 b) tata etaṃ pṛṣṭhyaṃ ṣaḍahaṃ dvādaśāhasyāñjasyamapaśyan
(1,5.10 c) te hyeva stomā bhavanti tāni pṛṣṭhāni tāni śastrāṇi
(1,5.10 d) te devā ihasāmivāsurupa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīmo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarasya pratimā
(1,5.10 e) ko hi tasmai manuṣyo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa yajeteti
(1,5.10 f) tata etaṃ viśvajitaṃ pṛṣṭhyaṣaḍahasyāñjasyamapaśyan
(1,5.10 g) te hyeva stomā bhavanti tāni pṛṣṭhāni tāni śastrāṇi
(1,5.10 h) te devā ihasāmivāsurupa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīmo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarasya pratimā
(1,5.10 i) ko hi tasmai manuṣyo yaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa yajeteti
(1,5.10 j) sa vā eṣa viśvajidyaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarasya pratimā
(1,5.10 k) eṣa ha prajānāṃ prajāpatiryadviśvajiditi brāhmaṇam || 10 ||

(1,5.11 ) puruṣaṃ ha vai nārāyaṇaṃ prajāpatiruvāca yajasya yajasveti
(1,5.11 ) sa hovāca yajasva yajasvetyevaṃ hāttha mā
(1,5.11 ) trirapi kṣata me vasavaḥ prātaḥsavanenāgū rudrā mādhyaṃdinasavanenādityāstṛtīyasavanena
(1,5.11 ) yajñavāstunyeva paryaśiṣo yajñavāstumityevamāśiṣo'haṃ vā etadveda yajñe vasavaḥ prātaḥsavanenāgū rudrā mādhyaṃdinasavanenādityāstṛtīyasavanena yajñavāstunyeva paryaśiṣo yajñavāstumityevamāśiṣo vidvāṃso nūnaṃ tvā yājayeyuḥ
(1,5.11 ) ete ha vā avidvāṃso yatrānṛgviddhotā bhavatyayajurvidadhvaryurasāmavidudgātābhṛgvaṅgirovidbrahmā
(1,5.11 ) yajasvaiva hanta tu te tadvakṣyāmi
(1,5.11 ) yathā sūtre maṇiriva sūtrametānyukthāhāni bhavanti sūtramiva vā maṇāviti
(1,5.11 ) tasmādya eva sarvavitsyāttaṃ brahmāṇaṃ kurvīta
(1,5.11 ) eṣa ha vai vidvāṃtsarvavidbrahmā yadbhṛgvaṅgirovit
(1,5.11 ) ete ha vā asya sarvasya śamayitāraḥ pālayitāras
(1,5.11 ) tamādbrahmā stute bahiḥpavamāne vācayati || 11 ||

(1,5.12 ) śyeno'si gāyatrachandā
(1,5.12 ) anu tvārabhe
(1,5.12 ) svasti mā saṃpārayeti
(1,5.12 ) sa yadāha śyeno'sīti somaṃ vā etadāha
(1,5.12 ) eṣa ha vā agnirbhūtvāsmiṃlloke saṃśāyayati
(1,5.12 ) tadyatsaṃśāyayati tasmāccheyanas
(1,5.12 ) taccheyanasya śyenatvam
(1,5.12 ) sa yadāha gāyatrachandā anutvārabha iti gāyatreṇa chandasā vasurbhirdevaiḥ prātaḥsavane'smiṃlloke'gniṃ santamanvārabhate
(1,5.12 ) sa yadāha svasti mā saṃpārayeti gāyatreṇaiva chandasā vasubhirdevaiḥ prātaḥsavane'smiṃlloke'gninā devena svasti mā saṃpārayeti gāyatreṇaivainaṃ tacchandasā vasubhirdevaiḥ prātaḥsavane'smiṃlloke'gninā devena svasti saṃpadyate ya evaṃ veda

(1,5.13 ) atha mādhyaṃdine pavamāne vācayati samrāḍasi triṣṭupchandā
(1,5.13 ) anu tvārabhe
(1,5.13 ) svasti mā saṃpārayeti
(1,5.13 ) sa yadāha samrāḍasīti somaṃ vā etadāha
(1,5.13 ) eṣa ha vai vāyurbhūtvāntarikṣaloke samrājati
(1,5.13 ) tadyatsamrājati tasmātsamrāṭ
(1,5.13 ) tatsamrājasya samrāṭtvam
(1,5.13 ) sa yadāha triṣṭupchandā anu tvārabha iti traiṣṭubhena chandasā rudrairdevairmādhyaṃdine savane'ntarikṣaloke vāyuṃ santamanvārabhate
(1,5.13 ) sa yadāha svasti mā saṃpārayeti traiṣṭubhenaiva chandasā rudrairdevairmādhyaṃdine savane antarikṣaloke vāyunā devena svasti mā saṃpārayeti
(1,5.13 ) traiṣṭubhenaivainaṃ tacchandasā rudrairdevairmādhyaṃdine savane antarikṣaloke vāyunā devena svasti saṃpadyate ya evaṃ veda || 13 ||

(1,5.14 ) athārbhave pavamāne vācayati svaro'si gayo'si jagacchandā
(1,5.14 ) anu tvārabhe
(1,5.14 ) svasti mā saṃpārayeti
(1,5.14 ) sa yadāha svaro'sīti somaṃ vā etadāha
(1,5.14 ) eṣa ha vai sūryo bhūtvāmuṣmiṃlloke svarati
(1,5.14 ) tadyatsvarati tasmātsvaras
(1,5.14 ) tatsvarasya svaratvam
(1,5.14 ) sa yadāha gayo'sīti somaṃ vā etadāha
(1,5.14 ) eṣa ha vai candramā bhūtvā sarvāṃllokān gacchati
(1,5.14 ) tadyadgacchati tasmādgayas
(1,5.14 ) tadgayasya gayatvam
(1,5.14 ) sa yadāha jagacchandā anu tvārabha iti jāgatena chandasādityairdevaistṛtīyasavane'muṣmiṃlloke sūryaṃ santamanvārabhate
(1,5.14 ) sa yadāha svasti mā saṃpārayeti jāgatenaiva chandasādityairdevaistṛtīyasavane'muṣmiṃlloke sūryeṇa devena svasti mā saṃpārayeti jāgatenaivainaṃ tacchandasādityairdevaistṛtīyasavane'muṣmiṃlloke sūryeṇa devena svasti saṃpadyate ya evaṃ veda || 14 ||

(1,5.15 ) atha saṃsthite saṃsthite savane vācayati mayi bhargo mayi maho mayi yaśo mayi sarvamiti
(1,5.15 ) pṛthivyeva bhargo'ntarikṣa eva maho dyaureva yaśo'pa eva sarvam
(1,5.15 ) agnireva bhargo vāyureva maha āditya eva yaśaścandramā eva sarvam [ed. ādityā]
(1,5.15 ) vasava eva bhargo rudrā eva maha ādityā eva yaśo viśvedevā eva sarvam
(1,5.15 ) gāyatryeva bhargastriṣṭubeva maho jagatyeva yaśo'nuṣṭubeva sarvam
(1,5.15 ) prācyeva bhargaḥ pratīcyeva maha udīcyeva yaśo dakṣiṇaiva sarvam
(1,5.15 ) vasanta eva bhargo grīṣma eva maho varṣā eva yaśaḥ śaradeva sarvam
(1,5.15 ) trivṛdeva bhargaḥ pañcadaśa eva mahaḥ saptadaśa eva yaśa ekaviṃśa eva sarvam
(1,5.15 ) ṛgveda eva bhargo yajurveda eva mahaḥ sāmaveda eva yaśo brahmaveda eva sarvam
(1,5.15 ) hotaiva bhargo'dhvaryureva maha udgātaiva yaśo brahmaiva sarvam
(1,5.15 ) vāgeva bhargaḥ prāṇa eva mahaścakṣureva yaśo mana eva sarvam || 15 ||

(1,5.16 ) sa yadāha mayi bharga iti pṛthivīmevaitallokānāmahāgniṃ devānāṃ vasūn devān devagaṇānāṃ gāyatraṃ chandasāṃ prācīṃ diśāṃ vasantamṛtūnāṃ trivṛtaṃ stomānāmṛgvedaṃ vedānāṃ hautraṃ hotrakāṇāṃ vācamindriyāṇām || 16 ||

(1,5.17 ) sa yadāha mayi maha ityantarikṣamevaitallokānāmāha vāyuṃ devānāṃ rudrān devān devagaṇānāṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ chandasāṃ pratīcīṃ diśāṃ grīṣmamṛtūnāṃ pañcadaśaṃ stomānāṃ yajurvedaṃ vedānāmādhvaryavaṃ hotrakāṇāṃ prāṇamindriyāṇām || 17 ||

(1,5.18 ) sa yadāha mayi yaśa iti divamevaitallokānāmāhādityaṃ devānāmādityān devagaṇānāṃ jāgataṃ chandasāmudīcīṃ diśāṃ varṣā ṛtūnāṃ saptadaśaṃ stomānāṃ sāmavedaṃ vedānāmaudgātraṃ hotrakāṇāṃ cakṣurindriyāṇām || 17 ||

(1,5.19 ) sa yadāha mayi sarvamityapa evaitallokānāmāha candramasaṃ devānāṃ viśvān devān devagaṇānāmānuṣṭubhaṃ chandasāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ diśāṃ śaradamṛtūnāmekaviṃśaṃ stomānāṃ brahmavedaṃ vedānāṃ brahmatvaṃ hotrakāṇāṃ mana indriyāṇām || 18 ||

(1,5.20 ) sa vā eṣa daśadhā catuḥ saṃpadyate
(1,5.20 ) daśa ca ha vai caturvirājo'kṣarāṇi
(1,5.20 ) taṃ garbhā upajīvanti
(1,5.20 ) śrīrvai virāḍ
(1,5.20 ) yaśo'nnādyam
(1,5.20 ) śriyameva tadvirājaṃ yaśasyannādyo pratiṣṭhāpayati
(1,5.20 ) pratiṣṭhantīridaṃ sarvamanupratiṣṭhati
(1,5.20 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 20 ||

(1,5.21 ) anarvāṇaṃ ha vai devaṃ dadhyaṅṅāṅgirasa upasīdaṃ ha yajñasya śnuṣṭiṃ samaśnavāmaha iti
(1,5.21 ) sa dadhyaṅṅāṅgiraso'bravīdyo vai saptadaśaṃ prajāpatiṃ yajñe'nvitaṃ veda nāsya yajño riṣyate
(1,5.21 ) na yajñapatiṃ riṣyanta iti
(1,5.21 ) tā vā etāḥ pañca vyāhṛtayo bhavantyo śrāvayāstu śrauṣaḍyaja ye yajāmahe vauṣaḍiti
(1,5.21 ) sa dadhyaṅṅāṅgiraso'bravīnna vayaṃ vidmo yadi brāhmaṇāḥ smo yadyabrāhmaṇāḥ smo yadi tasyarṣeḥ smo vānyasyeti
(1,5.21 ) anarvāṇaśca ha vā ṛtāvantaśca pitaraḥ svadhāyāmāvṛṣāyanta vayaṃ vadāmahai vayaṃ vadāmahā iti
(1,5.21 ) so'yātsvāyaṃbhuvo vā ṛtāvanto madayātāṃ na vayaṃ vadāmahā iti
(1,5.21 ) tasmātpravare pravriyamāṇe vācayeddevāḥ pitara iti tisraḥ
(1,5.21 ) ya eti saṃyajati sa bhavati yaśca na brūte yaśca na brūta iti brāhmaṇam || 21 ||

(1,5.22 ) sāvitraṃ ha smaitaṃ pūrve purastātpaśumālabhanta ityetarhi prājāpatyam
(1,5.22 ) yo hyeva savitā sa prajāpatiriti vadantas
(1,5.22 ) tasmādu samupyāgnīṃstena yajeran
(1,5.22 ) te samānadhiṣṇyā eva syurokhāsaṃbharaṇīyāyāḥ
(1,5.22 ) ukhāsaṃbharaṇīyāyāṃ vinyupayāgnīṃstayā yajeran
(1,5.22 ) te nānādhiṣṇyā eva syurā dīkṣaṇīyāyāḥ
(1,5.22 ) dīkṣaṇīyāyāṃ saṃnyupyāgnīṃstayā yajeran
(1,5.22 ) te samānadhiṣṇyā eva syurodavasānīyāyāḥ
(1,5.22 ) udavasānīyāyāṃ vinyupyāgnīṃstayā yajeran
(1,5.22 ) te nānādhiṣṇyā eva syuḥ
(1,5.22 ) atha yadi yajamānasyopatapetpārśvato'gnīnādhāya tāvadāsīta yāvadagadaḥ syāt
(1,5.22 ) yadi preyātsvaireva tamagnibhirdahet
(1,5.22 ) aśavāgnibhiritare yajamānā āsata iti vadantas
(1,5.22 ) tasya tadeva brāhmaṇaṃ yadadaḥ puraḥsavane
(1,5.22 ) pitṛmedha āśiṣo vyākhyātāḥ || 22 ||

(1,5.23 ) sāyaṃprātarhomau sthālīpāko navaśca yaḥ | baliśca pitṛyajñaścāṣṭakā saptamaḥ paśuḥ || ityete pākayajñāḥ
(1,5.23 ) agnyādheyamagnihotraṃ paurṇamāsyamāvāsye | naveṣṭiścāturmāsyāni paśubandho'tra saptamaḥ || ityete haviryajñāḥ
(1,5.23 ) agniṣṭomo'tyagniṣṭoma ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśimāṃstataḥ | vājapeyo'tirātraścāptoryāmātra saptamaḥ || ityete sutyāḥ
(1,5.23 ) ke sviddevā pravovājāḥ ke sviddevā abhidyavaḥ | ke sviddevā haviṣmantaḥ kiṃ svijjigāti sumnayuḥ ||
(1,5.23 ) ṛtava eva pravovājā māsā devā abhidyavaḥ | ardhamāsā haviṣmantastajjigāti sunmayuḥ ||
(1,5.23 ) kati svidrātrayaḥ katyahāni kati stotrāṇi kati śastrāṇyasya | kati svitsavanāḥ saṃvatsarasya stotriyāḥ padākṣarāṇi katyasya ||
(1,5.23 ) <dvāvatirātrau ṣaṭśatamagniṣṭomā dve viṃśatiśate ukthyānām | dvādaśa ṣoḍaśinaḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ ṣaḍahā vaiṣuvataṃ ca [Vaitṣ31.15]> ||
(1,5.23 ) ahānyasya viṃśatiśatāni trīṇyahaścaikaṃ tāvadasya | saṃvatsarasya savanāḥ sahasramasīti trīṇi ca saṃstutasya ||
(1,5.23 ) ṣaṭṣaṣṭiśca dve ca śate bhavata stutaśastrāṇāmayutaṃ caikamasya | stotriyāśca navatisahasrā dve niyute navatiścāti ṣaṭca ||
(1,5.23 ) aṣṭau śatānyayutāni triṃśaccaturnavatiśca padānyasya | saṃvatsarasya kavibhirmitasyaitāvatī madhyamā devamātrā ||
(1,5.23 ) ayutamekaṃ prayutāni triṃśaddve niyute tathā hyanusṛṣṭāḥ | aṣṭau śatāni nava cākṣarāṇyetāvānātmā paramaḥ prajāpateḥ ||
(1,5.23 ) ādyaṃ vaṣaṭkāraḥ pradānāntametamagniṣṭome parvaśaḥ sādhu kḷptam | saubheṣajaṃ chanda īpsan yadagnau catuḥśataṃ bahudhā hūyate yat ||
(1,5.23 ) prātaḥsavana stuta ekaviṃśo gāyatrastomamita eka eva | mādhyaṃdinaḥ saptadaśena kḷptastrayastriṃśena savanaṃ tṛtīyam || 23 ||

(1,5.24 ) śraddhāyāṃ retastapasā tapasvī vaiśvānaraḥ siṣice'patyamīpsan | tato jajñe lokajitsomajambhā ṛṣerṛṣiraṅgirāḥ saṃnabhūva ||
(1,5.24 ) ṛṣeryajñasya caturvidhasya śraddhāṃ yaḥ śreyasīṃ lokamamuṃ jigāya | yasmai vedāḥ prasṛtāḥ somabindu yuktā vahanti sukṛtāmu lokam ||
(1,5.24 ) ṛco'sya bhāgāṃścaturo vahantyukthaśastraiḥ pramudo modamānāḥ | grahairhavirbhiśca kṛtākṛtaśca yajūṃṣi bhāgāṃścaturo vahanti ||
(1,5.24 ) audumbaryāṃ sāmaghoṣeṇa tāvatsaviṣṭutibhiśca stomaiḥ chandasā | sāmāni bhāgāṃścaturo vahanti gītyā stomena saha prastāvena ca ||
(1,5.24 ) prāyaścittairbheṣajaiḥ saṃstuvanto'tharvāṇo'ṅgirasaśca śāntāḥ | brahmā brahmatvena pramudo modamānā asaṃsṛṣṭān bhāgāṃścaturo vahanti ||
(1,5.24 ) yo brahmavitso'bhikaro'stu vaḥ śivo dhiyā dhīro rakṣatu dharmametam | mā vaḥ pramattāmamṛtācca yajñātkarmācca yenānaṅgiraso'piyāsīt ||
(1,5.24 ) māyuṃ daśaṃ māruśastāḥ prameṣṭā mā me bhūryuktā vidahātha lokān | divyaṃ bhayaṃ rakṣata dharmamudyataṃ yajñaṃ kālāśa stutigopanāyanam ||
(1,5.24 ) hotā ca maitrāvaruṇaśca pādamacchāvākaḥ saha grāvastutaikam | ṛgbhi stuvanto'harahaḥ pṛthivyā agniṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇā dhārayanti ||
(1,5.24 ) adhvaryuḥ pratiprasthātā neṣṭonnetā nihitaṃ pādamekam | samantarikṣaṃ yajuṣā stuvanto vāyuṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇā dhārayanti ||
(1,5.24 ) sāmnodgātā chādayannapramatta audumbaryāṃ stobhadeyaḥ sagadgadaḥ | vidvān prastotā vidahātha suṣṭutiṃ subrahmaṇyaḥ pratihartātha yajñe ||
(1,5.24 ) sāmnā divyekaṃ nihitaṃ stuvantaḥ sūryaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇā dhārayanti | brahmā haikaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ saha potāgnīdhro nihitaṃ pādamekam ||
(1,5.24 ) atharvabhiraṅgirobhiśca gupto'psu candraṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇā dhārayanti | ṣoḍaśikaṃ hotrakā abhiṣṭuvanti vedeṣu yuktāḥ prapṛthakcaturdhā ||
(1,5.24 ) manīṣiṇo dīkṣitāḥ śraddadhānā hotāro guptā abhivahanti yajñam | dakṣiṇato brahmaṇasyoṃ janadityetāṃ vyāhṛtiṃ japan ||
(1,5.24 ) saptadaśaṃ sadasyaṃ taṃ kīrtayanti purā viduḥ | aṣṭādaśī dīkṣitā dīkṣitānāṃ yajñe patnī śraddadhāneha yuktā ||
(1,5.24 ) ekonaviṃśaḥ śamitā babhūva viṃśo yajñe gṛhapatireva sunvan | ekaviṃśatirevaiṣāṃ saṃsthāyāmaṅgiro vaha ||
(1,5.24 ) vedairabhiṣṭuto loko nānāveśāparājitaḥ | || 24 ||

(1,5.25 ) sapta sutyāḥ sapta ca pākayajñāḥ haviryajñāḥ sapta tathaikaviṃśatiḥ | sarve te yajñā aṅgiraso'piyanti nūtanā yānṛṣayo sṛjanti ye ca sṛṣṭāḥ purāṇaiḥ ||
(1,5.25 ) eteṣu vedeṣvapi caikamevāpavrajamṛtvijāṃ saṃbharanti | kṛṭstṛpātsacate tāmaśastiṃ viṣkandhamenaṃ visṛtaṃ prajāsu ||
(1,5.25 ) nivartante dakṣiṇā nīyamānāḥ sute some vitate yajñatantre | moghāśiṣo yantyanivartamānā aniṣṭayajñā na taranti lokān ||
(1,5.25 ) dvādaśavarṣaṃ brahmacaryaṃ pṛthagvedeṣu tatsmṛtam | evaṃ vyavasthitā vedāḥ sarva eva svakarmasu ||
(1,5.25 ) santi caiṣāṃ samānāḥ mantrāḥ kalpāśca brāhmaṇāni ca | vyavasthānaṃ tu tatsarvaṃ pṛthagvedeṣu tatsmṛtam ||
(1,5.25 ) ṛgvedasya pṛthivī sthānamantarikṣasthāno'dhvaraḥ | dyau sthānaṃ sāmavedasyāpo bhṛgvaṅgirasāṃ smṛtam ||
(1,5.25 ) agnirdevata ṛgvedasya yajurvedo vāyudevataḥ | ādityaḥ sāmavedasya candramā vaidyutaśca bhṛgvaṅgirasām ||
(1,5.25 ) trivṛtstoma ṛgvedasya yajūṃṣi pañcadaśena saha jajñire | saptadaśena sāmaveda ekaviṃśo brahmasaṃmitaḥ ||
(1,5.25 ) vāgadhyātmamṛgvedasya yajuṣāṃ prāṇa ucyate | cakṣuṣī sāmavedasya mano bhṛgvaṅgirasāṃ smṛtam ||
(1,5.25 ) ṛgbhiḥ saha gāyatraṃ jāgatamāhuryajūṃṣi traiṣṭubhena saha jajñire | uṣṇikkakubhyāṃ bhṛgvaṅgiraso jagatyā sāmāni kavayo vadanti ||
(1,5.25 ) ṛgbhiḥ pṛthivīṃ yajuṣāntarikṣaṃ sāmnā divaṃ lokajitsomajambhāḥ | atharvabhiraṅgirobhiśca gupto yajñaścatuṣpāddivamudvaheta ||
(1,5.25 ) ṛgbhiḥ suśasto yajuṣā pariṣkṛtaḥ saviṣṭutaḥ sāmajitsomajambhāḥ | atharvabhiraṅgirobhiśca gupto yajñaścatuṣpāddivamāruroha ||
(1,5.25 ) ṛco vidvān pṛthivīṃ veda saṃprati yajūṃṣi vidvān bṛhadantarikṣam | divaṃ veda sāmago yo vipaścitsarvān lokān yadbhṛgvaṅgirovit ||
(1,5.25 ) yāṃśca grāme yāṃścāraṇye japanti mantrānnārthān bahudhā janāsaḥ | sarve te yajñā aṅgiraso'piyanti nūtanā sā hi gatirbrahmaṇo yāvarārdhyā ||
(1,5.25 ) triviṣṭapaṃ tridivaṃ nākamuttamaṃ tametayā trayyā vidyayaiti | ata uttare brahmalokā mahānto'tharvaṇāmaṅgirasāṃ ca sā gatiḥ || atharvaṇāmaṅgirasāṃ ca sā gatiriti brāhmaṇam || 25 ||

(1,5.25 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇapūrvabhāge pañcamaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||

(1 ol) iti pūrvabrāhmaṇaṃ samāptam

(2,1.1 ) atha yadbrahmasadanāttṛṇaṃ nirasyati śodhayatyevainaṃ tat
(2,1.1 ) athopaviśatīdamahamarvāgvasoḥ sadane sīdāmīti

(2,1.1 ) tamevaitatpūrvaṃ sādayati
(2,1.1 ) ariṣṭaṃ yajñaṃ tanutāditi
(2,1.1 ) athopaviśva japati bṛhaspatirbrahmeti
(2,1.1 ) bṛhaspatirvā āṅgiraso devānāṃ brahmā
(2,1.1 ) tasminnevaitadanujñāmicchati praṇītāsu praṇīyamānāsu vācaṃ yacchatyā haviṣkṛta udvādanāt
(2,1.1 ) etadvai yajñasya dvāram
(2,1.1 ) tadetadaśūnyaṃ karoti
(2,1.1 ) iṣṭe ca sviṣṭakṛtyānuyājānāṃ prasavāditi
(2,1.1 ) etadvai yajñasya dvitīyaṃ dvāram
(2,1.1 ) tadevaitadaśūnyaṃ karoti
(2,1.1 ) yatparidhayaḥ paridhīyante yajñasya gopīthāya
(2,1.1 ) paridhīn paridhatte yajñasya sātmatvāya
(2,1.1 ) paridhīntsaṃmārṣṭi
(2,1.1 ) punātyevainān
(2,1.1 ) trirmadhyamam
(2,1.1 ) traya ime prāṇāḥ
(2,1.1 ) prāṇānevābhijayati
(2,1.1 ) trirdakṣiṇārdhyam
(2,1.1 ) trayo vai lokāḥ
(2,1.1 ) lokānevābhijayati
(2,1.1 ) triruttarārdhyaṃ
(2,1.1 ) trayo vai devalokāḥ
(2,1.1ś) devalokānevābhijayati
(2,1.1 a) trirupavājayati trayo vai devayānāḥ panthānas
(2,1.1 b) tānevābhijayati
(2,1.1 c) te vai dvādaśa bhavanti
(2,1.1 d) dvādaśa ha vai māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ
(2,1.1 e) saṃvatsarameva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.1 f) atho saṃvatsaramevāsmā upadadhāti svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai || 1 ||

(2,1.2 ) prajāpatirvai rudraṃ yajñānnirabhajat
(2,1.2 ) so'kāmayata
(2,1.2 ) meyamasmā ākūtiḥ samardhi yo mā yajñānnirabhākṣīditi
(2,1.2 ) sa yajñamabhyāyamyāvidhyat
(2,1.2 ) tadāviddhaṃ nirakṛntat
(2,1.2 ) tatprāśitramabhavat
(2,1.2 ) tadudayacchat
(2,1.2 ) tadbhagāya paryaharan
(2,1.2 ) tatpratyaikṣata
(2,1.2 ) tasya cakṣuḥ parāpatat
(2,1.2 ) tasmādāhurandho vai bhaga iti
(2,1.2 ) api ha taṃ necchedyamicchati
(2,1.2 ) tatsavitre paryaharan
(2,1.2 ) tatpratyagṛhāttasya pāṇī praciccheda
(2,1.2 ) tasmai hiraṇmayau pratyadadhus
(2,1.2 ) tasmāddhiraṇyapāṇiriti stutas
(2,1.2 ) tatpūṣṇe paryaharan
(2,1.2 ) tatprāśnāt
(2,1.2 ) tasya dantāḥ paropyanta
(2,1.2 ) tasmādāhuradantakaḥ pūṣā piṣṭabhājana iti
(2,1.2 ) tadidhmāyāṅgirasāya paryaharan
(2,1.2 ) tatprāśnāt
(2,1.2 ) tasya śiro vyapatat
(2,1.2 ) taṃ yajña evākalpayat
(2,1.2 ) sa eṣa idhmaḥ
(2,1.2ś) samidho ha purātanas
(2,1.2 a) tadbarhaya āṅgirasāya paryaharan
(2,1.2 b) tatprāśnāt
(2,1.2 c) tasyāṅgā parvāṇi vyasraṃsanta
(2,1.2 d) taṃ yajña evākalpayat
(2,1.2 e) tadetadbarhiḥ
(2,1.2 f) prastaro ha purātanas
(2,1.2 g) tadbṛhaspataya āṅgirasāya paryaharan
(2,1.2 h) so'bibhedbṛhaspatiritthaṃ vāva sya ārtimāriṣyatīti
(2,1.2 i) sa etaṃ mantramapaśyat
(2,1.2 j) <sūryasya tvā cakṣuṣā pratīkṣe [ড়ṣ20.57.11, Kauśṣ91.2, Vaitṣ3.8]> ityabravīt
(2,1.2 k) na hi sūryasya cakṣuḥ kiṃ cana hinasti
(2,1.2 l) so'bibhetpratigṛhṇantaṃ mā hiṃsiṣyatīti
(2,1.2 m) devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāṃ prasūtaḥ praśiṣā pratigṛhāmītyabravīt
(2,1.2 n) savitṛprasūta evainaṃ taddevatābhiḥ pratyagṛhṇāt
(2,1.2 o) tadvyūhya tṛṇāni prāgdaṇḍaṃ sthaṇḍile nidadhāti pṛthivyāstvā nābhau sādayāmīti
(2,1.2 p) pṛthivī vā annānāṃ śamayitrī tayaivainacchamayāṃ cakāra
(2,1.2 ṝ) so'bibhetprāśnantaṃ mā hiṃsiṣyatīti
(2,1.2 r) agneṣṭvāsyena prāśnāmītyabravīt
(2,1.2 s) na hyagnerāsyaṃ kiṃ cana hinasti
(2,1.2 t) so'bibhetprāśitaṃ mā hiṃsiṣyatīti
(2,1.2 u) indrasya tvā jaṭhare sādayāmītyabravīt
(2,1.2 v) na hīndrasya jaṭharaṃ kiṃ cana hinasti
(2,1.2 w) varuṇasyodara iti na hi varuṇasyodaraṃ kiṃ cana hinastīti || 2 ||

(2,1.3 ) atho āhurbrāhmaṇasyodara iti
(2,1.3 ) ātmāsyātmannātmānaṃ me mā hiṃsīḥ svāheti
(2,1.3 ) annaṃ vai sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāmātmā tenaivainacchamayāṃ cakāra
(2,1.3 ) prāśitamanumantrayate <yo'gnirnṛmaṇā nāma brāhmaṇeṣu praviṣṭaḥ | tasmin ma etatsuhutamastu prāśitraṃ tan mā mā hiṃsītparame vyoman [ড়ṣ20.57.15]> iti
(2,1.3 ) tatsarveṇa brahmaṇā prāśnāt
(2,1.3 ) tata enaṃ nāhinat
(2,1.3 ) tasmādyo brahmiṣṭhaḥ syāttaṃ brahmāṇaṃ kurvīta
(2,1.3 ) bṛhaspatirvai sarvaṃ brahma
(2,1.3 ) sarveṇa ha vā etadbrahmaṇā yajñaṃ dakṣiṇata udyacchate
(2,1.3 ) apa vā etasmātprāṇāḥ krāmanti ya āviddhaṃ prāśnāti
(2,1.3 ) adbhirmārjayitvā prāṇāntsaṃspṛśate vāṅma āsyanniti
(2,1.3 ) amṛtaṃ vai prāṇāḥ
(2,1.3 ) amṛtamāpaḥ
(2,1.3 ) prāṇāneva yathāsthānamupāhvayate
(2,1.3 ) tadu haika āhurindrāya paryaharanniti
(2,1.3 ) te devā abruvannindro vai devānāmojiṣṭho baliṣṭhas
(2,1.3 ) tasmā enatpariharateti
(2,1.3 ) tattasmai paryaharan
(2,1.3 ) tatsa brahmaṇā śayamāṃ cakāra
(2,1.3 ) tasmādāhurindro brahmeti
(2,1.3 ) yavamātraṃ bhavati
(2,1.3 ) yavamātraṃ vai viṣasya na hinasti
(2,1.3 ) yadadhastādabhidhārayati tasmādadhastātprakṣaraṇaṃ prajā arurna hinasti
(2,1.3 ) yadupariṣṭādabhidhārayati tasmādupariṣṭātprakṣaraṇaṃ prajā arurna hinasti
(2,1.3 ) yadubhayato'bhighārayatyubhayato'bhighāri prajā arurghātukaṃ syāt
(2,1.3ś) yatsamayābhiharedanabhividdhaṃ yajñasyābhividhyet || 3 ||

(2,1.4 ) agreṇa pariharati
(2,1.4 ) tīrthenaiva pariharati
(2,1.4 ) vi vā etadyajñaśchidyate yatprāśitraṃ pariharati
(2,1.4 ) yadāha brahman prasthāsyāmīti bṛhaspatirvai sarvaṃ brahma
(2,1.4 ) sarveṇa ha vā etadbrahmaṇā yajñaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ saṃdadhāti
(2,1.4 ) atho atra vā etarhi yajñaḥ śrito yatra brahmā tatraiva yajñaḥ śritas
(2,1.4 ) tata evainamālabhate
(2,1.4 ) yaddhastena pramīvedvepanaḥ syāt
(2,1.4 ) yacchīrṣṇā śīrṣaktimāntsyāt
(2,1.4 ) yattūṣṇīmāsītāsaṃpratto yajñaḥ syāt
(2,1.4 ) pratiṣṭhetyeva brūyāt
(2,1.4 ) vāci vai yajñaḥ śritaḥ
(2,1.4 ) yatra brahmā yatraiva yajñaḥ śritastata evainaṃ saṃprayacchati
(2,1.4 ) agnīdha ādadhāti
(2,1.4 ) agnimukhānevartūn prīṇāti
(2,1.4 ) athottarāsāmāhutīnāṃ pratiṣṭhityā
(2,1.4 ) atho samidvatyava juhoti
(2,1.4 ) paridhīntsaṃmārṣṭi
(2,1.4 ) punātyevainān
(2,1.4 ) sakṛtsakṛtsaṃmārṣṭi
(2,1.4 ) parāṅeva hyetarhi yajñaḥ
(2,1.4 ) catuḥ saṃpadyate
(2,1.4 ) atho catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ
(2,1.4 ) paśūnāmāptyai
(2,1.4 ) deva savitaretatte prāhetyāha prasūtyai
(2,1.4ś) bṛhaspatirbrahmetyāha
(2,1.4 a) sa hi brahmiṣṭhaḥ
(2,1.4 b) sa yajñaṃ pāhi sa yajñapatiṃ pāhi sa māṃ pāhi sa māṃ karmaṇyaṃ pāhītyāha
(2,1.4 c) yajñāya ca yajamānāya ca paśūnāmāptyai || 4 ||

(2,1.5 ) na vai paurṇamāsyāṃ nāmāvāsyāyāṃ dakṣiṇā dīyante
(2,1.5 ) ya eṣa odanaḥ pacyate dakṣiṇaiṣā dīyate yajñasyarddhyai
(2,1.5 ) iṣṭī vā etena yadyajate'tho vā etena pūrtī ya eṣa odanaḥ pacyate
(2,1.5 ) eṣa ha vā iṣṭāpūrtī ya enaṃ pacati || 5 ||

(2,1.6 ) dvayā vai devā yajamānasya gṛhamāgacchanti somapā anye'somapā anye
(2,1.6 ) hutādo'nye'hutādo'nye

(2,1.6 ) etaddevatya eṣa yaḥ purānījānaḥ
(2,1.6 ) ete ha vā etasya prajāyāḥ paśūnāmīśate
(2,1.6 ) te'syāprītā iṣamūrjamādāyāpakrāmanti
(2,1.6 ) yadanvāhāryamanvāharati tāneva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.6 ) dakṣiṇataḥsadbhyaḥ parihartavā āha
(2,1.6 ) dakṣiṇāvataiva yajñena yajate
(2,1.6 ) āhutibhireva devān hutādaḥ prīṇāti dakṣiṇābhirmanuṣyadevān
(2,1.6 ) te'smai prītā iṣamūrjaṃ niyacchanti || 6 ||

(2,1.7 ) devāśca ha vā asurāścāspardhanta
(2,1.7 ) te devāḥ prajāpatimevābhyayajanta
(2,1.7 ) anyo'nyasyāsannasurā ajuhavus
(2,1.7 ) te devā etamodanamapaśyan
(2,1.7 ) taṃ prajāpataye bhāgamanuniravapan
(2,1.7 ) taṃ bhāgaṃ paśyan prajāpatirdevānupāvartata
(2,1.7 ) tato devā abhavan parāsurāḥ
(2,1.7 ) sa ya evaṃvidvānetamodanaṃ pacati bhavatyātmanā parāsyāpriyo bhrātṛvyo bhavati
(2,1.7 ) prajāpatirvai devebhyo bhāgadheyāni vyakalpayat
(2,1.7 ) so'manyatātmānamantaragāmiti
(2,1.7 ) sa etamodanamabhaktamapaśyat
(2,1.7 ) tamātmane bhāgaṃ niravapat
(2,1.7 ) prajāpatervā eṣa bhāgaḥ
(2,1.7 ) aparimitaḥ syāt
(2,1.7 ) aparimito hi prajāpatiḥ
(2,1.7 ) prajāpaterbhāgo'syūrjasvān payasvān
(2,1.7 ) akṣito'si
(2,1.7 ) akṣityai tvā
(2,1.7 ) mā me kṣeṣṭhā amutrāmuṣmiṃlloka iha ca
(2,1.7 ) prāṇāpanau me pāhi
(2,1.7 ) samānavyānau me pāhi
(2,1.7 ) udānarūpe me pāhi
(2,1.7 ) ūrgasi
(2,1.7 ) ūrjaṃ me dhehi
(2,1.7 ) kurvato me mā kṣeṣṭhāḥ
(2,1.7ś) dadato me mopadasaḥ
(2,1.7 a) prajāpatimahaṃ tvayā samakṣamṛdhyāsamiti
(2,1.7 b) prajāpatimeva samakṣamṛdhnoti ya evaṃ veda ya evaṃ veda || 7 ||

(2,1.8 ) ye vā iha yajñairārdhnuvaṃsteṣāmetāni jyotīṃṣi yānyamūni nakṣatrāṇi
(2,1.8 ) tannakṣatrāṇāṃ nakṣatratvaṃ yanna kṣīyanti
(2,1.8 ) darśapūrṇamāsau vai yajñasyāvasānadarśau
(2,1.8 ) ye vā aniṣṭvā darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ somena yajante teṣāmetāni jyotīṃṣi yānyamūni nakṣatrāṇi patantīva
(2,1.8 ) tadyathā ha vā idamaspaṣṭāvasāne nehāvasāsyasi nehāvasāsyasīti nonudyanta evaṃ haivaite'muṣmāṃllokānnonudyante
(2,1.8 ) ta ete pracyavante || 8 ||

(2,1.9 ) yasya havirniruptaṃ purastāccandramā abhyudiyāttāṃstredhā tāṇḍulān vibhajet
(2,1.9 ) ye madhyamāstānagnaye dātre'ṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapet
(2,1.9 ) ye sthaviṣṭhāstānindrāya prādātre dadhani carum
(2,1.9 ) ye kṣodiṣṭhāstān viṣṇave śipiviṣṭāya śṛte carum [ed. kṣodisthās, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,1.9 ) paśavo vā ete'tiricyante
(2,1.9 ) tānevāpnoti
(2,1.9 ) tānavarunddhe
(2,1.9 ) agnirvai madhyamasya dātendro vai jyeṣṭhasya pradātā
(2,1.9 ) yadevedaṃ kṣudraṃ paśūnāṃ tadviṣṇoḥ śipiviṣṭam
(2,1.9 ) tadevāpnoti
(2,1.9 ) paśūnevāvarunddhe || 9 ||

(2,1.10 ) yā pūrvā paurṇamāsī sānumatiḥ
(2,1.10 ) yottarā rākā
(2,1.10 ) yā pūrvāmāvāsyā sā sinīvālī yottarā sā kuhūḥ
(2,1.10 ) candramā eva dhātā ca vidhātā ca
(2,1.10 ) yatpūrṇo'nyāṃ vasatyapūrṇo'nyāṃ tan mithunam
(2,1.10 ) yatpaśyatyanyāṃ nānyāṃ tan mithunam
(2,1.10 ) yadamāvāsyāyāścandramā adhi prajāyate tan mithunam
(2,1.10 ) tasmādevāsmai mithunātpaśūn prajanayati || 10 ||

(2,1.11 ) na dve yajeta
(2,1.11 ) yatpūrvayā saṃprati yatetottarayā chambaṭkuryāt
(2,1.11 ) yaduttarayā saṃprati yajeta pūrvayā chambaṭkuryāt
(2,1.11 ) neṣṭirbhavati na yajñas
(2,1.11 ) tadanu hrītamukhyapagalbho jāyate
(2,1.11 ) ekāmeva yajeta
(2,1.11 ) pragalbho haiva jāyate
(2,1.11 ) anādṛtya taddve yajeta
(2,1.11 ) yajñamukhameva pūrvayālabhate yajata uttarayā
(2,1.11 ) devatā evaṃ pūrvayāpnotīndriyamuttaryā
(2,1.11 ) devalokameva pūrvayāvarunddhe manuṣyalokamuttarayā bhūyaso yajñakratūnupaitya
(2,1.11 ) eṣā ha vai sumanā nāmeṣṭiḥ
(2,1.11 ) yamadyejānaṃ paścāccandramā abhyudiyādasmā asmiṃlloka ārdhukaṃ bhavati || 11 ||

(2,1.12 ) agnāvaiṣṇavamekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapeddarśapūrṇamāsāvāripsamāṇaḥ
(2,1.12 ) agnirvai sarvā devatā viṣṇuryajñaḥ
(2,1.12 ) devatāścaiva yajñaṃ cārabhata ṛddhyai
(2,1.12 ) ṛdhnotyeva
(2,1.12 ) ubhau sahārambhāvityāhuḥ
(2,1.12 ) udinnu śṛṅge sito mucyata iti
(2,1.12 ) darśo vā etayoḥ pūrvaḥ paurṇamāsa uttaraḥ
(2,1.12 ) atha yatparastātpaurṇamāsa ārabhyate tadyathā pūrvaṃ kriyate
(2,1.12 ) tadyatpaurṇamāsamārabhamāṇaḥ sarasvatyai caruṃ nirvapetsarasvate dvādaśakapālamamāvāsyā vai sarasvatī paurṇamāsaḥ sarasvāniti
(2,1.12 ) ubhāvevainau sahārabhata ṛddhyai
(2,1.12 ) ṛdhnotyeva || 12 ||

(2,1.13 ) agnaye pathikṛte'ṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapedyasya prajñāteṣṭiratipadyate
(2,1.13 ) bahiṣpathaṃ vā eṣa eti yasya prajñāteṣṭiratipadyate
(2,1.13 ) agnirvai devānāṃ pathikṛt
(2,1.13 ) tameva bhāgadheyenopāsarat
(2,1.13 ) sa enaṃ panthānamapinayati
(2,1.13 ) anaḍvān dakṣiṇā
(2,1.13 ) sa hi panthānamabhivahati || 13 ||

(2,1.14 ) agnaye vratapataye'ṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapedya āhitāgniḥ san pravaset
(2,1.14 ) bahu vā eṣa vratamatipātayati ya āhitāgniḥ san pravasati vratye'hani striyaṃ vopaiti māṃsaṃ vāśnāti
(2,1.14 ) agnirvai devānāṃ vratapatiḥ
(2,1.14 ) agnimetasya vratamagāt
(2,1.14 ) tasmādetasya vratamālambhayate || 14 ||

(2,1.15 ) agnaye vratabhṛte'ṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapedya āhitāgnirārtijamaśru kuryāt
(2,1.15 ) ānīto vā eṣa devānāṃ ya āhitāgnis
(2,1.15 ) tasmādetenāśru na kartavyam
(2,1.15 ) na hi devā aśru kurvanti
(2,1.15 ) agnirvai devānāṃ vratabhṛt
(2,1.15 ) agnimetasya vratamagāt
(2,1.15 ) tasmādetasya vratamālambhayate || 15 ||

(2,1.16 ) aindrāgnamusramanusṛṣṭamālabheta yasya pitā pitāmahaḥ somaṃ na pibet
(2,1.16 ) indriyeṇa vā eṣa vīryeṇa vyṛdhyate yasya pitā pitāmahaḥ somaṃ na pibati
(2,1.16 ) yadaindra indriyeṇaivainaṃ tadvīryeṇa samardhayati
(2,1.16 ) devatābhirvā eṣa vīryeṇa vyṛdhyate yasya pitā pitāmhaḥ somaṃ na pibati
(2,1.16 ) yadāgneyo'gnirvai sarvā devatāḥ
(2,1.16 ) sarvābhirevainaṃ taddevatābhiḥ samardhayati
(2,1.16 ) anusṛṣṭo bhavati
(2,1.16 ) anusṛṣṭa iva hyetasya somapītho yasya pitā pitāmahaḥ somaṃ na pibati
(2,1.16 ) tasmādeṣa eva tasyā devatāyāḥ paśūnāṃ samṛddhaḥ || 16 ||

(2,1.17 ) devā vā oṣadhīṣu pakvāsvājimayuḥ
(2,1.17 ) sa indro'vedagnirvāvemāḥ prathama ujjeṣyatīti
(2,1.17 ) so'bravīdyataro nau pūrva ujjayāttannau saheti
(2,1.17 ) tā agnirudajayat
(2,1.17 ) tadindro'nūdajayat
(2,1.17 ) sa eṣa aindrāgnaḥ sannāgnendraḥ
(2,1.17 ) ekā vai tarhi yavasya śnuṣṭirāsīdekā vrīherekā māṣasyaikā tilasya
(2,1.17 ) tadviśve devā abruvan vayaṃ vā etatprathayiṣyāmo bhāgo no'stviti
(2,1.17 ) tadbhūma eva vaiśvadevaḥ
(2,1.17 ) atho prathayatyetenaiva
(2,1.17 ) payasi syādvaiśvadevatvāya
(2,1.17 ) vaiśvadevaṃ hi payaḥ
(2,1.17 ) athemāvabrūtāṃ na vā ṛta āvābhyāmevaitadyūyaṃ prathayata mayi pratiṣṭhitamasau vṛṣṭyā pacati naitadito'bhyujjeṣyatīti
(2,1.17 ) bhāgo nāvastviti
(2,1.17 ) tābhyāṃ vā eṣa bhāgaḥ kriyata ujjityā eva
(2,1.17 ) atho pratiṣṭhityā eva yo dyāvāpṛthivīyaḥ
(2,1.17 ) saumīrvā oṣadhīḥ
(2,1.17 ) soma oṣadhīnāmadhirājaḥ
(2,1.17 ) yāśca grāmyā yāścāraṇyāstāsāmeṣa uddhāro yacchyāmākaḥ
(2,1.17 ) yacchyāmākaḥ saumyastameva bhāginaṃ kṛṇute
(2,1.17 ) yadakṛtvāgrayaṇaṃ navasyāśnīyāddevānāṃ bhāgaṃ pratikḷptamadyāt
(2,1.17 ) saṃvatsarādvā etadadhiprajāyate yadāgrayaṇam
(2,1.17 ) saṃvatsaraṃ vai brahmā
(2,1.17 ) tasmādbrahmā purastāddhomasaṃsthitahomeṣvāvapeta
(2,1.17 ) ekahāyanī dakṣiṇā
(2,1.17ś) sa hi saṃvatsarasya pratimā
(2,1.17 a) reta eva hyeṣo'prajātaḥ
(2,1.17 b) prajātyai || 17 ||

(2,1.18 ) atha haitadapratiratham <indrasya bāhū sthavirau vṛṣāṇau> iti
(2,1.18 ) etena ha vā indro'surān pratyajayat [ed.'surāna]
(2,1.18 ) aprati ha bhavatyetena yajamāno bhrātṛvyaṃ jayati
(2,1.18 ) saṃgrāme juhuyādaprati ha bhavati
(2,1.18 ) etena ha vai bharadvājaḥ pratardanaṃ samanahyat
(2,1.18 ) sa rāṣṭryabhavat
(2,1.18 ) yaṃ kāmayeta rāṣṭrī syāditi tametena saṃnahyet
(2,1.18 ) rāṣṭrī ha bhavati
(2,1.18 ) etena ha vā indro virājamabhyajayat
(2,1.18 ) daśaivānvāha
(2,1.18 ) daśākṣarā virāḍvairājaṃ vā etena yajamāno bhrātṛvyaṃ vṛṅkte
(2,1.18 ) tadu haika ekādaśānvāhuḥ
(2,1.18 ) ekādaśākṣarā vai triṣṭuptraiṣṭubho vajraḥ
(2,1.18 ) vajreṇaivaitadrakṣāṃsyapasedhati
(2,1.18 ) dakṣiṇato vai devānāṃ yajñaṃ rakṣāṃsyajighāṃsan
(2,1.18 ) tānyapratirathenāpāghnata
(2,1.18 ) tasmādbrahmāpratirathaṃ japanneti
(2,1.18 ) yadbrahmāpratirathaṃ japanneti yajñasyābhijityai rakṣasāmapahatyai rakṣasāmapahatyai || 18 ||

(2,1.19 ) athātaścāturmāsyānāṃ prayogaḥ
(2,1.19 ) phālgunyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ cāturmāsyāni prayuñjīta
(2,1.19 ) mukhaṃ vā etatsaṃvatsarasya yatphālgunī paurṇamāsī mukhamuttare phālgunyau pucchaṃ pūrve [ed. pūrva]
(2,1.19 ) tadyathā pravṛttasyāntau sametau syātāmevamevaitatsaṃvatsarasyāntau sametau bhavatas
(2,1.19 ) tadyatphālgunyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ cāturmāsyairyajate mukhata evaitatsaṃvatsaraṃ prayuṅkte
(2,1.19 ) atho bhaiṣajyayajñā vā ete yaccāturmāsyāni
(2,1.19 ) tasmādṛtusaṃdhiṣu prayujyante
(2,1.19 ) ṛtusaṃdhiṣu vai vyādhirjāyate
(2,1.19 ) tānyetānyaṣṭau havīṃsṣi bhavanti
(2,1.19 ) aṣṭau vai catasṛṇāṃ paurṇamāsīnāṃ havīṃṣi bhavanti
(2,1.19 ) catasṛṇāṃ vai paurṇamāsīnāṃ vaiśvadevaṃ samāsaḥ
(2,1.19 ) atha yadagniṃ manthanti prajāpatirvai vaiśvadevam
(2,1.19 ) prajātyā eva
(2,1.19 ) athaitaṃ daivaṃ garbhaṃ prajanayati
(2,1.19 ) atha yatsaptadaśa sāmidhenyaḥ saptadaśo vai prajāpatiḥ
(2,1.19 ) prajāpaterāptyai
(2,1.19 ) atha yatsadvantāvājyabhāgāvasisaṃtīti vai sadvantau bhavataḥ
(2,1.19 ) atha yadvirājau saṃyājye annaṃ vai śrīrvirāḍannādyasya śriyo'varuddhyai [ed.'varuddhyā'tha]
(2,1.19 ) atha yannava prayājā navānuyājā aṣṭau havīṃṣi vājinaṃ navamaṃ tannakṣatrīyāṃ virājamāpnoti
(2,1.19 ) atho āhurdaśanīṃ virājamiti prayājānuyājā havīṃṣyāghārāvājyabhāgāviti || 19 ||

(2,1.20 ) atha yadagnīṣomau prathamaṃ devatānāṃ yajatyagnīṣomau vai devānāṃ mukham
(2,1.20 ) mukhata eva taddevān prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atha yatsavitāraṃ yajatyasau vai savitā yo'sau tapati
(2,1.20 ) etameva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atha yatsarasvatīṃ yajati vāgvai sarasvatī
(2,1.20 ) vācametena prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atha yan pūṣaṇaṃ yajatyasau vai pūṣā yo'sau tapati
(2,1.20 ) etameva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atha yan marutaḥ svatavaso yajati ghorā vai marutaḥ svatavasas
(2,1.20 ) tāneva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atha yadviśvān devān yajatyete vai viśve devā yatsarve devās
(2,1.20 ) tāneva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atha yaddyāvāpṛthivyau yajati pratiṣṭhe vai dyāvāpṛthivyau
(2,1.20 ) pratiṣṭhityā eva
(2,1.20 ) atha yadvājino yajati paśavo vai vājinaḥ
(2,1.20 ) paśūneva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atho ṛtavo vai vājinaḥ
(2,1.20 ) ṛtūneva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atho chandāṃsi vai vājinaḥ
(2,1.20 ) chandāṃsyeva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.20 ) atho devāśvā vai vājinaḥ
(2,1.20 ) atra devāḥ sāśvā abhīṣṭāḥ prītā bhavanti
(2,1.20 ) atha yatparastātpaurṇamāsena yajate tathā hāsya pūrvapakṣe vaiśvadeveneṣṭaṃ bhavati || 20 ||

(2,1.21 ) vaiśvadevena vai prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata
(2,1.21 ) tāḥ sṛṣṭā aprasūtā varuṇasya yavāñjakṣus
(2,1.21 ) tā varuṇo varuṇapāśaiḥ pratyabandhāt
(2,1.21 ) tāḥ prajāḥ prajāpatiṃ pitarametyopāvadannupa taṃ yajñakratuṃ jānīhi yeneṣṭvā varuṇamaprīṇāt
(2,1.21 ) sa prīto varuṇaḥ
(2,1.21 ) varuṇapāśebhyaḥ sarvasmācca pāpmanaḥ saṃpramucyanta iti
(2,1.21 ) tata etaṃ prajāpatiryajñakratumapaśyadvaruṇapraghāsān
(2,1.21 ) tamāharat
(2,1.21 ) tenāyajata
(2,1.21 ) teneṣṭvā varuṇamaprīṇāt
(2,1.21 ) sa prīto varuṇo varuṇapāśebhyaḥ sarvasmācca pāpmanaḥ prajāḥ prāmuñcat
(2,1.21 ) pra ha vā etasya prajā varuṇapāśebhyaḥ sarvasmācca pāpmano mucyante
(2,1.21 ) ya evaṃ veda
(2,1.21 ) atha yadagniṃ praṇayanti yamevāmuṃ vaiśvadeve manthanti tameva tatpraṇayanti
(2,1.21 ) yan mathyate tasyoktaṃ brāhmaṇam
(2,1.21 ) atha yatsaptadaśa sāmidhenyaḥ sadvantāvājyabhāgau virājau saṃyājye teṣāmuktaṃ brāhmaṇam
(2,1.21 ) atha yannava prayājā navānuyājā navaitāni havīṃṣi
(2,1.21 ) samānāni tveva pañca saṃcarāṇi havīṃṣi bhavanti pauṣṇāntāni
(2,1.21 ) teṣāmuktaṃ brāhmaṇam || 21 ||

(2,1.22 ) atha yadaindrāgno dvādaśakapālo bhavati balaṃ vai teja indrāgnī
(2,1.22 ) balameva tattejasi pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,1.22 ) atha yadvāruṇyāmikṣendro vai varuṇaḥ [ed. āmīkṣendro, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,1.22 ) sa u vai payobhājanas
(2,1.22 ) tasmādvāruṇyāmikṣā [ed. amikṣā]
(2,1.22 ) atha yan mārutī payasyāpsu vai marutaḥ śritāḥ [ed. śritaḥ. corr. ড়tyal]
(2,1.22 ) āpo hi payaḥ
(2,1.22 ) athendrasya vai marutaḥ śrita aindraṃ payas
(2,1.22 ) tasmān mārutī payasyā
(2,1.22 ) atha yatkāya ekakapālaḥ prajāpatirvai kaḥ
(2,1.22 ) prajāpaterāptyā
(2,1.22 ) atho sukhasya vā etannāmadheyaṃ kamiti [ed. nāmagheyam]
(2,1.22 ) sukhameva tadadhyātman dhatte
(2,1.22 ) atha yan mithunau gāvau dadāti tatprajātyai rūpam
(2,1.22 ) ukthyā vājinaḥ
(2,1.22 ) atha yadapsu varuṇaṃ yajati sva evainaṃ tadāyatane prīṇāti
(2,1.22 ) atha yatparastātpaurṇamāsena yajate tathā hāsya pūrvapakṣe varuṇapraghāsairiṣṭaṃ bhavati || 22 ||

(2,1.23 ) aindro vā eṣa yajñakraturyatsākamedhās
(2,1.23 ) tadyathā mahārājaḥ purastātsenānīkāni vyuhyābhayaṃ panthānamanviyādevamevaitatpurastāddevatā yajati [ed. sainānīkāni, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,1.23 ) tadyathaivādaḥ somasya mahāvratamevamevaitadiṣṭimahāvratam
(2,1.23 ) atha yadagnimanīkavantaṃ prathamaṃ devatānāṃ yajatyagnirvai devānāṃ mukham
(2,1.23 ) mukhata eva taddevān prīṇāti
(2,1.23 ) atha yan madhyaṃdine marutaḥ sāṃtapanān yajatīndro vai marutaḥ saṃtapanāḥ
(2,1.23 ) aindraṃ mādhyaṃdinam
(2,1.23 ) tasmādetānindreṇopasaṃhitān yajati
(2,1.23 ) atha yatsāyaṃ gṛhamedhīyena caranti puṣṭikarma vai gṛhamedhīyaḥ
(2,1.23 ) sāyaṃ poṣaḥ paśūnām
(2,1.23 ) tasmātsāyaṃ gṛhamedhīyena caranti
(2,1.23 ) atahyacchvo bhūte gṛhamedhīyasya niṣkāśamiśreṇa pūrṇadarveṇa caranti pūrvedyuḥ karmaṇaivaitatprātaḥ karmopasaṃtanvanti
(2,1.23 ) atha yatprātarmarutaḥ krīḍino yajatīndro vai marutaḥ krīḍinas
(2,1.23 ) tasmādenānindreṇopasaṃhitān yajati
(2,1.23 ) atha yadagniṃ praṇayanti yamevāmuṃ vaiśvadeve manthanti tameva tatpraṇayanti
(2,1.23 ) yan mathyate tasyoktaṃ brāhmaṇam
(2,1.23 ) atha yatsaptadaśa sāmidhenyaḥ sadvantāvājyabhāgau virājau saṃyājye teṣāmuktaṃ brāhmaṇam
(2,1.23 ) atha yannava prayājā navānuyājā aṣṭau havīṃṣi samānāni tveva ṣaṭsaṃcarāṇi havīṃṣi bhavantyaindrāgnāntāni
(2,1.23 ) teṣāmuktaṃ brāhmaṇam
(2,1.23 ) atha yan mahendramantato yajatyantaṃ vai śreṣṭhī bhajate
(2,1.23 ) tasmādenamantato yajati
(2,1.23 ) atha yadvaiśvakarmaṇa ekakapālo'sau vai viśvakarmā yo'sau tapati
(2,1.23 ) etameva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.23 ) atha yadṛṣabhaṃ dadātyaindro ha yajñakratuḥ || 23 ||

(2,1.24 ) atha yadaparāhṇe pitṛyajñena carantyaparāhṇabhājo vai pitaras
(2,1.24 ) tasmādaparāhṇe pitṛyajñena caranti
(2,1.24 ) tadāhuryadaparapakṣabhājo vai pitaraḥ kasmādenān pūrvapakṣe yajantīti
(2,1.24 ) devā vā ete pitaras
(2,1.24 ) tasmādenān pūrvapakṣe yajantīti
(2,1.24 ) atha yadekāṃ sāmidhenīṃ triranvāha sakṛdha vai pitaras
(2,1.24 ) tasmādekāṃ sāmidhenīṃ triranvāha
(2,1.24 ) atha yadyajamānasyārṣeyaṃ nāha nedyajamānaṃ pravṛṇajānīti
(2,1.24 ) atha yatsomaṃ pitṛmantaṃ pitṝn vā somavataḥ pitṝn barhiṣadaḥ pitṝnagniṣvāttānityāvāhayati
(2,1.24 ) na haike svaṃ mahimānamāvāhayanti yajamānasyaiṣa mahimeti vadanta āvāhayediti tveva sthitam
(2,1.24 ) agnerhyeṣa mahimā bhavati
(2,1.24 ) oṃ svadhetyāśrāvayati
(2,1.24 ) astu svadheti pratyāśrāvayati
(2,1.24 ) svadhākāro hi pitṝṇām
(2,1.24 ) atha yatprayājānuyājebhyo barhiṣmantāvuddharati prajā vai barhirnetprajāṃ pitṛṣu dadhānīti
(2,1.24 ) te vai ṣaṭsaṃpadyante
(2,1.24 ) ṣaḍvā ṛtavaḥ
(2,1.24 ) ṛtavaḥ pitaraḥ
(2,1.24 ) pitṝṇāmāptyai || 24 ||

(2,1.25 ) atha yajjīvanavantāvājyabhāgau bhavato yajamānameva tajjīvayati
(2,1.25 ) atha yadekaikasya haviṣastisrastisro yājyā bhavanti hvayatyevainān prathamayā
(2,1.25 ) dvitīyayā gamayati
(2,1.25 ) praiva tṛtīyayā yacchati
(2,1.25 ) atho devayajñamevaitatpitṛyajñena vyāvartayati
(2,1.25 ) atho dakṣiṇāsaṃstho vai pitṛyajñas
(2,1.25 ) tamevaitadudaksaṃsthaṃ kurvanti
(2,1.25 ) atha yadagniṃ kavyavāhanamantato yajatyetatsviṣṭakṛto pitaras
(2,1.25 ) tasmādagniṃ kavyavāhanamantato yajati
(2,1.25 ) atha yadiḍāmupahūyāvaghrāya na prāśnanti paśavo vā iḍā
(2,1.25 ) netpaśūn pravṛṇajānīti
(2,1.25 ) atha yatsūktavāke yajamānasyāśiṣo'nvāha nedyajamānaṃ pravṛṇajānīti
(2,1.25 ) atha yatpatnīṃ na saṃyājayanti netpatnīṃ pravṛṇajānīti
(2,1.25 ) atha yatpavitravati mārjayante śāntirvai bheṣajamāpaḥ
(2,1.25 ) śāntirevaiṣā bheṣajamantato yajñe kriyate
(2,1.25 ) atha yadadhvaryuḥ pitṛbhyo nipṛṇāti jīvāneva tatpitṝnanu manuṣyāḥ pitaro'nupravahanti
(2,1.25 ) atho devayajñamevainaṃ pitṛyajñena vyāvartayanti
(2,1.25 ) atho dakṣiṇāsaṃstho vai pitṛyajñas
(2,1.25 ) tamevaitadudaksaṃsthaṃ kurvanti
(2,1.25 ) atha yatprāñco'bhyutkramyādityamupatiṣṭhante devaloko vā ādityaḥ
(2,1.25 ) pitṛlokaḥ pitaraḥ
(2,1.25 ) devalokamevaitatpitṛlokādupasaṃkrāmantīti
(2,1.25 ) atha yaddakṣiṇāñco'bhyutkramyāgnīnupatiṣṭhante prītyaiva taddeveṣvantato'rdhaṃ caranti
(2,1.25 ) atha yadudañco'bhyutkramya traiyaṃbakairyajante rudrameva tatsvasyāṃ diśi prīṇanti [ed. svāyāṃ butsee corrigenda p. 302]
(2,1.25 ) atho devayajñamevaitatpitṛyajñena vyāvartayanti
(2,1.25ś) atho dakṣiṇāsaṃstho vai pitṛyajñas
(2,1.25 a) tamevaitadudaksaṃsthaṃ kurvanti
(2,1.25 b) atha yadantata ādityeṣṭyā yajatīyaṃ vā aditiḥ
(2,1.25 c) asyāmevainamantataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,1.25 d) atha yatparastātpaurṇamāsena yajate tathā hāsya pūrvapakṣe sākamedhairiṣṭaṃ bhavati || 25 ||

(2,1.26 ) trayodaśaṃ vā etaṃ māsamāpnoti yacchunāsīryeṇa yajate
(2,1.26 ) etāvān vai saṃvatsaro yāvāneṣa trayodaśo māsaḥ
(2,1.26 ) atha yadagniṃ praṇayanti yamevāmuṃ vaiśvadeve manthanti tameva tatpraṇayanti
(2,1.26 ) yan mathyate tasyoktaṃ brāhmaṇam
(2,1.26 ) yadyu na mathyate paurṇamāsameva tantraṃ bhavati
(2,1.26 ) pratiṣṭhā vai paurṇamāsam
(2,1.26 ) pratiṣṭhityā evātha yadvāyuṃ yajati prāṇo vai vāyuḥ
(2,1.26 ) prāṇameva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.26 ) atha yacchunāsīraṃ yajati saṃvatsaro vai śunāsīraḥ
(2,1.26 ) saṃvatsarameva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.26 ) atha yatsūryaṃ yajatyasau vai sūryo yo'sau tapati
(2,1.26 ) etameva tena prīṇāti
(2,1.26 ) atha yacchvetāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadātyetasyaiva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
(2,1.26 ) atha yatprāyaścittapratinidhiṃ kurvanti svastyayanameva tatkurvanti
(2,1.26 ) yajñasyaiva śāntiryajamānasya bhaiṣajyāya
(2,1.26 ) tairvā etaiścāturmāsyairdevāḥ sarvān kāmānāpnuvaṃtsarvā iṣṭīḥ sarvamamṛtatvam
(2,1.26 ) sa vā eṣa prajāpatiḥ saṃvatsaraścaturviṃśo yaccāturmāsyāni
(2,1.26 ) tasya mukhameva vaiśvadevam
(2,1.26 ) bāhū varuṇapraghāsāḥ
(2,1.26 ) prāṇo'pāno vyāna ityetāstisra iṣṭayaḥ [ed. praṇo]
(2,1.26 ) ātmā mahāhaviḥ
(2,1.26 ) pratiṣṭhā śunāsīram
(2,1.26 ) sa vā eṣa prajāpatireva saṃvatsaro yaccāturmāsyāni
(2,1.26 ) sarvaṃ vai prajāpatiḥ
(2,1.26 ) sarvaṃ cāturmāsyāni
(2,1.26ś) tatsarveṇaiva sarvamāpnoti ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃ vidvāṃścāturmāsyairyajate cāturmāsyairyajate cāturmāsyairyajate

(2,1.26 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇottarabhāge prathamaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||

(2,2.1 ) oṃ māṃsīyanti vā āhitāgneragnayas
(2,2.1 ) ta enamevāgre'bhidhyāyanti yajamānam
(2,2.1 ) ya etamaindrāgnaṃ paśuṃ ṣaṣṭhe ṣaṣṭhe māsa ālabhate tenaivendrāgnibhyāṃ grasitamātmānaṃ niravadayate
(2,2.1 ) āyuṣkāma ālabheta
(2,2.1 ) prāṇāpānau vā indrāgnī
(2,2.1 ) prāṇāpānāvevātmani dhatte
(2,2.1 ) āyuṣmān bhavati
(2,2.1 ) prajākāma ālabheta
(2,2.1 ) prāṇāpānau vā indrāgnī
(2,2.1 ) prāṇāpānau prajā anuprajāyante
(2,2.1 ) prajāvān bhavati
(2,2.1 ) paśukāma ālabheta
(2,2.1 ) prāṇāpānau vā indrāgnī
(2,2.1 ) prāṇāpānau paśavo'nuprajāyante
(2,2.1 ) paśumān bhavati
(2,2.1 ) yāmaṃ śukaṃ hāritamālabheta śuṇṭhaṃ vā yaḥ kāmayetānāmayaḥ pitṛloke syāmiti [ed. śukaharitaṃ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.1 ) etena ha vai yamo'muṣmiṃlloka ārdhnot
(2,2.1 ) pitṛloka evārdhnoti
(2,2.1 ) tvāṣṭraṃ vaḍavamālabheta prajākāmaḥ
(2,2.1 ) prajāpatirvai prajāḥ sisṛkṣamāṇaḥ sa dvitīyaṃ mithunaṃ nāvindat[ed. sisṛksamāṇaḥ, correctedp. 302̭ ড়tyaḻ readnāvindata?]
(2,2.1 ) sa tvāṣṭraṃ vaḍavamapaśyat
(2,2.1 ) tvaṣṭā hi rūpāṇāṃ prajanayitā
(2,2.1 ) tena prajā asṛjata
(2,2.1 ) tena mithunamavindat
(2,2.1 ) prajāvān mithunavān bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānetamālabhate
(2,2.1ś) yonīn vā eṣa kāmyān paśūnālabhate yo'niṣṭvaindrāgnena kāmyaṃ paśumālabhata iṣṭvālambhaḥ samṛddhyai || 1 ||

(2,2.2 ) pañcadhā vai devā vyudakrāmannagnirvasubhiḥ somo rudrairindro marudbhirvaruṇa ādityairbṛhaspatirviśvairdevais
(2,2.2 ) te devā abruvannasurebhyo vā idaṃ bhrātṛvyebhyo radhyāmo yan mitho vipriyāḥ smaḥ
(2,2.2 ) yā na imāḥ priyāstanvastāḥ samavadyāmahā iti
(2,2.2 ) tāḥ samavādyanta
(2,2.2 ) tābhyaḥ sa nirṛcchādyo naḥ prathamo'nyo'nyasmai druhyāditi
(2,2.2 ) yattanvaḥ samavādyanta tattānūnaptrasya tānūnaptratvam
(2,2.2 ) tato devā abhavan parāsurās
(2,2.2 ) tasmādyaḥ satānūnaptriṇāṃ prathamo druhyati sa ārtimārchati
(2,2.2 ) yattānūnaptraṃ samavadyati bhrātṛvyābhibhūtyai
(2,2.2 ) bhavatyātmanā parāsyāpriyo bhrātṛvyo bhavati || 2 ||

(2,2.3 ) pañca kṛtvo'vadyati
(2,2.3 ) pāṅkto yajñaḥ
(2,2.3 ) pañcadhā hi te tāḥ samavādyanta
(2,2.3 ) āpataye tvā gṛhṇāmītyāha
(2,2.3 ) prāṇo vā āpatiḥ
(2,2.3 ) prāṇameva tena prīṇāti
(2,2.3 ) paripataye tvetyāha
(2,2.3 ) mano vai paripatiḥ
(2,2.3 ) mana eva tena prīṇāti
(2,2.3 ) tanūnaptra ityāha
(2,2.3 ) tanvo hi te tāḥ samavādyanta
(2,2.3 ) śākvarāyetyāha
(2,2.3 ) śaktyai hi te tāḥ samavādyanta
(2,2.3 ) śakmana ojiṣṭhāyetyāha
(2,2.3 ) ojiṣṭhaṃ hi te tadātmanaḥ samavādyanta
(2,2.3 ) anādhṛṣṭamityāha
(2,2.3 ) anādhṛṣṭaṃ hyetat
(2,2.3 ) anādhṛṣyamityāha
(2,2.3 ) anādhṛṣyaṃ hyetat
(2,2.3 ) devānāmoja ityāha
(2,2.3 ) devānāṃ hyetadojaḥ
(2,2.3 ) abhiśastipā ityāha
(2,2.3 ) abhiśastipā hyetat
(2,2.3 ) anabhiśastenyamityāha
(2,2.3 ) anabhiśastenyaṃ hyetat [ed. anabhiśastenaṃ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.3ś) anu me dīkṣāṃ dīkṣāpatirmanyatāmanu tapastapaspatirañjasā satyamupa geṣaṃ svite mā dhā ityāha yathāyajurevaitat || 3 ||

(2,2.4 ) ghṛtaṃ vai devā vajraṃ kṛtvā somamaghnan
(2,2.4 ) srucau bāhū
(2,2.4 ) tasmātsrucau saumīmāhutiṃ nāśāte
(2,2.4 ) avadhīyeta somas
(2,2.4 ) tasmātsrucau cājyaṃ cāntikamāhārṣīt
(2,2.4 ) antikamiva khalu vā asyaitatpracaranti yattānūnaptreṇa pracaranti
(2,2.4 ) aṃśuraṃśuṣṭe deva somāpyāyatāmindrāyaikadhanavida ityāha
(2,2.4 ) yadevāsyāpavāyate yan mīyate tadevāsyaitenāpyāyayanti
(2,2.4 ) ā tubhyamindraḥ pyāyatāmā tvamindrāya pyāyasvetyāha
(2,2.4 ) ubhāvevendraṃ ca somaṃ cāpyāyayanti
(2,2.4 ) āpyāyayāsmāntsakhīntsanyā medhayā prajayā dhanenetyāha
(2,2.4 ) ṛtvijo vā etasya sakhāyas
(2,2.4 ) tānevāsyaitenāpyāyayanti
(2,2.4 ) svasti te deva soma sutyāmudṛcamaśīyetyāha
(2,2.4 ) āśiṣamevaitāmāśāste
(2,2.4 ) pra vā etasmāllokāccyavante ye somamāpyāyayanti
(2,2.4 ) antarikṣadevatyo hi soma āpyāyitaḥ
(2,2.4 ) eṣṭā rāya eṣṭā vāmāni preṣe bhagāya
(2,2.4 ) ṛtamṛtavādibhyo namo dive namaḥ pṛthivyā iti
(2,2.4 ) dyāvāpṛthivībhyāmeva namaskṛtyāsmiṃlloke pratitiṣṭhati pratitiṣṭhati || 4 ||

(2,2.5 ) makha ityetadyajñanāmadheyaṃ chidrapratiṣedhasāmarthyāt
(2,2.5 ) chidraṃ khamityuktam
(2,2.5 ) tasya meti pratiṣedhaḥ
(2,2.5 ) mā chidraṃ kariṣyatīti
(2,2.5 ) chidro hi yajño bhinna ivodadhirvisravati
(2,2.5 ) tadvai khalu chidraṃ bhavatyṛtvigyajamānavimānādvā
(2,2.5 ) api vaiṣāṃ vyapekṣayā mantrakalpabrāhmaṇānāmaprayogādyathoktānāṃ vā dakṣiṇānāmapradānāddhīnādvātiriktādvotpātādbhuteṣu prāyaścittavyatikramāditi
(2,2.5 ) etadvai sarvaṃ brahmaṇyarpitam
(2,2.5 ) brahmaiva vidvān yadbhṛgvaṅgirovitsamyagadhīyānaścaritabrahmacaryo'nyūnānatiriktāṅgo'pramatto yajñaṃ rakṣati
(2,2.5 ) tasya pramādādyadi vāpyasāṃnidhyādyathā bhinnā nauragādhe mahatyudake saṃplaven matsyakacchapaśiṃśumāranakramakarapurīkayajaṣarajasapiśācānāṃ bhāgadheyaṃ bhavatyevamādīnāṃ cānyeṣāṃ vinaṣṭopajīvinām
(2,2.5 ) evaṃ khalvapi yajñaśchinnabhinno'padhvasta utpātādbhuto bahulo'tharvabhirasaṃskṛto'suragandharvayakṣarakṣasapiśācānāṃ bhāgadheyaṃ bhavatyevamādīnāṃ cānyeṣāṃ vinaṣṭopajīvināṃ
(2,2.5 ) tadapi ślokāḥ
(2,2.5 ) chinnabhinno'padhvasto viśruto bahudhā makhaḥ | iṣṭāpūrtadraviṇaṃgṛhya yajamānasyāvāpatat ||
(2,2.5 ) ṛtvijāṃ ca vināśāya rājño janapadasya ca | saṃvatsaraviriṣṭaṃ tadyatra yajño viriṣyate || [ed. viraṣyate, correctedp. 302]
(2,2.5 ) dakṣiṇāpravaṇībhūto yajño dakṣiṇataḥ smṛtaḥ | hīnāṅgo rakṣasāṃ bhāgo brahmavedādasaṃskṛtaḥ ||
(2,2.5 ) catuṣpātsakalo yajñaścāturhautravinirmitaḥ | caturvidhai sthito mantrairṛtvigbhirvedapāragaiḥ ||
(2,2.5 ) prāyaścittairanudhyānairanujñānānumantraṇaiḥ | homaiśca yajñavibhraṃśaṃ sarvaṃ brahmā prapūrayed || iti
(2,2.5 ) tasmādyajamāno bhṛgvaṅgirovidameva tatra brahmāṇaṃ vṛṇīyāt
(2,2.5 ) sa hi yajñaṃ tārayatīti brāhmaṇam || 5 ||

(2,2.6 ) yajño vai devebhya udakrāmanna vo'hamannaṃ bhaviṣyāmīti [ed. udakraman, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.6 ) neti devā abruvannannameva no bhaviṣyasīti
(2,2.6 ) taṃ devā vimethire
(2,2.6 ) sa ebhyo vihṛto na prababhūva
(2,2.6 ) te hocurdevāḥ
(2,2.6 ) na vai na itthaṃ vihṛto'laṃ bhaviṣyati
(2,2.6 ) hantemaṃ saṃbharāmeti
(2,2.6 ) taṃ saṃjabhrus
(2,2.6 ) taṃ saṃbhṛtyocuraśvināvimaṃ bhiṣajyatamiti [ed. aśviṇāv, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.6 ) aśvinau vai devānāṃ bhiṣajau
(2,2.6 ) aśvināvadhvaryū
(2,2.6 ) tasmādadhvaryū gharmaṃ saṃbharatas
(2,2.6 ) taṃ saṃbhṛtyocaturbrahman gharmeṇa pracariṣyāmo hotargharmamabhiṣṭuhyudgātaḥ sāmāni gāyeti
(2,2.6 ) pracarata gharmamityanujānāti
(2,2.6 ) brahmaprasūtā hi pracaranti
(2,2.6 ) brahma hedaṃ prasavānāmīśe
(2,2.6 ) savitṛprasūtatāyai
(2,2.6 ) <gharmaṃ tapāmi [ড়ṣ5.16.2, sakala atVaitṣ14.1]> <brahma jajñānam [ড়ṣ5.2.2, śṣ4.1.1, Vaitṣ14.1]> <iyaṃ pitryā rāṣṭryetvagre [ড়ṣ5.2.1, śṣ4.1.2, Vaitṣ14.1]>iti gharmaṃ tāpyamānamupāsīta śastravadardharcaśa āhāvapratigaravarjaṃ rūpasamṛddhābhiḥ
(2,2.6 ) etadvai yajñasya samṛddhaṃ yadrūpasamṛddham
(2,2.6 ) yatkarma kriyamāṇamṛgyajurvābhivadati svasti tasya yajñasya pāramaśnute ya evaṃ veda
(2,2.6 ) devamithunaṃ vā etadyadgharmas
(2,2.6 ) tasmādantardhāya pracaranti
(2,2.6 ) antarhitā vai mithunaṃ carantīti
(2,2.6 ) tadetaddevamithunamityācakṣate
(2,2.6 ) tasya yo gharmastacchiśnam
(2,2.6ś) yau śaphau tāvāṇḍyau
(2,2.6 a) yopayamanī te śroṇikapāle
(2,2.6 b) yatpayastadretas
(2,2.6 c) tadagnau devayonyāṃ reto brahmamayaṃ dhatte prajananāya
(2,2.6 d) so'gnirdevayonirṛṅmayo yajurmayaḥ sāmamayo brahmamayo'mṛtamaya āhutimayaḥ sarvendriyasaṃpanno yajamāna ūrdhvaḥ svargaṃ lokameti
(2,2.6 e) tadāhurna prathamayajñe pravargyaṃ kurvītānupanāmukā ha vā enamuttare yajñakratavo bhavantīti
(2,2.6 f) kāmaṃ tu yo'nūcānaḥ śrotriyaḥ syāttasya pravṛñjyāt
(2,2.6 g) ātmā vai sa yajñasyeti vijñāyate
(2,2.6 h) apaśirasā ha vā eṣa yajñena yajate yo'pravargyeṇa yajate
(2,2.6 i) śiro ha vā etadyajñasya yatpravargyas
(2,2.6 j) tasmātpravargyavataiva yājayennāpravagyeṇa
(2,2.6 k) tadapyeṣābhyanūktā <catvāri śṛṅgā [ড়ṣ8.13.3, sakala atṅBr1,2.16 ]>iti || 6 ||

(2,2.7 ) devāśca ha vā ṛṣayaścāsuraiḥ saṃyattā āsan [ed. ciāsuraiḥ]
(2,2.7 ) teṣāmasurāṇāmimāḥ puraḥ pratyabhijitā āsannayasmayī pṛthivī rajatāntarikṣaṃ hariṇī dyaus
(2,2.7 ) te devāḥ saṃghātaṃsaṃghātaṃ parājayanta
(2,2.7 ) te'viduranāyatanā hi vai smas
(2,2.7 ) tasmātparājayāmahā iti [ed. parājayāmaha, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.7 ) ta etāḥ puraḥ pratyakurvata havirdhānaṃ diva āgnīdhramantarikṣātsadaḥ pṛthivyās
(2,2.7 ) te devā abruvannupasadamupāyāma
(2,2.7 ) upasadā vai mahāpuraṃ jayantīti
(2,2.7 ) ta ebhyo lokebhyo niraghnan
(2,2.7 ) ekayāmuṣmāllokādekayāntarikṣādekayā pṛthivyās
(2,2.7 ) tasmādāhurupasadā vai mahāpuraṃ jayantīti
(2,2.7 ) ta ebhyo lokebhyo nirhatā ṛtūn prāviśan
(2,2.7 ) te ṣaḍupāyan
(2,2.7 ) tānupasadbhirevartubhyo niraghnan
(2,2.7 ) dvābhyāmamuṣmāllokāddvābhyāmantarikṣāddvābhyāṃ pṛthivyās
(2,2.7 ) ta ṛtubhyo nirhatāḥ saṃvatsaraṃ prāviśan
(2,2.7 ) te dvādaśopāyan
(2,2.7 ) tānupasadbhireva saṃvatsarānniraghnan
(2,2.7 ) catasṛbhiramuṣmāllokāccatasṛbhirantarikṣāccatasṛbhiḥ pṛthivyās
(2,2.7 ) te saṃvatsarānnirhatā ahorātre prāviśan
(2,2.7 ) te yatsāyamupāyaṃstenainān rātryā anudanta yatprātastenāhnas
(2,2.7 ) tasmādgauḥ sāyaṃ prātastanamāpyāyate prātaḥ sāyantanam
(2,2.7 ) tānupasadbhirevaibhyo lokebhyo nudamānā āyan
(2,2.7 ) tato devā abhavan parāsurāḥ
(2,2.7 ) sarvebhya evaibhyo lokebhyo bhrātṛvyaṃ nudamāna eti ya evaṃvidvānupasadamupaiti || 7 ||

(2,2.8 ) na dvādaśāgniṣṭomasyopasadaḥ syuḥ
(2,2.8 ) aśāntā nirmṛjyurna tisro'hīnasya
(2,2.8 ) upariṣṭādyajñakraturgarīyānabhiṣīdedyathā gururbhāro grīvā niḥśṛṇīyādārtimārchet
(2,2.8 ) dvādaśāhīnasya kuryāt
(2,2.8 ) pratyuttabdhyai sayatvāya
(2,2.8 ) tisro'gniṣṭomasyopasadaḥ syuḥ śāntyā anirmārgāya
(2,2.8 ) te devā asuryānimāṃllokānnānvavaitumadhṛṣṇuvan
(2,2.8 ) tānagninā mukhenānvavāyan
(2,2.8 ) yadagnimantyupasadāṃ pratīkāni bhavanti yathā kṣetrapatiḥ kṣetre'nvavanayatyevamevaitadagninā mukhenemāṃllokānabhinayanto yanti [ed.'nvavanayanty, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.8 ) yo ha vai devān sādhyān veda sidhyatyasmai
(2,2.8 ) ime vāva lokā yatsādhyā devāḥ
(2,2.8 ) sa ya evametāntsādhyān veda sidhyatyasmai
(2,2.8 ) sidhyatyamuṣmai sidhyatyasmai lokāya ya evaṃvidvānupasadamupaiti || 8 ||

(2,2.9 ) atha yatrāhādhvaryuragnīddevapatnīrvyācakṣva subrahmaṇya subrahmaṇyāmāhvayeti tadapareṇa gārhapatyaṃ prāṅmukhastiṣṭhannanavānannāgnīdhro devapatnīrvyācaṣṭe
(2,2.9 ) pṛthivyagneḥ patnī
(2,2.9 ) vāgvātasya patnī
(2,2.9 ) senendrasya patnī
(2,2.9 ) dhenā bṛhaspateḥ patnī
(2,2.9 ) pathyā pūṣṇaḥ patnī
(2,2.9 ) gāyatrī pasūnāṃ patnī
(2,2.9 ) triṣṭubrudrāṇāṃ patnī
(2,2.9 ) jagatyādityānāṃ patnī
(2,2.9 ) anuṣṭuṃ mitrasya patnī
(2,2.9 ) virāḍvaruṇasya patnī
(2,2.9 ) paṅktirviṣṇoḥ patnī
(2,2.9 ) dīkṣā somasya rājñaḥ patnīti
(2,2.9 ) ati bhrātṛvyānārohati nainaṃ bhrātṛvyā ārohantyupari bhrātṛvyānārohati ya evaṃvidvānāgnīdhro devapatnīrvyācaṣṭe || 9 || [ed. āruohanty, āruohati]

(2,2.10 ) yathā vai ratha ekaikamaramabhipratitiṣṭhan vartata evaṃ yajña ekaikāṃ tanvamabhipratitiṣṭhanneti
(2,2.10 ) purā pracaritorāgnīdhrīye hotavyāḥ
(2,2.10 ) etaddha vā uvāca vāsiṣṭhaḥ sātyahavyo'skan soma ityukte mā sūrkṣata pracarata prātarvāvādyāhaṃ somaṃ samasthāpayamiti
(2,2.10 ) nāsya soma skandati ya evaṃvidvāntsomaṃ pibati
(2,2.10 ) sa ha sma vai sa āsandyāmāsīnaḥ saktubhirupamathya somaṃ pibati
(2,2.10 ) ahaṃ vāva sarvato yajñaṃ veda ya etān veda
(2,2.10 ) na māmeṣa hiṃsiṣyatīti
(2,2.10 ) nainaṃ somapītho na peyo hinasti ya evaṃvidvāntsomaṃ pibati
(2,2.10 ) taṃ ha sma yadāhuḥ kasmāttvamidamāsandyāmāsīnaḥ saktubhirupamathya somaṃ pibasīti
(2,2.10 ) devatāsveva yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayāmītyabravīdbrāhmaṇo yasyaivaṃviduṣo yasyaivaṃvidvān yajñārtyā yajñe prāyaścittaṃ juhoti [edbrahmaṇo, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.10 ) devatāsveva yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,2.10 ) yajñārtiṃ pratijuhuyāt
(2,2.10 ) sayonitvāya
(2,2.10 ) trayastriṃśadvai yajñasya tanva iti
(2,2.10 ) ekānnatriṃśatstomabhāgās
(2,2.10 ) trīṇi savanāni
(2,2.10 ) yajñaścaturthaḥ
(2,2.10 ) stomabhāgairevaitatstomabhāgān pratiprayuṅkte savanaiḥ savanāni yajñena yajñam
(2,2.10 ) sarvā ha vā asya yajñasya tanvaḥ prayuktā bhavanti sarvā āptāḥ sarvā avaruddhāḥ
(2,2.10 ) devasya savituḥ prasave bṛhaspataye stuteti
(2,2.10 ) yadyadvai savitā devebhyaḥ prāsuvattenārdhnuvan
(2,2.10 ) savitṛprasūtā eva stuvanti
(2,2.10 ) ṛdhnuvanti
(2,2.10 ) ṛdhyante ha vā asya stomā yajñe
(2,2.10 ) ṛdhyate yajamāna ṛdhyate prajāyāḥ [ed. ṛdhyate ṛdhyate, correctedp. 302]
(2,2.10ś) ṛdhyate paśubhyaḥ
(2,2.10 a) ṛdhyate brahmaṇe yasyaivaṃvidvān brahmā bhavati || 10 ||

(2,2.11 ) devāśca ha vā asurāścāspardhanta
(2,2.11 ) te devāḥ samāvadeva yajñe kurvāṇā āsan
(2,2.11 ) yadeva davā akurvata tadasurā akurvata
(2,2.11 ) te na vyāvṛtamagacchan
(2,2.11 ) te devā abruvannayatemaṃ yajñaṃ tira uparyasurebhyastaṃsyāmaha iti
(2,2.11 ) tametābhirācchādyodakrāman yajūṃṣi yajñe samidhaḥ svāheti
(2,2.11 ) taṃ tira uparyasurebhyo yajñamatanvata
(2,2.11 ) tameṣāṃ yajñamasurā nānvavāyan
(2,2.11 ) tato devā abhavan parāsurāḥ
(2,2.11 ) sa ya evaṃvidvāṃstira uparyasurebhyo yajñaṃ tanute bhavatyātmanā parāsyāpriyo bhrātṛvyo bhavati
(2,2.11 ) etaireva juhuyātsamṛtayajñe caturbhiścaturbhiranvākhyāyam
(2,2.11 ) purastātprātaranuvākasya juhuyāt
(2,2.11 ) etāvān vai yajño yāvāneṣa yajñastaṃ vṛṅkte
(2,2.11 ) sayajño bhavati
(2,2.11 ) ayajña itaraḥ
(2,2.11 ) etaireva juhuyātpurastāddvādaśāhasya
(2,2.11 ) eṣa ha vai pratyakṣaṃ dvādaśāhas
(2,2.11 ) tamevālabhyaitaireva juhuyāt
(2,2.11 ) purastāddīkṣāyāḥ
(2,2.11 ) eṣā ha vai pratyakṣaṃ dīkṣā
(2,2.11 ) tāmevālabhyaitairevātithyamabhimṛśed<yajñena yajñamayajanta devāḥ [śṣ7.5.1, ড়ṣ20.2.2, Vaitṣ16.15]> iti || 11 ||

(2,2.12 ) yatra vijānāti brahmantsomo'skanniti tametayālabhyābhimantrayate<abhūddevaḥ savitā vandyo nu na idānīmahna upavācyo nṛbhiḥ | vi yo ratnā bhajati mānavebhyaḥ śreṣṭhaṃ no atra draviṇaṃ yathā dadhat[ṛV 4.54.1]>iti
(2,2.12 ) <ye agnayo apsvantar[śṣ3.21.1, Vaitṣ16.16, cf. ড়ṣ10.9.1?]> iti saptabhirabhijuhoti
(2,2.12 ) yadevāsyāvaskannaṃ bhavati tadevāsyaitadagnau svagākaroti
(2,2.12 ) agnirhi sukṛtīnāṃ haviṣāṃ pratiṣṭhā
(2,2.12 ) atha visṛpya vaipruṣān homāñjuhoti <drapsaścaskanda [ṛV 10.17.11, ড়ṣ20.13.7, śṣ18.4.28, Vaitṣ16.17]>iti [ed. juhvati, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.12 ) yā evāsyābhiṣūyamāṇasya vipruṣa skandantyaṃśurvā tā evāsyaitadāhavanīye svagākaroti [ed. āhavaṇīye]
(2,2.12 ) āhavanīyo hyāhutīnāṃ pratiṣṭhā
(2,2.12 ) <yaste drapsa skandati [ṛV 10.17.12 , ড়ṣ20.13.8 , Vaitṣ16.17]>iti
(2,2.12 ) stoko vai drapsaḥ
(2,2.12 ) <yaste aṃśurbāhucyuto dhiṣaṇāyā upasthāt[ṛV 10.17.12 , ড়ṣ20.13.8 , Vaitṣ16.17]>iti bāhubhirabhicyuto'ṃśuradhiṣavaṇābhyāmadhiskandati
(2,2.12 ) <adhvaryorvā pari yaḥ pavitrāttaṃ te juhomi manasā vaṣaṭkṛtam [ড়ṣ20.13.8 d, ṛV 10.17.12 d, Vaitṣ16.17]> iti
(2,2.12 ) tadyathā vaṣaṭkṛtaṃ svāhākṛtaṃ hutamevaṃ bhavati || 12 ||

(2,2.13 ) ṛṣayo vā indraṃ pratyakṣaṃ nāpaśyan
(2,2.13 ) taṃ vasiṣṭha eva pratyakṣamapaśyat
(2,2.13 ) so'bibheditarebhya ṛṣibhyo mā pravocaditi
(2,2.13 ) so'bravīdbrāhmaṇaṃ te vakṣyāmi yathā tvatpurohitāḥ prajāḥ prajaniṣyante
(2,2.13 ) athetarebhya ṛṣibhyo mā pravoca iti
(2,2.13 ) tasmā etān stomabhāgānuvāca
(2,2.13 ) tato vasiṣṭhapurohitāḥ prajāḥ prājāyanta
(2,2.13 ) stomo vā ete eteṣāṃ bhāgas
(2,2.13 ) tatstomabhāgānāṃ stomabhāgatvam
(2,2.13 ) raśmirasi kṣayāya tveti
(2,2.13 ) kṣayo vai devāḥ
(2,2.13 ) devebhya eva yajñaṃ prāha
(2,2.13 ) pretirasi dharmaṇe tveti
(2,2.13 ) dharmo manuṣyāḥ
(2,2.13 ) manuṣyebhya eva yajñaṃ prāha
(2,2.13 ) anvitirasi saṃdhirasi pratidhirasīti [ed. anitir, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,2.13 ) trayo vai lokāḥ
(2,2.13 ) lokeṣveva yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,2.13 ) viṣṭambho'sīti
(2,2.13 ) vṛṣṭimevāvarunddhe
(2,2.13 ) prāvo'syahnāṃsīti mithunameva karoti
(2,2.13 ) uśigasi praketo'si suditirasīti
(2,2.13 ) aṣṭau vasava ekādaśa rudrā dvādaśādityā vāgdvātriṃśī svarastrayastriṃśastrayastriṃśaddevāḥ
(2,2.13 ) devebhya eva yajñaṃ prāha
(2,2.13 ) ojo'si pitṛbhyastveti
(2,2.13ś) balameva tatpitṝnanusaṃtanoti
(2,2.13 a) tanturasi prajābhyastveti
(2,2.13 b) prajā eva paśūnanusaṃtanoti
(2,2.13 c) revadasyoṣadhībhyastveti
(2,2.13 d) oṣadhīṣveva yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,2.13 e) pṛtanāṣāḍasi paśubhyastveti
(2,2.13 f) prajā eva paśūnanusaṃtanoti
(2,2.13 g) abhijidasīti
(2,2.13 h) vajro vai ṣoḍaśī
(2,2.13 i) vyāvṛtto'sau vajras
(2,2.13 j) tasmādeṣo'nyairvyāvṛttaḥ
(2,2.13 k) nābhurasīti
(2,2.13 l) prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ
(2,2.13 m) prajāpatimevāvarunddhe || 13 ||

(2,2.14 ) adhipatirasi dharuṇo'si saṃsarpo'si vayodhā asīti
(2,2.14 ) prāṇo'pānaścakṣuḥ śrotramityetāni vai puruṣamakaran praṇānupaiti
(2,2.14 ) prajātyā eva
(2,2.14 ) trivṛdasi pravṛdasi svavṛdasyanuvṛdasīti
(2,2.14 ) mithunameva karoti
(2,2.14 ) āroho'si praroho'si saṃroho'syanuroho'sīti [ed. āruoho]
(2,2.14 ) prajāpatireva
(2,2.14 ) vasuko'si vasyaṣṭirasi veṣaśrīrasīti
(2,2.14 ) pratiṣṭhitireva
(2,2.14 ) ākramo'si saṃkramo'syutkramo'syutkrāntirasīti
(2,2.14 ) ṛddhireva
(2,2.14 ) yadyadvai savitā devebhyaḥ prāsuvattenārdhnuvan
(2,2.14 ) savitṛprasūtā eva stuvanti
(2,2.14 ) ṛdhnuvanti
(2,2.14 ) bṛhaspataye stuteti
(2,2.14 ) bṛhaspatirvā āṅgiraso devānāṃ brahmā
(2,2.14 ) tadanumatyaivoṃ bhūrjanaditi prātaḥsavane
(2,2.14 ) ṛgbhirevobhayato'tharvāṅgirobhirguptābhirguptai stuteti
(2,2.14 ) evoṃ bhuvo janaditi mādhyaṃdine savane
(2,2.14 ) yajurbhirevobhayato'tharvāṅgirobhirguptābhirguptaiḥ
(2,2.14 ) stutetyevoṃ svarjanaditi tṛtīyasavane
(2,2.14 ) sāmabhirevobhayato'tharvāṅgirobhirguptābhirguptai stutetyeva
(2,2.14 ) atha yadyahīna ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī vājapeyo'tirātro'ptoryāmā vā syātsarvābhiḥ sarvābhirata ūrdhvaṃ vyāhṛtibhiranujānāti
(2,2.14 ) oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svarjanadvṛdhatkaradruhan mahattacchamomindravanta stuteti sendrān māpagāyata sendrān stutetyeva
(2,2.14 ) indriyavānṛddhimān vaśīyān bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvāntstomabhāgairyajate || 14 ||

(2,2.15 ) yo ha vā āyatāṃśca pratiyatāṃśca stomabhāgān vidyātsa viṣpardhamānayoḥ samṛtasomayorbrahmā syāt
(2,2.15 ) <stuteṣe stutorje stuta devasya savituḥ save bṛhaspatiṃ vaḥ prajāpatiṃ vo vasūn vo devān rudrān vo devānādityān vo devān sādhyān vo devānāptyān vo devān viśvān vo devān sarvān vo devān viśvataspari havāmahe [Vaitṣ17.7]>
(2,2.15 ) <janebhyo'smākamastu kevalaḥ [Vaitṣ17.7 (cf. ড়ṣ5.4.9 )]>
(2,2.15 ) <itaḥ kṛṇotu vīryam [Vaitṣ17.7 (ড়ṣ5.4.9 )]> iti
(2,2.15 ) ete ha vā āyatāśca pratiyatāśca stomabhāgās
(2,2.15 ) tāñjapannuparyupari pareṣāṃ brahmāṇamavekṣeta
(2,2.15 ) tata eṣāmadhaḥśirā brahmā patati
(2,2.15 ) tato yajñas
(2,2.15 ) tato yajamānaḥ
(2,2.15 ) yajamāne'dhaḥśirasi patite sa deśo'dhaḥśirāḥ patati yasminnardhe yajante
(2,2.15 ) devāśca ha vā asurāśca samṛtasomau yajñāvatanutām
(2,2.15 ) atha bṛhaspatirāṅgiraso devānāṃ brahmā
(2,2.15 ) sa āyatāṃśca pratiyatāṃśca stomabhāgāñjapannuparyuparyasurāṇāṃ brahmāṇamavaikṣata
(2,2.15 ) tata eṣāmadhaḥśirā brahmāpatat
(2,2.15 ) tato yajñas
(2,2.15 ) tato'surā iti || 15 ||

(2,2.16 ) devā yajñaṃ parājayanta
(2,2.16 ) tamāgnīdhrātpunarupājayanta
(2,2.16 ) tadetadyajñasyāparājitaṃ yadāgnīdhram
(2,2.16 ) yadāgnīdhrāddhiṣṇyān viharati tata evainaṃ punastanute
(2,2.16 ) parājityai
(2,2.16 ) apa khalu vā ete gacchanti ye bahiṣpavamānaṃ sarpanti
(2,2.16 ) bahiṣpavamāne stuta āha
(2,2.16 ) agnīdagnīn vihara barhi stṛṇīhi puroḍāśānalaṃkurviti
(2,2.16 ) yajñamevāparājitya punastanvānā āyanti
(2,2.16 ) aṅgārairdve savane viharati śalākābhistṛtīyasavanaṃ saśrukratvāya
(2,2.16 ) atho saṃbhavatyevamevaitat
(2,2.16 ) dakṣiṇato vai devānāṃ yajñaṃ rakṣāṃsyajighāṃsan [ed. ajidhāṃsan]
(2,2.16 ) tānyāgnīdhreṇāpāghnata
(2,2.16 ) tasmāddakṣiṇāmukhastiṣṭhannagnītpratyāśrāvayati
(2,2.16 ) yajñasyābhijityai rakṣasāmapahatyai rakṣasāmapahatyai || 16 ||

(2,2.17 ) tadāhuratha kasmātsaumya evādhvare pravṛtāhutīrjuhvati na haviryajña iti
(2,2.17 ) akṛtsnā vā eṣā devayajyā yaddhaviryajñaḥ
(2,2.17 ) atha haiṣaiva kṛtsnā devayajyā yatsaumyo'dhvaras
(2,2.17 ) tasmātsaumya evādhvare pravṛtāhutīrjuhvati
(2,2.17 ) juṣṭo vāce bhūyāsaṃ juṣṭo vācaspataye devi vāgyadvāco madhumattamaṃ tasmin mā dhāḥ svāhā vāce svāhā vācapataye svāhā sarasvatyai svāhā sarasvatyā iti purastātsvāhākāreṇa juhoti [ed. svāhākareṇa]
(2,2.17 ) tasmādvāgata ūrdhvamutsṛṣṭā yajñaṃ vahati manasottarām
(2,2.17 ) manasā hi manaḥ prītam
(2,2.17 ) tadu haike saptāhutīrjuhvati sapta chandāṃsi pravṛttāni pratimantramiti vadantaḥ
(2,2.17 ) yathā mekhalā paryasyate medhyasya cāmedhyasya ca vihṛtyā evaṃ haivaite nyupyante medhasya cāmedhyasya ca vihṛtyai yajñasya vihṛtyai
(2,2.17 ) prācīnaṃ hi dhiṣṇyebhyo devānāṃ lokāḥ pratīcīnaṃ manuṣyāṇām
(2,2.17 ) tasmātsomaṃ pibatā prāñco dhiṣṇyā nopasarpyāḥ
(2,2.17 ) janaṃ hyetat
(2,2.17 ) devalokaṃ hyadhyārohanti
(2,2.17 ) teṣāmetadāyatanaṃ codayanaṃ ca yadāgnīdhraṃ ca sadaśca
(2,2.17 ) tadyo'vidvāntsaṃcaratyārtimārchatyi
(2,2.17 ) atha yo vidvāntsaṃcarati na sa dhiṣṇīyāmārtimārchati || 17 || [ed. vidvant]

(2,2.18 ) prajāpatirvai yajñas
(2,2.18 ) tasmintsarve kāmāḥ sarvā iṣṭīḥ sarvamamṛtatvam
(2,2.18 ) tasya haite goptāro yaddhiṣṇyās
(2,2.18 ) tāntsadaḥ prasrapsyannamaskaroti
(2,2.18 ) namo nama iti
(2,2.18 ) na hi namaskāramati devās
(2,2.18 ) te ha namasitāḥ kartāramatisṛjantīti
(2,2.18 ) tata etaṃ prajāpatiṃ yajñaṃ prapadyate namo nama iti
(2,2.18 ) na hi namaskāramati devāḥ
(2,2.18 ) sa tatraiva yajamānaḥ sarvān kāmānāpnoti sarvān kāmānāpnoti || 18 ||

(2,2.19 ) yo vai sadasyān gandharvān veda na sadasyāmārtimārchati
(2,2.19 ) sadaḥ prasrapsyan brūyādupadraṣṭre nama iti
(2,2.19 ) agnirvai draṣṭā tasmā u evātmānaṃ paridadāti
(2,2.19 ) sarvamāyureti na purā jarasaḥ pramīyate ya evaṃ veda
(2,2.19 ) sadaḥ prasṛpya brūyādupaśrotre nama iti
(2,2.19 ) vāyurvā upaśrotā
(2,2.19 ) tasmā u evātmānaṃ paridadāti
(2,2.19 ) sarvamāyureti na purā jarasaḥ pramīyate ya evaṃ veda
(2,2.19 ) sadaḥ prasarpan brūyādanukhyātre nama iti
(2,2.19 ) ādityo vā anukhyātā
(2,2.19 ) tasmā u evātmānaṃ paridadāti
(2,2.19 ) sarvamāyureti na purā jarasaḥ pramīyate ya evaṃ veda
(2,2.19 ) sadaḥ prasṛpto brūyādupadraṣṭre nama iti
(2,2.19 ) brāhmaṇo vā upadraṣṭā
(2,2.19 ) tasmā u evātmānaṃ paridadāti
(2,2.19 ) sarvamāyureti na purā jarasaḥ pramīyate ya evaṃ veda
(2,2.19 ) te vai sadasyā gandharvāḥ
(2,2.19 ) sa ya evametāntsadasyān gandharvānavidvāntsadaḥ prasarpati sa sadasyāmārtimārchati
(2,2.19 ) atha yo vidvāntsaṃcarati na sadasyāmārtimārchati
(2,2.19 ) etena ha sma vā aṅgirasaḥ sarvaṃ sadaḥ paryāhus
(2,2.19 ) te na sadasyāmārtimārchanti
(2,2.19 ) atha yān kāmayeta na sadasyāmārtimārcheyuriti tebhya etena sarvaṃ sadaḥ paribrūyāt
(2,2.19 ) te na sadasyāmārtimārchanti
(2,2.19 ) atha yaṃ kāmayeta pramīyateti tametebhya āvṛścet
(2,2.19 ) pramīyate || 19 ||

(2,2.20 ) tadāhuryadaindro yajño'tha kasmāddvāveva prātaḥsavane prasthitānāṃ pratyakṣādaindrībhyāṃ yajato hotā caiva brāhmaṇācchaṃsī ca [ed. aidrībhyāṃ]
(2,2.20 ) <idaṃ te somyaṃ madhu [ṛV 8.65.8]>iti hotā yajati
(2,2.20 ) <indra tvā vṛṣabhaṃ vayam [ṛV 3.40.1, Vaitṣ19.6]> iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsī
(2,2.20 ) nānādevatyābhiritare
(2,2.20 ) kathaṃ teṣāmaindryo bhavanti
(2,2.20 ) <mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe [ṛV 1.23.4 ]>iti maitrāvaruṇo yajati
(2,2.20 ) <varuṇaṃ somapītaye [ṛV 1.23.4 ]>iti
(2,2.20 ) yadvai kiṃ ca pītavattadaindraṃ rūpam
(2,2.20 ) tenendraṃ prīṇāti
(2,2.20 ) <maruto yasya hi kṣaye [ṛV 1.86.1 , śṣ20.1.2 ]>iti potā yajati
(2,2.20 ) <sa sugopātamo janaḥ [ṛV 1.86.1 , śṣ20.1.2 ]>iti [ed. sugopatamo, correctedp. 302]
(2,2.20 ) indro vai gopās
(2,2.20 ) tadaindraṃ rūpam
(2,2.20 ) tenendraṃ prīṇāti
(2,2.20 ) <agne patnīrihā vaha [ṛV 1.22.9 ]>iti neṣṭā yajati
(2,2.20 ) <tvaṣṭāraṃ somapītaye [ṛV 1.22.9 ]>iti
(2,2.20 ) yadvai kiṃ ca pītavattadaindraṃ rūpam
(2,2.20 ) tenendraṃ prīṇāti
(2,2.20 ) <ukṣānnāya vaśānnāya [ṛV 8.43.11 , ড়ṣ3.12.6 , śṣ3.21.6 20.1.3 ]>ityāgnīdhro yajati
(2,2.20 ) <somapṛṣṭhāya vedhase [ṛV 8.43.11 , ড়ṣ3.12.6 , śṣ3.21.6 20.1.3 ]>iti
(2,2.20 ) indro vai vedhās
(2,2.20 ) tadaindraṃ rūpam
(2,2.20 ) tenendraṃ prīṇāti
(2,2.20 ) <prātaryāvabhirā gataṃ devebhirjenyāvasū indrāgnī somapītaye [ṛV 8.38.7]>iti
(2,2.20 ) svayaṃsamṛddhā acchāvākasya
(2,2.20ś) evamu haitā aindryo bhavanti
(2,2.20 a) yannānādevatyāstenānyā devatāḥ prīṇāti
(2,2.20 b) yadgāyatryastenāgneyyas
(2,2.20 c) tasmādetābhistrayamavāptaṃ bhavati || 20 ||

(2,2.21 ) te vai khalu sarva eva mādhyaṃdine prasthitānāṃ pratyakṣādaindrībhiryajanti
(2,2.21 ) abhitṛṇavatībhireke
(2,2.21 ) <pibā somamabhi yamugra tardaḥ [ṛV 6.17.1]>iti hotā yajati
(2,2.21 ) <sa īṃ pāhi ya ṛjīṣī tarutraḥ [ṛV 6.17.2]>iti maitrāvaruṇaḥ
(2,2.21 ) <evā pāhi pratnathā mandatu tvā [ṛV 6.17.3]>iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsī
(2,2.21 ) <arvāṅehi somakāmaṃ tvāhuḥ [ṛV 1.104.9]>iti potā
(2,2.21 ) <tavāyaṃ somastvamehyarvāṅ[ṛV 3.35.6]> iti neṣṭā
(2,2.21 ) <indrāya somāḥ pradivo vidānāḥ [ṛV 3.36.2]>ityacchāvākaḥ
(2,2.21 ) <āpūrṇo asya kalaśaḥ svāhā [ṛV 3.32.15]>ityāgnīdhraḥ [ed. svahoty]
(2,2.21 ) evamu haitā abhitṛṇavatyo bhavanti
(2,2.21 ) indro vai prātaḥsavanaṃ nābhyajayat
(2,2.21 ) sa etābhirmādhyaṃdinaṃ savanamabhyatṛṇat
(2,2.21 ) tadyadetābhirmādhyaṃdinaṃ savanamabhyatṛṇattasmādetā abhitṛṇavatyo bhavanti || 21 ||

(2,2.22 ) tadāhuryadaindrārbhavaṃ tṛtīyasavanamatha kasmādeka eva tṛtīyasavane prasthitānāṃ pratyakṣādaindrārbhavyā yajati
(2,2.22 ) <indra ṛbhubhirvājavadbhiḥ samukṣitam [ṛV 3.60.5]> iti hotaiva
(2,2.22 ) nānādevatyābhiritare
(2,2.22 ) kathaṃ teṣāmaindrārbhavyo bhavanti
(2,2.22 ) <indrāvaruṇā sutapāvimaṃ sutam [ṛV 6.68.10, ড়ṣ20.7.5 , śṣ7.58.1 ]> iti maitrāvaruṇo yajati [ed. satam, correctedp. 302]
(2,2.22 ) <yuvo ratho adhvaro devavītaye [ড়ṣ20.7.5 , śṣ7.58.1 , ṛV 6.68.10 ]>iti bahūni vāha
(2,2.22 ) tadṛbhūṇāṃ rūpam
(2,2.22 ) <indraśca somaṃ pibataṃ bṛhaspataye [ṛV 4.50.10 , Vaitṣ22.11]>iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsī yajati
(2,2.22 ) <ā vāṃ viśantvindavaḥ svābhuvaḥ [ṛV 4.50.10 ]>iti bahūni vāha
(2,2.22 ) tadṛbhūṇāṃ rūpam
(2,2.22 ) <ā vo vahantu saptayo raghuṣyadaḥ [ṛV 1.85.6 ]>iti potā yajati
(2,2.22 ) <raghupatvānaḥ pra jigāta bāhubhiḥ [ṛV 1.85.6 ]>iti bahūni vāha
(2,2.22 ) tadṛbhūṇāṃ rūpam
(2,2.22 ) <ameva naḥ suhavā ā hi gantana [ṛV 2.36.3]>iti neṣṭā yajati
(2,2.22 ) gantaneti bahūni vāha
(2,2.22 ) tadṛbhūṇāṃ rūpam
(2,2.22 ) <indrāviṣṇū pibataṃ madhvo asya [ṛV 6.69.7 ]>ityacchāvāko yajati
(2,2.22 ) <ā vāmandhāṃsi madirāṇyagman [ṛV 6.69.7 ]>iti bahūni vāha
(2,2.22 ) tadṛbhūṇāṃ rūpam
(2,2.22 ) <imaṃ stomamarhate jātavedase [ṛV 1.94.1 ]>ityāgnīdhro yajati
(2,2.22 ) <rathamiva saṃ mahemā manīṣayā [ṛV 1.94.1 ]>iti bahūni vāha
(2,2.22 ) tadṛbhūṇāṃ rūpam
(2,2.22 ) evamu haitā aindrārbhavyo bhavanti
(2,2.22 ) yannānādevatyāstenānyā devatāḥ prīṇāti
(2,2.22 ) yadu jagatprāsāhā jāgatamu vai tṛtīyasavanam
(2,2.22ś) tṛtīyasavanasya samaṣṭyai || 22 ||

(2,2.23 ) vicakṣaṇavatīṃ vācaṃ bhāṣante canasitavatīm
(2,2.23 ) vicakṣayanti brāhmaṇam
(2,2.23 ) canasayanti prājāpatyam
(2,2.23 ) satyaṃ vadanti
(2,2.23 ) etadvai manuṣyeṣu satyaṃ yaccakṣus
(2,2.23 ) tasmādāhurācakṣāṇamadrāgiti
(2,2.23 ) sa yadāhādrākṣamiti tathāhāsya śraddadhati
(2,2.23 ) yadyu vai svayaṃ vai dṛṣṭaṃ bhavati na bahūnāṃ janānāmeṣa śraddadhāti
(2,2.23 ) tasmādvicakṣaṇavatīṃ vācaṃ bhāṣante canasitavatīm
(2,2.23 ) satyottarā haivaiṣāṃ vāguditā bhavati || 23 ||

(2,2.24 ) samṛtayajño vā eṣa yaddarśapūrṇamāsau
(2,2.24 ) kasya vāva devā yajñamāgacchanti kasya vā na
(2,2.24 ) bahūnāṃ vā etadyajamānānāṃ sāmānyamahas
(2,2.24 ) tasmātpūrvedyurdevatāḥ parigṛhṇīyāt
(2,2.24 ) yo ha vai pūrvedyurdevatāḥ parigṛhṇāti tasya śvo bhūte yajñamāgacchanti
(2,2.24 ) tasmādvihavyasya catasra ṛco japet
(2,2.24 ) yajñavido hi manyante eva soma eva samṛta iti yajño yajñena samṛtaḥ || 24 ||

(2,2.24 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇottarabhāge dvitīyaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||

(2,3.1 ) oṃ devapātraṃ vai vaṣaṭkāraḥ
(2,3.1 ) yadvaṣaṭkaroti devapātreṇaiva taddevatāstarpayati
(2,3.1 ) atho yadābhitṛṣyantīrabhisaṃsthaṃ tarpayatyevameva taddevatāstarpayati yadanuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.1 ) tadyathaivādo'śvān vā gā vā punarabhyākāraṃ tarpayatyevameva taddevatāstarpayati yadanuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.1 ) imānevāgnīnupāsata ityāhurdhiṣṇyān
(2,3.1 ) atha kasmātpūrvasminnevāgnau juhvati pūrvasmin vaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.1 ) yadeva somasyāgne vīhītyanuvaṣaṭkaroti tenaiva vaṣaṭkaroti dhiṣṇyān prīṇāti
(2,3.1 ) atha saṃsthitān somān bhakṣayantītyāhuryeṣāṃ nānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.1 ) tadāhuḥ ko nu somasya sviṣṭakṛdbhāga iti
(2,3.1 ) yadeva somasyāgne vīhītyanuvaṣaṭkaroti tenaiva saṃsthitān somān bhakṣayantītyāhuḥ
(2,3.1 ) sa u eṣa somasya sviṣṭakṛdbhāgo yadanuvaṣaṭkaroti || 1 ||

(2,3.2 ) vajro vai vaṣaṭkāraḥ
(2,3.2 ) sa yaṃ dviṣyāttaṃ manasā dhyāyan vaṣaṭkuryāt
(2,3.2 ) tasmiṃstadvajramāsthāpayati
(2,3.2 ) ṣaḍiti vaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.2 ) ṣaḍvā ṛtavaḥ
(2,3.2 ) ṛtūnāmāptyai
(2,3.2 ) vauṣaḍiti vaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.2 ) asau vāva vāvṛtavaḥ ṣaṭ
(2,3.2 ) etameva tadṛtuṣvādadhāti
(2,3.2 ) ṛtuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,3.2 ) tadu ha smāha vaida etāni vā etena ṣaṭpratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,3.2 ) dyaurantarikṣe pratiṣṭhitā
(2,3.2 ) antarikṣaṃ pṛthivyām
(2,3.2 ) pṛthivyapsu
(2,3.2 ) āpaḥ satye
(2,3.2 ) satyaṃ brahmaṇi
(2,3.2 ) brahma tapasīti
(2,3.2 ) etā eva taddevatāḥ pratiṣṭhānyāḥ pratitiṣṭhantīridaṃ sarvamanu pratitiṣṭhati [ed. pratisthānyāḥ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,3.2 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 2 ||

(2,3.3 ) trayo vai vaṣaṭkārāḥ
(2,3.3 ) vajro dhāmacchadriktaḥ
(2,3.3 ) sa yadevoccairbalaṃ vaṣaṭkaroti sa vajras
(2,3.3 ) taṃ taṃ praharati dviṣate bhrātṛvyāya vadhaṃ yo'sya stṛtyastasmai starītave
(2,3.3 ) tasmātsa bhrātṛvyavatā vaṣaṭkṛtyaḥ
(2,3.3 ) atha yaḥ samaḥ saṃtato nirhāṇacchatsva dhāmacchat
(2,3.3 ) taṃ taṃ prajāśca paśavaścānūpatiṣṭhante
(2,3.3 ) tasmātsa prajākāmena paśukāmena vaṣaṭkṛtyaḥ
(2,3.3 ) atha yenaiva ṣaḍaparādhnoti sa riktaḥ
(2,3.3 ) riṇaktyātmānaṃ riṇakti yajamānam
(2,3.3 ) pāpīyān vaṣaṭkartā bhavati pāpīyān yasmai vaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.3 ) tasmāttasyāśāṃ neyāt
(2,3.3 ) kiṃ svitsa yajamānasya pāpabhadramādriyeteti ha smāha yo'sya vaṣaṭkartā bhavati
(2,3.3 ) atraivainaṃ yathā kāmayeta tathā kuryāt
(2,3.3 ) yaṃ kāmayeta yathaivānījāno'bhūttathaivejānaḥ syāditi yathaivāsyarcaṃ brūyāttathaivāsya vaṣaṭkuryāt
(2,3.3 ) samānamevainaṃ tatkaroti
(2,3.3 ) yaṃ kāmayeta pāpīyān syādityuccaistarāmasyarcaṃ brūyānnīcaistarāṃ vaṣaṭkuryāt
(2,3.3 ) pāpīyāṃsamevainaṃ tatkaroti
(2,3.3 ) yaṃ kāmayeta śreyān syāditi nīcaistarāmasyarcaṃ brūyāduccaistarāṃ vaṣaṭkuryāt
(2,3.3 ) śreyāṃsamevainaṃ tatkaroti
(2,3.3 ) śriya evainaṃ tacchriyamādadhāti || 3 ||

(2,3.4 ) yasyai devatāyai havirgṛhītaṃ syāttāṃ manasā dhyāyan vaṣaṭkuryāt[ed. davatāyai]
(2,3.4 ) sākṣādeva taddevatāṃ prīṇāti
(2,3.4 ) pratyakṣāddevatāṃ parigṛhṇāti
(2,3.4 ) saṃtatamṛcā vaṣaṭkṛtyam
(2,3.4 ) saṃtatyai
(2,3.4 ) saṃdhīyate prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 4 ||

(2,3.5 ) vajro vai vaṣaṭkāraḥ
(2,3.5 ) sa u eṣa prahṛto'śānto dīdāya tasya ha na sarva eva śāntiṃ veda no pratiṣṭhām
(2,3.5 ) tasmāddhāpyetarhi bhūyāniva mṛtyus
(2,3.5 ) tasya haiṣaiva śāntireṣā pratiṣṭhā yadvāgiti
(2,3.5 ) vaṣaṭkṛtya vāgityanumantrayate
(2,3.5 ) vaṣaṭkāra mā māṃ pramṛkṣo māhaṃ tvāṃ pramṛkṣaṃ bṛhatā mana upahvaye vyānena śarīraṃ pratiṣṭhāsi pratiṣṭhāṃ gaccha pratiṣṭhāṃ mā gamayediti [ed. pratiṣṭhīsi, correctedp. 302]
(2,3.5 ) tadu smāha dīrghamevaitaitsadaprabhvojaḥ saha oja ityanumantrayeta
(2,3.5 ) ojaśca ha vai sahaśca vaṣaṭkārasya priyatame tanvau
(2,3.5 ) priyābhyāmeva tattanūbhyāṃ samardhayati
(2,3.5 ) priyayā tanvā samṛdhyate ya evaṃ veda || 5 ||

(2,3.6 ) vākca vai prāṇāpānau ca vaṣaṭkāras
(2,3.6 ) te vaṣaṭkṛte vaṣaṭkṛte vyutkrāmanti
(2,3.6 ) tānanumantrayate vāgojaḥ saha ojo mayi prāṇāpānāviti
(2,3.6 ) vācaṃ caiva tatprāṇāpānau ca hotātmani pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,3.6 ) sarvamāyureti
(2,3.6 ) na purā jarasaḥ pramīyate ya evaṃ veda
(2,3.6 ) <śaṃ no bhava hṛda ā pīta indo piteva soma sūnave suśevaḥ | sakheva sakhya uruśaṃsa dhīraḥ pra ṇa āyurjīvase soma tārīḥ [ṛV 8.48.4, Vaitṣ19.18]>ityātmānaṃ pratyabhimṛśati
(2,3.6 ) īśvaro vā eṣo'pratyabhimṛṣṭo yajamānasyāyuḥ pratyavahartumanarhan mā bhakṣayediti
(2,3.6 ) tadyadetena pratyabhimṛśatyāyurevāsmai tatpratirate
(2,3.6 ) āpyāyasva saṃ te payāṃsīti dvābhyāṃ camasānāpyāyayantyabhirūpābhyām
(2,3.6 ) yadyajñe'bhirūpaṃ tatsamṛddham || 6 ||

(2,3.7 ) prāṇā vā ṛtuyājās
(2,3.7 ) tadyadṛtuyājaiścaranti prāṇāneva tadyajamāne dadhati
(2,3.7 ) ṣaḍṛtuneti yajanti
(2,3.7 ) prāṇameva tadyajamāne dadhati
(2,3.7 ) catvāra ṛtubhiriti yajanti
(2,3.7 ) apānameva tadyajamāne dadhati
(2,3.7 ) dvirṛtunetyupariṣṭāt
(2,3.7 ) vyānameva tadyajamāne dadhati
(2,3.7 ) sa cāsu saṃbhṛtastredhā vihṛtaḥ prāṇo'pāno vyāna iti [ed.'pāṇo]
(2,3.7 ) tato'nyatra guṇitas
(2,3.7 ) tathā ha yajamānaḥ sarvamāyuretyasmiṃlloka ārdhnoti
(2,3.7 ) āpnotyamṛtatvamakṣitaṃ svarge loke
(2,3.7 ) te vā ete prāṇā eva yadṛtuyājās
(2,3.7 ) tasmādanavānaṃ tato yajanti [ed. anavānanto, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,3.7 ) prāṇānāṃ santatyai
(2,3.7 ) santantā iva hīme prāṇāḥ
(2,3.7 ) atho ṛtavo vā ṛtuyājāḥ
(2,3.7 ) saṃsthānuvaṣaṭkāraḥ [ed. anuvaṣaṭkuāro]
(2,3.7 ) yo'trānuvaṣaṭkuryādasaṃsthitānṛtūn saṃsthāpayet
(2,3.7 ) yastaṃ tatra brūyādasaṃsthitānṛtūn samatiṣṭhipadduḥṣamaṃ bhaviṣyatīti śaśvattathā syāt || 7 ||

(2,3.8 ) tadāhuryaddhotā yakṣaddhotā yakṣaditi maitrāvaruṇo hotre preṣyatyatha kasmādahotṛbhyaḥ sadbhyo hotrāśaṃsibhyo hotā yakṣaddhotā yakṣaditi preṣyatīti
(2,3.8 ) vāgvai hotā vāksarva ṛtvijaḥ
(2,3.8 ) vāgyakṣadvāgyakṣaditi
(2,3.8 ) atho sarve vā ete sapta hotāro'pi vā ṛcābhyuditaṃ sapta hotāra ṛtuthā yajantīti
(2,3.8 ) atha ya upariṣṭāddvādaśarcajāmitāyai
(2,3.8 ) te vai dvādaśa bhavanti
(2,3.8 ) dvādaśa vai māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ [ed. dvadaśa, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,3.8 ) saṃvatsaraḥ prajāpatiḥ
(2,3.8 ) prajāpatiryajñaḥ
(2,3.8 ) sa yo'tra bhakṣayedyastaṃ tatra brūyādaśānto bhakṣo'nanuvaṣaṭkṛta ātmānamantaragānna jīviṣyatīti tathā ha syāt [ed.'nānuvaṣaṭkṛta, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,3.8 ) yo vai bhakṣayetprāṇo bhakṣaḥ prāṇa ātmānamantaragāditi tathaiva bhavati
(2,3.8 ) limpedivaivāvajighredatra ca dvidevatyeṣu ceti
(2,3.8 ) tadu tatra śāsanaṃ vedayante
(2,3.8 ) atha yadabhū vyabhicarato nānyonyamanuprapadyete adhvaryū tasmādṛturṛtuṃ nānuprapadyate || 8 ||

(2,3.9 ) prajāpatirvai yatprajā asṛjata tā vai tāntā asṛjata
(2,3.9 ) tā hiṃkāreṇaivābhyajighrat[ed. hiṃkārena, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,3.9 ) tāḥ prajā aśvamāraṃstadbadhyate vā etadyajño yaddhavīṃṣi pacyante yatsomaḥ sūyate yatpaśurālabhyate
(2,3.9 ) hiṃkāreṇa vā etatprajāpatirhatamabhijighrati yajñasyāhatatāyai yajñasyāptyai yajñasya vīryavattāyā iti
(2,3.9 ) tasmādu hiṃkriyate
(2,3.9 ) tasmādu ya eva pitā putrāṇāṃ sūrkṣati sa śreṣṭho bhavati
(2,3.9 ) prajāpatirhi tamabhijighrati
(2,3.9 ) yacchakunirāṇḍamadhyāste yanna sūyate taddhi sāpi hiṃkṛṇoti
(2,3.9 ) atho khalvāhurmaharṣirvā etadyajñasyāgre geyamapaśyat
(2,3.9 ) tadetadyajñasyāgre geyaṃ yaddhiṃkāras
(2,3.9 ) taṃ devāśca ṛṣayaścābruvan vasiṣṭho'yamastu yo no yajñasyāgre geyamadrāgiti
(2,3.9 ) tadetadyajñasyāgre geyaṃ yaddhiṃkāras
(2,3.9 ) tato vai sa devānāṃ śreṣṭho'bhavat
(2,3.9 ) yena vai śreṣṭhastena vasiṣṭhas
(2,3.9 ) tasmādyasmin vāsiṣṭho brāhmaṇaḥ syāttaṃ dakṣiṇāyā nāntarīyāt[ed. syāṃt]
(2,3.9 ) tathā hāsya prīto hiṃkāro bhavati
(2,3.9 ) atha devāśca ha vā ṛṣayaśca yadṛksāme apaśyaṃste ha smaite apaśyan
(2,3.9 ) te yatraite apaśyaṃstata evainaṃ sarvaṃ dohamaduhan
(2,3.9 ) te vā ete dugdhe yātayāme ye ṛksāme
(2,3.9 ) te hiṃkāreṇaivāpyāyete
(2,3.9 ) hiṃkāreṇa vā ṛksāme āpīne yajamānāya dohaṃ duhāte
(2,3.9 ) tasmādu hiṃkṛtyādhvaryavaḥ somamabhiṣuṇvanti
(2,3.9 ) hiṃkṛtyodgātāraḥ sāmnā stuvanti
(2,3.9 ) hiṃkṛtyokthaśa ṛcārtvijyaṃ kurvanti
(2,3.9 ) hiṃkṛtyātharvāṇo brahmatvaṃ kurvanti
(2,3.9ś) tasmādu hiṃkriyate
(2,3.9 a) prajāpatirhi tamabhijighrati
(2,3.9 b) atho khalvāhureko vai prajāpatervrataṃ bibharti gaureva
(2,3.9 c) tadubhaye paśava upajīvanti ye ca grāmyā ye cāraṇyā iti || 9 ||

(2,3.10 ) devaviśaḥ kalpayitavyā ityāhuḥ
(2,3.10 ) chandaśchandasi pratiṣṭhāpyamiti
(2,3.10 ) śaṃsāvomityāhvayate prātaḥsavane tryakṣareṇa
(2,3.10 ) śaṃsāvo daivetyadhvaryuḥ pratigṛṇāti pañcākṣareṇa
(2,3.10 ) tadaṣṭākṣaraṃ saṃpadyate
(2,3.10 ) aṣṭākṣarā vai gāyatrī
(2,3.10 ) gāyatrīmevaitatpurastātprātaḥsavane'cīkḷpatām
(2,3.10 ) ukthaṃ vācītyāha śastvā caturakṣaram
(2,3.10 ) omukthaśā ityadhvaryuḥ pratigṛṇāti caturakṣaram
(2,3.10 ) tadaṣṭākṣaraṃ saṃpadyate
(2,3.10 ) aṣṭākṣarā vai gāyatrī
(2,3.10 ) gāyatrīmevaitadubhayataḥ prātaḥsavane'cīkḷpatām
(2,3.10 ) adhvaryo śaṃsāvomityāhvayate mādhyaṃdine ṣaḍakṣareṇa
(2,3.10 ) śaṃsāvo daivetyadhvaryuḥ pratigṛṇāti pañcākṣareṇa
(2,3.10 ) tadekādaśākṣaraṃ saṃpadyate
(2,3.10 ) ekādaśākṣarā vai triṣṭuptriṣṭubhamevaitatpurastān mādhyaṃdine'cīkḷpatām
(2,3.10 ) ukthaṃ vācīndrāyetyāha śastvā ṣaḍakṣaram
(2,3.10 ) omukthaśā yajetyadhvaryuḥ pratigṛṇāti pañcākṣaram
(2,3.10 ) tadekādaśākṣaraṃ saṃpadyate
(2,3.10 ) ekādaśākṣarā vai triṣṭuptriṣṭubhamevaitadubhayato mādhyaṃdine'cīkḷpatām
(2,3.10 ) adhvaryo śaṃśaṃsāvomityāhvayate tṛtīyasavane saptākṣareṇa
(2,3.10 ) śaṃsavo daivetyadhvaryuḥ pratigṛṇāti pañcākṣaram
(2,3.10 ) taddvādaśākṣaraṃ saṃpadyate
(2,3.10 ) dvādaśākṣarā vai jagatī
(2,3.10 ) jagatīmevaitatpurastāttṛtīyasavane'cīkḷpatām
(2,3.10ś) ukthaṃ vācīndrāya devebhya ityāha śastvā navākṣaram
