(2,3.10 a) omukthaśā ityadhvaryuḥ pratigṛṇāti tryakṣaram
(2,3.10 b) taddvādaśākṣaraṃ saṃpadyate
(2,3.10 c) dvādaśākṣarā vai jagatī
(2,3.10 d) jagatīmevaitadubhayatastṛtīyasavane'cīkḷpatāmiti
(2,3.10 e) etadvai tacchandaśchandasi pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,3.10 f) kalpayatyeva devaviśo ya evaṃ veda
(2,3.10 g) tadapyeṣābhyanūktā yadgāyatre adhi gāyatramāhitamiti || 10 ||

(2,3.11 ) athaitannānā chandāṃsyantareṇa gartā iva
(2,3.11 ) athaite sthaviṣṭhe baliṣṭhe nāntare devate
(2,3.11 ) tābhyāṃ pratipadyate
(2,3.11 ) tadgartaskandaṃ rohasya rūpaṃ svargyam
(2,3.11 ) tadanavānaṃ saṃkrāmet
(2,3.11 ) amṛtaṃ vai praṇavaḥ
(2,3.11 ) amṛtenaiva tan mṛtyuṃ tarati
(2,3.11 ) tadyathā matyena vā vaṃśena vā gartaṃ saṃkrāmedevaṃ tatpraṇavenopasaṃtanoti
(2,3.11 ) brahma ha vai praṇavaḥ
(2,3.11 ) brahmaṇaivāsmai tadbrahmopasaṃtanoti
(2,3.11 ) śuddhaḥ praṇavaḥ syātprajākāmānām
(2,3.11 ) makārāntaḥ pratiṣṭhākāmānām
(2,3.11 ) makārāntaḥ praṇavaḥ syāditi haika āhuḥ
(2,3.11 ) śuddha iti tveva sthitaḥ
(2,3.11 ) mīmāṃsitaḥ praṇavaḥ
(2,3.11 ) athāta iha śuddha iha pūrṇa iti
(2,3.11 ) śuddhaḥ praṇavaḥ syāt
(2,3.11 ) śastrānuvacanayormadhya iti ha smāha kauṣītakis
(2,3.11 ) tathā saṃhitaṃ bhavati
(2,3.11 ) makārānto'vasānārthe
(2,3.11 ) pratiṣṭhā vā avasānam
(2,3.11 ) pratiṣṭhityā eva
(2,3.11 ) athobhayoḥ kāmayorāptyai
(2,3.11 ) etau vai chandaḥpravāhāvavaraṃ chandaḥ paraṃ chando'tipravahatas
(2,3.11 ) tasyāyurna hinasti
(2,3.11ś) chandasāṃ chando'tiproḍhaṃ syāttatraiva yaṃ dviṣyāttaṃ manasā praiva vidhyet
(2,3.11 a) chandasāṃ kṛntatre dravati vā saṃ vā śīryata iti
(2,3.11 b) triḥ prathamāṃ triruttamāmanvāha yajñasyaiva tadbarhiso nahyati
(2,3.11 c) sthemne balāyāvisraṃsāya
(2,3.11 d) yadyapi chandaḥ prātaḥsavane yujyetārdharcaśa eva tasya śaṃsyaṃ gāyatryā rūpeṇa
(2,3.11 e) atho prātaḥsavanarūpeṇeti
(2,3.11 f) na triṣṭubjagatyāvetasmin sthāne'rdharcaśasye yatkiṃ cicchandaḥ prātaḥsavane yujyetāṃ paccha evainayoḥ śasyamiti sā sthitiḥ || 11 ||

(2,3.12 ) athāta ekāhasya prātaḥsavanam
(2,3.12 ) prajāpatiṃ ha vai yajñaṃ tanvānaṃ bahiṣpavamāna eva mṛtyurmṛtyupāśena pratyupākrāmata
(2,3.12 ) sa āgneyyā gāyatryājyaṃ pratyapadyata
(2,3.12 ) mṛtyurvāva taṃ paśyan prajāpatiṃ paryakrāmat
(2,3.12 ) taṃ sāmājyeṣṭhasīdat
(2,3.12 ) sa vāyavyā pra"ugaṃ pratyapadyata
(2,3.12 ) mṛtyurvāva taṃ paśyan prajāpatiṃ paryakrāmat
(2,3.12 ) taṃ mādhyaṃdine pavamāne'sīdat
(2,3.12 ) sa aindryā triṣṭubhā marutvatīyaṃ pratyapadyata
(2,3.12 ) mṛtyurvāva taṃ paśyan prajāpatiṃ paryakrāmat
(2,3.12 ) sa tenaiva draviṇe pūrvo niṣkevalyasya stotriyamāsīdat
(2,3.12 ) tamastṛṇot
(2,3.12 ) tasmādu ya eva pūrvamāsīdati sa tatstṛṇute vidvān
(2,3.12 ) mṛtyuranavakāśamapādravadaśaṃsaditaro niṣkevalyam
(2,3.12 ) tasmādekamevokthaṃ hotā marutvatīyena pratipadyate niṣkevalyameva
(2,3.12 ) atra hi prajāpatiṃ mṛtyurvyajahāt || 12 ||

(2,3.13 ) mitrāvaruṇāvabravīdyuvaṃ na imaṃ yajñasyāṅgamanusamāharataṃ maitrāvaruṇīyām
(2,3.13 ) tathetyabrūtām
(2,3.13 ) tau sayujau sabalau bhūtvā prāsahā mṛtyumatyaitām
(2,3.13 ) tau hyasyaitadyajñasyāṅgamanusamāharatāṃ maitrāvaruṇīyām
(2,3.13 ) tasmān maitrāvaruṇaḥ prātaḥsavane maitrāvaruṇāni śaṃsati
(2,3.13 ) tau hyasyaitadyajñasyāṅgamanusamāharatām
(2,3.13 ) yadveva maitrāvaruṇāni śaṃsati <prati vāṃ sūra udite vidhema namobhirmitrāvaruṇota havyaiḥ [ṛV 7.63.5]><uta vāmuṣaso budhi sākaṃ sūryasya raśmibhiḥ [ṛV 1.137.2]>ityṛcābhyanūktam
(2,3.13 ) <ā no mitrāvaruṇā [ṛV 3.62.16]><ā no gantaṃ riśādasā [ṛV 5.71.1]>iti maitrāvaruṇasya stotriyānurūpau
(2,3.13 ) <pra vo mitrāya gāyata [ṛV 5.68.1]>ityukthamukham
(2,3.13 ) <pra mitrayorvaruṇayoḥ [ṛV 7.66.1]>iti paryāsaḥ
(2,3.13 ) <ā yātaṃ mitrāvaruṇā [ṛV 7.66.19]>iti yajati
(2,3.13 ) ete eva taddevate yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,3.13 ) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.13 ) pratyevābhimṛśante
(2,3.13 ) nāpyāyayanti
(2,3.13 ) na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 13 ||

(2,3.14 ) indramabravīttvaṃ na imaṃ yajñasyāṅgamanusamāhara brāhmaṇācchaṃsīyām
(2,3.14 ) kena saheti
(2,3.14 ) sūryeneti
(2,3.14 ) tathetyabrūtām
(2,3.14 ) tau sayujau sabalau bhūtvā prāsahā mṛtyumatyaitām
(2,3.14 ) tau hyasyaitadyajñasyāṅgamanusamāharatāṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsīyām
(2,3.14 ) tasmādbrāhmaṇācchaṃsī prātaḥsavana aindrāṇi sūryanyaṅgāni śaṃsati
(2,3.14 ) tau hyasyaitadyajñasyāṅgamanusamāharatām
(2,3.14 ) yadvevaindrāṇi sūryanyaṅgāni śaṃsati<indra piba pratikāmaṃ sutasya prātaḥsāvastava hi pūrvapītiḥ [ṛV 10.112.1]>ityṛcābhyanūktam
(2,3.14 ) <ā yāhi suṣumā hi te [ṛV 8.17.1, Vaitṣ21.1]><ā no yāhi sutāvataḥ [ṛV 8.17.4, Vaitṣ21.1]>iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsina stotriyānurūpau
(2,3.14 ) <ayamu tvā vicarṣaṇe [ṛV 8.17.7, Vaitṣ21.2]>ityukthamukham
(2,3.14 ) <udghedabhiśrutāmagham [ṛV 8.93.1, Vaitṣ21.2]> iti paryāsaḥ
(2,3.14 ) <indra kratuvidam [ṛV 3.40.2]> iti yajati
(2,3.14 ) ete eva taddevate yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,3.14 ) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.14 ) pratyevābhimṛśante
(2,3.14 ) nāpyāyayanti
(2,3.14 ) na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 14 ||

(2,3.15 ) indrāgnī abravīdyuvaṃ na imaṃ yajñasyāṅgamanusamāharatamacchāvākīyām
(2,3.15 ) tathetyabrūtām
(2,3.15 ) tau sayujau sabalau bhūtvā prāsahā mṛtyumatyaitām
(2,3.15 ) tau hyasyaitadyajñasyāṅgamanusamāharatāmacchāvākīyām
(2,3.15 ) tasmādacchāvākaḥ prātaḥsavana aindrāgnāni śaṃsati
(2,3.15 ) tau hyasyaitadyajñasyāṅgamanusamāharatām
(2,3.15 ) yadvevaindrāgnāni śaṃsati <prātaryāvabhirā gataṃ devebhirjenyāvasū indrāgnī somapītaye [ṛV 8.38.7]>ityṛcābhyanūktam
(2,3.15 ) <indrāgnī ā gatam [ṛV 3.12.1]><tośā vṛtrahaṇā huve [ṛV 3.12.4]>ityacchāvākasya stotriyānurūpau [ed. toṣā, correctedp. 302]
(2,3.15 ) <indrāgnī apasaspari [ṛV 3.12.7]>ityukthamukham
(2,3.15 ) <ihendrāgnī upahvaye [ṛV 1.21.1]>iti paryāsaḥ
(2,3.15 ) <indrāgnī ā gatam> iti yajati
(2,3.15 ) ete eva taddevate yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,3.15 ) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,3.15 ) pratyevābhimṛśante
(2,3.15 ) nāpyāyayanti
(2,3.15 ) na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 15 ||

(2,3.16 ) atha śaṃsāvomiti stotriyāyānurūpāyokthamukhāya paridhānīyāyā iti catuścaturāhvayante
(2,3.16 ) catasro vai diśaḥ [ed. casasro]
(2,3.16 ) dikṣu tatpratitiṣṭhanti
(2,3.16 ) atho catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ
(2,3.16 ) paśūnāmāptyai
(2,3.16 ) atho catuṣparvāṇo hi prātaḥsavane hotrakās
(2,3.16 ) tasmāccatuḥ sarve gāyatrāṇi śaṃsanti
(2,3.16 ) gāyatraṃ hi prātaḥsavanam
(2,3.16 ) sarve samavatībhiḥ paridadhati
(2,3.16 ) tadyatsamavatībhiḥ paridadhati
(2,3.16 ) anto vai paryāsaḥ
(2,3.16 ) anta udarkaḥ
(2,3.16 ) antenaivāntaṃ paridadhati
(2,3.16 ) sarve madvatībhiryajanti
(2,3.16 ) tadyan madvatībhiryajanti sarve sutavatībhiḥ pītavatībhirabhirūpābhiryajanti
(2,3.16 ) yadyajñe'bhirūpaṃ tatsamṛddham
(2,3.16 ) sarve'nuvaṣaṭkurvanti
(2,3.16 ) sviṣṭakṛtvānuvaṣaṭkāraḥ
(2,3.16 ) netsviṣṭakṛtamantarayāmeti
(2,3.16 ) ayaṃ vai lokaḥ prātaḥsavanam
(2,3.16 ) tasya pañca diśaḥ pañcokthāni prātaḥsavanasya
(2,3.16 ) sa etaiḥ pañcabhirukthairetāḥ pañca diśa āpnotyetāḥ pañca diśa āpnoti || 16 ||

(2,3.17 ) ghnanti vā etatsomaṃ yadabhiṣuṇvanti
(2,3.17 ) yajñaṃ vā etaddhanti yaddakṣiṇā nīyante
(2,3.17 ) yajñaṃ vā etaddakṣayanti
(2,3.17 ) taddakṣiṇānāṃ dakṣiṇātvam
(2,3.17 ) svargo vai loko mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam
(2,3.17 ) yan mādhyaṃdine savane dakṣiṇā nīyante svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
(2,3.17 ) bahu deyam
(2,3.17 ) setuṃ vā etadyajamānaḥ saṃskurute
(2,3.17 ) svargasya lokasyākrāntyai prajākrāntyai
(2,3.17 ) dvābhyāṃ gārhapatye juhoti
(2,3.17 ) adhvaryurasyākrāntenākrāmayati
(2,3.17 ) āgneyyāgnīdhrīye
(2,3.17 ) antarikṣaṃ tena
(2,3.17 ) yan mādhyaṃdine savane dakṣiṇā nīyante svarga etena loke
(2,3.17 ) hiraṇyaṃ haste bhavati
(2,3.17 ) atha nayati
(2,3.17 ) satyaṃ vai hiraṇyam
(2,3.17 ) satyenaivainaṃ tannayati
(2,3.17 ) agreṇa gārhapatyaṃ jaghanena sado'ntarāgnīdhrīyaṃ ca sadaśca
(2,3.17 ) tā udīcīrantarāgnīdhrīyaṃ ca sadaśca cātvālaṃ cotsṛjanti
(2,3.17 ) etena ha sma vā aṅgirasaḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan
(2,3.17 ) tā vā etāḥ panthānamabhivahanti || 17 ||

(2,3.18 ) agnīdhe'gre dadāti
(2,3.18 ) yajñamukhaṃ vā agnīt
(2,3.18 ) yajñamukhenaiva tadyajñamukhaṃ samardhayati
(2,3.18 ) brahmaṇe dadāti
(2,3.18 ) prājāpatyo vai brahmā
(2,3.18 ) prajāpatimeva tayā prīṇāti
(2,3.18 ) ṛtvigbhyo dadāti
(2,3.18 ) hotrā eva tayā prīṇāti
(2,3.18 ) sadasyebhyo dadāti
(2,3.18 ) somapīthaṃ tayā niṣkrīṇīte
(2,3.18 ) na hi tasmā arhati somapīthaṃ tayā niṣkrīṇīyāt
(2,3.18 ) yāṃ śruśruvuṣa ārṣeyāya dadāti devaloke tayārdhnoti
(2,3.18 ) yāmaśruśruvuṣe'nārṣeyāya dadāti manuṣyaloke tayārdhnoti
(2,3.18 ) yāmaprasṛptāya dadāti vanaspatastayā prathante
(2,3.18 ) yāṃ yācamānāya dadāti bhrātṛvyaṃ tayā jinvīte
(2,3.18 ) yāṃ bhīṣā kṣatraṃ tayā brahmātīyāt
(2,3.18 ) yāṃ pratinudante sā vyāghrī dakṣiṇā
(2,3.18 ) yastāṃ punaḥ pratigṛhṇīyādvyāghryenaṃ bhūtvā pravlīnīyāt
(2,3.18 ) anyayā saha pratigṛhṇīyāt
(2,3.18 ) atha hainaṃ na pravlīnāti || 18 ||

(2,3.19 ) yadgāṃ dadāti vaiśvadevī vai gauḥ
(2,3.19 ) viśveṣāmeva taddevānāṃ tena priyaṃ dhāmopaiti
(2,3.19 ) yadajaṃ dadātyāgneyo vā ajaḥ
(2,3.19 ) agnereva tena priyaṃ dhāmopaiti
(2,3.19 ) yadaviṃ dadātyāvyaṃ tenāvajayati
(2,3.19 ) yatkṛtānnaṃ dadāti māṃsaṃ tena niṣkrīṇīte
(2,3.19 ) yadano vā ratho vā dadāti śarīraṃ tena
(2,3.19 ) yadvāso dadāti brṛhaspatiṃ tena
(2,3.19 ) yaddhiraṇyaṃ dadātyāyustena varṣīyaḥ kurute
(2,3.19 ) yadaśvaṃ dadāti sauryo vā aśvaḥ
(2,3.19 ) sūryasyaiva tena priyaṃ dhāmopaiti
(2,3.19 ) antataḥ pratihartre deyam
(2,3.19 ) raudrau vai pratihartā
(2,3.19 ) rudrameva tanniravajayati
(2,3.19 ) yan madhyataḥ pratihartre dadyān madhyato rudramanvavayajet
(2,3.19 ) svarbhānurvā āsuraḥ sūryaṃ tamasāvidhyat
(2,3.19 ) tadatrirapanunoda
(2,3.19 ) tadatriranvapaśyat
(2,3.19 ) yadātreyāya hiraṇyaṃ dadāti tama eva tenāpahate
(2,3.19 ) atho jyotirupariṣṭāddhārayati
(2,3.19 ) svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai || 19 ||

(2,3.20 ) athāta ekāhasyaiva mādhyaṃdinam
(2,3.20 ) ṛkca vā idamagne sāma cāstāṃ
(2,3.20 ) saiva nāmargāsīt
(2,3.20 ) amo nāma sāma
(2,3.20 ) sā vā ṛksāmopāvadan mithunaṃ saṃbhavāva prajātyā iti
(2,3.20 ) netyabravītsāma
(2,3.20 ) jyāyān vā ato mama mahimeti
(2,3.20 ) te dve bhūtvopāvadatām
(2,3.20 ) te na prati cana samavadata
(2,3.20 ) tāstisro bhūtvopāvadan
(2,3.20 ) yattisro bhūtvopāvadaṃstattisṛbhiḥ samabhavat
(2,3.20 ) yattisṛbhiḥ samabhavattasmāttisṛbhiḥ stuvanti
(2,3.20 ) tisṛbhirudgāyanti
(2,3.20 ) tisṛbhirhi sāma saṃmitaṃ bhavati
(2,3.20 ) tasmādekasya bahvyo jāyā bhavanti
(2,3.20 ) na haikasyā bahavaḥ saha patayaḥ
(2,3.20 ) yadvai tatsā cāmaśca samavadatāṃ tatsāmābhavat
(2,3.20 ) tatsāmnaḥ sāmatvam
(2,3.20 ) sāman bhavati
(2,3.20 ) śreṣṭhatāṃ gacchati
(2,3.20 ) yo vai bhavati sa sāman bhavati
(2,3.20 ) asāmanya iti ha nindante
(2,3.20 ) te vai pañcānyadbhūtvā pañcānyadbhūtvākalpetāmāhāvaśca hiṃkāraśca prastāvaśca prathamā cargudgīthaśca madhyamā ca pratīhāraścottamā ca nidhanaṃ ca vaṣaṭkāraśca te yatpañcānyadbhūtvā pañcānyadbhūtvākalpetāṃ tasmādāhuḥ pāṅkto yajñaḥ
(2,3.20 ) pāṅktāḥ paśava iti
(2,3.20 ) yadu virājaṃ daśinīmabhisaṃpadyetāṃ tasmādāhurvirāji yajño daśinyāṃ pratiṣṭhita iti
(2,3.20ś) yadu bṛhatyā pratipadyate bārhato vā eṣa ya eṣa tapati
(2,3.20 a) tadenaṃ svena rūpeṇa samardhayati [ed. rupeṇa]
(2,3.20 b) dve tisraḥ karoti punarādāyam
(2,3.20 c) prajātyai rūpam
(2,3.20 d) dvāvivāgre bhavatas
(2,3.20 e) tata upaprajāyete || 20 ||

(2,3.21 ) ātmā vai stotriyaḥ
(2,3.21 ) prajā anurūpaḥ
(2,3.21 ) patnī dhāyyā
(2,3.21 ) paśavaḥ pragāthaḥ
(2,3.21 ) gṛhāḥ sūktam
(2,3.21 ) yadantarātmaṃstannivit
(2,3.21 ) pratiṣṭhā paridhānīyānnaṃ yājyā
(2,3.21 ) so'smiṃśca loke bhavatyamuṣmiṃśca prajayā ca paśubhiśca gṛheṣu bhavati ya evaṃ veda || 21 ||

(2,3.22 ) stotriyaṃ śaṃsati
(2,3.22 ) ātmā vai stotriyaḥ
(2,3.22 ) sa madhyamayā vācā śaṃstavyaḥ
(2,3.22 ) ātmānamevāsya tatkalpayatyanurūpaṃ śaṃsati
(2,3.22 ) prajā vā anurūpaḥ
(2,3.22 ) tasmātpratirūpamanurūpaṃ kurvanti
(2,3.22 ) pratirūpo haivāsya prajāyāmājāyate nāpratirūpaḥ
(2,3.22 ) tasmātpratirūpamanurūpaṃ kurvanti
(2,3.22 ) sa uccaistarāmiva śaṃstavyaḥ
(2,3.22 ) prajāmevāsya tacchreyasīṃ karoti
(2,3.22 ) dhāyyāṃ śaṃsati
(2,3.22 ) patnī vai dhāyyā
(2,3.22 ) sā nīcaistarāmiva śaṃstavyā
(2,3.22 ) aprativādinī haivāsya gṛheṣu patnī bhavati yatraivaṃvidvānnīcaistarāṃ dhāyyāṃ śaṃsati
(2,3.22 ) pragāthaṃ śaṃsati
(2,3.22 ) paśavo vai pragāthaḥ
(2,3.22 ) saḥ svaravatyā vācā śaṃstavyaḥ
(2,3.22 ) paśavo vai pragāthaḥ
(2,3.22 ) paśavaḥ svaraḥ
(2,3.22 ) paśūnāmāptyai
(2,3.22 ) sūktaṃ śaṃsati
(2,3.22 ) gṛhā vai sūktam
(2,3.22 ) prativītam
(2,3.22 ) tatprativītatamayā vācā śaṃstavyam
(2,3.22 ) sa yadyapi ha dūrātpaśūṃllabhate gṛhānevainānājigamiṣati
(2,3.22ś) gṛhā hi paśūnāṃ pratiṣṭhā
(2,3.22 a) nividaṃ śaṃsati
(2,3.22 b) yadantarātmaṃstannivit
(2,3.22 c) tadevāsya tatkalpayati
(2,3.22 d) paridhānīyāṃ śaṃsati
(2,3.22 e) pratiṣṭhā vai paridhānīyā
(2,3.22 f) pratiṣṭhāyāmevainaṃ tataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,3.22 g) yājyayā yajati
(2,3.22 h) annaṃ vai yājyā
(2,3.22 i) annādyamevāsya tatkalpayati
(2,3.22 j) mūlaṃ vā etadyajñasya yaddhāyyāśca yājyāśca
(2,3.22 k) tadyadanyā anyā dhāyyāśca yājyāśca kuryurunmūlameva tadyajñaṃ kuryustasmāttāḥ samānya eva syuḥ || 22 ||

(2,3.23 ) tadāhuḥ kiṃdevatyo yajña iti
(2,3.23 ) aindra iti brūyāt
(2,3.23 ) aindre vāva yajñe sati yathābhāgamanyā devatā anvāyaṃstāḥ prātaḥsavane marutvatīye tṛtīyasavane ca
(2,3.23 ) atha haitatkevalamevendrasya yadūrdhvaṃ marutvatīyāt
(2,3.23 ) tasmātsarve niṣkevalyāni śaṃsanti
(2,3.23 ) yadeva niṣkevalyāni tatsvargasya lokasya rūpam
(2,3.23 ) yadveva niṣkevalyānyekaṃ ha vā agre savanamāsītprātaḥsavanameva
(2,3.23 ) atha haitaṃ prajāpatirindrāya jyeṣṭhāya putrāyaitatsavanaṃ niramimīta yan mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam
(2,3.23 ) tasmān mādhyaṃdine savane sarve niṣkevalyāni śaṃsanti
(2,3.23 ) yadeva niṣkevalyāni tatsvargasya lokasya rūpam
(2,3.23 ) yadveva niṣkevalyāni yā ha vai devatāḥ prātaḥsavane hotā śaṃsati tāḥ śastvā hotrāśaṃsino'nuśaṃsanti maitrāvaruṇaṃ tṛcaṃ pra'uge hotā śaṃsati
(2,3.23 ) tadubhayaṃ maitrāvaruṇaṃ maitrāvaruṇo'nuśaṃsati
(2,3.23 ) aindraṃ tṛcaṃ pra'uge hotā śaṃsati
(2,3.23 ) tadubhayamaindram
(2,3.23 ) aindraṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsyanuśaṃsati
(2,3.23 ) aindrāgnaṃ tṛcaṃ pra'uge hotā śaṃsati
(2,3.23 ) tadubhayamaindrāgnāgnamacchāvāko'nuśaṃsati
(2,3.23 ) atha haitatkevalamevendrasya yadūrdhvaṃ marutvatīyāt
(2,3.23 ) tasmātsarve niṣkevalyāni śaṃsanti
(2,3.23 ) yadeva niṣkevalyāni tatsvargasya lokasya rūpam
(2,3.23 ) yadveva niṣkevalyāni <yadedadevīrasahiṣṭa māyā athābhavatkevalaḥ somo asya [ṛV 7.98.5, śṣ20.87.5]>ityṛcābhyanūktam
(2,3.23 ) devān ha yajñaṃ tanvānānasurarakṣāṃsyajighāṃsan
(2,3.23 ) te'bruvan vāmadevaṃ tvaṃ na imaṃ yajñaṃ dakṣiṇato gopāyeti
(2,3.23 ) madhyato vasiṣṭham
(2,3.23 ) uttarato bharadvājam
(2,3.23ś) sarvānanu viśvāmitram
(2,3.23 a) tasmān maitrāvaruṇo vāmadevānna pracyavate vasiṣṭhādbrāhmaṇācchaṃsī bharadvājāa acchāvākaḥ sarve viśvāmitrāt
(2,3.23 b) eta evāsmai tadṛṣayo'haraharnamagā apramattā yajñaṃ rakṣanti ya evaṃ veda ya evaṃ veda || 23 ||

(2,3.23 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇottarabhāge tṛtīyaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||

(2,4.1 ) oṃ <kayā naścitra ā bhuvat[ṛV 4.31.1, śṣ20.124.1]> <kayā tvaṃ na ūtyā [ṛV 8.93.19]>iti maitrāvaruṇasya stotriyānurūpau
(2,4.1 ) <kastamindra tvāvasum [ṛV 7.32.14]> iti bārhataḥ pragāthas
(2,4.1 ) tasyopariṣṭādbrāhmaṇam
(2,4.1 ) <sadyo ha jāto vṛṣabhaḥ kanīnaḥ [ṛV 3.48.1]>ityukthamukham
(2,4.1 ) <evā tvāmindra vajrinnatra [ṛV 4.19.1]>iti paryāsaḥ
(2,4.1 ) <uśannu ṣu ṇaḥ sumanā upāke [ṛV 4.20.4]>iti yajati
(2,4.1 ) etāmeva taddevatāṃ yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,4.1 ) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,4.1 ) pratyevābhimṛśante [ed. seemsto give evābhimṛśanta]
(2,4.1 ) nāpyāyayanti
(2,4.1 ) na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 1 ||

(2,4.2 ) <taṃ vo dasmamṛtīṣahaṃ [ṛV 8.88.1, śṣ20.9.1]> <tattvā yāmi suvīryam [ṛV 8.3.9, śṣ20.9.4]> iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsina stotriyānurūpau
(2,4.2 ) <udu tye madhumattamā giraḥ [ṛV 8.3.15, śṣ20.59.1]>iti bārhataḥ pragāthaḥ
(2,4.2 ) paśavo vai pragāthaḥ
(2,4.2 ) paśavaḥ svaraḥ
(2,4.2 ) paśūnāmāptyai
(2,4.2 ) ato madhyaṃ vai sarveṣāṃ chandasāṃ bṛhatī
(2,4.2 ) madhyaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanānām
(2,4.2 ) tan madhyenaiva madhyaṃ samardhayati
(2,4.2 ) <indraḥ pūrbhidātiraddāsamarkaiḥ [ṛV 3.34.1, śṣ20.11.1]>ityukthamukham
(2,4.2 ) <udu brahmāṇyairata śravasyā [ṛV 7.23.1, śṣ20.12.1]>iti paryāsaḥ
(2,4.2 ) <evedindraṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ vajrabāhum [ṛV 7.23.6 , śṣ20.12.6 ]> iti paridadhāti
(2,4.2 ) <vasiṣṭhāso abhyarcantyarkaiḥ [ṛV 7.26.6 , śṣ20.12.6 ]>iti
(2,4.2 ) annaṃ vā arkaḥ
(2,4.2 ) annādyamevāsmai tatparidadhāti
(2,4.2 ) <sa na stuto vīravaddhātu gomat[ṛV 7.26.6 , śṣ20.12.6 ]>iti
(2,4.2 ) annaṃ vā arkaḥiti
(2,4.2 ) prajāṃ caivāsmai tatpaśūṃścāśāste
(2,4.2 ) <yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ [ṛV 7.26.6 , śṣ20.12.6 ]>iti svastimatī rūpasamṛddhā
(2,4.2 ) etadvai yajñasya samṛddhaṃ yadrūpasamṛddhaṃ yatkarma kriyamāṇamṛgyajurvābhivadati
(2,4.2 ) svasti tasya yajñasya pāramaśnute ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvān brāhmaṇācchaṃsyetayā paridadhāti
(2,4.2 ) <ṛjīṣī vajrī vṛṣabhasturāṣāṭ[ṛV 5.40.4, śṣ20.12.7]>iti yajati [ed. ṛjiṣī, correctedp. 302]
(2,4.2 ) etāmeva taddevatāṃ yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,4.2 ) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,4.2 ) pratyevābhimṛśante
(2,4.2 ) nāpyāyayanti
(2,4.2ś) na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 2 ||

(2,4.3 ) <tarobhirvo vidadvasum [ṛV 8.66.1]><taraṇiritsiṣāsati [ṛV 7.32.20]>ityacchāvākasya stotriyānurūpau
(2,4.3 ) <udinnvasya ricyate [ṛV 7.32.12, śṣ20.59.3]>iti bārhataḥ pragāthas
(2,4.3 ) tasyoktaṃ brāhmaṇam
(2,4.3 ) <bhūya idvāvṛdhe vīryāya [ṛV 6.30.1]>ityukthamukham
(2,4.3 ) <imāmū ṣu prabhṛtiṃ sātaye dhāḥ [ṛV 3.36.1]>iti paryāsas
(2,4.3 ) tasya daśamīmuddharati
(2,4.3 ) ghorasya vā āṅgirasasyaitadārṣaṃ nedyajñaṃ nirdahecchasyamānam
(2,4.3 ) <pibā vardhasva tava ghā sutāsaḥ [ṛV 3.36.3]>iti yajati
(2,4.3 ) etāmeva taddevatāṃ yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,4.3 ) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,4.3 ) pratyevābhimṛśante
(2,4.3 ) nāpyāyayanti
(2,4.3 ) na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 3 ||

(2,4.4 ) athādhvaryo śaṃsāvomiti stotriyāyānurūpāya pragāthāyokthamukhāya paridhānīyāyā iti pañca kṛtva āhvayante
(2,4.4 ) pañcapadā paṅktiḥ
(2,4.4 ) pāṅkto yajñaḥ
(2,4.4 ) sarva aindrāṇi traiṣṭubhāni śaṃsanti
(2,4.4 ) aindraṃ hi traiṣṭubhaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam
(2,4.4 ) sarve samavatībhiḥ paridadhati
(2,4.4 ) tadyatsamavatībhiḥ paridadhatyanto vai paryāso'nta udarkaḥ
(2,4.4 ) antenaivāntaṃ paridadhati
(2,4.4 ) sarve madvatībhiryajanti
(2,4.4 ) tadyan madvatībhiryajanti sarve sutavatībhiḥ pītavatībhirabhirūpābhiryajanti
(2,4.4 ) yadyajñe'bhirūpaṃ tatsamṛddham
(2,4.4 ) sarve'nuvaṣaṭkurvanti
(2,4.4 ) sviṣṭakṛtvānuvaṣaṭkāraḥ
(2,4.4 ) netsvikṛta antarayāmeti
(2,4.4 ) antarikṣaloko mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam
(2,4.4 ) tasya pañca diśaḥ
(2,4.4 ) pañcokthāni mādhyaṃdinasya savanasya
(2,4.4 ) sa etaiḥ pañcabhirukthairetāḥ pañca diśa āpnotyetāḥ pañca diśa āpnoti || 4 ||

(2,4.5 ) atha yadaupāsanaṃ tṛtīyasavana upāsyante pitṝneva tena prīṇāti
(2,4.5 ) upāṃśu pātnīvatasyāgnīdhro yajati
(2,4.5 ) reto vai pātnīvataḥ
(2,4.5 ) upāṃśviva vai retaḥ sicyate
(2,4.5 ) tannānuvaṣaṭkaroti nedretaḥ siktaṃ saṃsthāpayānīti
(2,4.5 ) asaṃsthitamiva vai retaḥ siktaṃ samṛddham
(2,4.5 ) saṃsthā vā eṣā yadanuvaṣaṭkāras
(2,4.5 ) tasmānnānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,4.5 ) neṣṭurupasthe dhiṣṇyānte vāsīno bhakṣayati
(2,4.5 ) patnībhājanaṃ vai neṣṭā
(2,4.5 ) agnītpatnīṣu reto dhatte
(2,4.5 ) retasaḥ siktāḥ prajāḥ prajāyante
(2,4.5 ) prajānāṃ prajananāya
(2,4.5 ) prajāvān prajanayiṣṇurbhavati
(2,4.5 ) prajātyai
(2,4.5 ) prajāyate prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 5 ||

(2,4.6 ) atha śākalāñjuhvati
(2,4.6 ) tadyathāhirjīrṇāyāstvaco nirmucyeteṣīkā vā muñjādevaṃ haivaite sarvasmātpāpmanaḥ saṃpramucyante ye śākalāñjuhvati
(2,4.6 ) droṇakalaśe dhānā bhavanti
(2,4.6 ) tāsāṃ hastairādadhati
(2,4.6 ) paśavo vai dhānās
(2,4.6 ) tā āhavanīyasya bhasmānte nivapanti
(2,4.6 ) yonirvai paśūnāmāhapanīyaḥ
(2,4.6 ) sva evaināṃstadgoṣṭhe nirapakrame nidadhati
(2,4.6 ) atha savyāvṛto'psu somānāpyāyayanti
(2,4.6 ) tān hāntarvedyāṃ sādayanti
(2,4.6 ) taddhi somasyāyatanam
(2,4.6 ) cātvālādapareṇādhvaryuścamasānadbhiḥ pūrayitvodīcaḥ prāṇidhāya haritāni tṛṇāni vyavadadhāti
(2,4.6 ) yadā vā āpaścauṣadhayaśca saṃgacchante'tha kṛtsnaḥ somaḥ saṃpadyate
(2,4.6 ) tā vaiṣṇavyarcā ninayanti
(2,4.6 ) yajño vai viṣṇuḥ
(2,4.6 ) yajña evainamantataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,4.6 ) atha yadbhakṣaḥ pratinidhiṃ kurvanti mānuṣenaivainaṃ tadbhakṣeṇa daivaṃ bhakṣamantardadhati || 6 ||

(2,4.7 ) pūtirvā eṣo'muṣmiṃlloke'dhvaryuṃ ca yajamānaṃ cābhivahati
(2,4.7 ) tadyadenaṃ dadhnānabhihutyāvabhṛthamupahareyuryathā kuṇapaṃ vātyevamevainaṃ tatkaroti
(2,4.7 ) atha yadenaṃ dadhnābhihutyāvabhṛthamupaharanti sarvamevainaṃ sayoniṃ saṃtanute
(2,4.7 ) samṛddhiṃ saṃbharanti
(2,4.7 ) <abhūddevaḥ savitā vandyo nu naḥ [ṛV 4.54.1]>iti juhoti
(2,4.7 ) sarvamevainaṃ saparvāṇaṃ saṃbharati [ed. avainaṃ]
(2,4.7 ) tisṛbhis
(2,4.7 ) trivṛddhi yajñaḥ
(2,4.7 ) drapsavatībhirabhijuhoti
(2,4.7 ) sarvamevainaṃ sarvāṅgaṃ saṃbharati
(2,4.7 ) saumībhirabhijuhoti
(2,4.7 ) sarvamevainaṃ sātmānaṃ saṃbharati
(2,4.7 ) pañcabhirabhijuhoti
(2,4.7 ) pāṅkto yajñaḥ
(2,4.7 ) yajñamevāvarunddhe
(2,4.7 ) pāṅktaḥ puruṣaḥ
(2,4.7 ) puruṣamevāpnoti
(2,4.7 ) pāṅktāḥ paśavaḥ
(2,4.7 ) paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati
(2,4.7 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 7 ||

(2,4.8 ) agnirvāva yama iyaṃ yamī
(2,4.8 ) kusīdaṃ vā etadyamasya yajamāna ādatte yadoṣadhībhirvediṃ stṛṇāti
(2,4.8 ) tāṃ yadanupoṣya prayāyādyātayerannenamamuṣmiṃlloke
(2,4.8 ) yame yatkusīdam <apamityamapratīttam [ড়ṣ16.49.10, śṣ6.117.1, Vaitṣ24.15]> iti vedimupoṣati
(2,4.8 ) ihaiva sanyamaṃ kusīdaṃ niravadāyānṛṇo bhūtvā svargaṃ lokameti
(2,4.8 ) <viśvalopa viśvadāvasya tvāsañjuhomi [ṭṣ3.3.8.2, Vaitṣ24.16]>ityāha hotādvā
(2,4.8 ) yajamānasyāparābhāvāya
(2,4.8 ) yadu miśramiva carantyañjalinā saktūn pradāvye juhuyāt
(2,4.8 ) eṣa ha vā agnirvaiśvānaro yatpradāvyaḥ
(2,4.8 ) svasyāmevainaṃ tadyonyāṃ sādayati || 8 ||

(2,4.9 ) ahnāṃ vidhānyāmekāṣṭakāyāmapūpaṃ catuḥśarāvaṃ paktvā prātaretena kakṣamupoṣet
(2,4.9 ) yadi dahati puṇyasamaṃ bhavati
(2,4.9 ) yati na dahati pāpasamaṃ bhavati
(2,4.9 ) etena ha sma vā aṅgirasaḥ purā vijñānena dīrghasatttramupayanti
(2,4.9 ) yo ha vā upadraṣṭāramupaśrotāramanukhyātārameva vidvān yajate samamuṣmiṃlloka iṣṭāpūrtena gacchate
(2,4.9 ) agnirvā upadraṣṭā
(2,4.9 ) vāyurvā upaśrotā
(2,4.9 ) ādityo vā anukhyātā
(2,4.9 ) tān ya evaṃvidvān yajate samamuṣmiṃlloka iṣṭāpūrtena gacchate
(2,4.9 ) <ayaṃ no nabhasaspatiḥ [ড়ṣ19.16.17, śṣ6.79.1, cf. ṭṣ3.3.8.5]>ityāha
(2,4.9 ) agnirvai nabhasaspatiḥ
(2,4.9 ) agnimeva tadāhaitaṃ no gopāyeti
(2,4.9 ) <sa tvaṃ no nabhasaspatiḥ [cf. ṭṣ3.3.8.6]>ityāha
(2,4.9 ) vāyurvai nabhasaspatiḥ
(2,4.9 ) vāyumeva tadāhaitaṃ no gopāyeti
(2,4.9 ) <deva saṃsphāna [ṭṣ3.3.8.6]>ityāha
(2,4.9 ) ādityo vai devaḥ saṃsphānaḥ
(2,4.9 ) ādityameva tadāhaitaṃ no gopāyeti
(2,4.9 ) <ayaṃ te yoniḥ [ড়ṣ3.34.1, śṣ3.20.1]>ityaraṇyoragniṃ samāropayet
(2,4.9 ) tadāhuryadaraṇyoḥ samārūḍho naśyedudasyāgniḥ sīdet
(2,4.9 ) punarādheyaḥ syāditi
(2,4.9 ) yā te agne yajñiyā tanūstayā me hyāroha tayā me hyāviśa
(2,4.9 ) ayaṃ te yonirityātmannagnīn samāropayet
(2,4.9 ) eṣa ha vā agneryoniḥ
(2,4.9 ) svasyāmevainaṃ tadyonyāṃ sādayati || 9 ||

(2,4.10 ) yo ha vā agniṣṭomaṃ sāhnaṃ vedāgniṣṭomasya sāhnasya sāyujyaṃ salokatāmaśnute ya evaṃ veda
(2,4.10 ) yo ha vā eṣa tapatyeṣo'gniṣṭoma eṣa sāhnas
(2,4.10 ) taṃ sahaivāhnā saṃsthāpayeyuḥ
(2,4.10 ) sāhno vai nāmaiṣaḥ
(2,4.10 ) tenāsaṃtvaramāṇāścareyuḥ
(2,4.10 ) yaddha vā idaṃ pūrvayoḥ savanayorasaṃtvaramāṇāścaranti tasmāddhedaṃ prācyo grāmatā bahulāviṣṭāḥ
(2,4.10 ) atha yaddhedaṃ tṛtīyasavane saṃtvaramāṇāścaranti tasmāddhedaṃ pratyañci dīrghāraṇyāni bhavanti
(2,4.10 ) yathaiva prātaḥsavana evaṃ mādhyaṃdine savana evaṃ tṛtīyasavane
(2,4.10 ) evamu ha yajamāno'pramāyuko bhavati
(2,4.10 ) tenāsaṃtvaramāṇāścareyuḥ
(2,4.10 ) yadā vā eṣa prātarudetyatha mandratamaṃ tapati
(2,4.10 ) tasmān mandratamayā vācā prātaḥsavane śaṃset
(2,4.10 ) atha yadābhyetyatha balīyastapati
(2,4.10 ) tasmādbalīyasyā vācā mādhyaṃdine savane śaṃset
(2,4.10 ) atho yadābhitarāmetyatho baliṣṭhatamaṃ tapati
(2,4.10 ) tasmādbaliṣṭhatamayā vācā tṛtīyasavane śaṃset
(2,4.10 ) evaṃ śaṃsedyadi vāca īśīta
(2,4.10 ) vāgghi śastram
(2,4.10 ) yayā tu vacottariṇyottariṇyotsaheta samāpanāya tayā pratipadyeta
(2,4.10 ) etatsuśastataramiva bhavati
(2,4.10 ) sa vā eṣa na kadā canāstamayati nodayati
(2,4.10 ) tadyadenaṃ paścādastamayatīti manyante'hna eva tadantaṃ gatvāthātmānaṃ viparyasyate
(2,4.10 ) aharevādhastātkṛṇute rātrīṃ parastāt
(2,4.10 ) sa vā eṣa na kadā canāstamayati nodayati
(2,4.10 ) tadyadenaṃ purastādudayatīti manyate rātrereva tadantaṃ gatvāthātmānaṃ viparyasyate
(2,4.10ś) rātrimevādhastātkṛṇute'haḥ parastāt
(2,4.10 a) sa vā eṣa na kadā canāstamayati nodayati
(2,4.10 b) na ha vai kadā cana nimrucati
(2,4.10 c) etasya ha sāyujyaṃ salokatāmaśnute ya evaṃ veda || 10 ||

(2,4.11 ) athāta ekāhasyaiva tṛtīyasavanam
(2,4.11 ) devāsurā vā eṣu lokeṣu samayatanta
(2,4.11 ) te devā asurānabhyajayam
(2,4.11 ) te jitā ahorātrayoḥ saṃdhiṃ samabhyavāguḥ
(2,4.11 ) sa hendra uvāceme vā asurā ahorātrayoḥ saṃdhiṃ samabhyavāguḥ
(2,4.11 ) kaścāhaṃ cemānasurānabhyutthāsyāmahā iti
(2,4.11 ) ahaṃ cetyagnirabravīt
(2,4.11 ) ahaṃ ceti varuṇaḥ
(2,4.11 ) ahaṃ ceti bṛhaspatiḥ
(2,4.11 ) ahaṃ ceti viṣṇus
(2,4.11 ) tānabhyutthāyāhorātrayoḥ saṃdhernirjaghnuḥ
(2,4.11 ) yadabhyutthāyāhorātrayoḥ saṃdhernirjaghnustasmādutthā
(2,4.11 ) abhyutthāya ha vai dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ nirhanti ya evaṃ veda
(2,4.11 ) so'gniraśvo bhūtvā prathamaḥ prajigāya
(2,4.11 ) yadagniraśvo bhūtvā prathamaḥ prajigāaya tasmādāgneyībhirukthāni praṇayanti
(2,4.11 ) yadagniraśvo bhūtvā prathamaḥ prajigāya tasmātsākamaśvam
(2,4.11 ) yatpañca devatā abhyuttasthustasmātpañca devatā ukthe śasyante
(2,4.11 ) yā vākso'gniḥ
(2,4.11 ) yaḥ prāṇaḥ sa varuṇaḥ
(2,4.11 ) yan manaḥ sa indraḥ
(2,4.11 ) yaccakṣuḥ sa bṛhaspatiḥ
(2,4.11 ) yacchrotraṃ sa viṣṇuḥ
(2,4.11 ) ete ha vā etān pañcabhiḥ prāṇaiḥ samīryodasthāpayan [ed. samīryudasthāpayan, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,4.11 ) tasmādu evaitāḥ pañca devatā ukthe śasyante || 11 ||

(2,4.12 ) prajāpatirhyetebhyaḥ pañcabhyaḥ prāṇebhyo'nyān devān sasṛje
(2,4.12 ) yadu cedaṃ kiṃ ca pāṅktam
(2,4.12 ) tatsṛṣṭvā vyājvalayat
(2,4.12 ) te hocurdevā mlāno'yaṃ pitā mayobhūḥ [ed. pitāmayo'bhūḥ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,4.12 ) punarimaṃ samīryotthāpayāmeti
(2,4.12 ) sa ha sattvamākhyāyābhyupatiṣṭhate
(2,4.12 ) yadi ha vā api nirṇiktasyaiva kulasya saṃdhyukṣeṇa yajate sattvaṃ haivākhyāyābhyupatiṣṭate
(2,4.12 ) yo vai prajāpatiḥ sa yajñaḥ
(2,4.12 ) sa etaireva pañcabhiḥ prāṇaiḥ samīryotthāpitaḥ
(2,4.12 ) ye ha vā enaṃ pañcabhiḥ prāṇaiḥ samīryotthāpayan
(2,4.12 ) tā u evaitāḥ pañca devatā ukthe śasyante || 12 ||

(2,4.13 ) tadāhuryaddvayordevatayo stuvata indrāgnyorityatha kasmādbhūyiṣṭhā devatā ukthe śasyanta iti [ed. bhūyiṣṭho, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,4.13 ) anto vā āgnimārutamantarukthānyanta āśvinam
(2,4.13 ) kanīyasīṣu devatāsu stuvate tiṣṭheti
(2,4.13 ) atha kasmādbhūyiṣṭho devatā ukthe śasyanta iti
(2,4.13 ) dve dve ukthamukhe bhavatastadyaddve dve || 13 ||

(2,4.14 ) atha yadaindrāvāruṇaṃ maitrāvarusyokthaṃ bhavatyaindrābārhaspatyaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsina ukthaṃ bhavatyaindrāvaiṣṇāvamacchāvākasyokthaṃ bhavati dve saṃśasyaṃsta aindraṃ ca vāruṇaṃ caikamaindrāvāruṇaṃ bhavati
(2,4.14 ) dve saṃśasyaṃsta aindraṃ ca bārhaspatyaṃ caikamaindrābārhaspatyaṃ bhavati
(2,4.14 ) dve saṃśasyaṃsta aindraṃ ca vaiṣṇavaṃ caikamaindrāvaiṣṇavaṃ bhavati
(2,4.14 ) dve dve ukthamukhe bhavatas
(2,4.14 ) tadyaddve dve || 14 ||

(2,4.15 ) atha yadaindrāvaruṇaṃ maitrāvaruṇasyokthaṃ bhavati<indrāvaruṇā sutapāvimaṃ sutaṃ somaṃ pibataṃ madyaṃ dhṛtavratau [śṣ7.58.1, ṛV 6.68.10]>ityṛcābhyanūktam
(2,4.15 ) madvaddhi tṛtīyasavanam
(2,4.15 ) <ehyū ṣu bravāṇi te [ṛV 6.16.16]><āgniragāmi bhārataḥ [ṛV 6.16.19]>iti maitrāvaruṇasya stotriyānurūpau
(2,4.15 ) <carṣaṇīdhṛtaṃ maghavānamukthyam [ṛV 3.51.1]> ityukthamukham
(2,4.15 ) tasyopariṣṭādbrāhmaṇam
(2,4.15 ) <astabhnāddyāmasuro viśvavedāḥ [ṛV 8.42.1]>iti vāruṇaṃ sāṃśaṃsikam [ed. astabhnādyām, correctedp. 302]
(2,4.15 ) ahaṃ ceti varuṇo'bravīt
(2,4.15 ) devatayoḥ saṃśaṃsāyānatiśaṃsāya
(2,4.15 ) <indrāvaruṇā yuvamadhvarāya naḥ [ṛV 7.82.1]>iti paryāsa aindrāvaruṇe
(2,4.15 ) aindrāvaruṇamasyaitannityamuktham
(2,4.15 ) tadetatsvasminnāyatane svasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,4.15 ) dvandvaṃ vā etā devatā bhūtvā vyajayanta
(2,4.15 ) vijityā eva
(2,4.15 ) atho dvandvasyaiva mithunasya prajātyai
(2,4.15 ) saikapādinī bhavati
(2,4.15 ) ekapādinyā hotā paridadhāti
(2,4.15 ) yatra hoturhotrakāṇāṃ yuñjanti tatsamṛddham
(2,4.15 ) tadvai khalv<ā vāṃ rājānāvadhvare vavṛtyām [ṛV 7.84.1]> iti [ed. āvāṃ, correctedp. 303]
(2,4.15 ) evameva kevalaparyāsaṃ kuryātkevalasūktam
(2,4.15 ) kevalasūktamevottarayorbhavati
(2,4.15 ) <indrāvaruṇā madhumattamasya [ড়ṣ20.7.6, śṣ7.58.2, ṛV 6.68.11]>iti yajati
(2,4.15 ) ete eva taddevate yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,4.15 ) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,4.15 ) pratyevābhimṛśante
(2,4.15 ) nāpyāyayanti na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 15 ||

(2,4.16 ) atha yadaindrābārhaspatyaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsina ukthaṃ bhavati<indraśca somaṃ pibataṃ bṛhaspate'smin yajñe mandasānā vṛṣaṇvasū [ṛV 4.50.10]> ityṛcābhyanūktam
(2,4.16 ) madvaddhi tṛtīyasavanam
(2,4.16 ) <vayamu tvāmapūrvya [ṛV 8.21.1, śṣ20.14.1|20.62.1]> <yo na idamidaṃ purā [ṛV 8.21.9, śṣ20.14.3|20.62.3, Vaitṣ25.3]>iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsina stotriyānurūpau
(2,4.16 ) <pra maṃhiṣṭhāya bṛhate bṛhadraye [ṛV 1.57.1, śṣ20.15.1]>ityukthamukham
(2,4.16 ) aindrājāgatam
(2,4.16 ) jāgatāḥ paśavaḥ
(2,4.16 ) paśūnāmāptyai
(2,4.16 ) jāgatamu vai tṛtīyasavanam
(2,4.16 ) tṛtīyasavanasya rūpam
(2,4.16 ) <udapruto na vayo rakṣamāṇāḥ [ṛV 10.68.1, śṣ20.16.1]>iti bārhaspatyaṃ sāṃśaṃsikam
(2,4.16 ) ahaṃ ceti bṛhaspatirabravīt
(2,4.16 ) devatayoḥ saṃśaṃsāyanatiśaṃsāya
(2,4.16 ) <acchā ma indraṃ matayaḥ svarvidaḥ [ṛV 10.43.1, śṣ20.17.1]>iti paryāsa aindrābārhaspatyaḥ
(2,4.16 ) aindrābārhaspatyamasyaitannityamuktham
(2,4.16 ) tadetatsvasminnāyatane svasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,4.16 ) dvandvaṃ vā etā devatā bhūtvā vyajayanta
(2,4.16 ) vijityā eva
(2,4.16 ) atho dvandvasyaiva mithunasya prajātyai
(2,4.16 ) <bṛhaspatirnaḥ pari pātu paścāt[ṛV 10.42|43|44.11 , ড়ṣ15.11.1|16.8.11 , śṣ7.51.1|20.17.11|20.89.11|20.94.11 ]>ityaindrābārhaspatyā paridadhāti
(2,4.16 ) indrābṛhaspatyoreva yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,4.16 ) <utottarasmādadharādaghāyorindraḥ purastāduta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyo varivaḥ kṛṇotu [ṛV 10.42|43|44.11 cd, ড়ṣ15.11.1|16.8.11 cd, śṣ7.51.1|20.17.11|20.89.11|20.94.11 cd]>iti
(2,4.16 ) sarvābhya eva digbhya āśiṣamāśāste nārtvī
(2,4.16 ) yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate so'smai kāmaḥ samṛdhyate ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvān brāhmaṇācchaṃsyetayā paridadhāti [ed. etasyā, corr. ড়tyaḽ ed. brāhmaṇācchaṃy]
(2,4.16 ) <bṛhaspate yuvamindraśca vasvaḥ [ṛV 7.97.10]>iti yajati
(2,4.16 ) ete eva taddevate yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,4.16ś) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,4.16 a) pratyevābhimṛśante
(2,4.16 b) nāpyāyayanti
(2,4.16 c) na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 16 ||

(2,4.17 ) atha yadaindrāvaiṣṇavamacchāvākasyokthaṃ bhavati<indrāviṣṇū madapatī madānāmā somaṃ yātaṃ draviṇo dadhānā [ṛV 6.69.3]>ityṛcābhyanūktam [ed. acchāvākasyoktaṃ]
(2,4.17 ) madvaddhi tṛtīyasavanam
(2,4.17 ) <adhā hīndra girvaṇaḥ [ṛV 8.98.7]><iyaṃ ta indra girvaṇaḥ [ṛV 8.13.4]>ityacchāvākasya stotriyānurūpau
(2,4.17 ) <ṛturjanitrī tasyā apaspari [ṛV 2.13.1]>ityukthamukham
(2,4.17 ) tasyoktaṃ brāhmaṇam
(2,4.17 ) <nū marto dayate saniṣyan [ṛV 7.100.1]>iti vaiṣṇavaṃ sāṃśaṃsikam [ed. sāṃśaṃsikaṃm]
(2,4.17 ) ahaṃ ceti viṣṇurabravīt
(2,4.17 ) devatayoḥ saṃśaṃsāyānatiśaṃsāya
(2,4.17 ) <saṃ vāṃ karmaṇā samiṣā hinomi [ṛV 6.69.1]>iti paryāsa aindrāvaiṣṇavaḥ
(2,4.17 ) aindrāvaiṣṇavamasyaitannityamuktham [ed. ukhaṃ]
(2,4.17 ) tadetatsvasminnāyatane svasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,4.17 ) dvandvaṃ vā etā devatā bhūtvā vyajayanta
(2,4.17 ) vijityā eva
(2,4.17 ) atho dvandvasyaiva mithunasya prajātyai
(2,4.17 ) <ubhā jigyathurna parā jayethe [ṛV 6.69.8, ড়ṣ20.16.3, śṣ7.44.1]> ityaindrāvaiṣṇavyarcā paridadhāti
(2,4.17 ) indrāviṣṇoreva yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,4.17 ) <indrāviṣṇū pibataṃ madhvo asya [ṛV 6.69.7]>iti yajati
(2,4.17 ) ete eva taddevate yathābhāgaṃ prīṇāti
(2,4.17 ) vaṣaṭkṛtyānuvaṣaṭkaroti
(2,4.17 ) pratyevābhimṛśante
(2,4.17 ) nāpyāyayanti
(2,4.17 ) na hyanārāśaṃsāḥ sīdanti || 17 ||

(2,4.18 ) athādhvaryo śaṃśaṃsāvomiti stotriyāyānurūpāyokthamukhāya paridhānīyāyā iti catuścaturāhvayante
(2,4.18 ) catasro vai diśaḥ
(2,4.18 ) dikṣu tatpratitiṣṭhante
(2,4.18 ) atho catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ
(2,4.18 ) paśūnāmāptyai
(2,4.18 ) atho catuṣparvāṇo hi tṛtīyasavane hotrakās
(2,4.18 ) tasmāccatuḥ
(2,4.18 ) sarve traiṣṭubhaṃ jāgatāni śaṃsanti
(2,4.18 ) jāgataṃ hi tṛtīyasavanam
(2,4.18 ) atha haitattraiṣṭubhāni
(2,4.18 ) apratibhūtamiva hi prātaḥsavane marutvatīye tṛtīyasavane ca hotrakāṇāṃ śastram
(2,4.18 ) dhītarasaṃ vā etatsavanaṃ yattṛtīyasavanam
(2,4.18 ) atha haitadadhītarasaṃ śukriyaṃ chando yattriṣṭubayātayāma
(2,4.18 ) savanasyaiva tatsarasatāyai
(2,4.18 ) sarve samavatībhiḥ paridadhati
(2,4.18 ) tadyatsamavatībhiḥ paridadhatyanto vai paryāso'nta udarko'ntaḥ sajāyā u ha vā avaināya
(2,4.18 ) antenaivāntaṃ paridadhati
(2,4.18 ) sarve madvatībhiryajanti
(2,4.18 ) tadyan madvatībhiryajanti sarve sutavatībhiḥ pītavatībhirabhirūpābhiryajanti
(2,4.18 ) yadyajñe'bhirūpaṃ tatsamṛddham
(2,4.18 ) sarve'nuvaṣaṭkurvanti
(2,4.18 ) sviṣṭakṛtvānuvaṣaṭkāraḥ
(2,4.18 ) netsviṣṭakṛta antarayāmeti
(2,4.18 ) asau vai lokastṛtīyasavanam
(2,4.18 ) tasya pañca diśaḥ
(2,4.18ś) pañcokthāni tṛtīyasavanasya
(2,4.18 a) sa etaiḥ pañcabhirukthairetāḥ pañca diśa āpnoti
(2,4.18 b) tadyadeṣāṃ lokānāṃ rūpaṃ yā mātrā tena rūpeṇa tayā mātrayemāṃllokānṛdhnotīmāṃllokānṛdhnotīti || 18 ||

(2,4.19 ) tadāhuḥ kiṃ ṣoḍaśinaḥ ṣoḍaśitvam
(2,4.19 ) ṣoḍaśa stotrāṇi ṣoḍaśa śastrāṇi ṣoḍaśabhirakṣarairādatte
(2,4.19 ) dve vā akṣare atiricyete ṣoḍaśino'nuṣṭubhamabhisaṃpannasya
(2,4.19 ) vāco vā etau stanau
(2,4.19 ) satyānṛte vāva te
(2,4.19 ) avatyenaṃ satyaṃ nainamanṛtaṃ hinasti ya evaṃ veda ya evaṃ veda || 19 || [ed. veda ya evaṃ vei]

(2,4.19 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇottarabhāge caturthaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||


(2,5.1 ) omaharvai devā āśrayanta rātrīmasurās
(2,5.1 ) te samāvadvīryā evāsan
(2,5.1 ) no vyāvartanta
(2,5.1 ) so'bravīdindraḥ kaścāhaṃ cemānasurān rātrīmanvaiṣyāvahā iti [ed. anvaiṣyāmaha, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,5.1 ) sa deveṣu na pratyavindat
(2,5.1 ) abibhayū rātrestamaso mṛtyos
(2,5.1 ) tama iva hi rātriḥ
(2,5.1 ) mṛtyurvai tamas
(2,5.1 ) tasmāddhāpyetarhi bhūyāniva naktaṃ sa yāvan mātramivāpakramya bibheti
(2,5.1 ) taṃ vai chandāṃsyevānvavāyan
(2,5.1 ) tadyacchandāṃsyevānvavāyaṃstasmādindraśca chandāṃsi ca rātriṃ vahanti
(2,5.1 ) na nivicchasyate na puroruṅna dhāyyā nānyā devatā
(2,5.1 ) indraśca hyeva chandāṃsi ca rātriṃ vahanti
(2,5.1 ) tān vai paryāyaiḥ paryāyamanudanta
(2,5.1 ) yatparyāyaiḥ paryāyamanudanta tasmātparyāyās
(2,5.1 ) tatparyāyāṇāṃ paryāyatvam
(2,5.1 ) tān vai prathamaireva paryāyaiḥ pūrvarātrādanudanta madhyamairmadhyarātrāduttamairapararātrāt
(2,5.1 ) apiśarvaryā apismasītyabruvan
(2,5.1 ) tadyadapiśarvaryā apismasītyabruvaṃstadapiśarvarāṇāmapiśarvaratvam
(2,5.1 ) śarvarāṇi khalu ha vā asyaitāni chandāṃsīti ha smāha
(2,5.1 ) etāni ha vā indraṃ rātryāstamaso mṛtyorabhipatyāvārayan
(2,5.1 ) tadapiśarvarāṇāmapiśarvaratvam || 1 ||

(2,5.2 ) prathameṣu paryāyeṣu stuvate prathamānyeva padāni punarādadate
(2,5.2 ) yadevaiṣāṃ manorathā āsaṃstadevaiṣāṃ tenādadate
(2,5.2 ) madhyameṣu paryāyeṣu stuvate madhyamānyeva padāni punarādadate
(2,5.2 ) yadevaiṣāmaśvā gāva āsaṃstadevaiṣāṃ tenādadate
(2,5.2 ) uttameṣu paryāyeṣu stuvata uttamānyeva padāni punarādadate
(2,5.2 ) yadevaiṣāṃ vāso hiraṇyaṃ maṇiradhyātmamāsīttadevaiṣāṃ tenādadate
(2,5.2 ) ā dviṣato vasu datte nirevainamebhyaḥ sarvebhyo lokebhyo nudate ya evaṃ veda || 2 ||

(2,5.3 ) pavamānavadaharityāhurna rātriḥ pavamānavatī
(2,5.3 ) kathamubhe pavamānavatī bhavataḥ
(2,5.3 ) kena te samāvadbhājau bhavata iti
(2,5.3 ) yadevendrāya madvane sutamidaṃ vaso sutamandha idaṃ hyanvojasā sutamiti stuvanti ca śaṃsanti ca tena rātriḥ pavamānavatī
(2,5.3 ) tenobhe pavamānavatī bhavatas
(2,5.3 ) tena te samāvadbhājau bhavataḥ
(2,5.3 ) pañcadaśastotramaharityāhurna rātriḥ pañcadaśastotrā
(2,5.3 ) kathamubhe pañcadaśastotre bhavataḥ
(2,5.3 ) kena te samāvadbhājau bhavata iti
(2,5.3 ) dvādaśa stotrāṇyapiśarvarāṇi
(2,5.3 ) tisṛbhirdevatābhiḥ saṃdhinā rāthaṃtareṇāśvināya stuvate
(2,5.3 ) tena rātriḥ pañcadaśastotrā
(2,5.3 ) tenobhe pañcadaśastotre bhavatas
(2,5.3 ) tena te samāvadbhājau bhavataḥ
(2,5.3 ) parimitaṃ stuvantyaparimitamanuśaṃsanti
(2,5.3 ) parimitaṃ bhūtamaparimitaṃ bhavyam
(2,5.3 ) aparimitānyevāvarundhyādityatiśaṃsati stomam
(2,5.3 ) ati vai prajāsyātmānamati paśavas
(2,5.3 ) tadyadevāsyātyātmānaṃ tadevāsyaitenāpyāyayanti
(2,5.3 ) atho dvayaṃ vā idaṃ sarvaṃ snehaścaiva tejaśca
(2,5.3 ) atha tadahorātrābhyāmāptam
(2,5.3 ) snehatejasorāptyai
(2,5.3 ) gāyatrān stotriyānurūpāñchaṃsanti
(2,5.3 ) tejo vai gāyatrī
(2,5.3 ) tamaḥ pāpmā rātris
(2,5.3ś) tena tejasā tamaḥ pāpmānaṃ taranti
(2,5.3 a) punarādāyaṃ śaṃsanti
(2,5.3 b) evaṃ hi sāmagā stuvate
(2,5.3 c) yathā stutamanuśastaṃ bhavati
(2,5.3 d) na hi tatstutaṃ yannānuśastam
(2,5.3 e) tadāhuratha kasmāduttamātpratīhārādāhūya sāmnā śastramupasaṃtanvantīti || 3 ||

(2,5.4 ) puruṣo vai yajñas
(2,5.4 ) tasya śira eva havirdhānam
(2,5.4 ) mukhamāhavanīyaḥ
(2,5.4 ) udaraṃ sadaḥ
(2,5.4 ) annamukthāni
(2,5.4 ) bāhū mārjālīyaścāgnīdhrīyaśca
(2,5.4 ) yā imā devatāste'ntaḥsadasaṃ dhiṣṇyāḥ
(2,5.4 ) pratiṣṭhe gārhapatyavrataśrapaṇāvityathāparam
(2,5.4 ) tasya mana eva brahmā
(2,5.4 ) prāṇa udgātā
(2,5.4 ) apānaḥ prastotā
(2,5.4 ) vyānaḥ pratihartā
(2,5.4 ) vāgghotā
(2,5.4 ) cakṣuradhvaryuḥ
(2,5.4 ) prajāti sadasyaḥ
(2,5.4 ) aṅgāni hotrāśaṃsinaḥ
(2,5.4 ) ātmā yajamānas
(2,5.4 ) tadyadadhvaryuḥ stotramupākaroti somaḥ pavata iti cakṣureva tatprāṇaiḥ saṃdadhāti
(2,5.4 ) atha yatprastotā brahmāṇamāmantrayate brahmantstoṣyāmaḥ praśāstariti mano'graṇīrbhavati
(2,5.4 ) eteṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ manasā hi prasūtā stomena stuyāmeti
(2,5.4 ) prāṇāneva tan manasā saṃdadhāti
(2,5.4 ) atha yadbrahmā stutetyuccairanujānāti mano vai brahmā
(2,5.4 ) mana eva tatprāṇaiḥ saṃdadhāti
(2,5.4 ) atha yatprastotā prastautyapānameva tatprāṇaiḥ saṃdadhāti
(2,5.4 ) atha yatpratihartā pratiharati vyānameva tadapānaiḥ saṃdadhāti
(2,5.4ś) atha yadudgātodgāyati samānameva tatprāṇaiḥ saṃdadhāti
(2,5.4 a) atha yaddhotā sāmnā śastramupasaṃtanoti vāgvai hotā vācameva tatprāṇaiḥ saṃdadhāti
(2,5.4 b) atha yatsadasyo brahmāṇamupāsīdati prajātirvai sadasyaḥ prajātimevāpnoti
(2,5.4 c) atha yaddhotrāśaṃsinaḥ sāmasaṃtatiṃ kurvantyaṅgāni vai hotrāśaṃsino'ṅgānyevāsya tatprāṇaiḥ saṃdadhāti
(2,5.4 d) atha yadyajamānaḥ stotramupāsīdatyātmā vai yajamāna ātmānamevāsya tatkalpayati
(2,5.4 e) tasmānnainaṃ bahirvedyabhyāśrāvayet
(2,5.4 f) nābhyudiyāt
(2,5.4 g) nābhyastamiyāt
(2,5.4 h) nādhiṣṇye pratapet
(2,5.4 i) netprāṇebhya ātmānamantaragāditi || 4 ||

(2,5.5 ) prathameṣu paryāyeṣu stuvate prathameṣu padeṣu ninardayanti prathamarātrādeva tadasurānnirghnanti
(2,5.5 ) madhyameṣu paryāyeṣu stuvate madhyameṣu padeṣu ninardayanti
(2,5.5 ) madhyamarātrādeva tadasurānnirghnanti
(2,5.5 ) uttameṣu paryāyeṣu stuvata uttameṣu padeṣu ninardayanti
(2,5.5 ) uttamarātrādeva tadasurānnirghnanti
(2,5.5 ) tadyathābhyāgāraṃ punaḥpunaḥ pāpmānaṃ nirharantyevamevaitatstotriyānurūpābhyāmahorātrābhyāmeva tadasurānnirghnanti
(2,5.5 ) gāyatrīṃ śaṃsanti tejo vai brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī
(2,5.5 ) teja evāsmai tadbrahmavarcasaṃ yajamāne dadhati
(2,5.5 ) gāyatrīḥ śastvā jagatīḥ śaṃsanti
(2,5.5 ) brahma ha vai jagatī
(2,5.5 ) brahmaṇaivāsmai tadbrahmavarcasaṃ yajamāne dadhati
(2,5.5 ) vyāhvayante gāyatrīśca jagatīścāntareṇa
(2,5.5 ) chandāṃsyeva tannānāvīryāṇi kurvanti
(2,5.5 ) jagatīḥ śastvā triṣṭubhaḥ śaṃsanti
(2,5.5 ) paśavo vai jagatī
(2,5.5 ) paśūneva tattriṣṭubbhiḥ paridadhati
(2,5.5 ) balaṃ vai vīryaṃ triṣṭup
(2,5.5 ) balameva tadvīrye'ntataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,5.5 ) andhasvatyo madvatyaḥ sutavatyaḥ pītavatyastriṣṭubho yājyāḥ samṛddhāḥ sulakṣaṇāḥ
(2,5.5 ) etadvai rātrīrūpam
(2,5.5 ) jāgṛyādrātrim
(2,5.5 ) yāvadu ha vai na vā stuvate na vā śasyate tāvadīśvarā asurā rakṣāṃsi ca yajñamanvavanayanti
(2,5.5 ) tasmādāhavanīyaṃ samiddhamāgnīdhrīyaṃ gārhapatyaṃ dhiṣṇyān samujjvalayateti bhāṣeran
(2,5.5 ) jvalayeran
(2,5.5 ) prakāśamivaiva tatsyāt
(2,5.5ś) ārebhantaḥ śayīran
(2,5.5 a) tān ha taḥ śreṣṭho vā iti pāpmā nābhivṛkṇoti
(2,5.5 b) te tamaḥ pāpmānamapāghnate te tamaḥ pāpmānamapāghnate || 5 ||

(2,5.6 ) viśvarūpaṃ vai tvāṣṭramindro'han
(2,5.6 ) sa tvaṣṭā hataputro'bhicaraṇīyamapendraṃ somamāharat
(2,5.6 ) tasyendro yajñaveśasaṃ kṛtvā prāsahā somamapibat
(2,5.6 ) sa viṣvaṅvyārchat
(2,5.6 ) tasmātsomo nānupahūtena [na] pātavyaḥ
(2,5.6 ) somapītho'sya vyardhuko bhavati
(2,5.6 ) tasya mukhātprāṇebhyaḥ śrīryaśāṃsyūrdhvānyudakrāman [ed. praṇebhyaḥ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,5.6 ) tāni paśūn prāviśan
(2,5.6 ) tasmātpaśavo yaśaḥ
(2,5.6 ) yaśo ha bhavati ya evaṃ veda
(2,5.6 ) tato'smā etadaśvinau ca sarasvatī ca yajñaṃ samabharantsautrāmaṇiṃ bhaiṣajyāya
(2,5.6 ) tayendramabhyaṣiñcan
(2,5.6 ) tato vai sa devānāṃ śreṣṭho'bhavat
(2,5.6 ) śreṣṭhaḥ svānāṃ cānyeṣāṃ ca bhavati ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvāntsautrāmaṇyābhiṣicyate || 6 ||

(2,5.7 ) atha sāma gāyati brahmā
(2,5.7 ) kṣatraṃ vai sāma
(2,5.7 ) kṣatreṇaivainaṃ tadabhiṣiñcati
(2,5.7 ) atho sāmrājyaṃ vai sāma
(2,5.7 ) sāmrājyenaivainaṃ tatsāmrājyaṃ gamayati
(2,5.7 ) atho sarveṣāṃ vā eṣa vedānāṃ raso yatsāma
(2,5.7 ) sarveṣāmeva tadvedānāṃ rasenābhiṣiñcati
(2,5.7 ) bṛhatyāṃ gāyati
(2,5.7 ) bṛhatyāṃ vā asāvādityaḥ śriyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ pratiṣṭhitastapati
(2,5.7 ) aindryāṃ bṛhatyāṃ gāyati
(2,5.7 ) aindro vā eṣa yajñakraturyatsautrāmaṇiḥ
(2,5.7 ) indrāyatana eṣa etarhi yo yajate
(2,5.7 ) sva evainaṃ tadāyatane prīṇāti
(2,5.7 ) atha kasmātsaṃśānāni nāma
(2,5.7 ) etairvai sāmabhirdevā indramindriyeṇa vīryeṇa samaśyan
(2,5.7 ) tathaivaitadyajamānā etaireva sāmabhir
(2,5.7 ) indriyeṇaiva vīryeṇa saṃśyanti [ed. vīryena]
(2,5.7 ) saṃśravase viśravase satyaśravase śravasa iti sāmāni bhavanti
(2,5.7 ) eṣvevainaṃ lokeṣu pratiṣṭhāpayati caturnidhanaṃ bhavati
(2,5.7 ) catasro vai diśaḥ
(2,5.7 ) dikṣu tatpratitiṣṭhante
(2,5.7 ) atho catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ
(2,5.7 ) paśūnāmāptyai
(2,5.7 ) tadāhuryadetatsāma gīyate'tha kvaitasya sāmna ukthaṃ kā pratiṣṭhā
(2,5.7 ) trayā devā ekādaśetyāhuḥ
(2,5.7ś) etadvā etasya sāmna ukthameṣā pratiṣṭhā
(2,5.7 a) trayastriṃśaṃ grahaṃ gṛhṇāti
(2,5.7 b) sāmnaḥ pratiṣṭhāyai pratiṣṭhāyai || 7 ||

(2,5.8 ) prajāpatirakāmayata vājamāpnuyāṃ svargaṃ lokamiti
(2,5.8 ) sa etaṃ vājapeyamapaśyat
(2,5.8 ) vājapeyo vā eṣa ya eṣa tapati
(2,5.8 ) vājametena yajamānaḥ svargaṃ lokamāpnoti
(2,5.8 ) śukravatyo jyotiṣmatyaḥ prātaḥsavane bhavanti
(2,5.8 ) tejo brahmavarcasaṃ tābhirāpnoti
(2,5.8 ) vājavatyo mādhyaṃdine savane
(2,5.8 ) svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
(2,5.8 ) annavatyo gaṇavatyaḥ paśumatyastṛtīyasavane bhavanti
(2,5.8 ) bhūmānaṃ tābhirāpnoti
(2,5.8 ) sarvaḥ saptadaśo bhavati
(2,5.8 ) prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ
(2,5.8 ) prajāpatimevāpnoti
(2,5.8 ) hiraṇyasraja ṛtvijo bhavanti
(2,5.8 ) mahasa eva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
(2,5.8 ) eṣa me'muṣmiṃlloke prakāśo'saditi
(2,5.8 ) jyotirvai hiraṇyam
(2,5.8 ) jyotiṣaivainamantardadhati
(2,5.8 ) ājiṃ dhāvanti
(2,5.8 ) yajamānamujjāpayanti
(2,5.8 ) nākaṃ rohati samahase rohati viśvamahase rohati sarvamahase rohati
(2,5.8 ) manuṣyalokādevainamantardadhati
(2,5.8 ) devasya savituḥ save svargaṃ lokaṃ varṣiṣṭhaṃ nākaṃ roheyamiti brahmā rathacakraṃ sarpati
(2,5.8 ) savitṛprasūta evainaṃ tatsarpati
(2,5.8 ) atho prajāpatirvai brahmā
(2,5.8ś) prajāpatimevainaṃ vajrādadhiprasuvati
(2,5.8 a) nākasyojjityai
(2,5.8 b) vājināṃ saṃtatyai
(2,5.8 c) vājisāmābhigāyati
(2,5.8 d) vājimān bhavati
(2,5.8 e) vājo vai svargaṃ lokaḥ
(2,5.8 f) svargameva tallokaṃ rohati
(2,5.8 g) viṣṇoḥ śipiviṣṭavatīṣu bṛhaduttamaṃ bhavati
(2,5.8 h) svargameva tallokaṃ rūḍhvā bradhnasya viṣṭapamatikrāmatyatikrāmati || 8 ||

(2,5.9 ) athāto'ptoryāmā
(2,5.9 ) prajāpatirvai yatprajā asṛjata tā vai tāntā asṛjata
(2,5.9 ) tāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ parācya evāsan
(2,5.9 ) nopāvartanta
(2,5.9 ) tā ekena stomenopāgṛhṇāt
(2,5.9 ) tā atyaricyanta
(2,5.9 ) tā dvābhyām
(2,5.9 ) tāḥ sarvais
(2,5.9 ) tasmātsarvastomas
(2,5.9 ) tā ekena pṛṣṭhenopāgṛhṇāt
(2,5.9 ) tā atyaricyanata
(2,5.9 ) tā dvābhyām
(2,5.9 ) tāḥ sarvais
(2,5.9 ) tasmātsarvapṛṣṭhas
(2,5.9 ) tā atiriktokthe vāravantīyenāvārayan
(2,5.9 ) tasmādeṣo'tiriktokthavān bhavati
(2,5.9 ) tasmādvāravantīyam
(2,5.9 ) tā yadāptvāyacchadato vā aptoryāmā
(2,5.9 ) atho prajā vā apturityāhuḥ
(2,5.9 ) prajānāṃ yamana iti haivaitaduktaṃ tā barhiḥ prajā aśnāyeran [ed. prajāśnāyeraṃs, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,5.9 ) tarhi haitaina yajate
(2,5.9 ) sa eṣo'ṣṭāpṛṣṭho bhavati
(2,5.9 ) tadyathānyasmin yajñe viśvajitaḥ pṛṣṭhamanu saṃcaraṃ bhavati kathametadevamatreti
(2,5.9 ) pitaiṣa yajñānām
(2,5.9 ) tadyathā śreṣṭhini saṃvaśreyurapi vidviṣāṇā evamevaitacchreṣṭhino vaśeyānnamannasyānucaryāya kṣamante || 9 ||

(2,5.10 ) tadyathaivādo'hna ukthānāmāgneyaṃ prathamaṃ bhavatyevamevaitadatrāpyāgneyaṃ prathamaṃ bhavati
(2,5.10 ) aindre vāva tatrottare [ed. aidre]
(2,5.10 ) aindre vā ete
(2,5.10 ) aindrāvaiṣṇavamacchāvākasyokthaṃ bhavati
(2,5.10 ) caturāhāvānyatiriktokthāni bhavanti
(2,5.10 ) paśavo     vā ukthāni
(2,5.10 ) catuṣṭayā vai paśavaḥ
(2,5.10 ) atho catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ
(2,5.10 ) paśūnāmāptyai
(2,5.10 ) ta ete stotriyānurūpāstṛcā ardharcaśasyāḥ
(2,5.10 ) pratiṣṭhā vā ardharcaḥ
(2,5.10 ) pratiṣṭhityā eva
(2,5.10 ) athaiteṣāmevāśvinānāṃ sūktānāṃ dve dve samāhāvamekaikamaharahaḥ śaṃsati
(2,5.10 ) aśvinau vai devānāṃ bhiṣajau
(2,5.10 ) tasmādāśvināni sūktāni śaṃsati
(2,5.10 ) tadaśvibhyāṃ pradaduridaṃ bhiṣajyatamiti
(2,5.10 ) kṣaitrapatyāḥ paridhānīyā bhavanti
(2,5.10 ) yatra hatastatprajā aśanāyantīḥ pipāsantīḥ saṃruddhā sthitā āsaṃstā dīnā etābhiryathākṣetraṃ pāyayāṃ cakāra tarpayāṃ cakāra [ed. ha tastat, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,5.10 ) atho iyaṃ vai kṣetraṃ pṛthivī
(2,5.10 ) asyāmadīnāyāmantataḥ pratiṣṭhāsyāmaha iti
(2,5.10 ) triṣṭubho yājyā bhavanti
(2,5.10 ) yatra hatastatprajā aśanāyantīḥ pipāsantīḥ saṃruddhā sthitā babhūvustā haivainā etābhiryathaukasaṃ vyavasāyayāṃ cakāra
(2,5.10 ) tasmādetā yājyā bhavanti tasmādetā yājyā bhavanti || 10 ||

(2,5.11 ) athāto'naikāhikam [ed. athato]
(2,5.11 ) śvastotriyamadyastotriyasyānurūpaṃ kurvanti prātaḥsavane
(2,5.11 ) ahīnameva tatsaṃtanvanti
(2,5.11 ) ahīnasya saṃtatyai
(2,5.11 ) tadyathā ha vā ekāhaḥ suta evamahīnaḥ sutas
(2,5.11 ) tadyathaikāhasya sutasya savanāni saṃtiṣṭhamānāni yantyevamahīnasya sutasyāhāni saṃtiṣṭhamānāni yanti
(2,5.11 ) tadyacchvastotriyamadyastotriyasyānurūpaṃ kurvanti prātaḥsavane
(2,5.11 ) aharevaṃ tadahno'nurūpaṃ kurvanti
(2,5.11 ) apareṇaiva tadahnāparamaharabhyārabhante tat
(2,5.11 ) tathā na mādhyaṃdine savane
(2,5.11 ) śrīrvai pṛṣṭhāni
(2,5.11 ) tāni tasminnevāvasthitāni bhavanti
(2,5.11 ) etenaiva vidhinā tṛtīyasavane
(2,5.11 ) na śvastotriyamadyastotriyasyānurūpaṃ kurvanti || 11 ||

(2,5.12 ) athāta ārambhaṇīyā eva
(2,5.12 ) <ṛjunītī no varuṇa> iti maitrāvaruṇasya <mitro nayatu vidvān [ṛV 1.90.1]> iti
(2,5.12 ) praṇetā vā eṣa hotrakāṇāṃ yan maitrāvaruṇas
(2,5.12 ) tasmādeṣā praṇetṛmatī bhavati [ed. praṇetrimatī, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,5.12 ) <indraṃ vo viśvataspari [ṛV 1.7.10 , śṣ20.39.1|20.70.16 ]> iti brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ
(2,5.12 ) <havāmahe janebhyaḥ [ṛV 1.7.10 , śṣ20.39.1|20.70.16 ]>itīndramevaitayāharaharnirhvayante
(2,5.12 ) na haivaiṣāṃ vihave'nya indraṃ vṛṅkte yatraivaṃvidvān brāhmaṇācchaṃsyetāmaharahaḥ śaṃsati
(2,5.12 ) <yatsoma ā sute naraḥ [ṛV 7.94.10 ]>ityacchāvākasya
(2,5.12 ) <indrāgnī ajohavuḥ [ṛV 7.94.10 ]>itīndrāgnī evaitayāharaharnirhvayante
(2,5.12 ) na haivaiṣāṃ vihave'nya indrāgnī vṛṅkte yatraivaṃvidvānacchāvāka etāmaharahaḥ śaṃsati
(2,5.12 ) tā vā etāḥ svargasya lokasya nāvaḥ saṃtāraṇyaḥ
(2,5.12 ) svargamevaitābhirlokamanusaṃtaranti || 12 ||

(2,5.13 ) athātaḥ paridhānīyā eva
(2,5.13 ) <te syāma deva varuṇa [ṛV 7.66.9 ]>iti maitrāvaruṇasya
(2,5.13 ) <iṣaṃ svaśca dhīmahi [ṛV 7.66.9 ]>ityayaṃ vai loka iṣamityasau lokaḥ svariti

(2,5.15 ) okaḥsārī vā indraḥ

(2,6.3 b) samānaṃ hi chandaḥ

(2,6.5 ) aparimitābhiruttarayoḥ savanayoraparimito vai svargo lokaḥ
(2,6.5 ) svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
(2,6.5 ) tadyathābhiheṣate pipāsate kṣipraṃ prayacchettādṛktat
(2,6.5 ) samānībhiḥ paridadhyuḥ
(2,6.5 ) saṃtato haivaiṣāmārabdho'visrasto yajño bhavati
(2,6.5 ) saṃtatamṛcā vaṣaṭkṛtyam
(2,6.5 ) saṃtatyai saṃdhīyate prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 5 ||

(2,6.6 ) tadāhuḥ kathaṃ dvyuktho hotaikasūkta ekokthā hotrā dvisūktā iti
(2,6.6 ) asau vai hotā yo'sau tapati
(2,6.6 ) sa vā eka eva
(2,6.6 ) tasmādekasūktaḥ
(2,6.6 ) sa yadvidhyāto dvāvivābhavati
(2,6.6 ) teja eva maṇḍalaṃ bhā aparaṃ śuklamaparaṃ kṛṣṇam
(2,6.6 ) tasmāda dvyukthaḥ
(2,6.6 ) raśmayo vāva hotrās
(2,6.6 ) te vā ekaikam
(2,6.6 ) tasmādekokthās
(2,6.6 ) tadyadekaikasya raśmerdvaudvau varṇau bhavatastasmāddvisūktāḥ
(2,6.6 ) saṃvatsaro vāva hotā
(2,6.6 ) sa vā eka eva
(2,6.6 ) tasmādekasūktas
(2,6.6 ) tasya yaddvayānyahāni bhavanti śītānyanyānyuṣṇānyanyāni tasmāddvyukthaḥ
(2,6.6 ) ṛtavo vāva hotrās
(2,6.6 ) te vā ekaikam
(2,6.6 ) tasmādekokthās
(2,6.6 ) tadyadekaikasyartau dvaudvau māsau bhavatastasmāddvisūktāḥ
(2,6.6 ) puruṣo vāva hotā
(2,6.6 ) sa vā eka eva
(2,6.6 ) tasmādekasūktaḥ
(2,6.6 ) sa yatpuruṣo bhavatyanyathaiva pratyaṅbhavatyanyathā prāṅtasmāddvyukthaḥ
(2,6.6 ) aṅgāni vāva hotrās
(2,6.6 ) tāni vā ekaikam
(2,6.6ś) tasmādekokthās
(2,6.6 a) tadyadekaikamaṅgaṃ dyutirbhavati tasmāddvisūktās
(2,6.6 b) tadāhuryaddvyuktho hotaikasūkta ekokthā hotrā dvisuktāḥ kathaṃ tatsamaṃ bhavati
(2,6.6 c) yadeva dvidevatyābhiryajantyatho yaddvisūktā hotrā iti brūyāt
(2,6.6 d) tadāhuryadagniṣṭoma eva sati yajñe dve hoturukthe atiricyete kathaṃ tato hotrā na vyavacchidyanta iti
(2,6.6 e) yadeva dvidevatyābhiryajantyatho yaddvisūktā hotrā iti brūyāt
(2,6.6 f) tadāhuryadagniṣṭoma eva sati yajñe sarvā devatāḥ sarvāṇi chandāṃsyāpyāyayanti
(2,6.6 g) atha katamena chandasāyātayāmānyukthāni praṇayanti kayā devatayeti
(2,6.6 h) gāyatreṇa chandasāgninā devatayeti brūyāt
(2,6.6 i) devān ha yajñaṃ tanvānānasurarakṣāṃsyabhicerire yajñaparvaṇi yajñameṣāṃ haniṣyāmastṛtīyasavanaṃ prati
(2,6.6 j) tṛtīyasavane ha yajñastvariṣṭo baliṣṭhaḥ
(2,6.6 k) pratanumeṣāṃ yajñaṃ haniṣyāma iti
(2,6.6 l) te varuṇaṃ dakṣiṇato'yojayan
(2,6.6 m) madhyato bṛhaspatim
(2,6.6 n) uttarato viṣṇum
(2,6.6 o) te'bruvannekaikāḥ smaḥ
(2,6.6 p) nedamutsahāmaha iti
(2,6.6 ṝ) stuto dvitīyo yenedaṃ saha vyaśnavāmahā iti
(2,6.6 r) tānindro'bravītsarve maddvitīyā stheti
(2,6.6 s) te sarva indradvitīyās
(2,6.6 t) tasmādaindrāvāruṇamaindrābārhaspatyamaindrāvaiṣṇavamanuśasyate
(2,6.6 u) dvitīyavanto ha vā etena svā bhavanti
(2,6.6 v) dvitīyavanto manyante ya evaṃ veda || 6 ||

(2,6.7 ) āgneyīṣu maitrāvaruṇasyokthaṃ praṇayanti
(2,6.7 ) vīryaṃ vā agniḥ
(2,6.7 ) vīryeṇaivāsmai tatpraṇayanti
(2,6.7 ) aindrāvāruṇamanuśasyate
(2,6.7 ) vīryaṃ vā indraḥ
(2,6.7 ) kṣatraṃ varuṇaḥ
(2,6.7 ) paśava ukthāni
(2,6.7 ) vīryeṇaiva tatkṣatreṇa cobhayataḥ paśūn parigṛhṇāti
(2,6.7 ) sthityai
(2,6.7 ) anapakrāntyai
(2,6.7 ) aindrīṣu brāhmaṇācchaṃsina ukthaṃ praṇayanti
(2,6.7 ) vīryaṃ vā indraḥ
(2,6.7 ) vīryeṇaivāsmai tatpraṇayanti
(2,6.7 ) aindrābārhaspatyamanuśasyate
(2,6.7 ) vīryaṃ vā indraḥ
(2,6.7 ) brahma bṛhaspatiḥ
(2,6.7 ) paśava ukthāni
(2,6.7 ) vīryeṇaiva tadbrahmaṇā cobhayataḥ paśūn parigṛhṇāti
(2,6.7 ) sthityai
(2,6.7 ) anapakrāntyai
(2,6.7 ) aindrīṣvacchāvākasyokthaṃ praṇayanti
(2,6.7 ) vīryaṃ vā indraḥ
(2,6.7 ) vīryeṇaivāsmai tatpraṇayanti
(2,6.7 ) aindrāvaiṣṇavamanuśasyate
(2,6.7 ) vīryaṃ vā indraḥ
(2,6.7ś) yajño viṣṇuḥ
(2,6.7 a) paśava ukthāni
(2,6.7 b) vīryeṇaiva tadyajñena cobhayataḥ paśūn parigṛhya kṣatre'ntataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,6.7 c) tasmādu kṣatriyo bhūyiṣṭhaṃ hi paśūnāmīśate [ed. bhuyiṣṭhaṃ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.7 d) yādhiṣṭhātā pradātā yasmai prattā vedā avaruddhās
(2,6.7 e) tānyetānyaindrāṇi jāgatāni śaṃsanti
(2,6.7 f) atho etaireva sendraṃ tṛtīyasavanametairjāgataṃ savanam
(2,6.7 g) dharāṇi ha vā asyaitānyukthāni bhavanti yannābhānediṣṭho vālakhilyo vṛṣākapirevayāmarut
(2,6.7 h) tasmāttāni sārdhamevopeyuḥ
(2,6.7 i) sārdhamidaṃ retaḥ siktaṃ samṛddhamekadhā prajanayāmeti
(2,6.7 j) ye ha vā etāni nānūpeyuryathā retaḥ siktaṃ vilumpetkumāraṃ vā jātamaṅgaśo vibhajettādṛktat
(2,6.7 k) tasmāttāni sārdhamevopeyuḥ
(2,6.7 l) sārdhamidaṃ retaḥ siktaṃ samṛddhamekadhā prajanayāmeti
(2,6.7 m) śilpāni śaṃsati devaśilpāni
(2,6.7 n) eteṣāṃ vai śilpānāmanukṛtīha śilpamadhigamyate
(2,6.7 o) hastī kaṃso vāso hiraṇyamaśvatarīrathaḥ śilpam
(2,6.7 p) śilpaṃ hāsya samadhigamyate ya evaṃ veda
(2,6.7 ṝ) yadeva śilpāni śaṃsati tatsvargasya lokasya rūpam
(2,6.7 r) yadveva śilpānyātmasaṃskṛtirvai śilpāni
(2,6.7 s) ātmānamevāsya tatsaṃskurvanti || 7 ||

(2,6.8 ) nābhānediṣṭhaṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.8 ) reto vai nābhānediṣṭhaḥ
(2,6.8 ) reta evāsya tatkalpayati
(2,6.8 ) tadretomiśraṃ bhavati
(2,6.8 ) <kṣmayā retaḥ saṃjagmāno ni ṣiñcat[ṛV 10.61.7 ]>iti
(2,6.8 ) retasaḥ samṛddhyā eva
(2,6.8 ) taṃ sanāraśaṃsaṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.8 ) prajā vai naraḥ
(2,6.8 ) vākśaṃsaḥ
(2,6.8 ) prajāsu tadvācaṃ dadhāti
(2,6.8 ) tasmādimāḥ prajā vadantyo jāyante
(2,6.8 ) taṃ haike purastātpragāthānāṃ śaṃsanti purustādāyatanā vāgiti vadantaḥ
(2,6.8 ) upariṣṭādeka upariṣṭādāyatanā vāgiti vadantaḥ
(2,6.8 ) madhya eva śaṃset
(2,6.8 ) madhyāyatanā vā iyaṃ vāk
(2,6.8 ) upariṣṭānnedīyasīva
(2,6.8 ) taṃ hotā retobhūtaṃ śastvā maitrāvaruṇāya saṃprayacchati
(2,6.8 ) etasya tvaṃ prāṇān kalpayeti
(2,6.8 ) vālakhilyāḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.8 ) prāṇā vai vālakhilyāḥ
(2,6.8 ) prāṇānevāsya tatkalpayati
(2,6.8 ) tā vihṛtāḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.8 ) vihṛtā vai prāṇāḥ
(2,6.8 ) prāṇenāpānaḥ
(2,6.8 ) apānena vyānaḥ
(2,6.8ś) sa pacchaḥ prathame sūkte viharati
(2,6.8 a) ardharcaśo dvitīye [ed. dvitiye, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.8 b) ṛkśaḥ tṛtīye
(2,6.8 c) sa yatprathame sūkte viharati vācaṃ caiva tan manaśca viharati
(2,6.8 d) yaddvitīye cakṣuścaiva tacchrotraṃ ca viharati
(2,6.8 e) yattṛtīye prāṇāṃ caiva tadātmānaṃ ca viharati
(2,6.8 f) tadupāpto viharetkāmaḥ
(2,6.8 g) neturvai pragāthāḥ kalpante
(2,6.8 h) atimarśameva viharet
(2,6.8 i) tathā vai pragāthāḥ kalpante
(2,6.8 j) yadevātimarśaṃ tatsvargasya lokasya rūpam
(2,6.8 k) yadvevātimarśamātmā vai bṛhatī
(2,6.8 l) prāṇāḥ satobṛhatī
(2,6.8 m) sa bṛhatīmaśaṃsīt
(2,6.8 n) sa ātmā
(2,6.8 o) atha satobṛhatīm
(2,6.8 p) te prāṇāḥ
(2,6.8 ṝ) atha bṛhatīm
(2,6.8 r) atha satobṛhatīm
(2,6.8 s) tadātmānaṃ prāṇaiḥ parivṛhanneti
(2,6.8 t) yadvevātimarśamātmā vai bṛhatī
(2,6.8 u) prajāḥ satobṛhatī
(2,6.8 v) sa bṛhatīmaśaṃsīt
(2,6.8 w) sa ātmā
(2,6.8 x) atha satobṛhatīm
(2,6.8 y) te prajāḥ
(2,6.8śś) atha bṛhatīm
(2,6.8 aa) atha satobṛhatīm
(2,6.8 bb) tadātmānaṃ prajayā parivṛhanneti
(2,6.8 cc) yadvevātimarśamātmā vai bṛhatī
(2,6.8 dd) paśavaḥ satobṛhatī
(2,6.8 ee) sa bṛhatīmaśaṃsīt
(2,6.8 ff) sa ātmā
(2,6.8 gg) atha satobṛhatīm
(2,6.8 hh) te paśavaḥ
(2,6.8 ii) atha bṛhatīm
(2,6.8 jj) atha satobṛhatīm
(2,6.8 kk) tadātmānaṃ paśubhiḥ parivṛhanneti
(2,6.8 ll) tasya maitrāvaruṇaḥ prāṇān kalpayitvā brāhmaṇācchaṃsine saṃprayacchati
(2,6.8 mm) etasya tvaṃ prajanayeti
(2,6.8 nn) sukīrtiṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.8 oo) devayonirvai sukīrtis
(2,6.8 pp) tadyajñiyāyāṃ devayonyāṃ yajamānaṃ prajanayati
(2,6.8 ṝṝ) vṛṣākapiṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.8 rr) ātmā vai vṛṣākapiḥ
(2,6.8 ss) ātmānamevāsya tatkalpayati
(2,6.8 tt) taṃ nyūṅkhayati
(2,6.8 uu) annaṃ vai nyūṅkhaḥ
(2,6.8 vv) annādyamevāsmai tatsaṃprayacchati yathā kumārāya jātāya stanam
(2,6.8 ww) sa pāṅkto bhavati
(2,6.8 xx) pāṅkto hyayaṃ puruṣaḥ pañcadhā vihitaḥ
(2,6.8 yy) lomāni tvagasthi majjā mastiṣkam
(2,6.8śśś) sa yāvāneva puruṣastāvantaṃ yajamānaṃ saṃskṛtyācchāvākāya saṃprayacchati
(2,6.8 aaa) etasya tvaṃ pratiṣṭhā kalpayeti
(2,6.8 bbb) evayāmarutaṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.8 ccc) pratiṣṭhā vā evayāmarut
(2,6.8 ddd) pratiṣṭhāyāmevainamantataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,6.8 eee) yājyayā yajati
(2,6.8 fff) annaṃ vai yājyā
(2,6.8 ggg) annādyamevāsmai tatsaṃprayacchati || 8 ||

(2,6.9 ) tāni vā etāni sahacarāṇītyācakṣate yannābhānediṣṭho vālakhilyo vṛṣākapirevayāmarut
(2,6.9 ) tāni saha vā śaṃsetsaha vā na śaṃset
(2,6.9 ) yadeṣāmantarīyāttadyajamānasyāntarīyāt
(2,6.9 ) yadi nābhānediṣṭhaṃ reto'syāntarīyāt
(2,6.9 ) yadi vālakhilyāḥ prāṇānasyāntarīyāt
(2,6.9 ) yadi vṛṣākapimātmānamasyāntarīyāt
(2,6.9 ) yadyevāyāmarutaṃ pratiṣṭhā vā evayāmarutpratiṣṭhāyā evainaṃ taṃ śrāvayeddaivyāśca mānuṣyāśca
(2,6.9 ) tāni saha vā śaṃsetsaha vā na śaṃset
(2,6.9 ) sa ha buḍila āśvitarāsyurviśvajito hotā sannīkṣāṃ cakra eteṣāṃ vā eṣāṃ śilpānāṃ viśvajiti sāṃvatsarike dve hoturukthe mādhyaṃdinamabhipracyavete
(2,6.9 ) hantāhamitthamevāyāparutaṃ śaṃsayānīti
(2,6.9 ) taddha tathā śaṃsayāṃ cakre
(2,6.9 ) taddha tathā śasyamāne gośla ājagama
(2,6.9 ) sa hovāca hotaḥ kathā te śastraṃ vicakraṃ plavata iti
(2,6.9 ) kiṃ hyabhūditi
(2,6.9 ) evayāmarudayamuttarataḥ śasyata iti
(2,6.9 ) sa hovācaindro vai mādhyaṃdinaḥ
(2,6.9 ) kathendraṃ mādhyaṃdinānninīṣasīti
(2,6.9 ) nendraṃ mādhyaṃdinānninīṣāmīti sa hovāca
(2,6.9 ) chandastvidamamādhyaṃdinaṃsāci
(2,6.9 ) jāgataṃ vātijāgataṃ vā
(2,6.9 ) sa u mārutaḥ
(2,6.9 ) maivaṃ saṃmṛṣṭeti
(2,6.9 ) sa hovācāramācchāvāketi
(2,6.9 ) athāsminnanuśāsanamīṣe
(2,6.9 ) sa hovācaindrameṣa viṣṇunyaṅgāni śaṃsati
(2,6.9ś) atha tvaṃ hoturupariṣṭādraudriyā dhāyyāyāḥ purastān mārutasya sūktasyāpyasyathā iti
(2,6.9 a) tatheti
(2,6.9 b) tadapyetarhi tathaiva śasyate
(2,6.9 c) yathā ṣaṣṭhe pṛṣṭhyāhani kalpata eva yajñaḥ kalpate yajamānasya prajātiḥ kathamatrāśasta eva nābhānediṣṭho bhavatyatha vālakhilyāḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.9 d) reto vā agre'tha prāṇāḥ
(2,6.9 e) evaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsī
(2,6.9 f) aśasta eva nābhānediṣṭho bhavatyatha vṛṣākapiṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.9 g) reto vā agre'thātmā kathamatra yajamānasya prajātiḥ kathaṃ prāṇā avaruddhā bhavantīti
(2,6.9 h) yajamānaṃ vā etena sarveṇa yajñakratunā saṃskurvanti
(2,6.9 i) sa yathā garbho yonyāmantareva prāṇānasyāntariyāt
(2,6.9 j) yadi vṛṣākapimātmānamasyāntariyādyadyeva yā saṃbhavañchete
(2,6.9 k) na ha vai sakṛdevāgre sarvaṃ saṃbhavati
(2,6.9 l) ekaikaṃ vā aṅgaṃ saṃbhavataḥ saṃbhavati
(2,6.9 m) sarvāṇi cetsamāne'hani kriyeran kalpata eva yajñaḥ kalpate yajamānasya prajātiḥ
(2,6.9 n) atha haivaivayāmarutaṃ hotā śaṃset
(2,6.9 o) tadyāsya pratiṣṭhā [ed. tasyāsya, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.9 p) tasyāmevainamantataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati pratiṣṭhāpayati || 9 || [ed. pratisthāpayati 2 , corr. ড়tyal]

(2,6.10 ) devakṣetraṃ vai ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ
(2,6.10 ) devakṣetraṃ vā eta āgacchanti ye ṣaṣṭhamaharāgacchanti
(2,6.10 ) na vai devā anyo'nyasya gṛhe vasanti narturṛtorgṛhe vasatītyāhus
(2,6.10 ) tadyathāyathamṛtvija ṛtuyājān yajantyasaṃpradāyam
(2,6.10 ) tadyadṛtūn kalpayanti yathāyathaṃ janitā
(2,6.10 ) tadāhurnartupraiṣaiḥ preṣyeyurnartupraiṣairvaṣaṭkuryuḥ
(2,6.10 ) vāgvā ṛtupraiṣāḥ
(2,6.10 ) āpyate vai vākṣaṣṭhe'hanīti
(2,6.10 ) yadṛtupraiṣaiḥ preṣyeyuryadṛtupraiṣairvaṣaṭkuryurvācameva tadāptāṃ śāntāmṛkṇavatīṃ vaharāvaṇīmṛccheyuḥ [ed. ṛktavatīṃ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.10 ) acyutādyajñasya cyaveran
(2,6.10 ) yajñātprāṇātprajāyāḥ paśubhyo jihmā īyus[ed. prāṇān, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.10 ) tasmādṛgmebhya eva preṣitavyam
(2,6.10 ) ṛgmebhyo'dhi vaṣaṭkṛtyam
(2,6.10 ) tanna vācamāptāṃ śāntāmṛktavatīṃ vaharāvaṇīmṛcchanti
(2,6.10 ) nācyutādyajñasya cyaveran
(2,6.10 ) na yajñātprāṇān prajāyāḥ paśubhyo jihmā yanti
(2,6.10 ) pārucchepīrupadadhati dvayoḥ savanayoḥ purastātprasthitayājyānām
(2,6.10 ) rohitaṃ vai nāmaitacchando yatpārucchepam
(2,6.10 ) etena ha vā indraḥ sapta svargāṃllokānārohat
(2,6.10 ) ārohati sapta svargāṃllokān ya evaṃ veda
(2,6.10 ) tadāhuryatpañcapadā eva pañcamasyāhno rūpaṃ ṣaṭpadāḥ ṣaṣṭhasyātha kasmātsaptapadāḥ ṣaṣṭhe'hani śasyanta iti
(2,6.10 ) ṣaḍbhireva padaiḥ ṣaṣṭhamaharavāpnuvantyavachidyevaitadaharyatsaptamam
(2,6.10 ) tadeva saptamena padenābhyāruhyā vasanti
(2,6.10 ) saṃtataistryahairavyavachinnairyanti ya evaṃvidvāṃsa upayanti || 10 ||

(2,6.11 ) devāsurā vā eṣu lokeṣu samayatanta
(2,6.11 ) te vai devāḥ ṣaṣṭhenāhnaibhyo lokebhyo'surān parāṇudanta
(2,6.11 ) teṣāṃ yānyantarhastāni vasūnyāsaṃstānyādāya samudraṃ prārūpyanta
(2,6.11 ) teṣāṃ vai devā anuhāyaitenaiva chandasāntarhastāni vasūnyādadata
(2,6.11 ) tadevaitatpadaṃ punaḥpadam
(2,6.11 ) sa evāṅkuśa ākuñcanāya
(2,6.11 ) ā dviṣato vasu datte nirevainamebhyaḥ sarvebhyo lokebhyo nudate ya evaṃ veda
(2,6.11 ) dyaurvai devatā ṣaṣṭhamaharvahati
(2,6.11 ) trayastriṃśa stomaḥ
(2,6.11 ) raivataṃ sāma
(2,6.11 ) aticchandaśchandaḥ
(2,6.11 ) yathādevatamenena yathāstomaṃ yathāsāma yathāchandasamṛdhnoti ya evaṃ veda
(2,6.11 ) yadvai samānodarkaṃ tatṣaṣṭhasyāhno rūpam
(2,6.11 ) yadyeva prathamamahastaduttamamahas
(2,6.11 ) tadevaitatpadaṃ punaryatṣaṣṭhaṃ yadaśvavadyadrathavadyatpunarāvṛttaṃ yatpunarnivṛttaṃ yadantarūpaṃ yadasau loko'bhyudito yannābhānediṣṭhaṃ yatpārucchepaṃ yannārāśaṃsaṃ yaddvaipadā yatsaptapadā yatkṛtaṃ yadraivataṃ tattṛtīyasyāhno rūpam
(2,6.11 ) etāni vai ṣaṣṭhasyāhno rūpāṇi
(2,6.11 ) chandasāmu ha ṣaṣṭhenāhnāktānāṃ raso'tyanedat
(2,6.11 ) taṃ prajāpatirudānānnārāśaṃsyā gāyatryā raibhyā triṣṭubhā parikṣityā jagatyā gāthayānuṣṭubhā [ed. gayatryā]
(2,6.11 ) etāni vai chandāṃsi ṣaṣṭhe'hani śastāni bhavantyayātayāmāni
(2,6.11 ) chanadasāmeva tatsarasatāyā ayātayāmatāyai
(2,6.11 ) sarasāni hāsya chandāṃsi ṣaṣṭhe'hani śastāni bhavanti
(2,6.11 ) sarasaiśchandobhiriṣṭaṃ bhavati sarasaiśchandobhiryajñaṃ tanute ya evaṃ veda || 11 ||
(2,6.12 ) atha yaddvaipadau stotriyānurūpau bhavata <imā nu kaṃ bhuvanā sīṣadhāma [ṛV 10.157.1, śṣ20.63.1, 20.124.4]>iti
(2,6.12 ) dvipādvai puruṣaḥ
(2,6.12 ) dvipratiṣṭhaḥ puruṣaḥ
(2,6.12 ) puruṣo vai yajñas
(2,6.12 ) tasmāddvaipadau stotriyānurūpau bhavataḥ
(2,6.12 ) atha sukīrtiṃ śaṃsaty<apendra prāco maghavannamitrān [ড়ṣ19.16.8 , śṣ20.125.1 , sakalapāṭha at2.6.4]> iti
(2,6.12 ) devayonirvai sukīrtiḥ
(2,6.12 ) sa ya evametāṃ devayonyāṃ sukīrtiṃ veda kīrtiṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati bhūtānāṃ kīrtimān svarge loke pratitiṣṭhati
(2,6.12 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda
(2,6.12 ) atha vṛṣākapiṃ śaṃsati <vi hi sotorasṛkṣata [ṛV 10.86.1, śṣ10.126.1]>iti
(2,6.12 ) ādityo vai vṛṣākapis
(2,6.12 ) tadyatkampayamāno reto varṣati tasmādvṛṣākapis
(2,6.12 ) tadvṛṣākapervṛṣākapitvam
(2,6.12 ) vṛṣākapiriva vai sa sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti ya evaṃ veda
(2,6.12 ) tasya tṛtīyeṣu pādeṣvādyantayornyūṅkhaninardān karoti
(2,6.12 ) annaṃ vai nyūṅkhaḥ [ed. nyuṅkho, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.12 ) balaṃ ninardaḥ
(2,6.12 ) annādyamevāsmai tadbale nidadhāti
(2,6.12 ) atha kuntāpaṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.12 ) kuyaṃ ha vai nāma kutsitaṃ bhavati
(2,6.12 ) tadyattapati tasmātkuntāpās
(2,6.12 ) tatkuntāpānāṃ kuntāpatvam
(2,6.12 ) tapyante'smai kuyāniti taptakuyaḥ svarge loke pratitiṣṭhati
(2,6.12 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda
(2,6.12 ) tasya caturdaśa prathamā bhavantīdaṃ janā upa śruteti
(2,6.12ś) tāḥ pragrāhaṃ śaṃsati yathā vṛṣākapim
(2,6.12 a) vārṣarūpaṃ hi
(2,6.12 b) vṛṣākapestannyāyametyeva
(2,6.12 c) atha raibhīḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.12 d) <vacyasva rebha vacyasva [ṛVKh5.9.1, śṣ20.127.4]>iti
(2,6.12 e) rebhanto vai devāścarṣayaśca svargaṃ lokamāyan
(2,6.12 f) tathaivaitadyajamānā rebhanta eva svargaṃ lokaṃ yanti
(2,6.12 g) tāḥ pragrāhametyeva
(2,6.12 h) atha pārikṣitāḥ śaṃsati rājño viśvajanīnasyeti
(2,6.12 i) saṃvatsaro vai parikṣit
(2,6.12 j) saṃvatsaro hīdaṃ sarvaṃ parikṣiyatīti
(2,6.12 k) atho khalvāhuragnirvai parikṣit
(2,6.12 l) agnirhīdaṃ sarvaṃ parikṣiyatīti
(2,6.12 m) atho khalvāhurgāthā evaitāḥ kāravyā rājñaḥ parikṣita iti sa nastadyathā kuryāt
(2,6.12 n) gāthā evaitāḥ śastā bhavanti
(2,6.12 o) yadyu vai gāthā agnereva gāthāḥ saṃvatsarasya veti brūyāt
(2,6.12 p) yadyu vai mantro'gnireva mantraḥ saṃvatsarasya veti brūyāt
(2,6.12 ṝ) tāḥ pragrāhametyeva
(2,6.12 r) atha kāravyāḥ śaṃsati<indraḥ kārumabūbudhat[ṛVKh5.11.1, śṣ20.127.11]>iti
(2,6.12 s) yadeva devāḥ kalyāṇaṃ karmākurvaṃstatkāravyābhiravāpnuvan
(2,6.12 t) tathaivaitadyajamānā yadeva devāḥ kalyāṇaṃ karma kurvanti tatkāravyābhiravāpnuvanti
(2,6.12 u) tāḥ pragrāhamityeva
(2,6.12 v) atha diśāṃ kḷptīḥ pūrvaṃ śastvā <yaḥ sabheyo vidathyaḥ [ṛVKh5.12.1, śṣ20.128.1]>iti janakalpā uttarāḥ śaṃsati <yo'nāktākṣo anabhyaktaḥ [ṛVKh5.13.1, śṣ20.128.6]>iti
(2,6.12 w) ṛtavo vai diśaḥ prajananas
(2,6.12 x) tadyaddiśāṃ kḷptīḥ pūrvaṃ śastvā <yaḥ sabheyo vidathyaḥ [ṛVKh5.12.1, śṣ20.128.1]>iti janakalpā uttarāḥ śaṃsatyṛtūneva tatkalpayati
(2,6.12 y) ṛtuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,6.12śś) pratiṣṭhantīridaṃ sarvamanupratitiṣṭhati
(2,6.12 aa) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda
(2,6.12 bb) tā ardharcaśaḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.12 cc) pratiṣṭhityā eva
(2,6.12 dd) athendragāthāḥ śaṃsati <yadindrādo dāśarājñe [ṛVKh5.14.1, śṣ20.128.12]>iti
(2,6.12 ee) indragāthābhirha vai devā asurānāgāyāthainānatyāyan
(2,6.12 ff) tathaivaitadyajamānā indragāthābhirevāpriyaṃ bhrātṛvyamāgāyāthainamatiyanti
(2,6.12 gg) tā ardharcaśaḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.12 hh) pratiṣṭhityā eva || 12 ||

(2,6.13 ) athaitaśapralāpaṃ śaṃsaty<etā aśvā ā plavante [ṛVKh5.15.1, śṣ20.129.1]>iti
(2,6.13 ) aitaśo ha muniryajñasyāyurdadarśa
(2,6.13 ) sa ha putrānuvāca putrakā yajñasyāyurabhidadarśam
(2,6.13 ) tadabhilapiṣyāmi mā mā dṛptaṃ manyadhvamiti
(2,6.13 ) tatheti
(2,6.13 ) tadabhilalāpa
(2,6.13 ) tasya hābhyagniraitaśāyano jyeṣṭhaḥ putro'bhidrutya mukhamapijagrāha bruvan dṛpto naḥ piteti
(2,6.13 ) sa hovāca dhiktvā jālmāparasya pāpiṣṭhāṃ te prajāṃ kariṣyāmīti yo me mukhaṃ prāgrahīryadi jālma mukhaṃ na prāgrahīṣyaḥ śatāyuṣaṃ gāmakariṣyaṃ sahasrāyuṣaṃ puruṣamiti
(2,6.13 ) tasmādabhyagnaya aitaśāyanā ājāneyāḥ santaḥ pāpiṣṭhā anyeṣāṃ balihṛtaḥ pitāyacchantāḥ svena prajāpatinā svayā devatayā
(2,6.13 ) yadaitaśapralāpastatsvargasya lokasya rūpam
(2,6.13 ) yadvevaitaśapralāpo'yātayāmā vā akṣitiraitaśapralāpaḥ [ed. aitaśaitaśapralāpo]
(2,6.13 ) ayātayāmā me yajño'sadakṣitirme yajño'saditi
(2,6.13 ) taṃ vā etamaitaśapralāpaṃ śaṃsati padāvagrāham [ed. vā aitaśaitaśapralāpaṃ]
(2,6.13 ) tāsāmuttamena padena praṇauti yathā nividaḥ [ed. prāṇauti, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.13 ) atha pravalhikāḥ pūrvaṃ śastvā <vitatau kiraṇau dvau [ṛVKh5.16.1, śṣ20.133.1]>iti pratirādhānuttarān śaṃsati <bhugityabhigataḥ [ṛVKh5.18.1, śṣ20.135.1]>iti [ed. pratirādhānuttarānḥ]
(2,6.13 ) pravalhikābhirha vai devā asurāṇāṃ rasān pravavṛhus
(2,6.13 ) tadyathābhirha vai devā asurāṇāṃ rasān pravavṛhustasmātpravalhikās
(2,6.13 ) tatpravalhikānāṃ pravalhikātvam
(2,6.13 ) tā vai pratirādhaiḥ pratyarādhnuvan
(2,6.13 ) tadyatpratirādhaiḥ pratyarādhnuvaṃstasmātpratirādhās
(2,6.13 ) tatpratirādhānāṃ pratirādhatvam
(2,6.13 ) pravalhikābhireva dviṣatāṃ bhrātṛvyāṇāṃ rasān pravalhikās
(2,6.13 ) tā vai pratirādhaiḥ pratirādhnuvanti
(2,6.13 ) tāḥ pragrāhamityeva
(2,6.13 ) athājijñāsenyāḥ śaṃsati<ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāk[ṛVKh5.17.1, śṣ20.134.1]>iti [ed. udāg, correctedp. 303]
(2,6.13ś) ājijñāsenyābhirha vai devā asurānājñāyāthainānatyāyan
(2,6.13 a) tathaivaitadyajamānā ājijñāsenyābhirevāpriyaṃ bhrātṛvyamājñāyāthainamatiyanti
(2,6.13 b) tā ardharcaśaḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.13 c) pratiṣṭhityā eva
(2,6.13 d) athātivādaṃ śaṃsati <vīme devā akraṃsata [ṛVKh5.19.1, śṣ20.135.4]>iti
(2,6.13 e) śrīrvā ativādas
(2,6.13 f) tamekarcaṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.13 g) ekastā vai śrīs
(2,6.13 h) tāṃ vai virebhaṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.13 i) virebhaiḥ śriyaṃ puruṣo vahatīti
(2,6.13 j) tāmardharcaśaḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.13 k) pratiṣṭhityā eva || 13 ||

(2,6.14 ) athādityāścāṅgirasīśca śaṃsaty<ādityā ha jaritaraṅgirobhyo adakṣiṇāmanayan [ṛVKh5.20.1, śṣ20.135.6]>iti
(2,6.14 ) taddevanīthamityācakṣate
(2,6.14 ) ādityāśca ha vā aṅgirasaśca svarge loke'spardhanta vayaṃ pūrve svareṣyāmo vayaṃ pūrva iti
(2,6.14 ) te hāṅgirasaḥ śvaḥsutyāṃ dadṛśus
(2,6.14 ) te hāgnimūcuḥ parehyādityebhyaḥ śvaḥsutyāṃ prabrūhīti
(2,6.14 ) athādityā adyasutyāṃ dadṛśus
(2,6.14 ) te hāgnimūcuradyasutyāsmākam
(2,6.14 ) teṣāṃ nastvaṃ hotāsītyupemastvāmiti
(2,6.14 ) sa etyāgniruvācāthādityā adyasutyāmīkṣante kaṃ vo hotāramavocan vāhvayante yuṣmākaṃ vayamiti
(2,6.14 ) te hāṅgirasaścukrudhurmā tvaṃ gamo nu vayamiti
(2,6.14 ) neti hāgniruvācānindyā vai māhvayante
(2,6.14 ) kilbiṣaṃ hi tadyo'nindyasya havaṃ naiti
(2,6.14 ) tasmādatidrūramatyalpamiti yajamānasya havamiyādeva
(2,6.14 ) kilbiṣaṃ hi tadyo'nindyasya havaṃ naiti
(2,6.14 ) tān hādityānaṅgiraso yājayāṃ cakrus
(2,6.14 ) tebhyo hīmāṃ pṛthivīṃ dakṣiṇāṃ ninyus
(2,6.14 ) tāṃ ha na pratijagṛhuḥ
(2,6.14 ) sā hīyaṃ nivṛttobhayataḥśīrṣṇī dakṣiṇāḥ śucā viddhāḥ śocamānā vyacaratkupitā māṃ na pratyagrahīṣuriti
(2,6.14 ) tasyā ete niradīryanta ya ete pradarā adhigamyante
(2,6.14 ) tasmānnivṛttadakṣiṇāṃ nopākuryānnaināṃ pramṛjet
(2,6.14 ) neddakṣiṇāṃ pramṛṇajānīti
(2,6.14 ) tasmādya evāsya samānajanmā bhrātṛvyaḥ syādvṛṇahūyustasmā enāṃ dadyāt
(2,6.14 ) tanna parācī dakṣiṇā vivṛṇakti
(2,6.14 ) dviṣati bhrātṛvye'ntataḥ śucaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati yo'yau tapati
(2,6.14 ) sa vai śaṃsaty<ādityā ha jaritaraṅgirobhyo dakṣiṇāmanayaṃstāṃ ha jaritaḥ pratyāyan [ṛVKh5.20.1 bc, śṣ20.135.6 bc]>iti
(2,6.14ś) na hīmāṃ pṛthivīṃ pratyāyan
(2,6.14 a) <tāmu ha jaritaḥ pratyāyan [ṛVKh5.20.1 , śṣ20.135.6 ]>iti prati hi te'mumāyan
(2,6.14 b) <tāṃ ha jaritarnaḥ pratyagṛbhṇan [ṛVKh5.20.2 , śṣ20.135.7 ]>iti
(2,6.14 c) na hīmāṃ pṛthivīṃ pratyagṛbhṇan
(2,6.14 d) <tāmu ha jaritarnaḥ pratyagṛbhṇa [ṛVKh5.20.2 , śṣ20.135.7 ]>iti pragṛhyādityamagṛbhṇan
(2,6.14 e) <ahānetarasaṃ na vicetanāni [ṛVKh5.20.2 , śṣ20.135.7 ]>ityeṣa ha vā ahnāṃ vicetā yo'sau tapati
(2,6.14 f) sa vai śaṃsati <yajñānetarasaṃ na purogavāsaḥ [ṛVKh5.20.2 , śṣ20.135.7 ]>iti
(2,6.14 g) eṣā ha vai yajñasya purogavī yaddakṣiṇā
(2,6.14 h) yathārhāmaḥ srastamatiretadantyeteṣa eveśvara unnetā
(2,6.14 i) <uta śveta āśupatvā uta padyābhiryaviṣṭha utemāśu mānaṃ piparti [ṛVKh5.20.3, śṣ20.135.8]>iti
(2,6.14 j) eṣa eva śveta eṣa śiśupatyaiṣa uta padyābhiryaviṣṭhaḥ
(2,6.14 k) <utemāśu mānaṃ piparti [ṛVKh5.20.3 , śṣ20.135.8 ]>iti
(2,6.14 l) <ādityā rudrā vasavastenuta idaṃ rādhaḥ pratigṛbhṇīhyaṅgiraḥ | idaṃ rādho vibhu prabhu idaṃ rādho bṛhatpṛthu devā dadatvāsuraṃ tadvo astu sucetanam | yuṣmāṃ astu dive dive pratyeva gṛbhāyata [ṛVKh5.20.45, śṣ20.135.910]>iti tadyadādityāścāṅgirasīśca śaṃsati svargatāyā evaitat [ed. yasṃāṃ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.14 m) aharahaḥ śaṃsati yathā nividaḥ
(2,6.14 n) atha bhūtechadaḥ śaṃsati <tvamindra śarma riṇā [ṛVKh5.21.1, śṣ20.135.11]>iti
(2,6.14 o) ime vai lokā bhūtechadaḥ
(2,6.14 p) asurān ha vai devā annaṃ secire bhūtena jighāṃsantastitīrṣamāṇās
(2,6.14 ṝ) tānime devāḥ sarvebhyo bhūtebhyo'chādayan
(2,6.14 r) tadyadetānime devāḥ sarvebhyo'chādayaṃstasmādbhūtechadas
(2,6.14 s) tadbhūtechadāṃ bhūtechadatvam
(2,6.14 t) chādayanti ha vāparamime lokāḥ
(2,6.14 u) sarvebhyo bhūtebhyo niraghnan
(2,6.14 v) sarvebhyo bhūtebhyo chandate ya evaṃ veda || 14 ||

(2,6.15 ) athāhanasyāḥ śaṃsati <yadasyā aṃhubhedyāḥ [ṛVKh5.22.1, śṣ20.136.1]>iti
(2,6.15 ) āhanasyādvā idaṃ sarvaṃ prajātam
(2,6.15 ) āhanasyādvā etadadhiprajāyate
(2,6.15 ) asyaiva sarvasyāptyai prajātyai
(2,6.15 ) tā vai ṣaṭśaṃset
(2,6.15 ) ṣaḍvā ṛtavaḥ
(2,6.15 ) ṛtavaḥ pitaraḥ
(2,6.15 ) pitaraḥ prajāpatiḥ
(2,6.15 ) prajāpatirāhanasyās
(2,6.15 ) tā daśa śaṃsediti śāmbhavyasya vacaḥ
(2,6.15 ) daśākṣarā virāṭ
(2,6.15 ) vairājo yajñas
(2,6.15 ) taṃ garbhā upajīvanti
(2,6.15 ) śrīrvai virāṭ
(2,6.15 ) yaśo'nnādyam
(2,6.15 ) śriyameva tadvirājaṃ yaśasyannādye pratiṣṭhāpayati
(2,6.15 ) pratitiṣṭhantīridaṃ sarvamanupratitiṣṭhati
(2,6.15 ) pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda
(2,6.15 ) tisraḥ śaṃsediti vātsyas
(2,6.15 ) trivṛdvai retaḥ siktaṃ saṃbhavatyāṇḍamulvaṃ jarāyu
(2,6.15 ) trivṛtpratyayaṃ mātā pitā yajjāyate tattṛtīyam
(2,6.15 ) abhūtodyamevaitadyaccaturthīṃ śaṃset
(2,6.15 ) sarvā eva ṣoḍaśa śaṃsediti haike
(2,6.15 ) kāmārto vai retaḥ siñcati [ed. kāmārtau, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.15 ) retasaḥ siktātprajāḥ prajāyante prajānāṃ prajananāya
(2,6.15ś) prajāvān prajanayiṣṇurbhavati prajātyai prajāyate prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda || 15 ||

(2,6.16 ) atha dādhikrīṃ śaṃsati <dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣam [ṛV 4.39.6, ṛVKh5.22.13, śṣ20.137.3]> iti
(2,6.16 ) tata uttarāḥ pāvamānīḥ śaṃsati <sutāso madhumattamāḥ [ṛV 9.101.4, śṣ20.137.4]>ityannaṃ vai dadhikrī [ed. uttārāḥ, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.16 ) pavitraṃ pāvamānyas
(2,6.16 ) tadu haike pāvamānībhireva pūrvaṃ śastvā tata uttarā dādhikrīṃ śaṃsantīyaṃ vāgannādyā yaḥ pavata iti vadantas
(2,6.16 ) tadu tathā na kūryādupanaśyati ha vāgaśanāyatī
(2,6.16 ) sa dādhikrīmeva pūrvaṃ śastvā tata uttarāḥ pāvamānīḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.16 ) tadyaddādhikrīṃ śaṃsatīyaṃ vāgāhanasyāṃ vācamavādīt
(2,6.16 ) taddevapavitreṇaiva vācaṃ punīte [ed. deva pavitreṇa, corr. ড়tyal]
(2,6.16 ) sā vā anuṣṭubbhavati
(2,6.16 ) vāgvā anuṣṭup
(2,6.16 ) tatsvenaiva chandasā vācaṃ punīte
(2,6.16 ) tāmardharcaśaḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.16 ) pratiṣṭhityā eva
(2,6.16 ) atha pāvamānīḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.16 ) pavitraṃ vai pāvamānyaḥ
(2,6.16 ) iyaṃ vāgāhanasyāṃ vācamavādīt
(2,6.16 ) tatpāvamānībhireva vācaṃ punīte
(2,6.16 ) tāḥ sarvā anuṣṭubho bhavanti
(2,6.16 ) vāgvā anuṣṭup
(2,6.16 ) tatsvenaiva chandasā vācaṃ punīte
(2,6.16 ) tā ardharcaśaḥ śaṃsati
(2,6.16 ) pratiṣṭhityā eva
(2,6.16 ) <ava drapso aṃśumatīmatiṣṭhat[ṛV 8.96.13, śṣ20.137.7]>ityetaṃ tṛcamaindrābārhaspatyaṃ sūktaṃ śaṃsati
(2,6.16 ) atha haitadutsṛṣṭam
(2,6.16 ) tadyadetaṃ tṛcamaindrābārhaspatyamantyaṃ tṛcamaindrājāgataṃ śaṃsati savanadhāraṇamidaṃ gulmaha iti vadantas
(2,6.16ś) tadu tathā na kuryāt
(2,6.16 a) triṣṭubāyatanā vā iyaṃ vāgeṣāṃ hotrakāṇāṃ yadaindrābārhaspatyā tṛtīyasavane
(2,6.16 b) tadyadetaṃ tṛcamaindrābārhaspatyamantyaṃ tṛcamaindrājāgataṃ śaṃsati sva evainaṃ tadāyatane prīṇāti svayordevatayoḥ
(2,6.16 c) kāmaṃ nityameva paridadhyāt
(2,6.16 d) kāmaṃ tṛcasyottamayā
(2,6.16 e) tadāhuḥ saṃśaṃsetṣaṣṭhe'hani na saṃśaṃset
(2,6.16 f) kathamanyeṣvahaḥsu saṃśaṃsati
(2,6.16 g) kathamatra na saṃśaṃsatīti
(2,6.16 h) atho khalvāhurnaiva saṃśaṃset
(2,6.16 i) svargau vai lokaḥ ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ
(2,6.16 j) asamāyī vai svargo lokaḥ
(2,6.16 k) kaścidvai svarge loke śamayatīti
(2,6.16 l) tasmānna saṃśaṃsati
(2,6.16 m) yadeva na saṃśaṃsati tatsvargasya lokasya rūpam
(2,6.16 n) yadvevaināḥ saṃśaṃsati yannābhānediṣṭho vālakhilyo vṛṣākapirevayāmarudetāni vā atrokthāni bhavanti tasmānna saṃśaṃsati
(2,6.16 o) aindro vṛṣākapiḥ
(2,6.16 p) sarvāṇi chandāṃsyaitaśapralāpaḥ
(2,6.16 ṝ) upāpto yadaindrābārhaspatyā tṛtīyasavane tadyadetaṃ tṛcamaindrābārhaspatyaṃ sūktaṃ śaṃsatyaindrābārhaspatyā paridhānīyā viśo adevīrabhyācarantīriti
(2,6.16 r) aparajanā ha vai viśo devīrna hyasyāparajanaṃ bhayaṃ bhavati
(2,6.16 s) śāntāḥ prajāḥ kḷptāḥ sahante yatraivaṃvidaṃ śaṃsati yatraivaṃvidaṃ śaṃsatīti brāhmaṇam || 16 ||

(2,6.16 ol) ityatharvavede gopathabrāhmaṇottarabhāge ṣaṣṭhaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ ||

(col) ityatharvavedabrāhmaṇapūrvottaraṃ samāptam ||
